	Would you like me to:

    Generate the actual Excel file with all formulas and validation (Python code to create XLSX)?
	I'll create a comprehensive Python script that generates a complete Excel file with all formulas, data validation, conditional formatting, and professional styling.

---

# COMPLETE EXCEL TEMPLATE GENERATOR

```python
"""
Complete Bridge GAD Excel Template Generator
Creates a professional Excel file with formulas, validation, and formatting
"""

import openpyxl
from openpyxl import Workbook
from openpyxl.styles import (
    Font, PatternFill, Alignment, Border, Side, 
    Protection, NamedStyle
)
from openpyxl.utils import get_column_letter
from openpyxl.worksheet.datavalidation import DataValidation
from openpyxl.formatting.rule import FormulaRule, CellIsRule
from openpyxl.worksheet.table import Table, TableStyleInfo
import os


class BridgeExcelTemplateGenerator:
    """
    Generates complete Excel template for Bridge GAD system
    """
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.wb = Workbook()
        # Remove default sheet
        self.wb.remove(self.wb.active)
        
        # Define styles
        self._define_styles()
        
        # Define named ranges for dropdowns
        self.named_ranges = {}
    
    def _define_styles(self):
        """Define cell styles"""
        
        # Header style
        self.header_style = NamedStyle(name="header_style")
        self.header_style.font = Font(bold=True, color="FFFFFF", size=11)
        self.header_style.fill = PatternFill(start_color="366092", end_color="366092", fill_type="solid")
        self.header_style.alignment = Alignment(horizontal="center", vertical="center", wrap_text=True)
        self.header_style.border = Border(
            left=Side(style='thin'), right=Side(style='thin'),
            top=Side(style='thin'), bottom=Side(style='thin')
        )
        
        # Sub-header style
        self.subheader_style = NamedStyle(name="subheader_style")
        self.subheader_style.font = Font(bold=True, color="000000", size=10)
        self.subheader_style.fill = PatternFill(start_color="B8CCE4", end_color="B8CCE4", fill_type="solid")
        self.subheader_style.alignment = Alignment(horizontal="left", vertical="center")
        self.subheader_style.border = Border(
            left=Side(style='thin'), right=Side(style='thin'),
            top=Side(style='thin'), bottom=Side(style='thin')
        )
        
        # Input cell style
        self.input_style = NamedStyle(name="input_style")
        self.input_style.fill = PatternFill(start_color="FFFFCC", end_color="FFFFCC", fill_type="solid")
        self.input_style.border = Border(
            left=Side(style='thin'), right=Side(style='thin'),
            top=Side(style='thin'), bottom=Side(style='thin')
        )
        
        # Calculated cell style
        self.calculated_style = NamedStyle(name="calculated_style")
        self.calculated_style.fill = PatternFill(start_color="E0E0E0", end_color="E0E0E0", fill_type="solid")
        self.calculated_style.font = Font(italic=True, color="666666")
        self.calculated_style.border = Border(
            left=Side(style='thin'), right=Side(style='thin'),
            top=Side(style='thin'), bottom=Side(style='thin')
        )
        self.calculated_style.protection = Protection(locked=True)
        
        # Validation error style
        self.error_style = NamedStyle(name="error_style")
        self.error_style.fill = PatternFill(start_color="FFCCCC", end_color="FFCCCC", fill_type="solid")
        
        # Valid style
        self.valid_style = NamedStyle(name="valid_style")
        self.valid_style.fill = PatternFill(start_color="CCFFCC", end_color="CCFFCC", fill_type="solid")
        
        # Add styles to workbook
        for style in [self.header_style, self.subheader_style, self.input_style, 
                     self.calculated_style, self.error_style, self.valid_style]:
            if style.name not in self.wb.named_styles:
                self.wb.add_named_style(style)
    
    def _create_dropdown_list(self, sheet, cell_range, options, prompt="Select from list"):
        """Create dropdown validation"""
        dv = DataValidation(
            type="list",
            formula1=f'"{",".join(options)}"',
            allow_blank=True
        )
        dv.prompt = prompt
        dv.promptTitle = "Selection"
        sheet.add_data_validation(dv)
        dv.add(cell_range)
    
    def _create_numeric_validation(self, sheet, cell_range, min_val, max_val, 
                                   prompt="Enter a number"):
        """Create numeric validation"""
        dv = DataValidation(
            type="decimal",
            operator="between",
            formula1=str(min_val),
            formula2=str(max_val),
            allow_blank=True
        )
        dv.prompt = f"{prompt} (min: {min_val}, max: {max_val})"
        dv.promptTitle = "Numeric Input"
        dv.error = f"Value must be between {min_val} and {max_val}"
        dv.errorTitle = "Invalid Input"
        sheet.add_data_validation(dv)
        dv.add(cell_range)
    
    def _set_column_widths(self, sheet, widths):
        """Set column widths"""
        for col_idx, width in enumerate(widths, 1):
            sheet.column_dimensions[get_column_letter(col_idx)].width = width
    
    def _apply_conditional_formatting(self, sheet, cell_range, formula, 
                                      true_fill="CCFFCC", false_fill="FFCCCC"):
        """Apply conditional formatting"""
        from openpyxl.formatting.rule import CellIsRule, FormulaRule
        
        # Green for valid/true
        green_fill = PatternFill(start_color=true_fill, end_color=true_fill, fill_type="solid")
        # Red for invalid/false
        red_fill = PatternFill(start_color=false_fill, end_color=false_fill, fill_type="solid")
        
        # Add rules
        sheet.conditional_formatting.add(cell_range, 
            FormulaRule(formula=[formula], fill=green_fill))
    
    def create_project_master_sheet(self):
        """Create PROJECT_MASTER sheet"""
        ws = self.wb.create_sheet("PROJECT_MASTER")
        
        # Title
        ws['A1'] = "PROJECT MASTER DATA"
        ws['A1'].font = Font(bold=True, size=14, color="366092")
        ws.merge_cells('A1:D1')
        
        # Headers
        headers = ['Field', 'Value', 'Unit', 'Description']
        for col, header in enumerate(headers, 1):
            cell = ws.cell(row=3, column=col, value=header)
            cell.style = "header_style"
        
        # Data rows
        fields = [
            ('Project Title', '', '', 'Full project name'),
            ('Project Code', 'PRJ-2024-001', '', 'Unique project code'),
            ('Bridge Name', 'Chambakkara Bridge', '', 'Bridge name'),
            ('Chainage (Km)', 600.000, 'Km', 'Chainage along alignment'),
            ('Package No', 'Pkg-01', '', 'Contract package number'),
            ('Client/Authority', '', '', 'NHAI/State PWD/etc'),
            ('Consultant', '', '', 'Design consultant'),
            ('Contractor', '', '', 'Executing agency'),
            ('Drawing No', '=CONCATENATE(B4,"-GAD-001")', '', 'Auto-generated'),
            ('Drawing Title', '=CONCATENATE("GAD of ",B5)', '', 'Auto-generated'),
            ('Revision', 'R0', '', 'Current revision'),
            ('Date', '', '', 'Drawing date (YYYY-MM-DD)'),
            ('Designed By', '', '', 'Design engineer'),
            ('Checked By', '', '', 'Checker'),
            ('Approved By', '', '', 'Approver')
        ]
        
        for row_idx, (field, value, unit, desc) in enumerate(fields, 4):
            ws.cell(row=row_idx, column=1, value=field).style = "subheader_style"
            
            value_cell = ws.cell(row=row_idx, column=2, value=value)
            if isinstance(value, str) and value.startswith('='):
                value_cell.value = value
                value_cell.style = "calculated_style"
            else:
                value_cell.style = "input_style"
            
            ws.cell(row=row_idx, column=3, value=unit).alignment = Alignment(horizontal="center")
            ws.cell(row=row_idx, column=4, value=desc).alignment = Alignment(wrap_text=True)
        
        # Set column widths
        self._set_column_widths(ws, [30, 35, 10, 40])
        
        # Add dropdown for Client
        self._create_dropdown_list(ws, 'B8', 
            ['NHAI', 'State PWD', 'Railways', 'Municipal Corporation', 'Private'],
            "Select client/authority")
        
        # Add date validation for date field
        dv = DataValidation(type="date", allow_blank=True)
        dv.prompt = "Enter date in YYYY-MM-DD format"
        ws.add_data_validation(dv)
        dv.add('B15')
        
        return ws
    
    def create_bridge_selection_sheet(self):
        """Create BRIDGE_SELECTION sheet"""
        ws = self.wb.create_sheet("BRIDGE_SELECTION")
        
        ws['A1'] = "BRIDGE TYPE SELECTION"
        ws['A1'].font = Font(bold=True, size=14, color="366092")
        ws.merge_cells('A1:D1')
        
        # Headers
        headers = ['Parameter', 'Value', 'Options', 'Description']
        for col, header in enumerate(headers, 1):
            cell = ws.cell(row=3, column=col, value=header)
            cell.style = "header_style"
        
        # Data
        selections = [
            ('Bridge Category', '', 'Major/Minor/Viaduct/ROB/RUB/FOB/Culvert', 'Type of bridge'),
            ('Bridge Type', '', 'PSC/Steel/Composite/Arch/Truss', 'Superstructure type'),
            ('Span Configuration', 'Simply Supported', 'Simply/Continuous/Cantilever', 'Structural system'),
            ('Span Count', 3, '1-20', 'Number of spans'),
            ('Carriageway Config', '2-Lane', '1/2/4/6-Lane/Multi-level', 'Traffic lanes'),
            ('Design Code', 'IRC:5-2015', 'IRC:5/IRC:6/MORTH', 'Design standard'),
            ('Loading Class', 'IRC Class A', 'Class A/70R/AA/Special', 'Design loading'),
            ('Seismic Zone', 'Zone III', 'Zone II/III/IV/V', 'Seismic zone'),
            ('Wind Zone', 'Basic 39 m/s', '33/39/44/47/50/55', 'Basic wind speed'),
            ('Foundation Type', 'Pile', 'Open/Pile/Well/Raft', 'Foundation type')
        ]
        
        for row_idx, (param, value, options, desc) in enumerate(selections, 4):
            ws.cell(row=row_idx, column=1, value=param).style = "subheader_style"
            ws.cell(row=row_idx, column=2, value=value).style = "input_style"
            ws.cell(row=row_idx, column=3, value=options).alignment = Alignment(wrap_text=True)
            ws.cell(row=row_idx, column=4, value=desc)
        
        # Add dropdowns
        self._create_dropdown_list(ws, 'B4', 
            ['Major Bridge', 'Minor Bridge', 'Viaduct', 'ROB', 'RUB', 'FOB', 'Culvert'])
        
        self._create_dropdown_list(ws, 'B5',
            ['RCC T-Beam', 'PSC I-Girder', 'PSC Box Girder', 'Steel Composite', 
             'Solid Slab', 'Voided Slab', 'Arch', 'Truss', 'Cable Stayed'])
        
        self._create_dropdown_list(ws, 'B6',
            ['Simply Supported', 'Continuous', 'Cantilever', 'Cable Stayed', 'Suspension'])
        
        self._create_dropdown_list(ws, 'B8',
            ['1-Lane', '2-Lane', '4-Lane', '6-Lane', 'Multi-level'])
        
        self._create_dropdown_list(ws, 'B9',
            ['IRC:5-2015', 'IRC:6-2017', 'IRC:21-2000', 'IRC:24-2010', 'MORTH'])
        
        self._create_dropdown_list(ws, 'B10',
            ['IRC Class A', 'IRC 70R', 'IRC AA', 'Special Vehicle'])
        
        self._create_dropdown_list(ws, 'B11',
            ['Zone II', 'Zone III', 'Zone IV', 'Zone V'])
        
        self._create_dropdown_list(ws, 'B13',
            ['Open', 'Pile', 'Well', 'Raft', 'Combined'])
        
        # Conditional formatting for foundation type
        self._apply_conditional_formatting(ws, 'B13', '$B$13="Pile"')
        
        self._set_column_widths(ws, [25, 20, 35, 35])
        
        return ws
    
    def create_geometry_input_sheet(self):
        """Create GEOMETRY_INPUT sheet with formulas"""
        ws = self.wb.create_sheet("GEOMETRY_INPUT")
        
        ws['A1'] = "GEOMETRY PARAMETERS"
        ws['A1'].font = Font(bold=True, size=14, color="366092")
        ws.merge_cells('A1:E1')
        
        # Section A: Alignment
        ws['A3'] = "A. ALIGNMENT DATA"
        ws['A3'].font = Font(bold=True, size=11)
        ws.merge_cells('A3:E3')
        
        alignment_data = [
            ('Road Width', 12.0, 'm', 3, 30, 'Total road width'),
            ('Design Speed', 100, 'kmph', 20, 150, 'Design speed'),
            ('Horizontal Curve R', 0, 'm', 0, 10000, '0 = Straight'),
            ('Gradient', 2.0, '%', -10, 10, '+ = Upward')
        ]
        
        row = 4
        for field, value, unit, min_v, max_v, desc in alignment_data:
            ws.cell(row=row, column=1, value=field).style = "subheader_style"
            cell = ws.cell(row=row, column=2, value=value)
            cell.style = "input_style"
            cell.number_format = '0.00'
            self._create_numeric_validation(ws, f'B{row}', min_v, max_v)
            ws.cell(row=row, column=3, value=unit)
            ws.cell(row=row, column=4, value=desc)
            row += 1
        
        # Section B: Bridge Geometry
        ws[f'A{row+1}'] = "B. BRIDGE GEOMETRY"
        ws[f'A{row+1}'].font = Font(bold=True, size=11)
        ws.merge_cells(f'A{row+1}:E{row+1}')
        
        row += 2
        start_span_row = row
        
        # Span count with formula
        ws.cell(row=row, column=1, value="Span Count").style = "subheader_style"
        ws.cell(row=row, column=2, value=3).style = "input_style"
        self._create_numeric_validation(ws, f'B{row}', 1, 20)
        ws.cell(row=row, column=3, value="nos")
        ws.cell(row=row, column=4, value="Number of spans")
        row += 1
        
        # Dynamic span length inputs (up to 20 spans)
        for i in range(1, 21):
            ws.cell(row=row, column=1, value=f"Span-{i} Length").style = "subheader_style"
            cell = ws.cell(row=row, column=2, value=30.0 if i <= 3 else "")
            cell.style = "input_style"
            cell.number_format = '0.00'
            self._create_numeric_validation(ws, f'B{row}', 3, 200)
            ws.cell(row=row, column=3, value="m")
            ws.cell(row=row, column=4, value=f"Span {i} length")
            row += 1
        
        # Total length formula
        total_row = row
        ws.cell(row=row, column=1, value="TOTAL BRIDGE LENGTH").style = "subheader_style"
        # Formula to sum B{start_span_row+1} to B{start_span_row+20}
        formula = f"=SUM(B{start_span_row+1}:B{start_span_row+20})"
        cell = ws.cell(row=row, column=2, value=formula)
        cell.style = "calculated_style"
        cell.number_format = '0.00'
        ws.cell(row=row, column=3, value="m")
        ws.cell(row=row, column=4, value="Auto-calculated")
        row += 1
        
        # Longest span
        ws.cell(row=row, column=1, value="Longest Span").style = "subheader_style"
        formula = f"=MAX(B{start_span_row+1}:B{start_span_row+20})"
        cell = ws.cell(row=row, column=2, value=formula)
        cell.style = "calculated_style"
        cell.number_format = '0.00'
        ws.cell(row=row, column=3, value="m")
        row += 1
        
        # Average span
        ws.cell(row=row, column=1, value="Average Span").style = "subheader_style"
        formula = f"=AVERAGE(B{start_span_row+1}:B{start_span_row+20})"
        cell = ws.cell(row=row, column=2, value=formula)
        cell.style = "calculated_style"
        cell.number_format = '0.00'
        ws.cell(row=row, column=3, value="m")
        row += 2
        
        # Section C: Cross Section
        ws[f'A{row}'] = "C. CROSS SECTION"
        ws[f'A{row}'].font = Font(bold=True, size=11)
        ws.merge_cells(f'A{row}:E{row}')
        row += 1
        
        cross_section = [
            ('Carriageway Width', 12.0, 'm', 3, 20, 'Clear width'),
            ('Footpath Width (L)', 0.0, 'm', 0, 5, '0 if none'),
            ('Footpath Width (R)', 0.0, 'm', 0, 5, '0 if none'),
            ('Crash Barrier Width', 0.5, 'm', 0.3, 0.6, 'Each side'),
            ('Kerb Width', 0.75, 'm', 0.15, 0.75, 'Each side'),
            ('Median Width', 0.0, 'm', 0, 10, '0 if no median')
        ]
        
        for field, value, unit, min_v, max_v, desc in cross_section:
            ws.cell(row=row, column=1, value=field).style = "subheader_style"
            cell = ws.cell(row=row, column=2, value=value)
            cell.style = "input_style"
            cell.number_format = '0.00'
            self._create_numeric_validation(ws, f'B{row}', min_v, max_v)
            ws.cell(row=row, column=3, value=unit)
            ws.cell(row=row, column=4, value=desc)
            row += 1
        
        # Overall width formula
        ws.cell(row=row, column=1, value="OVERALL WIDTH").style = "subheader_style"
        # Sum of carriageway + footpaths + barriers + kerbs + median
        formula = "=B31+B32+B33+2*B34+2*B35+B36"
        cell = ws.cell(row=row, column=2, value=formula)
        cell.style = "calculated_style"
        cell.number_format = '0.00'
        ws.cell(row=row, column=3, value="m")
        ws.cell(row=row, column=4, value="Auto-calculated")
        row += 1
        
        # Half width
        ws.cell(row=row, column=1, value="Half Width").style = "subheader_style"
        formula = "=B37/2"
        cell = ws.cell(row=row, column=2, value=formula)
        cell.style = "calculated_style"
        cell.number_format = '0.00'
        ws.cell(row=row, column=3, value="m")
        row += 2
        
        # Section D: Skew
        ws[f'A{row}'] = "D. SKEW AND CURVATURE"
        ws[f'A{row}'].font = Font(bold=True, size=11)
        ws.merge_cells(f'A{row}:E{row}')
        row += 1
        
        ws.cell(row=row, column=1, value="Skew Angle").style = "subheader_style"
        cell = ws.cell(row=row, column=2, value=15.0)
        cell.style = "input_style"
        cell.number_format = '0.0'
        self._create_numeric_validation(ws, f'B{row}', 0, 60)
        ws.cell(row=row, column=3, value="degrees")
        ws.cell(row=row, column=4, value="0 = Square bridge")
        row += 1
        
        ws.cell(row=row, column=1, value="Skew Direction").style = "subheader_style"
        ws.cell(row=row, column=2, value="RIGHT").style = "input_style"
        self._create_dropdown_list(ws, f'B{row}', ['LEFT', 'RIGHT', 'NONE'])
        ws.cell(row=row, column=3, value="-")
        row += 1
        
        ws.cell(row=row, column=1, value="Curved Bridge").style = "subheader_style"
        ws.cell(row=row, column=2, value="NO").style = "input_style"
        self._create_dropdown_list(ws, f'B{row}', ['YES', 'NO'])
        ws.cell(row=row, column=3, value="-")
        row += 1
        
        ws.cell(row=row, column=1, value="Curve Radius").style = "subheader_style"
        ws.cell(row=row, column=2, value=0).style = "input_style"
        ws.cell(row=row, column=3, value="m")
        ws.cell(row=row, column=4, value="0 = Straight")
        row += 1
        
        # Degree of curve formula
        ws.cell(row=row, column=1, value="Degree of Curve").style = "subheader_style"
        formula = "=IF(B44>0, 1718.87/B44, 0)"
        cell = ws.cell(row=row, column=2, value=formula)
        cell.style = "calculated_style"
        cell.number_format = '0.00'
        ws.cell(row=row, column=3, value="degrees")
        
        # Conditional formatting for skew validation
        self._apply_conditional_formatting(ws, 'B40', '$B$40<=45')
        
        self._set_column_widths(ws, [30, 15, 10, 40])
        
        return ws
    
    def create_superstructure_sheet(self):
        """Create SUPERSTRUCTURE sheet"""
        ws = self.wb.create_sheet("SUPERSTRUCTURE")
        
        ws['A1'] = "SUPERSTRUCTURE DETAILS"
        ws['A1'].font = Font(bold=True, size=14, color="366092")
        ws.merge_cells('A1:E1')
        
        headers = ['Element', 'Type/Value', 'Unit', 'Options', 'Description']
        for col, header in enumerate(headers, 1):
            cell = ws.cell(row=3, column=col, value=header)
            cell.style = "header_style"
        
        # Section A: Deck
        ws['A4'] = "A. DECK CONFIGURATION"
        ws['A4'].font = Font(bold=True, size=11)
        ws.merge_cells('A4:E4')
        
        deck_data = [
            ('Superstructure Type', 'T-BEAM', '-', 
             'RCC T-Beam/PSC I-Girder/Box Girder/Solid Slab', 'Type'),
            ('Deck Material', 'M35', '-', 
             'M30/M35/M40/M45/M50/M55', 'Concrete grade'),
            ('Deck Thickness', 250, 'mm', '150-500', 'Slab thickness'),
            ('Wearing Coat Type', 'Bituminous', '-', 
             'Bituminous/Cement Concrete/Polymer', 'Surface type'),
            ('Wearing Coat Thickness', 65, 'mm', '50-100', 'Wearing coat'),
            ('Wearing Coat Slope', 2.5, '%', '1.0-4.0', 'Cross slope'),
            ('Camber', 0, 'mm', '0-150', 'Pre-camber')
        ]
        
        row = 5
        for field, value, unit, options, desc in deck_data:
            ws.cell(row=row, column=1, value=field).style = "subheader_style"
            ws.cell(row=row, column=2, value=value).style = "input_style"
            ws.cell(row=row, column=3, value=unit)
            ws.cell(row=row, column=4, value=options)
            ws.cell(row=row, column=5, value=desc)
            row += 1
        
        # Dropdowns
        self._create_dropdown_list(ws, 'B5', 
            ['RCC T-Beam', 'PSC I-Girder', 'PSC Box Girder', 'Solid Slab', 
             'Voided Slab', 'Steel Composite'])
        
        self._create_dropdown_list(ws, 'B6',
            ['M30', 'M35', 'M40', 'M45', 'M50', 'M55'])
        
        self._create_dropdown_list(ws, 'B8',
            ['Bituminous', 'Cement Concrete', 'Polymer'])
        
        # Section B: Girder
        ws[f'A{row+1}'] = "B. GIRDER DETAILS (For T-Beam/I-Girder)"
        ws[f'A{row+1}'].font = Font(bold=True, size=11)
        ws.merge_cells(f'A{row+1}:E{row+1}')
        row += 2
        
        girder_data = [
            ('Girder Type', 'Standard', '-', 'Standard/Custom', 'Selection'),
            ('Girder Depth', 2000, 'mm', '1000-5000', 'Overall depth'),
            ('Girder Width', 300, 'mm', '200-600', 'Web width'),
            ('Web Thickness', 300, 'mm', '200-400', 'Stem width'),
            ('Flange Width (Top)', 2500, 'mm', '600-2000', 'Top flange'),
            ('Flange Width (Bottom)', 600, 'mm', '300-800', 'Bottom flange'),
            ('Girder Spacing', 3000, 'mm', '1500-5000', 'Center-to-center'),
        ]
        
        for field, value, unit, options, desc in girder_data:
            ws.cell(row=row, column=1, value=field).style = "subheader_style"
            ws.cell(row=row, column=2, value=value).style = "input_style"
            ws.cell(row=row, column=3, value=unit)
            ws.cell(row=row, column=4, value=options)
            ws.cell(row=row, column=5, value=desc)
            row += 1
        
        # Number of girders formula
        ws.cell(row=row, column=1, value="Number of Girders").style = "subheader_style"
        formula = "=CEILING(GEOMETRY_INPUT!B37/B19,1)+1"
        cell = ws.cell(row=row, column=2, value=formula)
        cell.style = "calculated_style"
        ws.cell(row=row, column=3, value="nos")
        row += 1
        
        # Span/depth ratio
        ws.cell(row=row, column=1, value="Span/Depth Ratio").style = "subheader_style"
        formula = "=GEOMETRY_INPUT!B23/(B13/1000)"
        cell = ws.cell(row=row, column=2, value=formula)
        cell.style = "calculated_style"
        cell.number_format = '0.0'
        ws.cell(row=row, column=3, value="-")
        ws.cell(row=row, column=5, value="Should be 15-25")
        row += 1
        
        # Conditional formatting for span/depth ratio
        self._apply_conditional_formatting(ws, f'B{row-1}', 
            'AND(B21>=15, B21<=25)')
        
        self._set_column_widths(ws, [25, 15, 10, 35, 35])
        
        return ws
    
    def create_substructure_sheet(self):

            ws.cell(row=row, column=2, value=value).style = "input_style"

    def create_hydraulic_sheet(self):
        """Create HYDRAULIC_DATA sheet"""
        ws = self.wb.create_sheet("HYDRAULIC_DATA")
        
        ws['A1'] = "HYDRAULIC AND SCOUR DATA"
        ws['A1'].font = Font(bold=True, size=14, color="366092")
        ws.merge_cells('A1:E1')
        
        headers = ['Parameter', 'Value', 'Unit', 'Source', 'Description']
        for col, header in enumerate(headers, 1):
            cell = ws.cell(row=3, column=col, value=header)
            cell.style = "header_style"
        
        hydraulic_data = [
            ('Design Discharge', 1500, 'cumecs', 'Hydrology', 'Q-100'),
            ('High Flood Level (HFL)', 182.5, 'm', 'Survey', 'HFL'),
            ('Low Water Level (LWL)', 178.0, 'm', 'Survey', 'LWL'),
            ('Normal Water Level', 180.0, 'm', 'Survey', 'NWL'),
            ('Maximum Water Level', 183.0, 'm', 'Design', 'Max WL'),
            ('Scour Level', 172.2, 'm', 'Calculated', 'Laceys formula'),
            ('Design Scour Level', 171.7, 'm', 'Calculated', 'Scour + safety'),
            ('Scour Depth', '=B4-B7', 'm', 'Formula', 'Auto-calculated'),
            ('Waterway Required', 180, 'm', 'Calculated', 'Laceys'),
            ('Waterway Provided', '=GEOMETRY_INPUT!B23', 'm', 'Formula', 'Auto'),
            ('Afflux', 0.5, 'm', 'Design', 'Head loss'),
            ('Velocity', 2.5, 'm/s', 'Design', 'Design velocity'),
            ('Bed Level', 175.5, 'm', 'Survey', 'River bed'),
            ('Foundation Level', '=B7', 'm', 'Calculated', 'Below scour'),
            ('Freeboard', 2.0, 'm', 'IRC:5', 'Above HFL'),
            ('Minimum Soffit Level', '=B4+B16', 'm', 'Formula', 'HFL + freeboard'),
            ('Actual Soffit Level', 184.735, 'm', 'Design', 'Design level'),
            ('Vertical Clearance', '=B18-B4', 'm', 'Formula', 'Soffit - HFL')
        ]
        
        row = 4
        for field, value, unit, source, desc in hydraulic_data:
            ws.cell(row=row, column=1, value=field).style = "subheader_style"
            cell = ws.cell(row=row, column=2, value=value)
            if isinstance(value, str) and value.startswith('='):
                cell.style = "calculated_style"
            else:
                cell.style = "input_style"
                cell.number_format = '0.000' if unit == 'm' else '0.0'
            ws.cell(row=row, column=3, value=unit)
            ws.cell(row=row, column=4, value=source)
            ws.cell(row=row, column=5, value=desc)
            row += 1
        
        # Conditional formatting for clearance check
        self._apply_conditional_formatting(ws, 'B19', '$B$19>=5.5')
        
        self._set_column_widths(ws, [25, 15, 10, 15, 35])
        
        return ws

    def create_validation_sheet(self):
        """Create VALIDATION sheet with checks"""
        ws = self.wb.create_sheet("VALIDATION")
        
        ws['A1'] = "VALIDATION CHECKS"
        ws['A1'].font = Font(bold=True, size=14, color="366092")
        ws.merge_cells('A1:E1')
        
        headers = ['Check ID', 'Description', 'Formula', 'Status', 'Result']
        for col, header in enumerate(headers, 1):
            cell = ws.cell(row=3, column=col, value=header)
            cell.style = "header_style"
        
        checks = [
            ('V001', 'Project code format', 
             '=IF(REGEXMATCH(PROJECT_MASTER!B4,"^[A-Z]+-\\d{4}-\\d{3}$"),"✓","✗")'),
            ('V002', 'Chainage positive', 
             '=IF(PROJECT_MASTER!B6>0,"✓","✗")'),
            ('V003', 'Span count matches', 
             '=IF(COUNTA(GEOMETRY_INPUT!B11:B30)=GEOMETRY_INPUT!B10,"✓","✗")'),
            ('V004', 'Span lengths valid', 
             '=IF(AND(GEOMETRY_INPUT!B11:B30>=3,GEOMETRY_INPUT!B11:B30<=200),"✓","✗")'),
            ('V005', 'Total length reasonable', 
             '=IF(GEOMETRY_INPUT!B31<1000,"✓","⚠")'),
            ('V006', 'Width consistent', 
             '=IF(GEOMETRY_INPUT!B37>GEOMETRY_INPUT!B31,"✓","✗")'),
            ('V007', 'Skew angle limit', 
             '=IF(GEOMETRY_INPUT!B40<=60,"✓","✗")'),
            ('V008', 'Span/depth ratio', 
             '=IF(AND(SUPERSTRUCTURE!B21>=15,SUPERSTRUCTURE!B21<=25),"✓","⚠")'),
            ('V009', 'HFL > LWL', 
             '=IF(HYDRAULIC_DATA!B4>HYDRAULIC_DATA!B5,"✓","✗")'),
            ('V010', 'Scour depth adequate', 
             '=IF(HYDRAULIC_DATA!B8>0,"✓","✗")'),
            ('V011', 'Soffit above HFL', 
             '=IF(HYDRAULIC_DATA!B18>HYDRAULIC_DATA!B4,"✓","✗")'),
            ('V012', 'Freeboard adequate', 
             '=IF(HYDRAULIC_DATA!B16>=1.5,"✓","⚠")'),
            ('V013', 'Vertical clearance', 
             '=IF(HYDRAULIC_DATA!B19>=5.5,"✓","✗")'),
            ('V014', 'Foundation below scour', 
             '=IF(FOUNDATION_DETAILS!B12<HYDRAULIC_DATA!B7,"✓","✗")'),
            ('V015', 'Material grades valid', 
             '=IF(AND(MATERIALS!B4<>"",MATERIALS!B8<>""),"✓","✗")')
        ]
        
        row = 4
        for check_id, desc, formula in checks:
            ws.cell(row=row, column=1, value=check_id).style = "subheader_style"
            ws.cell(row=row, column=2, value=desc)
            ws.cell(row=row, column=3, value=formula).font = Font(size=8)
            
            # Status cell with formula
            status_cell = ws.cell(row=row, column=4, value=formula)
            
            # Conditional formatting
            green_fill = PatternFill(start_color="CCFFCC", end_color="CCFFCC", fill_type="solid")
            red_fill = PatternFill(start_color="FFCCCC", end_color="FFCCCC", fill_type="solid")
            yellow_fill = PatternFill(start_color="FFFFCC", end_color="FFFFCC", fill_type="solid")
            
            ws.conditional_formatting.add(f'D{row}',
                FormulaRule(formula=[f'$D${row}="✓"'], fill=green_fill))
            ws.conditional_formatting.add(f'D{row}',
                FormulaRule(formula=[f'$D${row}="✗"'], fill=red_fill))
            ws.conditional_formatting.add(f'D{row}',
                FormulaRule(formula=[f'$D${row}="⚠"'], fill=yellow_fill))
            
            # Result text
            result_formula = f'=IF(D{row}="✓","PASS",IF(D{row}="⚠","WARNING","FAIL"))'
            ws.cell(row=row, column=5, value=result_formula)
            
            row += 1
        
        # Overall status
        ws[f'A{row+1}'] = "OVERALL STATUS:"
        ws[f'A{row+1}'].font = Font(bold=True)
        
        overall_formula = ('=IF(COUNTIF(D4:D18,"✗")>0,"CRITICAL ERRORS FOUND",'
                          'IF(COUNTIF(D4:D18,"⚠")>0,"WARNINGS - REVIEW REQUIRED",'
                          '"ALL CHECKS PASSED ✓"))')
        ws[f'B{row+1}'] = overall_formula
        ws[f'B{row+1}'].font = Font(bold=True, size=12)
        
        self._set_column_widths(ws, [12, 35, 50, 10, 15])
        
        return ws

            ("", ""),
            ("STEP 7: HYDRAULIC DATA", ""),
            ("1. Go to HYDRAULIC_DATA sheet", ""),
            ("2. Enter HFL, LWL, and other hydraulic levels", ""),
            ("3. Check calculated scour depth and clearances", ""),
            ("", ""),
            ("STEP 8: VALIDATION", ""),
            ("1. Go to VALIDATION sheet", ""),
            ("2. Check all validation checks show ✓ (green)", ""),
            ("3. Fix any ✗ (red) errors before proceeding", ""),
            ("4. Review any ⚠ (yellow) warnings", ""),
            ("", ""),
            ("STEP 9: OUTPUT SETTINGS", ""),
            ("1. Go to DRAWING_CONTROL sheet", ""),
            ("2. Select desired output formats and scales", ""),
            ("3. Choose which drawings to generate", ""),
            ("", ""),
            ("STEP 10: GENERATE DRAWINGS", ""),
            ("1. Save the Excel file", ""),
            ("2. Run the Python script: python batch_processor_core.py -i your_file.xlsx -o output", ""),
            ("3. Check output folder for generated drawings", ""),
            ("", ""),
            ("IMPORTANT NOTES:", ""),
            ("• Yellow cells are for INPUT", ""),
            ("• Gray cells are CALCULATED automatically (do not edit)", ""),
            ("• Green validation = OK, Red = ERROR, Yellow = WARNING", ""),
            ("• Use dropdown lists where available", ""),
            ("• All dimensions are in meters unless specified otherwise", ""),
            ("• Save file before running batch processor", ""),
            ("", ""),
            ("SUPPORT:", ""),
            ("For issues or questions, contact: support@bridgecad.com", ""),
        ]
        
        row = 3
        for line1, line2 in instructions:
            if line1.startswith("STEP"):
                ws.cell(row=row, column=1, value=line1).font = Font(bold=True, size=12, color="366092")
            elif line1.startswith("IMPORTANT") or line1.startswith("SUPPORT"):
                ws.cell(row=row, column=1, value=line1).font = Font(bold=True, size=11, color="FF0000")
            else:
                ws.cell(row=row, column=1, value=line1)
            row += 1
        
        ws.column_dimensions['A'].width = 80
        
        return ws
    
    def protect_calculated_cells(self):
        """Protect calculated cells from editing"""
        for sheet_name in self.wb.sheetnames:
            ws = self.wb[sheet_name]
            for row in ws.iter_rows():
                for cell in row:
                    if cell.style == "calculated_style":
                        cell.protection = Protection(locked=True)
                    else:
                        cell.protection = Protection(locked=False)
            
            # Enable protection (password optional)
            ws.protection.sheet = True
            ws.protection.password = None  # No password for usability
    
    def save(self, filepath: str):
        """Save workbook"""
        # Create directory if needed
        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(filepath) if os.path.dirname(filepath) else '.', 
                   exist_ok=True)
        
        # Protect calculated cells
        self.protect_calculated_cells()
        
        # Save
        self.wb.save(filepath)
        print(f"Excel template saved: {filepath}")
        print(f"Sheets: {self.wb.sheetnames}")
        return filepath


def create_complete_template(output_path: str = "Bridge_GAD_Template.xlsx"):
    """
    Create complete Excel template
    """
    print("=" * 80)
    print("CREATING BRIDGE GAD EXCEL TEMPLATE")
    print("=" * 80)
    
    generator = BridgeExcelTemplateGenerator()
    
    # Create all sheets
    print("Creating sheets...")
    generator.create_project_master_sheet()
    generator.create_bridge_selection_sheet()
    generator.create_geometry_input_sheet()
    generator.create_superstructure_sheet()
    generator.create_substructure_sheet()
    generator.create_foundation_sheet()
    generator.create_hydraulic_sheet()
    generator.create_materials_sheet()
    generator.create_drawing_control_sheet()
    generator.create_validation_sheet()
    generator.create_instructions_sheet()
    
    # Save
    generator.save(output_path)
    
    print("\n" + "=" * 80)
    print("TEMPLATE CREATION COMPLETE")
    print("=" * 80)
    print(f"File: {output_path}")
    print(f"Total sheets: {len(generator.wb.sheetnames)}")
    print("\nFeatures included:")
    print("  ✓ Data validation (dropdowns)")
    print("  ✓ Numeric validation (min/max)")
    print("  ✓ Formulas for auto-calculation")
    print("  ✓ Conditional formatting")
    print("  ✓ Cell protection (calculated cells locked)")
    print("  ✓ Professional styling")
    print("  ✓ Validation checks")
    print("  ✓ Instructions")
    print("=" * 80)
    
    return output_path


if __name__ == "__main__":
    # Create template
```

```python
# Generate template
from bridge_excel_template import create_complete_template

template_path = create_complete_template("My_Bridge_Template.xlsx")

from batch_processor_core import BatchProcessor, BatchConfig

config = BatchConfig(
    input_excel_path=template_path,
    output_base_directory="output",
    max_workers=4

processor = BatchProcessor(config)
processor.run()
```

# ADDITIONAL EXCEL SHEETS MODULE

## SHEET 15: COST_ESTIMATION

**Purpose:** Automated cost estimation with rate analysis and quantity calculations

### Structure:

| Section | Fields | Formulas/Notes |
|---------|--------|--------------|
| **A. Input Data** | Bridge Type, Span Count, Foundation Type | Dropdowns from previous sheets |
| **B. Quantities** | Concrete (m³), Steel (MT), Earthwork (m³) | Auto-populated from geometry sheets |
| **C. Rates** | Unit rates for materials, labor, equipment | Database lookup |
| **D. Cost Breakup** | Material cost, Labor cost, Equipment cost | Calculated |
| **E. Summary** | Total cost, Cost per m², Cost per m³ | Final output |

### Detailed Columns:

| Cell | Item | Unit | Quantity | Rate | Amount | Formula |
|------|------|------|----------|------|--------|---------|

| B11 | Expansion joints | m | `=EXP_JOINT_LENGTH` | ₹12,000 | `=C11*D11` | |
| **B. SUBSTRUCTURE** |
| B15 | RCC M30 in piers | m³ | `=PIER_VOL` | ₹8,500 | `=C15*D15` | |
| B16 | RCC M30 in abutments | m³ | `=ABUT_VOL` | ₹8,500 | `=C16*D16` | |

| 3 | Material procurement | 14 | `=START_DATE` | `=C4+D4-1` | - | - | Materials |
| **B. FOUNDATION** |
| 4 | Excavation | 10 | `=E3+1` | `=C5+D5-1` | 2 | Excavator: 1 | Trenches |

| 11 | Deck slab casting | 20 | `=E11+1` | `=C12+D12-1` | 10 | Labor: 25 | Deck |
| 12 | Curing | 14 | `=E12+1` | `=C13+D13-1` | 12 | - | Cured |
| **E. FINISHING** |
| 13 | Wearing coat | 10 | `=E13+1` | `=C14+D14-1` | 13 | Labor: 10 | Wearing |

| 15 | Road work | 8 | `=E15+1` | `=C16+D16-1` | 15 | Labor: 20 | Road |
| 16 | Demobilization | 5 | `=E16+1` | `=C17+D17-1` | 16 | Labor: 10 | Handover |
| **F. SUMMARY** |
| | **Total Duration** | `=SUM(D2:D17)` | | | | | |
| | **Critical Path** | `=MAX(E2:E17)-MIN(C2:C17)+1` | | | | | |

### Gantt Chart Data (for automatic chart generation):

| Day | Stage 1 | Stage 2 | Stage 3 | Stage 4 | ... |
|-----|---------|---------|---------|---------|-----|
| 1 | 1 | 0 | 1 | 0 | |
| 2 | 1 | 0 | 1 | 0 | |
| ... | | | | | |
| 100 | 0 | 0 | 0 | 0 | |

Formula: `=IF(AND(Day>=Start, Day<=End), 1, 0)`

### Resource Loading:

| Resource Type | Daily Requirement | Total Days | Total Cost |
|---------------|-------------------|------------|------------|
| Skilled Labor | `=COUNTIF(Resources,"Skilled")` | `=SUM(Durations)` | `=B2*C2*1200` |
| Unskilled Labor | `=COUNTIF(Resources,"Unskilled")` | `=SUM(Durations)` | `=B3*C3*800` |
| Excavator | `=COUNTIF(Resources,"Excavator")` | `=SUMIF(Activities,"Excav*",Duration)` | `=B4*C4*3500` |
| Crane | `=COUNTIF(Resources,"Crane")` | `=SUMIF(Activities,"Lifting*",Duration)` | `=B5*C5*8000` |
| Concrete Pump | `=COUNTIF(Resources,"Pump")` | `=SUMIF(Activities,"*Concreting*",Duration)` | `=B6*C6*4500` |

## SHEET 17: BAR_BENDING_SCHEDULE (BBS)

**Purpose:** Complete reinforcement detailing with bar marks, shapes, and quantities

### Structure:

| Bar Mark | Description | Bar Dia (mm) | No. of Bars | Length (m) | Total Length (m) | Unit Weight (kg/m) | Total Weight (kg) | Shape Code | Shape Diagram |
|----------|-------------|------------|-------------|------------|------------------|-------------------|-------------------|------------|---------------|
| **A. DECK SLAB** |
| D1 | Main bars - top | 16 | `=DECK_WIDTH/0.15+1` | `=SPAN_LENGTH+0.5` | `=D2*E2` | 1.58 | `=F2*G2` | T1 | [Diagram] |
| D2 | Main bars - bottom | 16 | `=D2` | `=E2` | `=D3*E3` | 1.58 | `=F3*G3` | T1 | [Diagram] |
| D3 | Distribution bars | 12 | `=SPAN_LENGTH/0.20+1` | `=DECK_WIDTH+0.3` | `=D4*E4` | 0.89 | `=F4*G4` | T1 | [Diagram] |
| D4 | Extra bars at support | 20 | 20 | 3.0 | `=D5*E5` | 2.47 | `=F5*G5` | T2 | [Diagram] |
| **B. GIRDERS** |
| G1 | Main longitudinal - top | 25 | 4 | `=SPAN+2*DEV_LEN` | `=D7*E7` | 3.85 | `=F7*G7` | T1 | [Diagram] |
| G2 | Main longitudinal - bottom | 25 | 8 | `=SPAN+2*DEV_LEN` | `=D8*E8` | 3.85 | `=F8*G8` | T1 | [Diagram] |
| G3 | Stirrups | 10 | `=(SPAN/0.15)+1` | `=2*(WEB_DEPTH+WEB_WIDTH)+0.20` | `=D9*E9` | 0.62 | `=F9*G9` | T3 | [Diagram] |
| G4 | Bent-up bars | 20 | 4 | `=SPAN+0.5` | `=D10*E10` | 2.47 | `=F10*G10` | T4 | [Diagram] |
| G5 | Side face reinforcement | 12 | 4 | `=SPAN` | `=D11*E11` | 0.89 | `=F11*G11` | T1 | [Diagram] |
| **C. PIERS** |
| P1 | Vertical bars | 25 | 24 | `=PIER_HEIGHT+1.0` | `=D13*E13` | 3.85 | `=F13*G13` | T1 | [Diagram] |
| P2 | Helical ties | 10 | `=(PIER_HEIGHT/0.15)+1` | `=PIER_PERIMETER` | `=D14*E14` | 0.62 | `=F14*G14` | T5 | [Diagram] |
| P3 | Pier cap main | 20 | 20 | `=CAP_LENGTH` | `=D15*E15` | 2.47 | `=F15*G15` | T1 | [Diagram] |
| P4 | Pier cap stirrups | 12 | `=(CAP_LENGTH/0.15)+1` | `=CAP_DEPTH+CAP_WIDTH` | `=D16*E16` | 0.89 | `=F16*G16` | T3 | [Diagram] |
| **D. ABUTMENTS** |
| A1 | Stem vertical | 25 | 20 | `=STEM_HEIGHT+1.0` | `=D18*E18` | 3.85 | `=F18*G18` | T1 | [Diagram] |
| A2 | Stem horizontal | 16 | `=STEM_HEIGHT/0.15+1` | `=STEM_THICKNESS` | `=D19*E19` | 1.58 | `=F19*G19` | T1 | [Diagram] |
| A3 | Footing bottom | 20 | 30 | `=FOOT_LENGTH` | `=D20*E20` | 2.47 | `=F20*G20` | T1 | [Diagram] |
| A4 | Footing top | 16 | 30 | `=FOOT_LENGTH` | `=D21*E21` | 1.58 | `=F21*G21` | T1 | [Diagram] |
| **E. PILES** |
| PL1 | Main bars | 20 | 12 | `=PILE_LEN+0.5` | `=D23*E23` | 2.47 | `=F23*G23` | T1 | [Diagram] |
| PL2 | Spiral | 10 | `=PILE_LEN/0.15+1` | `=PILE_CIRCUM` | `=D24*E24` | 0.62 | `=F24*G24` | T6 | [Diagram] |
| **F. TOTALS** |
| | **Grand Total** | | | | `=SUM(F2:F25)` | | `=SUM(H2:H25)` | | |

### Shape Code Reference:

| Code | Shape | Formula | Diagram |
|------|-------|---------|---------|
| T1 | Straight | Length = L | ─────── |
| T2 | L-bend | Length = L + 2×Hook | └─────┘ |
| T3 | Rectangular Stirrup | Length = 2(a+b) + Hook | ┌─────┐ |
| T4 | Bent-up | Length = L + 0.42×H | ╱─────╲ |
| T5 | Helical | Length = n×√((πD)² + p²) | ~~~~~~~ |
| T6 | Spiral | Length = n×√((πD)² + p²) | ///// |

### Unit Weight Reference:
- 6mm: 0.22 kg/m
- 8mm: 0.39 kg/m
- 10mm: 0.62 kg/m
- 12mm: 0.89 kg/m
- 16mm: 1.58 kg/m
- 20mm: 2.47 kg/m
- 25mm: 3.85 kg/m
- 32mm: 6.31 kg/m

### Bending Schedule Summary:

| Dia (mm) | Total Length (m) | Total Weight (kg) | Weight % |
|----------|------------------|-------------------|----------|
| 6 | `=SUMIF(Dia,6,Total_Length)` | `=SUMIF(Dia,6,Weight)` | `=C2/$C$9` |
| 8 | `=SUMIF(Dia,8,Total_Length)` | `=SUMIF(Dia,8,Weight)` | `=C3/$C$9` |
| 10 | `=SUMIF(Dia,10,Total_Length)` | `=SUMIF(Dia,10,Weight)` | `=C4/$C$9` |
| 12 | `=SUMIF(Dia,12,Total_Length)` | `=SUMIF(Dia,12,Weight)` | `=C5/$C$9` |
| 16 | `=SUMIF(Dia,16,Total_Length)` | `=SUMIF(Dia,16,Weight)` | `=C6/$C$9` |
| 20 | `=SUMIF(Dia,20,Total_Length)` | `=SUMIF(Dia,20,Weight)` | `=C7/$C$9` |
| 25 | `=SUMIF(Dia,25,Total_Length)` | `=SUMIF(Dia,25,Weight)` | `=C8/$C$9` |
| **TOTAL** | `=SUM(B2:B8)` | `=SUM(C2:C8)` | 100% |

---

## PYTHON INTEGRATION CODE

### File: `additional_sheets_reader.py`

```python
"""
Additional Sheets Reader for Cost Estimation, Construction Sequence, and BBS
Reads and processes the three additional Excel sheets
"""

import pandas as pd
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Dict, List, Optional
from datetime import datetime, timedelta


@dataclass
class CostItem:
    """Individual cost item"""
    item_code: str
    description: str
    unit: str
    quantity: float
    rate: float
    amount: float
    category: str


@dataclass
class ConstructionActivity:
    """Construction sequence activity"""
    stage: int
    activity: str
    duration_days: int
    start_date: datetime
    end_date: datetime
    dependencies: List[int]
    resources: Dict[str, int]  # Resource type: count
    output: str


@dataclass
class BarBendingItem:
    """Bar bending schedule item"""
    bar_mark: str
    description: str
    diameter: int
    num_bars: int
    length: float
    shape_code: str
    total_weight: float


class AdditionalSheetsReader:
    """Read and process additional Excel sheets"""
    
    def __init__(self, excel_path: str):
        self.excel_path = excel_path
        self.cost_data = []
        self.schedule_data = []
        self.bbs_data = []
        
    def read_cost_estimation(self) -> Dict:
        """Read COST_ESTIMATION sheet"""
        df = pd.read_excel(self.excel_path, sheet_name='COST_ESTIMATION', header=2)
        
        cost_items = []
        total_cost = 0
        
        for _, row in df.iterrows():
            if pd.notna(row['Item']) and pd.notna(row['Amount']):
                item = CostItem(
                    item_code=str(row.get('Item', '')),
                    description=str(row.get('Description', '')),
                    unit=str(row.get('Unit', '')),
                    quantity=float(row.get('Quantity', 0)),
                    rate=float(row.get('Rate', 0)),
                    amount=float(row.get('Amount', 0)),
                    category=str(row.get('Category', 'General'))
                )
                cost_items.append(item)
                total_cost += item.amount
        
        # Calculate derived metrics
        deck_area = self._get_deck_area()
        total_concrete = sum(item.quantity for item in cost_items 
                           if 'concrete' in item.description.lower())
        
        return {
            'items': cost_items,
            'total_cost': total_cost,
            'contingencies': total_cost * 0.03,
            'grand_total': total_cost * 1.03,
            'cost_per_m2': total_cost / deck_area if deck_area > 0 else 0,
            'cost_per_m3': total_cost / total_concrete if total_concrete > 0 else 0,
            'category_wise': self._categorize_costs(cost_items)
        }
    
    def read_construction_sequence(self) -> Dict:
        """Read CONSTRUCTION_SEQUENCE sheet"""
        df = pd.read_excel(self.excel_path, sheet_name='CONSTRUCTION_SEQUENCE', header=1)
        
        activities = []
        start_date = datetime.now()
        
        for _, row in df.iterrows():
            if pd.notna(row['Stage']) and pd.notna(row['Activity']):
                duration = int(row.get('Duration (Days)', 0))
                
                activity = ConstructionActivity(
                    stage=int(row['Stage']),
                    activity=str(row['Activity']),
                    duration_days=duration,
                    start_date=start_date,
                    end_date=start_date + timedelta(days=duration-1),
                    dependencies=[],  # Parse from Dependencies column
                    resources={},  # Parse from Resources column
                    output=str(row.get('Output', ''))
                )
                activities.append(activity)
                start_date = activity.end_date + timedelta(days=1)
        
        # Calculate critical path
        total_duration = sum(a.duration_days for a in activities)
        
        return {
            'activities': activities,
            'total_duration': total_duration,
            'critical_path': self._calculate_critical_path(activities),
            'resource_loading': self._calculate_resource_loading(activities),
            'gantt_data': self._generate_gantt_data(activities)
        }
    
    def read_bar_bending_schedule(self) -> Dict:
        """Read BAR_BENDING_SCHEDULE sheet"""
        df = pd.read_excel(self.excel_path, sheet_name='BAR_BENDING_SCHEDULE', header=1)
        
        bbs_items = []
        
        for _, row in df.iterrows():
            if pd.notna(row['Bar Mark']) and pd.notna(row['Total Weight']):
                item = BarBendingItem(
                    bar_mark=str(row['Bar Mark']),
                    description=str(row['Description']),
                    diameter=int(row['Bar Dia (mm)']),
                    num_bars=int(row['No. of Bars']),
                    length=float(row['Length (m)']),
                    shape_code=str(row.get('Shape Code', 'T1')),
                    total_weight=float(row['Total Weight'])
                )
                bbs_items.append(item)
        
        # Summarize by diameter
        dia_summary = {}
        for item in bbs_items:
            if item.diameter not in dia_summary:
                dia_summary[item.diameter] = {'length': 0, 'weight': 0}
            dia_summary[item.diameter]['length'] += item.length * item.num_bars
            dia_summary[item.diameter]['weight'] += item.total_weight
        
        total_weight = sum(item.total_weight for item in bbs_items)
        
        return {
            'items': bbs_items,
            'total_weight': total_weight,
            'by_diameter': dia_summary,
            'category_wise': self._categorize_bbs(bbs_items),
            'cutting_lengths': self._calculate_cutting_lengths(bbs_items)
        }
    
    def _get_deck_area(self) -> float:
        """Get deck area from GEOMETRY_INPUT sheet"""
        try:
            df = pd.read_excel(self.excel_path, sheet_name='GEOMETRY_INPUT')
            length = float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Total_Length', 'Value'].values[0])
            width = float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Overall_Width', 'Value'].values[0])
            return length * width
        except:
            return 0
    
    def _categorize_costs(self, items: List[CostItem]) -> Dict:
        """Group costs by category"""
        categories = {}
        for item in items:
            cat = item.category
            if cat not in categories:
                categories[cat] = 0
            categories[cat] += item.amount
        return categories
    
    def _calculate_critical_path(self, activities: List[ConstructionActivity]) -> List[int]:
        """Calculate critical path (simplified)"""
        # In real implementation, use CPM algorithm
        return [a.stage for a in activities]
    
    def _calculate_resource_loading(self, activities: List[ConstructionActivity]) -> Dict:
        """Calculate resource requirements"""
        resources = {}
        for activity in activities:
            for res_type, count in activity.resources.items():
                if res_type not in resources:
                    resources[res_type] = {'count': 0, 'days': 0}
                resources[res_type]['count'] = max(resources[res_type]['count'], count)
                resources[res_type]['days'] += activity.duration_days
        return resources
    
    def _generate_gantt_data(self, activities: List[ConstructionActivity]) -> List[Dict]:
        """Generate data for Gantt chart"""
        gantt = []
        for activity in activities:
            gantt.append({
                'stage': activity.stage,
                'activity': activity.activity,
                'start': activity.start_date,
                'end': activity.end_date,
                'duration': activity.duration_days
            })
        return gantt
    
    def _categorize_bbs(self, items: List[BarBendingItem]) -> Dict:
        """Group BBS by structural element"""
        categories = {
            'Deck Slab': [],
            'Girders': [],
            'Piers': [],
            'Abutments': [],
            'Piles': []
        }
        
        for item in items:
            desc = item.description.lower()
            if 'deck' in desc or 'slab' in desc:
                categories['Deck Slab'].append(item)
            elif 'girder' in desc:
                categories['Girders'].append(item)
            elif 'pier' in desc:
                categories['Piers'].append(item)
            elif 'abutment' in desc or 'stem' in desc or 'footing' in desc:
                categories['Abutments'].append(item)
            elif 'pile' in desc:
                categories['Piles'].append(item)
        
        return {k: len(v) for k, v in categories.items()}
    
    def _calculate_cutting_lengths(self, items: List[BarBendingItem]) -> Dict:
        """Calculate optimized cutting lengths"""
        # Group by diameter
        by_dia = {}
        for item in items:
            if item.diameter not in by_dia:
                by_dia[item.diameter] = []
            by_dia[item.diameter].extend([item.length] * item.num_bars)
        
        # Calculate standard lengths (12m bars)
        cutting_plan = {}
        for dia, lengths in by_dia.items():
            total_length = sum(lengths)
            std_bar_length = 12.0
            num_full_bars = int(total_length / std_bar_length)
            remaining = total_length % std_bar_length
            
            cutting_plan[dia] = {
                'total_required': total_length,
                'standard_bars_needed': num_full_bars + (1 if remaining > 0 else 0),
                'wastage': std_bar_length - remaining if remaining > 0 else 0,
                'wastage_percent': ((std_bar_length - remaining) / total_length * 100) 
                                  if remaining > 0 else 0
            }
        
        return cutting_plan


class CostEstimator:
    """Calculate costs from quantities"""
    
    RATES_DB = {
        'RCC_M35': {'rate': 8500, 'unit': 'm³'},
        'RCC_M30': {'rate': 8000, 'unit': 'm³'},
        'RCC_M25': {'rate': 7500, 'unit': 'm³'},
        'STEEL_HYSD': {'rate': 65000, 'unit': 'MT'},
        'PRESTRESS': {'rate': 95000, 'unit': 'MT'},
        'BEARING_ELA': {'rate': 45000, 'unit': 'nos'},
        'EXP_JOINT': {'rate': 12000, 'unit': 'm'},
        'PILE_BORED_1200': {'rate': 9500, 'unit': 'm'},
        'WELL_SINK': {'rate': 25000, 'unit': 'm'},
        'EXCAV_SOFT': {'rate': 350, 'unit': 'm³'},
        'EXCAV_HARD': {'rate': 550, 'unit': 'm³'},
        'BACKFILL': {'rate': 280, 'unit': 'm³'}
    }
    
    def __init__(self, quantities: Dict):
        self.quantities = quantities
        self.items = []
    
    def calculate(self) -> Dict:
        """Calculate total cost"""
        total = 0
        
        for item_code, qty in self.quantities.items():
            if item_code in self.RATES_DB:
                rate_info = self.RATES_DB[item_code]
                amount = qty * rate_info['rate']
                
                self.items.append({
                    'code': item_code,
                    'quantity': qty,
                    'unit': rate_info['unit'],
                    'rate': rate_info['rate'],
                    'amount': amount
                })
                total += amount
        
        return {
            'items': self.items,
            'total': total,
            'with_contingency': total * 1.03
        }


class BarBendingScheduleGenerator:
    """Generate BBS from structural elements"""
    
    UNIT_WEIGHTS = {
        6: 0.222, 8: 0.395, 10: 0.617, 12: 0.888,
        16: 1.578, 20: 2.466, 25: 3.853, 32: 6.313
    }
    
    def __init__(self, bridge_model):
        self.model = bridge_model
        self.bbs = []
    
    def generate_deck_slab_bbs(self) -> List[Dict]:
        """Generate BBS for deck slab"""
        deck_width = self.model.section_dimensions.carriageway_width
        span = self.model.spans[0].length  # Typical span
        
        items = []
        
        # Main bars (perpendicular to traffic)
        main_spacing = 0.15  # 150mm
        num_main = int(deck_width / main_spacing) + 1
        main_length = span + 0.5  # Development length
        
        items.append({
            'mark': 'D1',
            'desc': 'Main bars - top layer',
            'dia': 16,
            'nos': num_main,
            'length': main_length,
            'shape': 'T1',
            'weight': num_main * main_length * self.UNIT_WEIGHTS[16]
        })
        
        # Distribution bars
        dist_spacing = 0.20  # 200mm
        num_dist = int(span / dist_spacing) + 1
        dist_length = deck_width + 0.3
        
        items.append({
            'mark': 'D2',
            'desc': 'Distribution bars',
            'dia': 12,
            'nos': num_dist,
            'length': dist_length,
            'shape': 'T1',
            'weight': num_dist * dist_length * self.UNIT_WEIGHTS[12]
        })
        
        return items
    
    def generate_girder_bbs(self) -> List[Dict]:
        """Generate BBS for girders"""
        span = self.model.spans[0].length
        girder_depth = 2.0  # Typical
        
        items = []
        
        # Main bars - bottom
        items.append({
            'mark': 'G1',
            'desc': 'Main longitudinal - bottom',
            'dia': 25,
            'nos': 8,
            'length': span + 1.0,  # Development
            'shape': 'T1',
            'weight': 8 * (span + 1.0) * self.UNIT_WEIGHTS[25]
        })
        
        # Stirrups
        stirrup_spacing = 0.15
        num_stirrups = int(span / stirrup_spacing) + 1
        stirrup_perimeter = 2 * (girder_depth + 0.3)  # Approximate
        
        items.append({
            'mark': 'G2',
            'desc': 'Stirrups',
            'dia': 10,
            'nos': num_stirrups,
            'length': stirrup_perimeter,
            'shape': 'T3',
            'weight': num_stirrups * stirrup_perimeter * self.UNIT_WEIGHTS[10]
        })
        
        return items
    
    def export_to_excel(self, output_path: str):
        """Export BBS to Excel format"""
        df = pd.DataFrame(self.bbs)
        df.to_excel(output_path, sheet_name='BBS', index=False)
        return output_path
```

# SHEET 11: CROSS_SECTION_INPUT

## Purpose
Define complete cross-sectional geometry for all bridge elements including deck slab, girders, kerbs, footpaths, railings, and drainage.

---

## SECTION A: DECK CROSS-SECTION GEOMETRY

| Cell | Parameter | Input/Formula | Unit | Validation | Notes |
|------|-----------|---------------|------|------------|-------|
| **A1** | **DECK CROSS-SECTION PARAMETERS** | Header | - | - | |
| A3 | Overall Deck Width (W) | Input | m | 5.0 - 50.0 | Total width including footpaths |
| A4 | Carriageway Width (Wc) | `=W-2*KERB_WIDTH-2*FOOTPATH_WIDTH` | m | Auto | Calculated |
| A5 | Deck Thickness at Center (td) | Input | mm | 200 - 500 | Minimum thickness |
| A6 | Deck Thickness at Edge (te) | Input | mm | 200 - 600 | Usually thicker at edges |
| A7 | Deck Thickness Variation | Dropdown | - | Linear/Parabolic/Constant | Profile type |
| A8 | Cross Slope (n) | Input | % | 1.5 - 4.0 | For drainage |
| A9 | Wearing Coat Thickness (tw) | Input | mm | 50 - 100 | M30 or Asphalt |
| A10 | Wearing Coat Type | Dropdown | - | M30 Concrete/Asphalt/PMC | Material |

### Deck Thickness Profile Formula:
```
If Linear: t(x) = td + (te-td)*|2x/W|
If Parabolic: t(x) = td + (te-td)*(2x/W)²
If Constant: t(x) = td
```

---

## SECTION B: GIRDER CROSS-SECTION

| Cell | Parameter | Input/Formula | Unit | Validation | Notes |
|------|-----------|---------------|------|------------|-------|
| **A12** | **GIRDER CROSS-SECTION** | Header | - | - | |
| A14 | Girder Type | Dropdown | - | T-Girder/I-Girder/Box/Plate | Structural system |
| A15 | Number of Girders | Input | nos | 2 - 12 | Must be ≥ 2 |
| A16 | Girder Spacing (Sg) | `=Wc/(N_GIRDERS-1)` | m | Auto | Center-to-center |
| A17 | Web Thickness (tw) | Input | mm | 200 - 500 | Minimum 200mm |
| A18 | Web Depth (Dw) | Input | mm | 1000 - 4000 | Varies with span |
| A19 | Bottom Flange Width (bf) | Input | mm | 400 - 1500 | Compression zone |
| A20 | Bottom Flange Thickness (tf) | Input | mm | 200 - 400 | |
| A21 | Top Flange Width (if I-girder) | Input | mm | 400 - 1000 | Optional |
| A22 | Top Flange Thickness | Input | mm | 200 - 350 | |
| A23 | Haunch Width (wh) | Input | mm | 0 - 300 | Deck to girder connection |
| A24 | Haunch Thickness (th) | Input | mm | 0 - 200 | |
| A25 | Girder Concrete Grade | Dropdown | - | M35/M40/M45/M50 | |
| A26 | Girder Span-to-Depth Ratio | `=SPAN_LENGTH/(Dw/1000)` | - | Auto | Should be 12-20 |

### Girder Section Properties (Auto-Calculated):

| Cell | Property | Formula | Unit |
|------|----------|---------|------|
| A28 | Area of Concrete | `=tw*Dw + bf*tf` | mm² |
| A29 | Moment of Inertia (Ixx) | `=(tw*Dw³)/12 + (bf*tf³)/12 + bf*tf*(Dw+tf/2-Ybar)²` | mm⁴ |
| A30 | Section Modulus (Z) | `=Ixx/(Dw/2)` | mm³ |
| A31 | Self-weight per m | `=AREA_CONC*25/10⁶` | kN/m |

### Drainage Capacity Check:
```
Flow per m width: Q = (Rainfall × Catchment Area) / 1000
Required drain area: A_req = Q / Velocity (assume 1.5 m/s)
```

```python
CROSS_SECTION_VALIDATION = {
    'Overall_Deck_Width': {'type': 'decimal', 'min': 5.0, 'max': 50.0},
    'Deck_Thickness': {'type': 'decimal', 'min': 200, 'max': 600},
    'Cross_Slope': {'type': 'decimal', 'min': 1.5, 'max': 4.0},
    'Number_of_Girders': {'type': 'integer', 'min': 2, 'max': 12},
    'Web_Thickness': {'type': 'decimal', 'min': 200, 'max': 500},
    'Web_Depth': {'type': 'decimal', 'min': 1000, 'max': 4000},
    'Kerb_Height': {'type': 'decimal', 'min': 150, 'max': 600},
    'Railing_Height': {'type': 'decimal', 'min': 750, 'max': 1100},
    'Post_Spacing': {'type': 'decimal', 'min': 1.5, 'max': 3.0},
    'Pipe_Diameter': {'type': 'decimal', 'min': 150, 'max': 300},
    
    'Dropdown_Lists': {
        'Deck_Thickness_Variation': ['Linear', 'Parabolic', 'Constant'],
        'Wearing_Coat_Type': ['M30 Concrete', 'Asphalt', 'Polymer Modified Concrete'],
        'Girder_Type': ['T-Girder', 'I-Girder', 'Box Girder', 'Plate Girder'],
        'Girder_Concrete_Grade': ['M35', 'M40', 'M45', 'M50', 'M55'],
        'Footpath_Surface': ['PCC', 'Tiles', 'Asphalt', 'Concrete'],
        'Railing_Type': ['Crash Barrier', 'Guard Rail', 'Combination', 'Pedestrian Railing'],
        'Post_Section': ['Circular', 'Square', 'I-section', 'Rectangular'],
        'Rail_Section': ['W-beam', 'Thrie-beam', 'Concrete', 'Metal Railing'],
        'Impact_Resistance': ['H1', 'H2', 'H3', 'H4'],
        'Drainage_Type': ['Pipe', 'Channel', 'Scupper', 'Combined'],
        'Joint_Type': ['Strip Seal', 'Modular', 'Finger Plate', 'Comb Type'],
        'Bearing_Type': ['Elastomeric', 'POT-PTFE', 'Rocker', 'Roller', 'Spherical'],
        'Fixity': ['Fixed', 'Free', 'Guided']
    }
}
```

## CONDITIONAL FORMATTING

| Condition | Format | Applies To |
|-----------|--------|------------|
| Span-to-Depth Ratio < 12 | Red background | A26 | Too stiff |
| Span-to-Depth Ratio > 20 | Yellow background | A26 | Check deflection |
| Girder Spacing > 3.5m | Orange background | A16 | Check deck design |
| Kerb Height < 150mm | Red text | A36 | Below minimum |
| Cross Slope < 2.0% | Yellow background | A8 | Inadequate drainage |

# SHEET 12: LONGITUDINAL_SECTION_INPUT

## Purpose
Define complete longitudinal profile including vertical alignment, grades, curves, and elevation data.

---

## SECTION A: VERTICAL ALIGNMENT

| Cell | Parameter | Input/Formula | Unit | Validation | Notes |
|------|-----------|---------------|------|------------|-------|
| **A1** | **VERTICAL ALIGNMENT** | Header | - | - | |
| A3 | Chainage Start | Input | m | 0 | |
| A4 | Chainage End | Input | m | > Start | Total length |
| A5 | Total Bridge Length | `=A4-A3` | m | Auto | |
| A6 | Datum Level | Input | m | - | Reference elevation |
| A7 | Formation Level Start | Input | m | - | Road level |
| A8 | Formation Level End | Input | m | - | Road level |
| A9 | Average Grade | `=(A8-A7)/A5*100` | % | Auto | |
| A10 | Maximum Grade | Input | % | 0 - 6.0 | IRC limit |
| A11 | Minimum Grade | Input | % | 0 - 0.5 | For drainage |
| A12 | Grade Change Location | Input | m | 0 - A5 | Point of change |

---

## SECTION B: VERTICAL CURVES

| Cell | Parameter | Input/Formula | Unit | Validation | Notes |
|------|-----------|---------------|------|------------|-------|
| **A14** | **VERTICAL CURVES** | Header | - | - | |
| A16 | Number of Vertical Curves | Input | nos | 0 - 5 | Crest or sag |
| A17 | Curve 1 Type | Dropdown | - | Crest/Sag | |
| A18 | Curve 1 Chainage (PVI) | Input | m | 0 - A5 | Point of intersection |
| A19 | Curve 1 Elevation (PVI) | Input | m | - | |
| A20 | Curve 1 Grade In (g1) | Input | % | -10 to +10 | |
| A21 | Curve 1 Grade Out (g2) | Input | % | -10 to +10 | |
| A22 | Curve 1 Grade Diff (A) | `=g2-g1` | % | Auto | |
| A23 | Curve 1 Design Speed | Input | km/h | 30 - 120 | |
| A24 | Curve 1 K Value | `=V²/(127*(SSD/3.6)²)` | - | Auto | IRC formula |
| A25 | Curve 1 Length (L) | `=K*abs(A)` | m | Auto | |
| A26 | Curve 1 Tangent Distance | `=L/2` | m | Auto | |
| A27 | Curve 1 BVC Chainage | `=PVI-T` | m | Auto | Begin vertical curve |
| A28 | Curve 1 EVC Chainage | `=PVI+T` | m | Auto | End vertical curve |
| A29 | Curve 1 Lowest/Highest Point | Formula | m | Auto | For drainage |

### Vertical Curve Elevation Formula:
```
Elevation at distance x from BVC:
y = y_BVC + g1*x + (A*x²)/(2L)

Where:
- y_BVC = Elevation at BVC
- g1 = Initial grade (%)
- A = Grade difference (%)
- L = Curve length (m)
- x = Distance from BVC (m)
```

---

## SECTION C: SPAN ARRANGEMENT

| Cell | Parameter | Input/Formula | Unit | Validation | Notes |
|------|-----------|---------------|------|------------|-------|
| **A31** | **SPAN ARRANGEMENT** | Header | - | - | |
| A33 | Number of Spans | Input | nos | 1 - 20 | |
| A34 | Span Configuration | Dropdown | - | Equal/Variable/Hybrid | |
| A35 | Standard Span Length | Input | m | 10 - 60 | If equal |
| A36 | End Span Length (if variable) | Input | m | 0.7-0.8 × Standard | |
| A37 | Pier Thickness | Input | m | 0.8 - 3.0 | |
| A38 | Abutment Thickness | Input | m | 0.5 - 1.5 | |
| A39 | Expansion Joint Gap | Input | mm | 20 - 80 | |

### Span Length Table (Dynamic):

| Span No | Chainage Start | Chainage End | Length | Pier/Abutment | Soffit Level | Formation Level | Construction Depth |
|---------|---------------|--------------|--------|---------------|--------------|-----------------|-------------------|
| 1 | `=Start` | `=Start+L1` | `=L1` | Abutment 1 | `=FL1-Girder_Depth-Deck-tc` | `=FL1` | `=FL1-Soffit` |
| 2 | `=End1` | `=End1+L2` | `=L2` | Pier 1 | `=FL2-Girder_Depth-Deck-tc` | `=FL2` | `=FL2-Soffit` |
| ... | | | | | | | |

---

## SECTION D: SOFFIT PROFILE

| Cell | Parameter | Input/Formula | Unit | Validation | Notes |
|------|-----------|---------------|------|------------|-------|
| **A42** | **SOFFIT PROFILE** | Header | - | - | |
| A44 | Soffit Profile Type | Dropdown | - | Constant/Parabolic/Straight | |
| A45 | Minimum Soffit Clearance | Input | m | 1.5 - 15.0 | Above HFL |
| A46 | Required Navigational Clearance | Input | m | 0 - 20 | If waterway |
| A47 | Soffit Slope (if sloped) | Input | % | 0 - 2.0 | For drainage |
| A48 | Parabolic Crown Height | Input | mm | 0 - 500 | If parabolic |
| A49 | Haunch at Support | Input | mm | 100 - 500 | Thickening |
| A50 | Haunch at Mid-span | Input | mm | 0 - 200 | |

### Soffit Level Calculation:
```
For Constant Profile:
Soffit = Formation Level - (Deck + Girder + Wearing)

For Parabolic Profile:
Soffit(x) = Soffit_support - (Soffit_support - Soffit_mid)*(1 - (2x/L)²)

Where x = distance from support, L = span length
```

---

```python
LONGITUDINAL_VALIDATION = {
    'Total_Length': {'type': 'decimal', 'min': 10, 'max': 2000},
    'Maximum_Grade': {'type': 'decimal', 'min': 0, 'max': 6.0},
    'Number_of_Spans': {'type': 'integer', 'min': 1, 'max': 20},
    'Standard_Span': {'type': 'decimal', 'min': 10, 'max': 100},
    'Pier_Thickness': {'type': 'decimal', 'min': 0.8, 'max': 3.0},
    'Minimum_Clearance': {'type': 'decimal', 'min': 1.5, 'max': 50},
    'Design_Discharge': {'type': 'decimal', 'min': 0, 'max': 50000},
    'Freeboard': {'type': 'decimal', 'min': 1.0, 'max': 5.0},
    
    'Dropdown_Lists': {
        'Curve_Type': ['Crest', 'Sag'],
        'Span_Configuration': ['Equal', 'Variable', 'Hybrid', 'End_Spans_Reduced'],
        'Soffit_Profile': ['Constant', 'Parabolic', 'Straight_Sloped', 'Stepped'],
        'Pier_Type': ['Solid Wall', 'Hammerhead', 'Multi-column', 'Single Column', 'Frame']
    },
    
    'Conditional': {
        'If_Waterway_Yes': ['Design_Discharge', 'HFL', 'LWL', 'Bed_Level'],
        'If_Variable_Spans': ['End_Span_Length']
    }
}
```
