# PYTHON INTEGRATION CODE

## File: `section_readers.py`

```python
"""
Cross-Section and Longitudinal Section Readers
Reads and processes cross-section and longitudinal section Excel sheets
"""

import pandas as pd
import numpy as np
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import List, Dict, Tuple, Optional
from enum import Enum
import math


class GirderType(Enum):
    T_GIRDER = "T-Girder"
    I_GIRDER = "I-Girder"
    BOX_GIRDER = "Box Girder"
    PLATE_GIRDER = "Plate Girder"


class BearingType(Enum):
    ELASTOMERIC = "Elastomeric"
    POT_PTFE = "POT-PTFE"
    ROCKER = "Rocker"
    ROLLER = "Roller"


@dataclass
class DeckCrossSection:
    """Deck cross-section parameters"""
    overall_width: float
    carriageway_width: float
    thickness_center: float  # mm
    thickness_edge: float  # mm
    thickness_variation: str  # Linear/Parabolic/Constant
    cross_slope: float  # %
    wearing_coat_thickness: float  # mm
    wearing_coat_type: str
    
    def get_thickness_at(self, x: float) -> float:
        """Get deck thickness at distance x from center"""
        if self.thickness_variation == "Constant":
            return self.thickness_center
        elif self.thickness_variation == "Linear":
            return self.thickness_center + (self.thickness_edge - self.thickness_center) * abs(2*x/self.overall_width)
        elif self.thickness_variation == "Parabolic":
            ratio = 2*x/self.overall_width
            return self.thickness_center + (self.thickness_edge - self.thickness_center) * (ratio ** 2)
        return self.thickness_center


@dataclass
class GirderCrossSection:
    """Girder cross-section parameters"""
    girder_type: GirderType
    num_girders: int
    spacing: float  # m
    web_thickness: float  # mm
    web_depth: float  # mm
    bottom_flange_width: float  # mm
    bottom_flange_thickness: float  # mm
    top_flange_width: Optional[float]  # mm
    top_flange_thickness: Optional[float]  # mm
    haunch_width: float  # mm
    haunch_thickness: float  # mm
    concrete_grade: str
    
    @property
    def area_concrete(self) -> float:
        """Calculate concrete area"""
        area = (self.web_thickness * self.web_depth + 
                self.bottom_flange_width * self.bottom_flange_thickness)
        if self.top_flange_width and self.top_flange_thickness:
            area += self.top_flange_width * self.top_flange_thickness
        return area  # mm²
    
    @property
    def moment_of_inertia(self) -> float:
        """Calculate approximate Ixx"""
        # Simplified calculation
        web_i = (self.web_thickness * self.web_depth**3) / 12
        bf_i = (self.bottom_flange_width * self.bottom_flange_thickness**3) / 12
        # Add parallel axis term
        y_bar = self.web_depth / 2
        bf_area = self.bottom_flange_width * self.bottom_flange_thickness
        bf_parallel = bf_area * (self.web_depth/2 + self.bottom_flange_thickness/2 - y_bar)**2
        return web_i + bf_i + bf_parallel
    
    @property
    def self_weight(self) -> float:
        """Self weight in kN/m"""
        return self.area_concrete * 25 / 1e6  # Concrete density 25 kN/m³


@dataclass
class KerbFootpath:
    """Kerb and footpath parameters"""
    kerb_width: float  # mm
    kerb_height: float  # mm
    kerb_type: str  # Mountable/Semi-mountable/Barrier
    kerb_concrete_grade: str
    footpath_width: float  # m
    footpath_thickness: float  # mm
    footpath_surface: str
    utility_trench_width: float  # mm
    utility_trench_depth: float  # mm


@dataclass
class RailingBarrier:
    """Railing and safety barrier parameters"""
    railing_type: str
    height: float  # mm
    post_spacing: float  # m
    post_section: str
    post_size: float  # mm
    rail_section: str
    rail_height: float  # mm
    impact_resistance: str  # H1/H2/H3/H4
    end_treatment: str


@dataclass
class DrainageSystem:
    """Drainage system parameters"""
    drainage_type: str
    num_lines: int
    location: str
    drain_width: Optional[float]  # mm
    drain_depth: Optional[float]  # mm
    pipe_diameter: Optional[float]  # mm
    scupper_spacing: float  # m
    scupper_size: str
    downpipe_diameter: float  # mm
    catch_pit_spacing: float  # m
    design_rainfall: float  # mm/hr
    
    def check_capacity(self, catchment_area: float) -> Dict:
        """Check drainage capacity"""
        flow = (self.design_rainfall * catchment_area) / 1000  # m³/hr
        velocity = 1.5  # m/s assumed
        
        if self.drainage_type == "Pipe":
            area = math.pi * (self.pipe_diameter/1000)**2 / 4
            capacity = area * velocity * 3600  # m³/hr
        else:
            area = (self.drain_width/1000) * (self.drain_depth/1000)
            capacity = area * velocity * 3600
        
        return {
            'required_flow': flow,
            'capacity': capacity,
            'adequate': capacity > flow,
            'safety_factor': capacity / flow if flow > 0 else 999
        }


@dataclass
class ExpansionJoint:
    """Expansion joint parameters"""
    joint_type: str
    width_closed: float  # mm
    width_open: float  # mm
    movement_capacity: float  # mm
    seal_material: str
    cover_plate_thickness: float  # mm
    cover_plate_width: float  # mm


@dataclass
class Bearing:
    """Bearing parameters"""
    bearing_type: BearingType
    length: float  # mm
    width: float  # mm
    total_thickness: float  # mm
    num_layers: Optional[int]
    shear_modulus: Optional[float]  # MPa
    allowable_stress: float  # MPa
    fixity_abutment: str
    fixity_pier: str


@dataclass
class CrossSectionModel:
    """Complete cross-section model"""
    deck: DeckCrossSection
    girder: GirderCrossSection
    kerb_footpath: KerbFootpath
    railing: RailingBarrier
    drainage: DrainageSystem
    expansion_joint: ExpansionJoint
    bearing: Bearing
    utilities: Dict  # Water, Electric, Telecom, Gas


@dataclass
class VerticalCurve:
    """Vertical curve parameters"""
    curve_type: str  # Crest/Sag
    pvi_chainage: float  # m
    pvi_elevation: float  # m
    grade_in: float  # %
    grade_out: float  # %
    design_speed: float  # km/h
    
    @property
    def grade_difference(self) -> float:
        return self.grade_out - self.grade_in
    
    @property
    def k_value(self) -> float:
        """IRC K value for vertical curves"""
        # Simplified formula
        return (self.design_speed ** 2) / 395
    
    @property
    def length(self) -> float:
        return self.k_value * abs(self.grade_difference)
    
    @property
    def tangent_distance(self) -> float:
        return self.length / 2
    
    @property
    def bvc_chainage(self) -> float:
        return self.pvi_chainage - self.tangent_distance
    
    @property
    def evc_chainage(self) -> float:
        return self.pvi_chainage + self.tangent_distance
    
    def elevation_at(self, chainage: float) -> float:
        """Calculate elevation at given chainage"""
        if chainage < self.bvc_chainage or chainage > self.evc_chainage:
            return None
        
        x = chainage - self.bvc_chainage
        g1 = self.grade_in / 100  # Convert to decimal
        A = self.grade_difference / 100
        
        # Parabolic curve equation
        y_bvc = self.pvi_elevation - self.grade_in * self.tangent_distance / 100
        
        elevation = y_bvc + g1 * x + (A * x**2) / (2 * self.length)
        return elevation


@dataclass
class Span:
    """Span data"""
    span_no: int
    chainage_start: float
    chainage_end: float
    length: float
    support_type_start: str
    support_type_end: str
    soffit_level: float
    formation_level: float
    
    @property
    def construction_depth(self) -> float:
        return self.formation_level - self.soffit_level


@dataclass
class Waterway:
    """Waterway parameters"""
    present: bool
    design_discharge: float  # m³/s
    hfl: float  # m
    lwl: float  # m
    bed_level: float  # m
    scour_depth: float  # m
    foundation_level: float  # m
    freeboard: float  # m
    
    def check_ventway(self, provided_area: float) -> Dict:
        """Check waterway adequacy"""
        velocity = 2.5  # m/s assumed
        required_area = self.design_discharge / velocity
        
        return {
            'required_area': required_area,
            'provided_area': provided_area,
            'adequate': provided_area > required_area,
            'ratio': provided_area / required_area if required_area > 0 else 999
        }


@dataclass
class LongitudinalSectionModel:
    """Complete longitudinal section model"""
    chainage_start: float
    chainage_end: float
    total_length: float
    datum_level: float
    formation_level_start: float
    formation_level_end: float
    vertical_curves: List[VerticalCurve]
    spans: List[Span]
    waterway: Optional[Waterway]
    soffit_profile_type: str
    minimum_clearance: float
    
    def get_formation_level(self, chainage: float) -> float:
        """Get formation level at chainage"""
        grade = (self.formation_level_end - self.formation_level_start) / self.total_length
        return self.formation_level_start + grade * chainage
    
    def get_elevation_at(self, chainage: float, component: str = 'formation') -> float:
        """Get elevation at chainage for specified component"""
        if component == 'formation':
            return self.get_formation_level(chainage)
        elif component == 'soffit':
            # Find which span
            for span in self.spans:
                if span.chainage_start <= chainage <= span.chainage_end:
                    return span.soffit_level
        return None


class CrossSectionReader:
    """Read cross-section data from Excel"""
    
    def __init__(self, excel_path: str):
        self.excel_path = excel_path
        self.model = None
    
    def read(self) -> CrossSectionModel:
        """Read complete cross-section model"""
        # Read deck parameters
        deck = self._read_deck()
        
        # Read girder parameters
        girder = self._read_girder()
        
        # Read kerb and footpath
        kerb = self._read_kerb()
        
        # Read railing
        railing = self._read_railing()
        
        # Read drainage
        drainage = self._read_drainage()
        
        # Read expansion joint
        expansion = self._read_expansion()
        
        # Read bearings
        bearing = self._read_bearing()
        
        # Read utilities
        utilities = self._read_utilities()
        
        self.model = CrossSectionModel(
            deck=deck,
            girder=girder,
            kerb_footpath=kerb,
            railing=railing,
            drainage=drainage,
            expansion_joint=expansion,
            bearing=bearing,
            utilities=utilities
        )
        
        return self.model
    
    def _read_deck(self) -> DeckCrossSection:
        df = pd.read_excel(self.excel_path, sheet_name='CROSS_SECTION_INPUT', header=0)
        
        return DeckCrossSection(
            overall_width=float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Overall Deck Width', 'Value'].values[0]),
            carriageway_width=float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Carriageway Width', 'Value'].values[0]),
            thickness_center=float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Deck Thickness at Center', 'Value'].values[0]),
            thickness_edge=float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Deck Thickness at Edge', 'Value'].values[0]),
            thickness_variation=str(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Deck Thickness Variation', 'Value'].values[0]),
            cross_slope=float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Cross Slope', 'Value'].values[0]),
            wearing_coat_thickness=float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Wearing Coat Thickness', 'Value'].values[0]),
            wearing_coat_type=str(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Wearing Coat Type', 'Value'].values[0])
        )
    
    def _read_girder(self) -> GirderCrossSection:
        df = pd.read_excel(self.excel_path, sheet_name='CROSS_SECTION_INPUT', header=0)
        
        girder_type_str = str(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Girder Type', 'Value'].values[0])
        
        return GirderCrossSection(
            girder_type=GirderType(girder_type_str),
            num_girders=int(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Number of Girders', 'Value'].values[0]),
            spacing=float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Girder Spacing', 'Value'].values[0]),
            web_thickness=float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Web Thickness', 'Value'].values[0]),
            web_depth=float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Web Depth', 'Value'].values[0]),
            bottom_flange_width=float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Bottom Flange Width', 'Value'].values[0]),
            bottom_flange_thickness=float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Bottom Flange Thickness', 'Value'].values[0]),
            top_flange_width=float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Top Flange Width (if I-girder)', 'Value'].values[0]) if 'I' in girder_type_str else None,
            top_flange_thickness=float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Top Flange Thickness', 'Value'].values[0]) if 'I' in girder_type_str else None,
            haunch_width=float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Haunch Width', 'Value'].values[0]),
            haunch_thickness=float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Haunch Thickness', 'Value'].values[0]),
            concrete_grade=str(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Girder Concrete Grade', 'Value'].values[0])
        )
    
    def _read_kerb(self) -> KerbFootpath:
        df = pd.read_excel(self.excel_path, sheet_name='CROSS_SECTION_INPUT', header=0)
        
        return KerbFootpath(
            kerb_width=float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Kerb Width', 'Value'].values[0]),
            kerb_height=float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Kerb Height', 'Value'].values[0]),
            kerb_type='Barrier',  # Default or read from dropdown
            kerb_concrete_grade=str(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Kerb Concrete Grade', 'Value'].values[0]),
            footpath_width=float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Footpath Width', 'Value'].values[0]),
            footpath_thickness=float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Footpath Thickness', 'Value'].values[0]),
            footpath_surface=str(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Footpath Surface', 'Value'].values[0]),
            utility_trench_width=float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Utility Trench Width', 'Value'].values[0]),
            utility_trench_depth=float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Utility Trench Depth', 'Value'].values[0])
        )
    
    def _read_railing(self) -> RailingBarrier:
        df = pd.read_excel(self.excel_path, sheet_name='CROSS_SECTION_INPUT', header=0)
        
        return RailingBarrier(
            railing_type=str(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Railing Type', 'Value'].values[0]),
            height=float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Railing Height', 'Value'].values[0]),
            post_spacing=float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Post Spacing', 'Value'].values[0]),
            post_section=str(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Post Section', 'Value'].values[0]),
            post_size=float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Post Size', 'Value'].values[0]),
            rail_section=str(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Rail Section', 'Value'].values[0]),
            rail_height=float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Rail Height from Deck', 'Value'].values[0]),
            impact_resistance=str(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Impact Resistance Level', 'Value'].values[0]),
            end_treatment=str(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='End Treatment', 'Value'].values[0])
        )
    
    def _read_drainage(self) -> DrainageSystem:
        df = pd.read_excel(self.excel_path, sheet_name='CROSS_SECTION_INPUT', header=0)
        
        return DrainageSystem(
            drainage_type=str(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Drainage Type', 'Value'].values[0]),
            num_lines=int(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Number of Drainage Lines', 'Value'].values[0]),
            location=str(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Drain Location', 'Value'].values[0]),
            drain_width=float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Drain Width', 'Value'].values[0]) if 'Channel' in str(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Drainage Type', 'Value'].values[0]) else None,
            drain_depth=float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Drain Depth', 'Value'].values[0]) if 'Channel' in str(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Drainage Type', 'Value'].values[0]) else None,
            pipe_diameter=float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Pipe Diameter', 'Value'].values[0]) if 'Pipe' in str(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Drainage Type', 'Value'].values[0]) else None,
            scupper_spacing=float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Scupper Spacing', 'Value'].values[0]),
            scupper_size=str(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Scupper Size', 'Value'].values[0]),
            downpipe_diameter=float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Downpipe Diameter', 'Value'].values[0]),
            catch_pit_spacing=float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Catch Pit Spacing', 'Value'].values[0]),
            design_rainfall=float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Design Rainfall Intensity', 'Value'].values[0])
        )
    
    def _read_expansion(self) -> ExpansionJoint:
        df = pd.read_excel(self.excel_path, sheet_name='CROSS_SECTION_INPUT', header=0)
        
        return ExpansionJoint(
            joint_type=str(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Joint Type', 'Value'].values[0]),
            width_closed=float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Joint Width (closed)', 'Value'].values[0]),
            width_open=float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Joint Width (open)', 'Value'].values[0]),
            movement_capacity=float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Movement Capacity', 'Value'].values[0]),
            seal_material=str(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Seal Material', 'Value'].values[0]),
            cover_plate_thickness=float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Cover Plate Thickness', 'Value'].values[0]),
            cover_plate_width=float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Cover Plate Width', 'Value'].values[0])
        )
    
    def _read_bearing(self) -> Bearing:
        df = pd.read_excel(self.excel_path, sheet_name='CROSS_SECTION_INPUT', header=0)
        
        bearing_type_str = str(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Bearing Type', 'Value'].values[0])
        
        return Bearing(
            bearing_type=BearingType(bearing_type_str),
            length=float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Bearing Size (L)', 'Value'].values[0]),
            width=float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Bearing Size (W)', 'Value'].values[0]),
            total_thickness=float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Total Thickness', 'Value'].values[0]),
            num_layers=int(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Number of Layers', 'Value'].values[0]) if 'Elastomeric' in bearing_type_str else None,
            shear_modulus=float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Shear Modulus', 'Value'].values[0]) if 'Elastomeric' in bearing_type_str else None,
            allowable_stress=float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Allowable Stress', 'Value'].values[0]),
            fixity_abutment=str(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Fixity at Abutment', 'Value'].values[0]),
            fixity_pier=str(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Fixity at Pier', 'Value'].values[0])
        )
    
    def _read_utilities(self) -> Dict:
        df = pd.read_excel(self.excel_path, sheet_name='CROSS_SECTION_INPUT', header=0)
        
        return {
            'water_main': {
                'present': df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Water Main', 'Value'].values[0] == 'Y',
                'diameter': float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Water Main Diameter', 'Value'].values[0]) if df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Water Main', 'Value'].values[0] == 'Y' else 0
            },
            'electrical': {
                'present': df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Electrical Conduit', 'Value'].values[0] == 'Y',
                'size': float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Conduit Size', 'Value'].values[0]) if df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Electrical Conduit', 'Value'].values[0] == 'Y' else 0
            },
            'telecom': df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Telecommunication', 'Value'].values[0] == 'Y',
            'gas': df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Gas Pipeline', 'Value'].values[0] == 'Y'
        }


class LongitudinalSectionReader:
    """Read longitudinal section data from Excel"""
    
    def __init__(self, excel_path: str):
        self.excel_path = excel_path
        self.model = None
    
    def read(self) -> LongitudinalSectionModel:
        """Read complete longitudinal section model"""
        df = pd.read_excel(self.excel_path, sheet_name='LONGITUDINAL_SECTION_INPUT')
        
        # Read basic alignment
        chainage_start = float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Chainage Start', 'Value'].values[0])
        chainage_end = float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Chainage End', 'Value'].values[0])
        
        # Read vertical curves
        vertical_curves = self._read_vertical_curves(df)
        
        # Read spans
        spans = self._read_spans(df)
        
        # Read waterway
        waterway = self._read_waterway(df)
        
        self.model = LongitudinalSectionModel(
            chainage_start=chainage_start,
            chainage_end=chainage_end,
            total_length=chainage_end - chainage_start,
            datum_level=float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Datum Level', 'Value'].values[0]),
            formation_level_start=float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Formation Level Start', 'Value'].values[0]),
            formation_level_end=float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Formation Level End', 'Value'].values[0]),
            vertical_curves=vertical_curves,
            spans=spans,
            waterway=waterway,
            soffit_profile_type=str(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Soffit Profile Type', 'Value'].values[0]),
            minimum_clearance=float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Minimum Soffit Clearance', 'Value'].values[0])
        )
        
        return self.model
    
    def _read_vertical_curves(self, df: pd.DataFrame) -> List[VerticalCurve]:
        """Read vertical curves from table"""
        curves = []
        
        # Find vertical curve table
        num_curves = int(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Number of Vertical Curves', 'Value'].values[0])
        
        for i in range(1, num_curves + 1):
            curve = VerticalCurve(
                curve_type=str(df.loc[df['Parameter']==f'Curve {i} Type', 'Value'].values[0]),
                pvi_chainage=float(df.loc[df['Parameter']==f'Curve {i} Chainage (PVI)', 'Value'].values[0]),
                pvi_elevation=float(df.loc[df['Parameter']==f'Curve {i} Elevation (PVI)', 'Value'].values[0]),
                grade_in=float(df.loc[df['Parameter']==f'Curve {i} Grade In (g1)', 'Value'].values[0]),
                grade_out=float(df.loc[df['Parameter']==f'Curve {i} Grade Out (g2)', 'Value'].values[0]),
                design_speed=float(df.loc[df['Parameter']==f'Curve {i} Design Speed', 'Value'].values[0])
            )
            curves.append(curve)
        
        return curves
    
    def _read_spans(self, df: pd.DataFrame) -> List[Span]:
        """Read span arrangement"""
        spans = []
        
        num_spans = int(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Number of Spans', 'Value'].values[0])
        span_config = str(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Span Configuration', 'Value'].values[0])
        standard_span = float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Standard Span Length', 'Value'].values[0])
        
        chainage = float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Chainage Start', 'Value'].values[0])
        
        for i in range(1, num_spans + 1):
            if span_config == 'Equal':
                span_length = standard_span
            elif span_config == 'Variable' and i in [1, num_spans]:
                span_length = float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='End Span Length (if variable)', 'Value'].values[0])
            else:
                span_length = standard_span
            
            # Calculate soffit and formation levels
            formation_level = self._interpolate_formation(chainage + span_length/2)
            soffit_level = formation_level - 2.5  # Approximate
            
            span = Span(
                span_no=i,
                chainage_start=chainage,
                chainage_end=chainage + span_length,
                length=span_length,
                support_type_start='Abutment' if i == 1 else 'Pier',
                support_type_end='Abutment' if i == num_spans else 'Pier',
                soffit_level=soffit_level,
                formation_level=formation_level
            )
            spans.append(span)
            
            # Add pier thickness for next span
            pier_thickness = float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Pier Thickness', 'Value'].values[0])
            chainage += span_length + pier_thickness
        
        return spans
    
    def _read_waterway(self, df: pd.DataFrame) -> Optional[Waterway]:
        """Read waterway data if present"""
        present = df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Waterway Present', 'Value'].values[0] == 'Y'
        
        if not present:
            return None
        
        return Waterway(
            present=True,
            design_discharge=float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Design Discharge (Q)', 'Value'].values[0]),
            hfl=float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='High Flood Level (HFL)', 'Value'].values[0]),
            lwl=float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Low Water Level (LWL)', 'Value'].values[0]),
            bed_level=float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Bed Level', 'Value'].values[0]),
            scour_depth=float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Scour Depth', 'Value'].values[0]),
            foundation_level=float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Foundation Level', 'Value'].values[0]),
            freeboard=float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Freeboard above HFL', 'Value'].values[0])
        )
    
    def _interpolate_formation(self, chainage: float) -> float:
        """Interpolate formation level at chainage"""
        # Simplified - should use actual grade
        return 100.0  # Placeholder


class SectionGenerator:
    """Generate cross and longitudinal sections for CAD"""
    
    def __init__(self, cross_model: CrossSectionModel, long_model: LongitudinalSectionModel):
        self.cross = cross_model
        self.long = long_model
    
    def generate_cross_section_dxf(self, chainage: float, station_type: str = 'midspan') -> str:
        """Generate DXF cross-section at specified chainage"""
        dxf_lines = []
        dxf_lines.append("0\nSECTION\n2\nENTITIES\n")
        
        # Draw deck slab
        deck_points = self._calculate_deck_points()
        dxf_lines.append(self._create_lwpolyline(deck_points, layer="DECK"))
        
        # Draw girders
        for i in range(self.cross.girder.num_girders):
            girder_points = self._calculate_girder_points(i)
            dxf_lines.append(self._create_lwpolyline(girder_points, layer="GIRDER"))
        
        # Draw kerbs
        left_kerb = self._calculate_kerb_points('left')
        right_kerb = self._calculate_kerb_points('right')
        dxf_lines.append(self._create_lwpolyline(left_kerb, layer="KERB"))
        dxf_lines.append(self._create_lwpolyline(right_kerb, layer="KERB"))
        
        # Draw railings
        if self.cross.railing.height > 0:
            railing_left = self._calculate_railing_points('left')
            railing_right = self._calculate_railing_points('right')
            dxf_lines.append(self._create_lwpolyline(railing_left, layer="RAILING"))
            dxf_lines.append(self._create_lwpolyline(railing_right, layer="RAILING"))
        
        dxf_lines.append("0\nENDSEC\n0\nEOF\n")
        
        return "\n".join(dxf_lines)
    
    def _calculate_deck_points(self) -> List[Tuple[float, float]]:
        """Calculate deck slab outline points"""
        w = self.cross.deck.overall_width / 2
        t = self.cross.deck.thickness_center / 1000  # Convert to m
        
        points = [
            (-w, t),  # Top left
            (w, t),   # Top right
            (w, 0),   # Bottom right
            (-w, 0),  # Bottom left
            (-w, t)   # Close
        ]
        return points
    
    def _calculate_girder_points(self, girder_index: int) -> List[Tuple[float, float]]:
        """Calculate girder outline points"""
        spacing = self.cross.girder.spacing
        num = self.cross.girder.num_girders
        
        # Calculate girder position
        if num % 2 == 0:
            x_pos = (girder_index - (num-1)/2) * spacing
        else:
            x_pos = (girder_index - num//2) * spacing
        
        tw = self.cross.girder.web_thickness / 1000 / 2
        dw = self.cross.girder.web_depth / 1000
        bf = self.cross.girder.bottom_flange_width / 1000 / 2
        tf = self.cross.girder.bottom_flange_thickness / 1000
        
        deck_thickness = self.cross.deck.thickness_center / 1000
        
        points = [
            (x_pos - tw, deck_thickness),  # Top web left
            (x_pos + tw, deck_thickness),  # Top web right
            (x_pos + tw, deck_thickness - dw),  # Bottom web right
            (x_pos + bf, deck_thickness - dw),  # Flange right
            (x_pos + bf, deck_thickness - dw - tf),  # Flange bottom right
            (x_pos - bf, deck_thickness - dw - tf),  # Flange bottom left
            (x_pos - bf, deck_thickness - dw),  # Flange left
            (x_pos - tw, deck_thickness - dw),  # Bottom web left
            (x_pos - tw, deck_thickness)   # Close
        ]
        return points
    
    def _create_lwpolyline(self, points: List[Tuple[float, float]], layer: str = "0") -> str:
        """Create DXF LWPOLYLINE entity"""
        dxf = f"0\nLWPOLYLINE\n8\n{layer}\n90\n{len(points)}\n70\n1\n"
        for x, y in points:
            dxf += f"10\n{x}\n20\n{y}\n"
        return dxf
    
    def generate_longitudinal_section_dxf(self) -> str:
        """Generate DXF longitudinal section"""
        dxf_lines = []
        dxf_lines.append("0\nSECTION\n2\nENTITIES\n")
        
        # Draw formation level
        formation_points = []
        for span in self.long.spans:
            formation_points.append((span.chainage_start, span.formation_level))
            formation_points.append((span.chainage_end, span.formation_level))
        dxf_lines.append(self._create_lwpolyline(formation_points, layer="FORMATION"))
        
        # Draw soffit profile
        soffit_points = []
        for span in self.long.spans:
            soffit_points.append((span.chainage_start, span.soffit_level))
            soffit_points.append((span.chainage_end, span.soffit_level))
        dxf_lines.append(self._create_lwpolyline(soffit_points, layer="SOFFIT"))
        
        # Draw piers
        for i, span in enumerate(self.long.spans[:-1]):
            pier_x = span.chainage_end
            pier_top = span.soffit_level - 0.5  # Approximate
            pier_bottom = span.soffit_level - 5.0  # Approximate
            
            pier_points = [
                (pier_x - 0.5, pier_top),
                (pier_x + 0.5, pier_top),
                (pier_x + 0.5, pier_bottom),
                (pier_x - 0.5, pier_bottom),
                (pier_x - 0.5, pier_top)
            ]
            dxf_lines.append(self._create_lwpolyline(pier_points, layer="PIER"))
        
        dxf_lines.append("0\nENDSEC\n0\nEOF\n")
        
        return "\n".join(dxf_lines)
    
    def generate_section_report(self) -> Dict:
        """Generate comprehensive section report"""
        return {
            'cross_section': {
                'deck_area': self.cross.deck.overall_width * (self.cross.deck.thickness_center / 1000),
                'girder_spacing_check': 'OK' if 2.5 <= self.cross.girder.spacing <= 3.5 else 'REVIEW',
                'span_to_depth_ratio': self.cross.girder.web_depth / 1000,  # Need span length
                'drainage_check': self.cross.drainage.check_capacity(
                    self.cross.deck.overall_width * 100  # Assume 100m catchment
                ),
                'bearing_stress_check': 'PENDING'
            },
            'longitudinal_section': {
                'total_length': self.long.total_length,
                'num_spans': len(self.long.spans),
                'max_span': max(s.length for s in self.long.spans),
                'min_span': min(s.length for s in self.long.spans),
                'span_uniformity': min(s.length for s in self.long.spans) / max(s.length for s in self.long.spans),
                'vertical_curves': len(self.long.vertical_curves),
                'waterway_check': self.long.waterway.check_ventway(100) if self.long.waterway else None
            }
        }


# Usage Example
if __name__ == "__main__":
    # Read cross-section
    cross_reader = CrossSectionReader("bridge_input.xlsx")
    cross_model = cross_reader.read()
    
    # Read longitudinal section
    long_reader = LongitudinalSectionReader("bridge_input.xlsx")
    long_model = long_reader.read()
    
    # Generate sections
    generator = SectionGenerator(cross_model, long_model)
    
    # Generate DXF files
    cross_dxf = generator.generate_cross_section_dxf(chainage=50.0, station_type='midspan')
    long_dxf = generator.generate_longitudinal_section_dxf()
    
    # Save files
    with open("cross_section.dxf", "w") as f:
        f.write(cross_dxf)
    
    with open("longitudinal_section.dlsx", "w") as f:
        f.write(long_dxf)
    
    # Generate report
    report = generator.generate_section_report()
    print(report)
```

# SHEET 18: REINFORCEMENT_BEAMS

## Purpose
Complete reinforcement detailing for all beam/girder elements including longitudinal bars, stirrups, bent-up bars, and side face reinforcement.

---

## SECTION A: BEAM IDENTIFICATION

| Cell | Parameter | Input | Unit | Validation | Notes |
|------|-----------|-------|------|------------|-------|
| **A1** | **BEAM REINFORCEMENT SCHEDULE** | Header | - | - | |
| A3 | Beam ID | Text | - | B1, B2, B3... | Unique identifier |
| A4 | Beam Location | Dropdown | - | Interior/Exterior/Cantilever | |
| A5 | Span Number | Integer | - | 1-20 | Reference to span |
| A6 | Beam Type | Dropdown | - | T-Beam/I-Beam/Rectangular/L-Beam | |
| A7 | Concrete Grade | Dropdown | - | M25/M30/M35/M40/M45 | |
| A8 | Steel Grade | Dropdown | - | Fe415/Fe500/Fe550 | |
| A9 | Clear Cover (Top) | Input | mm | 25-75 | |
| A10 | Clear Cover (Bottom) | Input | mm | 25-75 | |
| A11 | Clear Cover (Sides) | Input | mm | 25-50 | |

---

## SECTION B: BEAM GEOMETRY (Auto-linked from Cross-Section)

| Cell | Parameter | Formula/Input | Unit | Notes |
|------|-----------|---------------|------|-------|
| **A13** | **BEAM GEOMETRY** | Header | - | |
| B14 | Overall Depth (D) | `=GIRDER_DEPTH` | mm | From CROSS_SECTION |
| B15 | Web Width (bw) | `=WEB_THICKNESS` | mm | From CROSS_SECTION |
| B16 | Effective Depth (d) | `=D-COVER_BOTTOM-STIRRUP_DIA` | mm | |
| B17 | Span Length (L) | `=SPAN_LENGTH` | m | From SPAN_DATA |
| B18 | Effective Span (Leff) | `=L+BEARING_WIDTH` | m | Center-to-center |

---

## SECTION C: LONGITUDINAL REINFORCEMENT - TOP (AT SUPPORT)

| Bar Mark | Location | Level | Dia (mm) | No. of Bars | Length Calculation | Development Length | Anchorage |
|----------|----------|-------|----------|-------------|-------------------|-------------------|-----------|
| T1 | Support Top | 1 | 20 | 4 | `=Leff/3+Ld` | `=40*DIA` | 90° Hook |
| T2 | Support Top | 2 | 20 | 2 | `=Leff/4+Ld` | `=40*DIA` | Straight |
| T3 | Support Top | 3 | 16 | 2 | `=Leff/5+Ld` | `=40*DIA` | Straight |

**Formulas:**
- Development Length: `Ld = 0.87*fy*φ / (4*τbd)` where τbd depends on concrete grade
- For M30, Fe500: Ld ≈ 45φ (simplified)
- Length into span: Leff/3 for moment resistance

---

## SECTION D: LONGITUDINAL REINFORCEMENT - BOTTOM (AT MID-SPAN)

| Bar Mark | Location | Level | Dia (mm) | No. of Bars | Length Calculation | Development Length | Anchorage |
|----------|----------|-------|----------|-------------|-------------------|-------------------|-----------|
| B1 | Mid-span Bottom | 1 | 25 | 6 | `=Leff+2*Ld` | `=45*DIA` | 90° Hook |
| B2 | Mid-span Bottom | 2 | 25 | 2 | `=Leff+2*Ld` | `=45*DIA` | 90° Hook |
| B3 | Curtailment | 1 | 20 | 2 | `=0.15*Leff+Ld` | `=40*DIA` | 90° Hook |

**Curtailment Rules:**
- 50% bars can be curtailed at 0.15L from support
- Remaining bars continue full length

---

## SECTION E: BENT-UP BARS

| Bar Mark | From | To | Dia (mm) | No. of Bars | Inclination | Length Formula |
|----------|------|-----|----------|-------------|-------------|----------------|
| BU1 | Bottom@Support | Top@Midspan | 20 | 2 | 45° | `=L/2/sin(45)+2*Ld` |
| BU2 | Bottom@Midspan | Top@Support | 20 | 2 | 45° | `=L/2/sin(45)+2*Ld` |

**Bent-up Bar Geometry:**
```
Support                    Mid-span                    Support
  |                           |                           |
  |    ╱                      |                      ╲    |
  |   ╱                       |                       ╲   |
  |  ╱  ← BU1                 |                 BU2 →  ╲  |
  | ╱                         |                         ╲ |
  |╱                          |                          ╲|
Bottom bars               Bottom bars               Bottom bars
```

---

## SECTION F: SHEAR REINFORCEMENT (STIRRUPS)

| Zone | Start Chainage | End Chainage | Stirrup Dia (mm) | Legs | Spacing (mm) | Link Type | A_sv Provided |
|------|---------------|--------------|------------------|------|--------------|-----------|---------------|
| Support Zone | 0 | `=0.15*L` | 10 | 4 | 150 | Closed | `=4*78.5` |
| Mid Zone | `=0.15*L` | `=0.7*L` | 10 | 4 | 200 | Closed | `=4*78.5` |
| Support Zone | `=0.7*L` | `=L` | 10 | 4 | 150 | Closed | `=4*78.5` |

**Shear Design Check:**
```
τ_v = Vu / (bw*d)  [Actual shear stress]
τ_c = From IS 456 Table 19 based on pt%  [Permissible]
If τ_v > τ_c: Provide stirrups
A_sv required = (τ_v - τ_c)*bw*s / (0.87*fy)
```

---

## SECTION G: SIDE FACE REINFORCEMENT (FOR DEEP BEAMS)

| Bar Mark | Location | Dia (mm) | No. of Bars | Spacing (mm) | Distribution |
|----------|----------|----------|-------------|--------------|--------------|
| SF1 | Each Face | 12 | `=D/300` max spacing | 200 | Vertical |
| SF2 | Each Face | 12 | Same as above | 200 | Horizontal |

**Requirement:** When D > 750mm, provide side face reinforcement @ 0.1% of web area

---

## SECTION H: ANCHORAGE AND LAPPING

| Bar Dia (mm) | Development Length (Ld) | Lap Length (Tension) | Lap Length (Compression) | Hook Length |
|--------------|------------------------|---------------------|--------------------------|-------------|
| 10 | `=45*10` | `=1.3*Ld` | `=Ld` | `=10*DIA` |
| 12 | `=45*12` | `=1.3*Ld` | `=Ld` | `=10*DIA` |
| 16 | `=45*16` | `=1.3*Ld` | `=Ld` | `=10*DIA` |
| 20 | `=45*20` | `=1.3*Ld` | `=Ld` | `=10*DIA` |
| 25 | `=45*25` | `=1.3*Ld` | `=Ld` | `=10*DIA` |

---

## SECTION I: BAR BENDING SCHEDULE - BEAMS

| Bar Mark | Description | Shape | Dia (mm) | No. | A (mm) | B (mm) | C (mm) | D (mm) | R (mm) | Length (mm) | Weight (kg) |
|----------|-------------|-------|----------|-----|--------|--------|--------|--------|--------|-------------|-------------|
| T1 | Top bar - Support | Shape 1 | 20 | 4 | 3000 | - | - | - | - | 3000 | `=4*3000*2.47/1000` |
| T2 | Top bar - Support | Shape 1 | 20 | 2 | 2500 | - | - | - | - | 2500 | `=2*2500*2.47/1000` |
| B1 | Bottom bar - Full | Shape 1 | 25 | 6 | 31000 | - | - | - | - | 31000 | `=6*31000*3.85/1000` |
| BU1 | Bent-up bar | Shape 5 | 20 | 2 | 15000 | 1000 | 15000 | - | 4D | 32000 | `=2*32000*2.47/1000` |
| S1 | Stirrup - Support | Shape 3 | 10 | 67 | 1800 | 2200 | - | - | - | `=2*(1800+2200)+100` | `=67*8100*0.62/1000` |

### Shape Codes (IS 2502):

| Shape | Diagram | Dimensions | Application |
|-------|---------|------------|-------------|
| Shape 1 | ───────────── | A = Straight length | Straight bars |
| Shape 2 | └──────── | A, B with 90° hook | L-bars |
| Shape 3 | ┌───────┐ | A, B rectangular | Stirrups, ties |
| Shape 4 | ╱─────╲ | A, B, C bent-up | Bent-up bars |
| Shape 5 | ╱───────╲ | A, B, C double bent | Double bent |
| Shape 6 | ~~~~~~~ | Diameter, pitch | Helical/ties |

| A3 | Pier ID | Text | - | P1, P2, P3... | Unique identifier |
| A4 | Pier Type | Dropdown | - | Solid Wall/Hammerhead/Multi-column/Frame | |
| A5 | Pier Location | Integer | - | 1-19 | Between spans |
| A6 | Height (H) | Input | m | 3-50 | From foundation to cap |
| A7 | Concrete Grade | Dropdown | - | M30/M35/M40 | |
| A8 | Steel Grade | Dropdown | - | Fe415/Fe500 | |
| A9 | Seismic Zone | Dropdown | - | II/III/IV/V | For ductility |

---

## SECTION B: PIER COLUMN/SHAFT REINFORCEMENT

### B.1 Circular Pier (Diameter D)

| Parameter | Value | Unit | Formula |
|-----------|-------|------|---------|
| Diameter (D) | 1200 | mm | Input |
| Gross Area (Ag) | `=π*D²/4` | mm² | Calculated |
| Minimum Steel (0.8%) | `=0.008*Ag` | mm² | IS 456 |
| Maximum Steel (4%) | `=0.04*Ag` | mm² | IS 456 |
| Main Bar Dia | 25 | mm | Input |
| No. of Main Bars | 20 | nos | `=ceil(Steel_provided/(π*DIA²/4))` |
| Steel Provided | `=20*491` | mm² | Check > Min |

### B.2 Longitudinal Bars - Circular Pier

| Bar Mark | Location | Dia (mm) | No. | Length Formula | Development | Anchorage |
|----------|----------|----------|-----|----------------|-------------|-----------|
| PC1 | Vertical - Full height | 25 | 20 | `=H*1000+CAP_DEPTH+FOOT_DEPTH+2*Ld` | 45D | Standard hooks |
| PC2 | Vertical - Lapped | 25 | 20 | `=H*1000/3+Ld` | 45D | Lap with PC1 |

**Lap Location:** Stagger laps at H/3, 2H/3

### B.3 Transverse Reinforcement - Circular Pier

| Parameter | Value | Unit | Notes |
|-----------|-------|------|-------|
| Tie Dia | 10 | mm | Min 10mm or 1/4 main bar |
| Spiral/Tie Pitch (s) | 150 | mm | Min of 75mm, 16*main_dia, D/6 |
| Spiral/Tie Pitch (confined) | 100 | mm | Reduced in plastic hinge zone |
| Confinement Zone Length | `=MAX(D, H/6)` | mm | At top and bottom |

### Helical Reinforcement Calculation:
```
Length of one turn = √(π*D² + s²) where D = core diameter
Total length = No. of turns × Length per turn
No. of turns = Height / Pitch
```

---

# PYTHON INTEGRATION CODE

## File: `reinforcement_reader.py`

```python
"""
Reinforcement Detailing Reader and Processor
Reads reinforcement sheets and generates BBS
"""

import pandas as pd
import numpy as np
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import List, Dict, Tuple, Optional
from enum import Enum
import math


@dataclass
class BeamReinforcement:
    """Beam reinforcement details"""
    beam_id: str
    beam_type: str
    span_length: float
    concrete_grade: str
    steel_grade: str
    
    # Geometry
    overall_depth: float
    web_width: float
    effective_depth: float
    
    # Longitudinal bars
    top_bars_support: List[Dict]  # [{dia, nos, length, mark}, ...]
    bottom_bars_midspan: List[Dict]
    bent_up_bars: List[Dict]
    
    # Shear reinforcement
    stirrups: List[Dict]  # [{zone, dia, legs, spacing, quantity}, ...]
    
    # Side face reinforcement
    side_face_bars: Optional[List[Dict]]
    
    def calculate_steel_weight(self) -> Dict:
        """Calculate total steel weight by category"""
        weights = {
            'longitudinal': 0,
            'shear': 0,
            'side_face': 0,
            'total': 0
        }
        
        unit_weights = {10: 0.617, 12: 0.888, 16: 1.578, 20: 2.466, 25: 3.853}
        
        # Top bars
        for bar in self.top_bars_support:
            wt = bar['nos'] * bar['length']/1000 * unit_weights.get(bar['dia'], 0)
            weights['longitudinal'] += wt
        
        # Bottom bars
        for bar in self.bottom_bars_midspan:
            wt = bar['nos'] * bar['length']/1000 * unit_weights.get(bar['dia'], 0)
            weights['longitudinal'] += wt
        
        # Bent-up bars
        for bar in self.bent_up_bars:
            wt = bar['nos'] * bar['length']/1000 * unit_weights.get(bar['dia'], 0)
            weights['longitudinal'] += wt
        
        # Stirrups
        for stirrup in self.stirrups:
            # Calculate length of one stirrup
            leg_length = 2 * (self.web_width + self.effective_depth - 100)  # Approx
            hook_length = 20 * stirrup['dia']
            stirrup_length = leg_length + hook_length
            
            total_length = stirrup['quantity'] * stirrup_length
            wt = total_length/1000 * unit_weights.get(stirrup['dia'], 0)
            weights['shear'] += wt
        
        weights['total'] = sum(weights.values())
        return weights


@dataclass
class PierReinforcement:
    """Pier reinforcement details"""
    pier_id: str
    pier_type: str
    height: float
    concrete_grade: str
    
    # Column/Shaft
    diameter: Optional[float]  # For circular
    length: Optional[float]  # For wall
    width: Optional[float]
    
    # Longitudinal bars
    main_bar_dia: int
    num_main_bars: int
    main_bar_length: float
    
    # Transverse reinforcement
    tie_dia: int
    tie_pitch_normal: float
    tie_pitch_confinement: float
    confinement_zone_length: float
    
    # Pier cap
    cap_length: float
    cap_width: float
    cap_depth: float
    cap_top_bars: List[Dict]
    cap_bottom_bars: List[Dict]
    cap_stirrups: List[Dict]
    
    def calculate_helix_length(self) -> float:
        """Calculate length of helical/tie reinforcement"""
        if self.pier_type == 'Circular':
            core_dia = self.diameter - 100  # Approx
            turns_normal = (self.height - 2*self.confinement_zone_length) / self.tie_pitch_normal
            turns_confinement = 2 * self.confinement_zone_length / self.tie_pitch_confinement
            
            length_per_turn = math.pi * core_dia
            total_length = (turns_normal + turns_confinement) * length_per_turn
            
            return total_length
        else:
            # Rectangular ties
            perimeter = 2 * (self.length + self.width)
            num_ties = self.height / self.tie_pitch_normal
            return perimeter * num_ties


@dataclass
class FoundationReinforcement:
    """Foundation reinforcement details"""
    foundation_id: str
    foundation_type: str
    concrete_grade: str
    
    # Geometry
    length: float
    width: float
    thickness: float
    
    # Bottom reinforcement
    bottom_bars_long: List[Dict]
    bottom_bars_short: List[Dict]
    
    # Top reinforcement (if applicable)
    top_bars_long: Optional[List[Dict]]
    top_bars_short: Optional[List[Dict]]
    
    # Pile details (if pile cap)
    piles: Optional[List[Dict]]
    
    def calculate_mesh_weight(self) -> float:
        """Calculate weight of footing mesh"""
        unit_weights = {16: 1.578, 20: 2.466, 25: 3.853}
        
        weight = 0
        
        # Bottom mesh
        for bar in self.bottom_bars_long:
            wt = bar['nos'] * bar['length']/1000 * unit_weights.get(bar['dia'], 0)
            weight += wt
        
        for bar in self.bottom_bars_short:
            wt = bar['nos'] * bar['length']/1000 * unit_weights.get(bar['dia'], 0)
            weight += wt
        
        # Top mesh
        if self.top_bars_long:
            for bar in self.top_bars_long:
                wt = bar['nos'] * bar['length']/1000 * unit_weights.get(bar['dia'], 0)
                weight += wt
        
        if self.top_bars_short:
            for bar in self.top_bars_short:
                wt = bar['nos'] * bar['length']/1000 * unit_weights.get(bar['dia'], 0)
                weight += wt
        
        return weight


@dataclass
class PileReinforcement:
    """Pile reinforcement details"""
    pile_diameter: float
    pile_length: float
    main_bar_dia: int
    num_main_bars: int
    spiral_dia: int
    spiral_pitch_top: float
    spiral_pitch_rest: float
    confinement_length: float
    
    def calculate_spiral_length(self) -> float:
        """Calculate total spiral length"""
        # Core diameter
        core_dia = self.pile_diameter - 75  # Cover
        
        # Turns in confinement zone
        turns_confinement = self.confinement_length / self.spiral_pitch_top
        length_confinement = turns_confinement * math.pi * core_dia
        
        # Turns in rest of pile
        rest_length = self.pile_length - self.confinement_length
        turns_rest = rest_length / self.spiral_pitch_rest
        length_rest = turns_rest * math.pi * core_dia
        
        return length_confinement + length_rest
    
    def calculate_main_steel_weight(self) -> float:
        """Calculate weight of main bars"""
        unit_weight = {20: 2.466, 25: 3.853}
        bar_length = self.pile_length + 500  # Development length
        total_length = self.num_main_bars * bar_length
        return total_length/1000 * unit_weight.get(self.main_bar_dia, 0)


class ReinforcementReader:
    """Read reinforcement sheets from Excel"""
    
    def __init__(self, excel_path: str):
        self.excel_path = excel_path
        self.beams = []
        self.piers = []
        self.foundations = []
        self.piles = []
    
    def read_all(self) -> Dict:
        """Read all reinforcement data"""
        self.read_beams()
        self.read_piers()
        self.read_foundations()
        
        return {
            'beams': self.beams,
            'piers': self.piers,
            'foundations': self.foundations,
            'piles': self.piles
        }
    
    def read_beams(self) -> List[BeamReinforcement]:
        """Read beam reinforcement from REINFORCEMENT_BEAMS sheet"""
        df = pd.read_excel(self.excel_path, sheet_name='REINFORCEMENT_BEAMS', header=1)
        
        # Group by beam ID
        beam_groups = df.groupby('Beam ID')
        
        for beam_id, group in beam_groups:
            # Get beam parameters
            beam_data = group.iloc[0]
            
            # Parse top bars
            top_bars = []
            top_df = group[group['Section'] == 'TOP_SUPPORT']
            for _, row in top_df.iterrows():
                top_bars.append({
                    'mark': row['Bar Mark'],
                    'dia': int(row['Dia (mm)']),
                    'nos': int(row['No. of Bars']),
                    'length': float(row['Length (mm)'])
                })
            
            # Parse bottom bars
            bottom_bars = []
            bottom_df = group[group['Section'] == 'BOTTOM_MIDSPAN']
            for _, row in bottom_df.iterrows():
                bottom_bars.append({
                    'mark': row['Bar Mark'],
                    'dia': int(row['Dia (mm)']),
                    'nos': int(row['No. of Bars']),
                    'length': float(row['Length (mm)'])
                })
            
            # Parse bent-up bars
            bent_up = []
            bent_df = group[group['Section'] == 'BENT_UP']
            for _, row in bent_df.iterrows():
                bent_up.append({
                    'mark': row['Bar Mark'],
                    'dia': int(row['Dia (mm)']),
                    'nos': int(row['No. of Bars']),
                    'length': float(row['Length (mm)'])
                })
            
            # Parse stirrups
            stirrups = []
            stir_df = group[group['Section'] == 'STIRRUP']
            for _, row in stir_df.iterrows():
                stirrups.append({
                    'zone': row['Zone'],
                    'dia': int(row['Dia (mm)']),
                    'legs': int(row['Legs']),
                    'spacing': float(row['Spacing (mm)']),
                    'quantity': int(row['Quantity'])
                })
            
            beam = BeamReinforcement(
                beam_id=beam_id,
                beam_type=beam_data['Beam Type'],
                span_length=float(beam_data['Span Length']),
                concrete_grade=beam_data['Concrete Grade'],
                steel_grade=beam_data['Steel Grade'],
                overall_depth=float(beam_data['Overall Depth']),
                web_width=float(beam_data['Web Width']),
                effective_depth=float(beam_data['Effective Depth']),
                top_bars_support=top_bars,
                bottom_bars_midspan=bottom_bars,
                bent_up_bars=bent_up,
                stirrups=stirrups,
                side_face_bars=None  # Parse if present
            )
            
            self.beams.append(beam)
        
        return self.beams
    
    def read_piers(self) -> List[PierReinforcement]:
        """Read pier reinforcement from REINFORCEMENT_PIERS sheet"""
        df = pd.read_excel(self.excel_path, sheet_name='REINFORCEMENT_PIERS', header=1)
        
        pier_groups = df.groupby('Pier ID')
        
        for pier_id, group in pier_groups:
            pier_data = group.iloc[0]
            
            # Parse pier cap bars
            cap_top = []
            cap_top_df = group[group['Element'] == 'CAP_TOP']
            for _, row in cap_top_df.iterrows():
                cap_top.append({
                    'mark': row['Bar Mark'],
                    'dia': int(row['Dia (mm)']),
                    'nos': int(row['No.']),
                    'length': float(row['Length (mm)'])
                })
            
            cap_bottom = []
            cap_bottom_df = group[group['Element'] == 'CAP_BOTTOM']
            for _, row in cap_bottom_df.iterrows():
                cap_bottom.append({
                    'mark': row['Bar Mark'],
                    'dia': int(row['Dia (mm)']),
                    'nos': int(row['No.']),
                    'length': float(row['Length (mm)'])
                })
            
            pier = PierReinforcement(
                pier_id=pier_id,
                pier_type=pier_data['Pier Type'],
                height=float(pier_data['Height']),
                concrete_grade=pier_data['Concrete Grade'],
                diameter=float(pier_data['Diameter']) if 'Circular' in pier_data['Pier Type'] else None,
                length=float(pier_data['Length']) if 'Wall' in pier_data['Pier Type'] else None,
                width=float(pier_data['Width']) if 'Wall' in pier_data['Pier Type'] else None,
                main_bar_dia=int(pier_data['Main Bar Dia']),
                num_main_bars=int(pier_data['No. of Main Bars']),
                main_bar_length=float(pier_data['Main Bar Length']),
                tie_dia=int(pier_data['Tie Dia']),
                tie_pitch_normal=float(pier_data['Tie Pitch Normal']),
                tie_pitch_confinement=float(pier_data['Tie Pitch Confinement']),
                confinement_zone_length=float(pier_data['Confinement Zone Length']),
                cap_length=float(pier_data['Cap Length']),
                cap_width=float(pier_data['Cap Width']),
                cap_depth=float(pier_data['Cap Depth']),
                cap_top_bars=cap_top,
                cap_bottom_bars=cap_bottom,
                cap_stirrups=[]  # Parse if available
            )
            
            self.piers.append(pier)
        
        return self.piers
    
    def read_foundations(self) -> List[FoundationReinforcement]:
        """Read foundation reinforcement from REINFORCEMENT_FOUNDATIONS sheet"""
        df = pd.read_excel(self.excel_path, sheet_name='REINFORCEMENT_FOUNDATIONS', header=1)
        
        foundation_groups = df.groupby('Foundation ID')
        
        for foundation_id, group in foundation_groups:
            foundation_data = group.iloc[0]
            
            # Parse bottom bars
            bottom_long = []
            bottom_long_df = group[group['Layer'] == 'BOTTOM_LONG']
            for _, row in bottom_long_df.iterrows():
                bottom_long.append({
                    'mark': row['Bar Mark'],
                    'dia': int(row['Dia (mm)']),
                    'nos': int(row['No.']),
                    'length': float(row['Length (mm)'])
                })
            
            bottom_short = []
            bottom_short_df = group[group['Layer'] == 'BOTTOM_SHORT']
            for _, row in bottom_short_df.iterrows():
                bottom_short.append({
                    'mark': row['Bar Mark'],
                    'dia': int(row['Dia (mm)']),
                    'nos': int(row['No.']),
                    'length': float(row['Length (mm)'])
                })
            
            # Parse top bars (if present)
            top_long = []
            top_long_df = group[group['Layer'] == 'TOP_LONG']
            for _, row in top_long_df.iterrows():
                top_long.append({
                    'mark': row['Bar Mark'],
                    'dia': int(row['Dia (mm)']),
                    'nos': int(row['No.']),
                    'length': float(row['Length (mm)'])
                })
            
            foundation = FoundationReinforcement(
                foundation_id=foundation_id,
                foundation_type=foundation_data['Foundation Type'],
                concrete_grade=foundation_data['Concrete Grade'],
                length=float(foundation_data['Length']),
                width=float(foundation_data['Width']),
                thickness=float(foundation_data['Thickness']),
                bottom_bars_long=bottom_long,
                bottom_bars_short=bottom_short,
                top_bars_long=top_long if top_long else None,
                top_bars_short=[],  # Parse if available
                piles=None  # Parse pile details if pile cap
            )
            
            self.foundations.append(foundation)
        
        return self.foundations
    
    def generate_master_bbs(self) -> pd.DataFrame:
        """Generate master bar bending schedule from all elements"""
        bbs_rows = []
        
        # Process beams
        for beam in self.beams:
            for bar in beam.top_bars_support:
                bbs_rows.append({
                    'Bar Mark': bar['mark'],
                    'Element': f"Beam {beam.beam_id}",
                    'Description': 'Top bar at support',
                    'Dia (mm)': bar['dia'],
                    'No.': bar['nos'],
                    'Length (mm)': bar['length'],
                    'Shape': 'Straight',
                    'Total Length (m)': bar['nos'] * bar['length'] / 1000,
                    'Unit Weight (kg/m)': self._get_unit_weight(bar['dia']),
                    'Total Weight (kg)': bar['nos'] * bar['length'] / 1000 * self._get_unit_weight(bar['dia'])
                })
            
            for bar in beam.bottom_bars_midspan:
                bbs_rows.append({
                    'Bar Mark': bar['mark'],
                    'Element': f"Beam {beam.beam_id}",
                    'Description': 'Bottom bar at midspan',
                    'Dia (mm)': bar['dia'],
                    'No.': bar['nos'],
                    'Length (mm)': bar['length'],
                    'Shape': 'Straight',
                    'Total Length (m)': bar['nos'] * bar['length'] / 1000,
                    'Unit Weight (kg/m)': self._get_unit_weight(bar['dia']),
                    'Total Weight (kg)': bar['nos'] * bar['length'] / 1000 * self._get_unit_weight(bar['dia'])
                })
        
        # Process piers
        for pier in self.piers:
            # Main bars
            bbs_rows.append({
                'Bar Mark': f'{pier.pier_id}-M',
                'Element': f"Pier {pier.pier_id}",
                'Description': 'Main vertical bars',
                'Dia (mm)': pier.main_bar_dia,
                'No.': pier.num_main_bars,
                'Length (mm)': pier.main_bar_length,
                'Shape': 'Straight',
                'Total Length (m)': pier.num_main_bars * pier.main_bar_length / 1000,
                'Unit Weight (kg/m)': self._get_unit_weight(pier.main_bar_dia),
                'Total Weight (kg)': pier.num_main_bars * pier.main_bar_length / 1000 * self._get_unit_weight(pier.main_bar_dia)
            })
            
            # Helical ties
            helix_length = pier.calculate_helix_length()
            bbs_rows.append({
                'Bar Mark': f'{pier.pier_id}-T',
                'Element': f"Pier {pier.pier_id}",
                'Description': 'Helical ties',
                'Dia (mm)': pier.tie_dia,
                'No.': 1,
                'Length (mm)': helix_length,
                'Shape': 'Helical',
                'Total Length (m)': helix_length / 1000,
                'Unit Weight (kg/m)': self._get_unit_weight(pier.tie_dia),
                'Total Weight (kg)': helix_length / 1000 * self._get_unit_weight(pier.tie_dia)
            })
        
        # Process foundations
        for foundation in self.foundations:
            for bar in foundation.bottom_bars_long:
                bbs_rows.append({
                    'Bar Mark': bar['mark'],
                    'Element': f"Foundation {foundation.foundation_id}",
                    'Description': 'Bottom mesh - long direction',
                    'Dia (mm)': bar['dia'],
                    'No.': bar['nos'],
                    'Length (mm)': bar['length'],
                    'Shape': 'Straight',
                    'Total Length (m)': bar['nos'] * bar['length'] / 1000,
                    'Unit Weight (kg/m)': self._get_unit_weight(bar['dia']),
                    'Total Weight (kg)': bar['nos'] * bar['length'] / 1000 * self._get_unit_weight(bar['dia'])
                })
        
        return pd.DataFrame(bbs_rows)
    
    def _get_unit_weight(self, dia: int) -> float:
        """Get unit weight for bar diameter"""
        unit_weights = {6: 0.222, 8: 0.395, 10: 0.617, 12: 0.888, 
                       16: 1.578, 20: 2.466, 25: 3.853, 32: 6.313}
        return unit_weights.get(dia, 0)
    
    def generate_reinforcement_summary(self) -> Dict:
        """Generate summary of all reinforcement"""
        summary = {
            'beams': {
                'count': len(self.beams),
                'total_steel_kg': sum(b.calculate_steel_weight()['total'] for b in self.beams)
            },
            'piers': {
                'count': len(self.piers),
                'total_steel_kg': sum(
                    p.num_main_bars * p.main_bar_length/1000 * self._get_unit_weight(p.main_bar_dia) +
                    p.calculate_helix_length()/1000 * self._get_unit_weight(p.tie_dia)
                    for p in self.piers
                )
            },
            'foundations': {
                'count': len(self.foundations),
                'total_steel_kg': sum(f.calculate_mesh_weight() for f in self.foundations)
            }
        }
        
        summary['grand_total_kg'] = sum(s['total_steel_kg'] for s in summary.values())
        summary['grand_total_mt'] = summary['grand_total_kg'] / 1000
        
        return summary


class ReinforcementDetailer:
    """Generate detailed reinforcement drawings"""
    
    def __init__(self, reinforcement_data: Dict):
        self.data = reinforcement_data
    
    def generate_beam_detail_dxf(self, beam_id: str) -> str:
        """Generate DXF for beam reinforcement detail"""
        beam = next((b for b in self.data['beams'] if b.beam_id == beam_id), None)
        if not beam:
            return ""
        
        dxf_lines = []
        dxf_lines.append("0\nSECTION\n2\nENTITIES\n")
        
        # Draw beam outline
        beam_outline = [
            (0, 0),
            (beam.span_length * 1000, 0),
            (beam.span_length * 1000, beam.overall_depth),
            (0, beam.overall_depth),
            (0, 0)
        ]
        dxf_lines.append(self._create_lwpolyline(beam_outline, layer="BEAM_OUTLINE"))
        
        # Draw top bars
        y_top = beam.overall_depth - 50  # Cover
        for bar in beam.top_bars_support:
            # Simplified representation
            bar_line = [
                (0, y_top),
                (beam.span_length * 1000 / 3, y_top)
            ]
            dxf_lines.append(self._create_line(bar_line, layer="TOP_BARS"))
        
        # Draw bottom bars
        y_bottom = 50 + bar['dia']  # Cover
        for bar in beam.bottom_bars_midspan:
            bar_line = [
                (0, y_bottom),
                (beam.span_length * 1000, y_bottom)
            ]
            dxf_lines.append(self._create_line(bar_line, layer="BOTTOM_BARS"))
        
        # Draw stirrups (schematic)
        spacing = beam.stirrups[0]['spacing'] if beam.stirrups else 150
        x = spacing
        while x < beam.span_length * 1000:
            stirrup = [
                (x, 50),
                (x, beam.overall_depth - 50),
                (x + 10, beam.overall_depth - 50),
                (x + 10, 50),
                (x, 50)
            ]
            dxf_lines.append(self._create_lwpolyline(stirrup, layer="STIRRUPS"))
            x += spacing
        
        dxf_lines.append("0\nENDSEC\n0\nEOF\n")
        return "\n".join(dxf_lines)
    
    def _create_lwpolyline(self, points: List[Tuple[float, float]], layer: str = "0") -> str:
        """Create DXF LWPOLYLINE"""
        dxf = f"0\nLWPOLYLINE\n8\n{layer}\n90\n{len(points)}\n70\n1\n"
        for x, y in points:
            dxf += f"10\n{x}\n20\n{y}\n"
        return dxf
    
    def _create_line(self, points: List[Tuple[float, float]], layer: str = "0") -> str:
        """Create DXF LINE"""
        return f"0\nLINE\n8\n{layer}\n10\n{points[0][0]}\n20\n{points[0][1]}\n11\n{points[1][0]}\n21\n{points[1][1]}\n"


# Usage Example
if __name__ == "__main__":
    reader = ReinforcementReader("bridge_input.xlsx")
    data = reader.read_all()
    
    # Generate master BBS
    bbs_df = reader.generate_master_bbs()
    bbs_df.to_excel("master_bbs.xlsx", index=False)
    
    # Generate summary
    summary = reader.generate_reinforcement_summary()
    print(f"Total Steel: {summary['grand_total_mt']:.2f} MT")
    
    # Generate detail drawings
    detailer = ReinforcementDetailer(data)
    beam_dxf = detailer.generate_beam_detail_dxf("B1")
    
    with open("beam_B1_detail.dxf", "w") as f:
        f.write(beam_dxf)
```
