# SHEET 21: SKEW_3D_GEOMETRY

## Purpose
Complete 3D geometric definition for skew bridges including coordinate systems, transformation matrices, intersection calculations, and spatial orientation of all elements.

---

## SECTION A: SKEW DEFINITION AND COORDINATE SYSTEMS

### A.1 Global Coordinate System (GCS)

| Parameter | Symbol | Value | Unit | Description |
|-----------|--------|-------|------|-------------|
| GCS Origin X | X₀ | 0 | m | Project datum |
| GCS Origin Y | Y₀ | 0 | m | Project datum |
| GCS Origin Z | Z₀ | 100 | m | Datum elevation |
| GCS Orientation | - | Grid North | - | Reference direction |

### A.2 Bridge Alignment Coordinate System (BCS)

| Parameter | Symbol | Value | Unit | Formula |
|-----------|--------|-------|------|---------|
| Bridge Chainage Start | CS | 0 | m | Along bridge |
| Bridge Chainage End | CE | 100 | m | Along bridge |
| Bridge Bearing | θ_b | 45 | ° | From North |
| Bridge Grade | G_b | 2.0 | % | Vertical slope |
| BCS Origin X | X_b | `=X₀` | m | Start abutment |
| BCS Origin Y | Y_b | `=Y₀` | m | Start abutment |
| BCS Origin Z | Z_b | `=Z₀` | m | Start elevation |

### A.3 Skew Definition

| Parameter | Symbol | Value | Unit | Description |
|-----------|--------|-------|------|-------------|
| Skew Angle | α | 30 | ° | Acute angle between bridge and crossing |
| Skew Type | - | Right/Left | - | Right skew: bridge rotates clockwise |
| Crossing Type | - | Road/River/Rail | - | Type of intersecting feature |
| Crossing Width | W_c | 20 | m | Width of crossing feature |
| Crossing Chainage | C_skew | 50 | m | Where skew occurs along bridge |

### A.4 Skew Angle Relationships

```
Skew Angle (α) = Angle between bridge centerline and crossing feature

For Right Skew:
  α = 90° - (Bridge Bearing - Crossing Bearing)
  
For Left Skew:
  α = 90° + (Bridge Bearing - Crossing Bearing)

Acute Skew: α < 90°
Obtuse Skew: α > 90° (typically designed as acute in opposite direction)
```

**Skew Classification:**
| Skew Angle | Classification | Design Considerations |
|------------|---------------|---------------------|
| 0° - 15° | Normal | Minimal effects |
| 15° - 30° | Moderate Skew | Adjust bearings |
| 30° - 45° | Significant Skew | Special detailing required |
| 45° - 60° | Severe Skew | Complex analysis needed |
| > 60° | Very Severe | Alternative alignment preferred |

---

## SECTION B: 3D COORDINATE TRANSFORMATION MATRICES

### B.1 Transformation: BCS → GCS

**Rotation Matrix (Horizontal):**
```
Rz(θ) = | cos(θ)  -sin(θ)   0 |
        | sin(θ)   cos(θ)   0 |
        |   0        0      1 |
```

**Grade Transformation (Vertical):**
```
Ry(G) = | cos(φ)   0   sin(φ) |
        |   0      1     0    |
        | -sin(φ)  0   cos(φ) |

where φ = arctan(G/100)
```

**Combined Transformation Matrix:**
```
T_BCS→GCS = Rz(θ_b) × Ry(G_b)

Point_GCS = T_BCS→GCS × Point_BCS + Origin_GCS
```

### B.2 Excel Implementation

| Cell | Calculation | Formula | Result |
|------|-------------|---------|--------|
| B20 | cos(θ_b) | `=COS(RADIANS(45))` | 0.707 |
| B21 | sin(θ_b) | `=SIN(RADIANS(45))` | 0.707 |
| B22 | tan(φ) | `=2/100` | 0.02 |
| B23 | cos(φ) | `=COS(ATAN(0.02))` | 0.9998 |
| B24 | sin(φ) | `=SIN(ATAN(0.02))` | 0.0200 |

**Transformation Formulas in Excel:**
```
X_GCS = X_b + (CS × cos(θ_b) × cos(φ))
Y_GCS = Y_b + (CS × sin(θ_b) × cos(φ))
Z_GCS = Z_b + (CS × sin(φ))
```

---

## SECTION C: SKEW INTERSECTION GEOMETRY

### C.1 Intersection Point Calculation

| Element | Parameter | Formula | Value |
|---------|-----------|---------|-------|
| Bridge Centerline | Parametric | `P_b(t) = (X_b + t·cos(θ_b), Y_b + t·sin(θ_b))` | - |
| Crossing Centerline | Parametric | `P_c(s) = (X_c + s·cos(θ_c), Y_c + s·sin(θ_c))` | - |
| Intersection | Solve | `P_b(t) = P_c(s)` | (X_i, Y_i) |

**Intersection Solution:**
```
t = [(Y_c - Y_b)·cos(θ_c) - (X_c - X_b)·sin(θ_c)] / sin(θ_b - θ_c)
s = [(Y_c - Y_b)·cos(θ_b) - (X_c - X_b)·sin(θ_b)] / sin(θ_b - θ_c)

Intersection Point:
X_i = X_b + t·cos(θ_b)
Y_i = Y_b + t·sin(θ_b)
```

### C.2 Skew Bridge Deck Corners

**Deck Corner Coordinates (in BCS):**

| Corner | Chainage | Offset | X_BCS | Y_BCS | Z_BCS |
|--------|----------|--------|-------|-------|-------|
| A1 (Start, Left) | 0 | -W/2 | 0 | -6.0 | 0 |
| A2 (Start, Right) | 0 | +W/2 | 0 | +6.0 | 0 |
| B1 (End, Left) | L | -W/2 | 100 | -6.0 | 2.0 |
| B2 (End, Right) | L | +W/2 | 100 | +6.0 | 2.0 |

**Transformed to GCS:**

| Corner | X_GCS | Y_GCS | Z_GCS |
|--------|-------|-------|-------|
| A1 | `=X₀+X_BCS·cos(θ)-Y_BCS·sin(θ)` | `=Y₀+X_BCS·sin(θ)+Y_BCS·cos(θ)` | `=Z₀+Z_BCS` |
| A2 | Calculated | Calculated | Calculated |
| B1 | Calculated | Calculated | Calculated |
| B2 | Calculated | Calculated | Calculated |

### C.3 Skew Effect on Deck Slab

**Skew Correction Factors:**

| Parameter | Normal Bridge | Skew Bridge | Correction |
|-----------|--------------|-------------|------------|
| Effective Span | L | `=L/cos(α)` | Increases |
| Deck Area | `=L×W` | `=L×W/cos(α)` | Increases |
| Girder Length | L | `=L/cos(α)` | Increases |
| End Diaphragm | Perpendicular | Skewed | Complex |

---

## SECTION D: GIRDER LENGTH CALCULATIONS IN SKEW

### D.1 Girder Length Variation

**For N girders at spacing S:**

| Girder | Offset from Center | Chainage Start | Chainage End | Length | Skew Correction |
|--------|-------------------|----------------|--------------|--------|---------------|
| G1 | `-((N-1)/2)×S` | `=CS+ΔL₁` | `=CE+ΔL₁` | `=CE-CS` | `+ΔL₁/cos(α)` |
| G2 | `-((N-3)/2)×S` | `=CS+ΔL₂` | `=CE+ΔL₂` | `=CE-CS` | `+ΔL₂/cos(α)` |
| ... | ... | ... | ... | ... | ... |
| G_N | `+((N-1)/2)×S` | `=CS+ΔL_N` | `=CE+ΔL_N` | `=CE-CS` | `+ΔL_N/cos(α)` |

**Length Correction:**
```
ΔL = Offset × tan(α)

Girder Length = Standard Length ± ΔL/cos(α)
```

### D.2 Girder Length Table

| Girder ID | Offset (m) | Start Chainage | End Chainage | Length (m) | Difference from Standard |
|-----------|------------|----------------|--------------|------------|--------------------------|
| G1 (Exterior) | -3.75 | -0.65 | 99.35 | 100.65 | +0.65 |
| G2 | -1.25 | -0.22 | 99.78 | 100.22 | +0.22 |
| G3 | +1.25 | +0.22 | 100.22 | 100.22 | +0.22 |
| G4 (Exterior) | +3.75 | +0.65 | 100.65 | 100.65 | +0.65 |

---

## SECTION E: BEARING ORIENTATION IN SKEW

### E.1 Bearing Orientation Angles

| Location | Skew Angle | Bearing Orientation | Rotation from Bridge |
|----------|------------|---------------------|---------------------|
| Abutment 1 | 30° | Parallel to Abutment | 30° |
| Pier 1 | 30° | Parallel to Pier | 30° |
| Pier 2 | 30° | Parallel to Pier | 30° |
| Abutment 2 | 30° | Parallel to Abutment | 30° |

### E.2 Bearing Force Components

**Force Transformation:**

| Force Component | Along Bridge | Across Bridge | Vertical |
|-----------------|--------------|---------------|----------|
| F_x (Longitudinal) | `=F_L×cos(α)` | `=F_L×sin(α)` | 0 |
| F_y (Transverse) | `=F_T×sin(α)` | `=F_T×cos(α)` | 0 |
| F_z (Vertical) | 0 | 0 | `=F_V` |

### E.3 Expansion Joint Orientation

| Joint | Orientation Angle | Gap Width (Perpendicular) | Gap Width (Along Bridge) |
|-------|------------------|---------------------------|--------------------------|
| EJ1 | 30° | 40mm | `=40/cos(30°)` = 46.2mm |
| EJ2 | 30° | 40mm | `=40/cos(30°)` = 46.2mm |

---

## SECTION F: 3D INTERSECTION SURFACE CALCULATIONS

### F.1 Roadway Intersection Surface

**For Bridge over Road:**

| Parameter | Value | Unit | Description |
|-----------|-------|------|-------------|
| Road Width | 12.0 | m | Crossing width |
| Road Crown Height | 0.15 | m | Cross slope |
| Road Cross Slope | 2.5 | % | Drainage |
| Road Profile | Vertical Curve | - | Alignment |

**Intersection Surface Equations:**
```
Bridge Deck: Z_b(x,y) = Z_b0 + G_b×x/100
Road Surface: Z_r(x,y) = Z_r0 + G_r×x_r/100 + C_r×y²

Clearance Check: Z_b(x,y) - Z_r(x,y) ≥ Minimum
```

### F.2 Clearance Envelope

| Location | Bridge Soffit | Road Surface | Clearance | Status |
|----------|-------------|--------------|-----------|--------|
| Center | 102.50 | 98.20 | 4.30 | OK |
| Edge Left | 102.45 | 98.35 | 4.10 | OK |
| Edge Right | 102.45 | 98.05 | 4.40 | OK |
| Corner A1 | 102.40 | 98.50 | 3.90 | REVIEW |

---

## SECTION G: SKEW ANGLE EFFECTS ON STRUCTURAL BEHAVIOR

### G.1 Load Distribution Factors

| Skew Angle | Moment Factor | Shear Factor | Torsion Factor |
|------------|---------------|--------------|----------------|
| 0° | 1.00 | 1.00 | 0.00 |
| 15° | 0.98 | 1.02 | 0.05 |
| 30° | 0.94 | 1.08 | 0.15 |
| 45° | 0.88 | 1.15 | 0.30 |

**Correction Formula (IRC):**
```
M_skew = M_straight × (1 - 0.006×α)  [α in degrees]
V_skew = V_straight × (1 + 0.005×α)
T_skew = M_straight × 0.01×α
```

### G.2 End Zone Effects

| Zone | Length | Reinforcement Increase |
|------|--------|----------------------|
| End Zone 1 | `=1.5×W/tan(α)` | +25% |
| End Zone 2 | `=W/tan(α)` | +15% |
| Interior | Remainder | Standard |

---

# PYTHON 3D GEOMETRY MODULE

## File: `skew_geometry_3d.py`

```python
"""
3D Geometry Calculations for Skew Bridge Intersections
Handles coordinate transformations, intersection calculations, and spatial geometry
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import List, Tuple, Dict, Optional
import math
from enum import Enum


class SkewType(Enum):
    RIGHT = "Right"  # Clockwise rotation
    LEFT = "Left"    # Counter-clockwise rotation


class CrossingType(Enum):
    ROAD = "Road"
    RIVER = "River"
    RAILWAY = "Railway"
    CANAL = "Canal"


@dataclass
class Point3D:
    """3D Point in space"""
    x: float
    y: float
    z: float
    
    def __add__(self, other):
        return Point3D(self.x + other.x, self.y + other.y, self.z + other.z)
    
    def __sub__(self, other):
        return Point3D(self.x - other.x, self.y - other.y, self.z - other.z)
    
    def to_array(self) -> np.ndarray:
        return np.array([self.x, self.y, self.z])
    
    @staticmethod
    def from_array(arr: np.ndarray):
        return Point3D(arr[0], arr[1], arr[2])
    
    def distance_to(self, other: 'Point3D') -> float:
        return math.sqrt((self.x - other.x)**2 + 
                        (self.y - other.y)**2 + 
                        (self.z - other.z)**2)


@dataclass
class Vector3D:
    """3D Vector"""
    dx: float
    dy: float
    dz: float
    
    def magnitude(self) -> float:
        return math.sqrt(self.dx**2 + self.dy**2 + self.dz**2)
    
    def normalize(self) -> 'Vector3D':
        mag = self.magnitude()
        return Vector3D(self.dx/mag, self.dy/mag, self.dz/mag)
    
    def dot(self, other: 'Vector3D') -> float:
        return self.dx * other.dx + self.dy * other.dy + self.z * other.dz
    
    def cross(self, other: 'Vector3D') -> 'Vector3D':
        return Vector3D(
            self.dy * other.dz - self.dz * other.dy,
            self.dz * other.dx - self.dx * other.dz,
            self.dx * other.dy - self.dy * other.dx
        )
    
    def to_array(self) -> np.ndarray:
        return np.array([self.dx, self.dy, self.dz])


@dataclass
class CoordinateSystem:
    """3D Coordinate System definition"""
    origin: Point3D
    x_axis: Vector3D  # Local X direction
    y_axis: Vector3D  # Local Y direction
    z_axis: Vector3D  # Local Z direction (up)
    
    def __post_init__(self):
        # Ensure orthonormal
        self.x_axis = self.x_axis.normalize()
        self.z_axis = self.z_axis.normalize()
        # Y = Z × X to ensure right-handed
        self.y_axis = Vector3D.from_array(
            np.cross(self.z_axis.to_array(), self.x_axis.to_array())
        ).normalize()
    
    def transform_to_global(self, point_local: Point3D) -> Point3D:
        """Transform point from local to global coordinates"""
        # Create rotation matrix
        R = np.column_stack([
            self.x_axis.to_array(),
            self.y_axis.to_array(),
            self.z_axis.to_array()
        ])
        
        # Rotate and translate
        local_array = point_local.to_array()
        global_array = R @ local_array + self.origin.to_array()
        
        return Point3D.from_array(global_array)
    
    def transform_from_global(self, point_global: Point3D) -> Point3D:
        """Transform point from global to local coordinates"""
        R = np.column_stack([
            self.x_axis.to_array(),
            self.y_axis.to_array(),
            self.z_axis.to_array()
        ])
        
        # Inverse rotation (transpose for orthonormal)
        R_inv = R.T
        
        global_array = point_global.to_array()
        local_array = R_inv @ (global_array - self.origin.to_array())
        
        return Point3D.from_array(local_array)


@dataclass
class SkewBridgeGeometry:
    """Complete skew bridge geometry definition"""
    
    # Bridge parameters
    bridge_length: float
    bridge_width: float
    bridge_bearing: float  # Degrees from North
    bridge_grade: float    # Percentage
    
    # Skew parameters
    skew_angle: float      # Degrees
    skew_type: SkewType
    crossing_type: CrossingType
    crossing_width: float
    crossing_chainage: float
    crossing_bearing: float
    
    # Vertical profile
    elevation_start: float
    
    def __post_init__(self):
        # Calculate derived parameters
        self.skew_radians = math.radians(self.skew_angle)
        self.bridge_bearing_rad = math.radians(self.bridge_bearing)
        self.grade_radians = math.atan(self.bridge_grade / 100)
        
        # Create coordinate systems
        self._setup_coordinate_systems()
    
    def _setup_coordinate_systems(self):
        """Setup bridge and crossing coordinate systems"""
        
        # Bridge BCS
        bridge_x = Vector3D(
            math.cos(self.bridge_bearing_rad),
            math.sin(self.bridge_bearing_rad),
            0
        )
        bridge_z = Vector3D(
            -math.sin(self.grade_radians) * math.cos(self.bridge_bearing_rad),
            -math.sin(self.grade_radians) * math.sin(self.bridge_bearing_rad),
            math.cos(self.grade_radians)
        )
        
        self.bridge_cs = CoordinateSystem(
            origin=Point3D(0, 0, self.elevation_start),
            x_axis=bridge_x,
            y_axis=Vector3D(0, 0, 0),  # Will be calculated
            z_axis=bridge_z
        )
        
        # Crossing coordinate system
        crossing_x = Vector3D(
            math.cos(math.radians(self.crossing_bearing)),
            math.sin(math.radians(self.crossing_bearing)),
            0
        )
        
        self.crossing_cs = CoordinateSystem(
            origin=self._calculate_crossing_origin(),
            x_axis=crossing_x,
            y_axis=Vector3D(0, 0, 0),
            z_axis=Vector3D(0, 0, 1)
        )
    
    def _calculate_crossing_origin(self) -> Point3D:
        """Calculate crossing origin based on intersection"""
        # Bridge point at crossing chainage
        t = self.crossing_chainage
        
        bridge_x = t * math.cos(self.bridge_bearing_rad)
        bridge_y = t * math.sin(self.bridge_bearing_rad)
        bridge_z = self.elevation_start + t * math.tan(self.grade_radians)
        
        return Point3D(bridge_x, bridge_y, bridge_z)
    
    def calculate_intersection(self) -> Dict:
        """Calculate intersection geometry between bridge and crossing"""
        
        # Bridge centerline parametric: P = O + t*d
        bridge_origin = np.array([0, 0])
        bridge_dir = np.array([
            math.cos(self.bridge_bearing_rad),
            math.sin(self.bridge_bearing_rad)
        ])
        
        # Crossing centerline
        crossing_origin = np.array([
            self.crossing_cs.origin.x,
            self.crossing_cs.origin.y
        ])
        crossing_dir = np.array([
            math.cos(math.radians(self.crossing_bearing)),
            math.sin(math.radians(self.crossing_bearing))
        ])
        
        # Solve for intersection
        # P1 + t*d1 = P2 + s*d2
        dx = crossing_origin[0] - bridge_origin[0]
        dy = crossing_origin[1] - bridge_origin[1]
        
        det = bridge_dir[0] * crossing_dir[1] - bridge_dir[1] * crossing_dir[0]
        
        if abs(det) < 1e-10:
            return {"error": "Lines are parallel"}
        
        t = (dx * crossing_dir[1] - dy * crossing_dir[0]) / det
        s = (dx * bridge_dir[1] - dy * bridge_dir[0]) / det
        
        intersection = Point3D(
            bridge_origin[0] + t * bridge_dir[0],
            bridge_origin[1] + t * bridge_dir[1],
            self.elevation_start + t * math.tan(self.grade_radians)
        )
        
        return {
            "intersection_point": intersection,
            "bridge_parameter": t,
            "crossing_parameter": s,
            "skew_angle_calculated": math.degrees(
                math.acos(np.dot(bridge_dir, crossing_dir))
            )
        }
    
    def get_deck_corners(self) -> Dict[str, Point3D]:
        """Calculate 3D coordinates of deck corners"""
        
        half_width = self.bridge_width / 2
        
        # Define corners in BCS
        corners_bcs = {
            'A1': Point3D(0, -half_width, 0),                    # Start, Left
            'A2': Point3D(0, half_width, 0),                     # Start, Right
            'B1': Point3D(self.bridge_length, -half_width, 0),   # End, Left
            'B2': Point3D(self.bridge_length, half_width, 0)     # End, Right
        }
        
        # Transform to GCS
        corners_gcs = {}
        for name, point in corners_bcs.items():
            corners_gcs[name] = self.bridge_cs.transform_to_global(point)
        
        return corners_gcs
    
    def get_girder_lines(self, num_girders: int, girder_spacing: float) -> Dict:
        """Calculate girder centerlines with skew corrections"""
        
        girders = {}
        offset_start = -((num_girders - 1) / 2) * girder_spacing
        
        for i in range(num_girders):
            girder_id = f"G{i+1}"
            offset = offset_start + i * girder_spacing
            
            # Calculate start and end points considering skew
            if self.skew_type == SkewType.RIGHT:
                chainage_start = -offset * math.tan(self.skew_radians)
                chainage_end = self.bridge_length - offset * math.tan(self.skew_radians)
            else:
                chainage_start = offset * math.tan(self.skew_radians)
                chainage_end = self.bridge_length + offset * math.tan(self.skew_radians)
            
            # Length calculation
            length = (chainage_end - chainage_start) / math.cos(self.skew_radians)
            
            # 3D coordinates
            start_bcs = Point3D(chainage_start, offset, 0)
            end_bcs = Point3D(chainage_end, offset, 0)
            
            girders[girder_id] = {
                'offset': offset,
                'chainage_start': chainage_start,
                'chainage_end': chainage_end,
                'length': length,
                'start_3d': self.bridge_cs.transform_to_global(start_bcs),
                'end_3d': self.bridge_cs.transform_to_global(end_bcs),
                'length_diff': length - self.bridge_length
            }
        
        return girders
    
    def get_bearing_orientations(self, pier_locations: List[float]) -> Dict:
        """Calculate bearing orientations at supports"""
        
        bearings = {}
        
        # Abutment 1
        bearings['A1'] = {
            'location': 'Abutment 1',
            'chainage': 0,
            'orientation': self.skew_angle,
            'rotation_matrix': self._get_skew_rotation_matrix(self.skew_angle)
        }
        
        # Piers
        for i, loc in enumerate(pier_locations, 1):
            bearings[f'P{i}'] = {
                'location': f'Pier {i}',
                'chainage': loc,
                'orientation': self.skew_angle,
                'rotation_matrix': self._get_skew_rotation_matrix(self.skew_angle)
            }
        
        # Abutment 2
        bearings['A2'] = {
            'location': 'Abutment 2',
            'chainage': self.bridge_length,
            'orientation': self.skew_angle,
            'rotation_matrix': self._get_skew_rotation_matrix(self.skew_angle)
        }
        
        return bearings
    
    def _get_skew_rotation_matrix(self, angle_deg: float) -> np.ndarray:
        """Get rotation matrix for skew angle"""
        angle_rad = math.radians(angle_deg)
        return np.array([
            [math.cos(angle_rad), -math.sin(angle_rad), 0],
            [math.sin(angle_rad), math.cos(angle_rad), 0],
            [0, 0, 1]
        ])
    
    def calculate_clearance_envelope(self, 
                                     crossing_profile: callable,
                                     num_points: int = 50) -> Dict:
        """Calculate clearance between bridge and crossing"""
        
        clearances = []
        
        for i in range(num_points + 1):
            t = i / num_points
            chainage = t * self.bridge_length
            
            # Bridge soffit elevation
            bridge_x = chainage * math.cos(self.bridge_bearing_rad)
            bridge_y = chainage * math.sin(self.bridge_bearing_rad)
            bridge_z_soffit = (self.elevation_start + 
                              chainage * math.tan(self.grade_radians) -
                              self._get_construction_depth(chainage))
            
            # Crossing surface
            crossing_z = crossing_profile(bridge_x, bridge_y)
            
            clearance = bridge_z_soffit - crossing_z
            
            clearances.append({
                'chainage': chainage,
                'bridge_x': bridge_x,
                'bridge_y': bridge_y,
                'bridge_soffit': bridge_z_soffit,
                'crossing_surface': crossing_z,
                'clearance': clearance,
                'adequate': clearance >= 5.5  # Minimum clearance
            })
        
        return {
            'clearances': clearances,
            'minimum_clearance': min(c['clearance'] for c in clearances),
            'critical_locations': [c for c in clearances if c['clearance'] < 5.5]
        }
    
    def _get_construction_depth(self, chainage: float) -> float:
        """Get construction depth at chainage"""
        # Simplified - would reference actual section
        return 2.5  # meters
    
    def get_load_distribution_factors(self) -> Dict:
        """Calculate skew correction factors for load distribution"""
        
        alpha = self.skew_angle
        
        return {
            'moment_factor': 1 - 0.006 * alpha,
            'shear_factor': 1 + 0.005 * alpha,
            'torsion_factor': 0.01 * alpha,
            'deflection_factor': 1 - 0.004 * alpha,
            'reaction_factor': 1 + 0.008 * alpha
        }
    
    def generate_3d_model_data(self, 
                              num_girders: int = 4,
                              girder_spacing: float = 2.5) -> Dict:
        """Generate complete 3D model data"""
        
        model_data = {
            'bridge_parameters': {
                'length': self.bridge_length,
                'width': self.bridge_width,
                'skew_angle': self.skew_angle,
                'skew_type': self.skew_type.value,
                'bearing': self.bridge_bearing,
                'grade': self.bridge_grade
            },
            'coordinate_systems': {
                'bridge_origin': {
                    'x': self.bridge_cs.origin.x,
                    'y': self.bridge_cs.origin.y,
                    'z': self.bridge_cs.origin.z
                },
                'bridge_axes': {
                    'x': (self.bridge_cs.x_axis.dx, self.bridge_cs.x_axis.dy, self.bridge_cs.x_axis.dz),
                    'y': (self.bridge_cs.y_axis.dx, self.bridge_cs.y_axis.dy, self.bridge_cs.y_axis.dz),
                    'z': (self.bridge_cs.z_axis.dx, self.bridge_cs.z_axis.dy, self.bridge_cs.z_axis.dz)
                }
            },
            'deck_corners': self.get_deck_corners(),
            'girders': self.get_girder_lines(num_girders, girder_spacing),
            'intersection': self.calculate_intersection(),
            'load_factors': self.get_load_distribution_factors()
        }
        
        return model_data


class SkewIntersectionVisualizer:
    """Visualize skew bridge geometry"""
    
    def __init__(self, geometry: SkewBridgeGeometry):
        self.geometry = geometry
    
    def generate_dxf_3d(self, filename: str):
        """Generate 3D DXF file"""
        
        lines = []
        lines.append("0\nSECTION\n2\nHEADER\n")
        lines.append("9\n$ACADVER\n1\nAC1027\n")
        lines.append("0\nENDSEC\n")
        lines.append("0\nSECTION\n2\nENTITIES\n")
        
        # Deck surface as 3D face
        corners = self.geometry.get_deck_corners()
        
        # Create deck surface (two triangles)
        # Triangle 1: A1-A2-B1
        lines.append(self._create_3dface(
            corners['A1'], corners['A2'], corners['B1'], corners['B1'],
            layer="DECK"
        ))
        
        # Triangle 2: A2-B2-B1
        lines.append(self._create_3dface(
            corners['A2'], corners['B2'], corners['B1'], corners['B1'],
            layer="DECK"
        ))
        
        # Girders as lines
        girders = self.geometry.get_girder_lines(4, 2.5)
        for girder_id, girder in girders.items():
            lines.append(self._create_line_3d(
                girder['start_3d'], girder['end_3d'], layer="GIRDERS"
            ))
        
        # Crossing feature
        intersection = self.geometry.calculate_intersection()
        if "error" not in intersection:
            center = intersection['intersection_point']
            # Draw crossing centerline
            crossing_start = Point3D(
                center.x - 20 * math.cos(math.radians(self.geometry.crossing_bearing)),
                center.y - 20 * math.sin(math.radians(self.geometry.crossing_bearing)),
                center.z
            )
            crossing_end = Point3D(
                center.x + 20 * math.cos(math.radians(self.geometry.crossing_bearing)),
                center.y + 20 * math.sin(math.radians(self.geometry.crossing_bearing)),
                center.z
            )
            lines.append(self._create_line_3d(
                crossing_start, crossing_end, layer="CROSSING"
            ))
        
        lines.append("0\nENDSEC\n0\nEOF\n")
        
        with open(filename, 'w') as f:
            f.write('\n'.join(lines))
    
    def _create_3dface(self, p1: Point3D, p2: Point3D, p3: Point3D, p4: Point3D, 
                       layer: str = "0") -> str:
        """Create DXF 3DFACE entity"""
        return (f"0\n3DFACE\n8\n{layer}\n"
                f"10\n{p1.x}\n20\n{p1.y}\n30\n{p1.z}\n"
                f"11\n{p2.x}\n21\n{p2.y}\n31\n{p2.z}\n"
                f"12\n{p3.x}\n22\n{p3.y}\n32\n{p3.z}\n"
                f"13\n{p4.x}\n23\n{p4.y}\n33\n{p4.z}\n")
    
    def _create_line_3d(self, p1: Point3D, p2: Point3D, layer: str = "0") -> str:
        """Create DXF LINE entity"""
        return (f"0\nLINE\n8\n{layer}\n"
                f"10\n{p1.x}\n20\n{p1.y}\n30\n{p1.z}\n"
                f"11\n{p2.x}\n21\n{p2.y}\n31\n{p2.z}\n")
    
    def generate_report(self) -> str:
        """Generate text report of geometry"""
        
        report = []
        report.append("=" * 60)
        report.append("SKEW BRIDGE 3D GEOMETRY REPORT")
        report.append("=" * 60)
        
        report.append(f"\nBridge Parameters:")
        report.append(f"  Length: {self.geometry.bridge_length} m")
        report.append(f"  Width: {self.geometry.bridge_width} m")
        report.append(f"  Bearing: {self.geometry.bridge_bearing}°")
        report.append(f"  Grade: {self.geometry.bridge_grade}%")
        
        report.append(f"\nSkew Parameters:")
        report.append(f"  Skew Angle: {self.geometry.skew_angle}°")
        report.append(f"  Skew Type: {self.geometry.skew_type.value}")
        report.append(f"  Crossing: {self.geometry.crossing_type.value}")
        
        report.append(f"\nDeck Corners (Global Coordinates):")
        corners = self.geometry.get_deck_corners()
        for name, point in corners.items():
            report.append(f"  {name}: ({point.x:.3f}, {point.y:.3f}, {point.z:.3f})")
        
        report.append(f"\nGirder Lines:")
        girders = self.geometry.get_girder_lines(4, 2.5)
        for girder_id, data in girders.items():
            report.append(f"  {girder_id}:")
            report.append(f"    Offset: {data['offset']:.2f} m")
            report.append(f"    Length: {data['length']:.3f} m")
            report.append(f"    Diff from standard: {data['length_diff']:.3f} m")
        
        report.append(f"\nLoad Distribution Factors:")
        factors = self.geometry.get_load_distribution_factors()
        for factor, value in factors.items():
            report.append(f"  {factor}: {value:.4f}")
        
        report.append("\n" + "=" * 60)
        
        return '\n'.join(report)


class SkewGeometryReader:
    """Read skew geometry from Excel sheet"""
    
    def __init__(self, excel_path: str):
        self.excel_path = excel_path
    
    def read(self) -> SkewBridgeGeometry:
        """Read skew geometry from Excel"""
        
        df = pd.read_excel(self.excel_path, sheet_name='SKEW_3D_GEOMETRY', header=1)
        
        # Extract parameters
        bridge_length = float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Bridge Chainage End', 'Value'].values[0])
        bridge_width = float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Overall Deck Width', 'Value'].values[0])
        bridge_bearing = float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Bridge Bearing', 'Value'].values[0])
        bridge_grade = float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Bridge Grade', 'Value'].values[0])
        
        skew_angle = float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Skew Angle', 'Value'].values[0])
        skew_type_str = str(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Skew Type', 'Value'].values[0])
        crossing_type_str = str(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Crossing Type', 'Value'].values[0])
        crossing_width = float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Crossing Width', 'Value'].values[0])
        crossing_chainage = float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Crossing Chainage', 'Value'].values[0])
        crossing_bearing = float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='Crossing Bearing', 'Value'].values[0])
        
        elevation_start = float(df.loc[df['Parameter']=='GCS Origin Z', 'Value'].values[0])
        
        skew_type = SkewType.RIGHT if 'Right' in skew_type_str else SkewType.LEFT
        crossing_type = CrossingType(crossing_type_str)
        
        return SkewBridgeGeometry(
            bridge_length=bridge_length,
            bridge_width=bridge_width,
            bridge_bearing=bridge_bearing,
            bridge_grade=bridge_grade,
            skew_angle=skew_angle,
            skew_type=skew_type,
            crossing_type=crossing_type,
            crossing_width=crossing_width,
            crossing_chainage=crossing_chainage,
            crossing_bearing=crossing_bearing,
            elevation_start=elevation_start
        )


# Example Usage
if __name__ == "__main__":
    # Create skew bridge geometry
    geometry = SkewBridgeGeometry(
        bridge_length=100.0,
        bridge_width=12.0,
        bridge_bearing=45.0,
        bridge_grade=2.0,
        skew_angle=30.0,
        skew_type=SkewType.RIGHT,
        crossing_type=CrossingType.ROAD,
        crossing_width=20.0,
        crossing_chainage=50.0,
        crossing_bearing=90.0,
        elevation_start=100.0
    )
    
    # Generate 3D model data
    model_data = geometry.generate_3d_model_data(num_girders=4, girder_spacing=2.5)
    
    # Print report
    visualizer = SkewIntersectionVisualizer(geometry)
    print(visualizer.generate_report())
    
    # Generate DXF
    visualizer.generate_dxf_3d("skew_bridge_3d.dxf")
    
    # Check clearances
    def road_profile(x, y):
        # Simple road profile
        return 98.0 + 0.02 * abs(y - 50)
    
    clearance_data = geometry.calculate_clearance_envelope(road_profile)
    print(f"\nMinimum Clearance: {clearance_data['minimum_clearance']:.2f} m")
```

|-------|---------|-------|--------|
| Skew Angle | α | < 60° | OK/NG |
| Girder Length Diff | `ΔL/L` | < 5% | OK/NG |
| Bearing Rotation | `θ_skew` | < 45° | OK/NG |
| End Zone Length | `1.5W/tan(α)` | > 2×W | OK/NG |
| Clearance at Intersection | `Z_bridge - Z_crossing` | > 5.5m | OK/NG |

### H.2 Structural Validation

| Check | Requirement | Calculation |
|-------|-------------|-------------|
| Torsion Check | `T < 0.1×M` | Calculate from skew |
| End Diaphragm | Required if α > 20° | Provide rigid diaphragm |
| Bearing Fixity | Consider horizontal displacement | Guided bearings |
| Expansion Joint | Accommodate skew movement | Orient perpendicular to movement |

# SHEET 22: SKEW_LONGITUDINAL_SECTION

## Purpose
Calculate longitudinal profiles for skew bridges showing varying soffit elevations across the deck width due to skew geometry.

---

## SECTION A: SKEW BRIDGE SOFFIT GEOMETRY FUNDAMENTALS

### A.1 Skew Bridge Characteristics

```
NORMAL BRIDGE (α = 0°)          SKEW BRIDGE (α = 30°)
                                    
    ┌─────────────┐                ┌──────────────┐
    │             │                ╱              ╲
    │  SOFFIT     │    vs.       ╱    SOFFIT      ╲
    │  (Level)    │             ╱   (Sloped)       ╲
    │             │            ╱                    ╲
    └─────────────┘           └────────────────────┘
    
    Perpendicular to              At angle to girders
    girders
```

**Key Concept:** In a skew bridge, the soffit line is NOT perpendicular to the girders. This creates:
- Varying soffit elevations across the deck width
- Different construction depths for each girder
- Sloped end faces (not vertical)
- Complex bearing seat elevations

### A.2 Skew Soffit Parameters

| Parameter | Symbol | Value | Unit | Description |
|-----------|--------|-------|------|-------------|
| Skew Angle | α | 30 | ° | Acute angle between bridge and support |
| Deck Width | W | 12.0 | m | Total width |
| Span Length | L | 30.0 | m | Along bridge centerline |
| Number of Girders | N | 4 | nos | |
| Girder Spacing | S | 2.5 | m | Center-to-center |
| Soffit Profile Type | - | Parabolic | - | Constant/Parabolic/Sloped |

---

## SECTION B: SOFFIT PROFILE DEFINITIONS

### B.1 Profile Type 1: Constant Depth (Horizontal Soffit)

**Characteristics:**
- Soffit is horizontal (constant elevation)
- Deck follows grade line
- Construction depth varies with deck profile

**Elevation Formula:**
```
Soffit_Elevation(x, y) = Soffit_Level_at_Support

Where:
- x = chainage along bridge
- y = offset from centerline
```

**Construction Depth:**
```
D(x) = Deck_Elevation(x) - Soffit_Elevation
```

---

### B.2 Profile Type 2: Parabolic Soffit (Crowned)

**Characteristics:**
- Soffit curved vertically for drainage/aesthetics
- Maximum depth at mid-span
- Minimum depth at supports

**Elevation Formula:**
```
Soffit_Elevation(x, y) = Support_Soffit + (4×h×x/L)×(1 - x/L)

Where:
- h = crown height (rise at mid-span)
- x = distance from support
- L = span length
```

**Crown Height Table:**

| Span (m) | Crown Height (mm) | Application |
|----------|-------------------|-------------|
| 10-15 | 150-200 | Short spans |
| 15-25 | 200-300 | Medium spans |
| 25-40 | 300-500 | Long spans |
| >40 | 500-800 | Very long spans |

---

### B.3 Profile Type 3: Straight Sloped Soffit

**Characteristics:**
- Soffit slopes in one direction for drainage
- Constant slope along span
- May slope across width or along length

**Elevation Formula (Longitudinal Slope):**
```
Soffit_Elevation(x, y) = Support_Soffit + (Soffit_Slope × x / 100)
```

**Elevation Formula (Transverse Slope):**
```
Soffit_Elevation(x, y) = Center_Soffit + (Transverse_Slope × y / 100)
```

---

### B.4 Profile Type 4: Combined Profile (Skew Bridge Standard)

**Characteristics:**
- Parabolic along bridge centerline
- Sloped across width due to skew
- Most common for skew bridges

**Elevation Formula:**
```
Soffit_Elevation(x, y) = Base_Soffit(x) + (y × tan(α))

Where:
- Base_Soffit(x) = Parabolic profile along centerline
- y × tan(α) = Skew correction across width
```

---

## SECTION C: PER-GIRDER SOFFIT ELEVATION CALCULATIONS

### C.1 Girder Position Definitions

| Girder | Offset (y) | Offset Direction | tan(α) Effect |
|--------|------------|------------------|---------------|
| G1 (Exterior) | -3.75 m | Left | -2.165 m |
| G2 | -1.25 m | Left | -0.722 m |
| G3 | +1.25 m | Right | +0.722 m |
| G4 (Exterior) | +3.75 m | Right | +2.165 m |

**Skew Effect Calculation:**
```
For α = 30°: tan(30°) = 0.577

Elevation_Difference = y × tan(α)

G1: -3.75 × 0.577 = -2.165 m (lower)
G4: +3.75 × 0.577 = +2.165 m (higher)
```

---

### C.2 Soffit Elevation Table (Parabolic Profile)

**Parameters:**
- Span: 30 m
- Crown: 300 mm
- Support Soffit (Center): 98.50 m
- Skew: 30°

| Chainage (m) | Center Soffit | G1 Soffit | G2 Soffit | G3 Soffit | G4 Soffit | Formation Level |
|--------------|---------------|-----------|-----------|-----------|-----------|-----------------|
| 0.0 (Support) | 98.500 | 96.335 | 97.778 | 99.222 | 100.665 | 101.500 |
| 3.75 (L/8) | 98.613 | 96.448 | 97.891 | 99.335 | 100.778 | 101.575 |
| 7.50 (L/4) | 98.725 | 96.560 | 98.003 | 99.447 | 100.890 | 101.650 |
| 11.25 (3L/8) | 98.838 | 96.673 | 98.116 | 99.560 | 101.003 | 101.725 |
| 15.0 (Mid) | 98.850 | 96.685 | 98.128 | 99.572 | 101.015 | 101.800 |
| 18.75 (5L/8) | 98.838 | 96.673 | 98.116 | 99.560 | 101.003 | 101.875 |
| 22.50 (3L/4) | 98.725 | 96.560 | 98.003 | 99.447 | 100.890 | 101.950 |
| 26.25 (7L/8) | 98.613 | 96.448 | 97.891 | 99.335 | 100.778 | 102.025 |
| 30.0 (Support) | 98.500 | 96.335 | 97.778 | 99.222 | 100.665 | 102.100 |

**Excel Formulas:**
```
Center Soffit: =SUPPORT_SOFFIT + (4*CROWN*CHAINAGE/SPAN)*(1-CHAINAGE/SPAN)

G1 Soffit: =Center_Soffit + (-3.75)*TAN(RADIANS(30))
G2 Soffit: =Center_Soffit + (-1.25)*TAN(RADIANS(30))
G3 Soffit: =Center_Soffit + (1.25)*TAN(RADIANS(30))
G4 Soffit: =Center_Soffit + (3.75)*TAN(RADIANS(30))
```

## SECTION D: END ELEVATION CALCULATIONS

### D.1 Skewed End Face Geometry

**Support Face Orientation:**
- Perpendicular to bridge centerline in plan
- Vertical in elevation (for bearings)
- Soffit elevation varies linearly across width

**End Elevation Coordinates:**

| Point | Plan Position | Soffit Elevation | Deck Elevation | Construction Depth |
|-------|---------------|------------------|----------------|-------------------|
| A (G1) | y = -3.75 | 96.335 | 101.500 | 5.165 |
| B (G2) | y = -1.25 | 97.778 | 101.500 | 3.722 |
| C (G3) | y = +1.25 | 99.222 | 101.500 | 2.278 |
| D (G4) | y = +3.75 | 100.665 | 101.500 | 0.835 |

**End Face Slope:**
```
Slope = (Elevation_D - Elevation_A) / Deck_Width
      = (100.665 - 96.335) / 12.0
      = 4.33 / 12.0
      = 36.1% or 19.8°
```

---

### D.2 Bearing Seat Elevations

| Bearing | Chainage | Offset | Soffit Elevation | Bearing Height | Seat Elevation |
|---------|----------|--------|------------------|----------------|----------------|
| B1 (G1) | 0.0 | -3.75 | 96.335 | 0.300 | 96.635 |
| B2 (G2) | 0.0 | -1.25 | 97.778 | 0.300 | 98.078 |
| B3 (G3) | 0.0 | +1.25 | 99.222 | 0.300 | 99.522 |
| B4 (G4) | 0.0 | +3.75 | 100.665 | 0.300 | 100.965 |

**Bearing Seat Slope:**
```
Seat_Slope = (100.965 - 96.635) / 7.5
           = 4.33 / 7.5
           = 57.7% or 29.9°
```

**Important:** Bearing seats must be:
1. Level (horizontal) for each bearing
2. At correct elevation for each girder
3. Properly oriented for skew angle

```
Required Cross-Fall: 2.5%

Deck Surface: Sloped for drainage
Soffit: Sloped due to skew

Effective Construction Depth varies
```

## SECTION H: FORMWORK AND FALSEWORK

### H.1 Soffit Formwork Requirements

**Formwork Challenges:**
1. Sloped soffit requires sloped formwork
2. Each girder has different elevation
3. End diaphragm is skewed

**Formwork Elevations:**

| Point | Formwork Bottom | Formwork Top | Height |
|-------|-----------------|--------------|--------|
| G1 Support | 95.835 | 96.335 | 0.500 |
| G2 Support | 97.278 | 97.778 | 0.500 |
| G3 Support | 98.722 | 99.222 | 0.500 |
| G4 Support | 100.165 | 100.665 | 0.500 |

**Falsework Heights:**
```
Falsework_Height(x,y) = Soffit_Elevation(x,y) - Ground_Level(x,y)
```

**Maximum Falsework Height:** At G4 support (if ground is level)

---

## SECTION I: PYTHON LONGITUDINAL SECTION CALCULATOR

```python
"""
Skew Bridge Longitudinal Section Calculator
Calculates varying soffit elevations for skew bridges
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import List, Dict, Tuple, Callable
import math


class SoffitProfileType:
    CONSTANT = "Constant"
    PARABOLIC = "Parabolic"
    STRAIGHT_SLOPED = "Straight_Sloped"
    COMBINED = "Combined"


@dataclass
class SkewLongitudinalSection:
    """Longitudinal section calculator for skew bridges"""
    
    # Bridge parameters
    span_length: float  # m
    deck_width: float   # m
    skew_angle: float   # degrees
    
    # Girder arrangement
    num_girders: int
    girder_spacing: float  # m
    
    # Soffit profile
    profile_type: str
    support_soffit_center: float  # m elevation
    crown_height: float = 0.0     # m (for parabolic)
    soffit_slope: float = 0.0     # % (for sloped)
    
    # Deck profile
    formation_level_start: float  # m
    formation_level_end: float    # m
    
    def __post_init__(self):
        self.skew_rad = math.radians(self.skew_angle)
        self.tan_skew = math.tan(self.skew_rad)
        
        # Calculate girder positions
        self.girder_offsets = self._calculate_girder_offsets()
        
        # Generate station points
        self.stations = np.linspace(0, self.span_length, 21)  # Every 5%
    
    def _calculate_girder_offsets(self) -> Dict[str, float]:
        """Calculate Y-offset for each girder from centerline"""
        offsets = {}
        start_offset = -((self.num_girders - 1) / 2) * self.girder_spacing
        
        for i in range(self.num_girders):
            girder_id = f"G{i+1}"
            offsets[girder_id] = start_offset + i * self.girder_spacing
        
        return offsets
    
    def _calculate_center_soffit(self, chainage: float) -> float:
        """Calculate soffit elevation at centerline for given chainage"""
        
        if self.profile_type == SoffitProfileType.CONSTANT:
            return self.support_soffit_center
        
        elif self.profile_type == SoffitProfileType.PARABOLIC:
            # Parabolic profile: h(x) = 4*h*(x/L)*(1-x/L)
            x_ratio = chainage / self.span_length
            rise = 4 * self.crown_height * x_ratio * (1 - x_ratio)
            return self.support_soffit_center + rise
        
        elif self.profile_type == SoffitProfileType.STRAIGHT_SLOPED:
            slope_decimal = self.soffit_slope / 100
            return self.support_soffit_center + slope_decimal * chainage
        
        elif self.profile_type == SoffitProfileType.COMBINED:
            # Parabolic along length + transverse slope
            x_ratio = chainage / self.span_length
            rise = 4 * self.crown_height * x_ratio * (1 - x_ratio)
            return self.support_soffit_center + rise
        
        return self.support_soffit_center
    
    def calculate_soffit_elevation(self, chainage: float, 
                                    girder_id: str = None,
                                    offset: float = None) -> float:
        """Calculate soffit elevation at specific chainage and offset"""
        
        # Get centerline elevation
        center_elev = self._calculate_center_soffit(chainage)
        
        # Apply skew effect if offset provided
        if offset is None and girder_id is not None:
            offset = self.girder_offsets.get(girder_id, 0)
        
        if offset is not None:
            # Skew effect: elevation changes across width
            skew_effect = offset * self.tan_skew
            return center_elev + skew_effect
        
        return center_elev
    
    def generate_longitudinal_table(self) -> pd.DataFrame:
        """Generate complete longitudinal section table"""
        
        data = []
        
        for chainage in self.stations:
            row = {'Chainage': chainage}
            
            # Formation level
            formation = self._interpolate_formation(chainage)
            row['Formation_Level'] = formation
            
            # Center soffit
            center_soffit = self._calculate_center_soffit(chainage)
            row['Soffit_Center'] = center_soffit
            
            # Per-girder soffit
            for girder_id, offset in self.girder_offsets.items():
                soffit = self.calculate_soffit_elevation(chainage, girder_id=girder_id)
                row[f'Soffit_{girder_id}'] = soffit
                row[f'Depth_{girder_id}'] = formation - soffit
            
            # Average depth
            depths = [row[f'Depth_{g}'] for g in self.girder_offsets.keys()]
            row['Avg_Depth'] = np.mean(depths)
            row['Min_Depth'] = min(depths)
            row['Max_Depth'] = max(depths)
            row['Depth_Range'] = max(depths) - min(depths)
            
            data.append(row)
        
        return pd.DataFrame(data)
    
    def _interpolate_formation(self, chainage: float) -> float:
        """Interpolate formation level at chainage"""
        grade = (self.formation_level_end - self.formation_level_start) / self.span_length
        return self.formation_level_start + grade * chainage
    
    def generate_end_elevation(self, support_chainage: float = 0) -> pd.DataFrame:
        """Generate end elevation data"""
        
        data = []
        
        # Points across deck width
        y_positions = np.linspace(-self.deck_width/2, self.deck_width/2, 25)
        
        for y in y_positions:
            soffit = self.calculate_soffit_elevation(support_chainage, offset=y)
            formation = self._interpolate_formation(support_chainage)
            
            data.append({
                'Offset_Y': y,
                'Soffit_Elevation': soffit,
                'Formation_Elevation': formation,
                'Construction_Depth': formation - soffit,
                'Distance_From_G1': y - min(self.girder_offsets.values())
            })
        
        return pd.DataFrame(data)
    
    def calculate_bearing_seats(self, support_chainage: float) -> pd.DataFrame:
        """Calculate bearing seat elevations"""
        
        data = []
        bearing_height = 0.300  # Typical bearing height
        
        for girder_id, offset in self.girder_offsets.items():
            soffit = self.calculate_soffit_elevation(support_chainage, girder_id=girder_id)
            
            data.append({
                'Girder': girder_id,
                'Offset': offset,
                'Soffit_Elevation': soffit,
                'Bearing_Height': bearing_height,
                'Seat_Elevation': soffit + bearing_height,
                'Seat_Thickness_Required': self._calculate_seat_thickness(soffit)
            })
        
        df = pd.DataFrame(data)
        
        # Calculate seat slope
        seat_elevations = df['Seat_Elevation'].values
        df['Seat_Slope'] = (seat_elevations[-1] - seat_elevations[0]) / \
                          (df['Offset'].values[-1] - df['Offset'].values[0])
        
        return df
    
    def _calculate_seat_thickness(self, soffit_elev: float) -> float:
        """Calculate required seat thickness"""
        # Minimum 150mm or as per design
        return max(0.150, 0.200)  # Simplified
    
    def generate_tendon_profile(self, 
                                 tendon_profile_func: Callable,
                                 girder_id: str = None) -> pd.DataFrame:
        """Generate prestressing tendon profile"""
        
        data = []
        
        for chainage in self.stations:
            soffit = self.calculate_soffit_elevation(chainage, girder_id=girder_id)
            eccentricity = tendon_profile_func(chainage)
            
            data.append({
                'Chainage': chainage,
                'Soffit_Elevation': soffit,
                'Tendon_Eccentricity': eccentricity,
                'Tendon_Elevation': soffit + eccentricity,
                'Tendon_Drape': eccentricity
            })
        
        return pd.DataFrame(data)
    
    def check_clearances(self, 
                        obstruction_func: Callable,
                        num_points_width: int = 10) -> Dict:
        """Check clearances against obstructions"""
        
        results = {
            'clearances': [],
            'minimum_clearance': float('inf'),
            'critical_points': []
        }
        
        y_positions = np.linspace(-self.deck_width/2, self.deck_width/2, num_points_width)
        
        for chainage in self.stations:
            for y in y_positions:
                soffit = self.calculate_soffit_elevation(chainage, offset=y)
                obstruction = obstruction_func(chainage, y)
                clearance = soffit - obstruction
                
                point_data = {
                    'chainage': chainage,
                    'offset_y': y,
                    'soffit': soffit,
                    'obstruction': obstruction,
                    'clearance': clearance
                }
                
                results['clearances'].append(point_data)
                
                if clearance < results['minimum_clearance']:
                    results['minimum_clearance'] = clearance
                
                if clearance < 5.5:  # Minimum clearance
                    results['critical_points'].append(point_data)
        
        return results
    
    def generate_dxf_longitudinal(self, filename: str, 
                                   girder_id: str = None):
        """Generate DXF file for longitudinal section"""
        
        lines = []
        lines.append("0\nSECTION\n2\nENTITIES\n")
        
        # Select girder or use centerline
        if girder_id is None:
            offsets = [0]
            labels = ["Centerline"]
        else:
            offsets = [self.girder_offsets[girder_id]]
            labels = [girder_id]
        
        # Scale factors for drawing
        x_scale = 1.0  # Horizontal scale
        z_scale = 5.0  # Vertical exaggeration
        
        for offset, label in zip(offsets, labels):
            # Soffit line
            soffit_points = []
            for chainage in self.stations:
                elev = self.calculate_soffit_elevation(chainage, offset=offset)
                x = chainage * x_scale
                z = elev * z_scale
                soffit_points.append((x, z))
            
            lines.append(self._create_lwpolyline_2d(soffit_points, layer="SOFFIT"))
            
            # Formation line
            formation_points = []
            for chainage in self.stations:
                elev = self._interpolate_formation(chainage)
                x = chainage * x_scale
                z = elev * z_scale
                formation_points.append((x, z))
            
            lines.append(self._create_lwpolyline_2d(formation_points, layer="FORMATION"))
        
        lines.append("0\nENDSEC\n0\nEOF\n")
        
        with open(filename, 'w') as f:
            f.write('\n'.join(lines))
    
    def _create_lwpolyline_2d(self, points: List[Tuple[float, float]], 
                               layer: str = "0") -> str:
        """Create 2D DXF LWPOLYLINE"""
        dxf = f"0\nLWPOLYLINE\n8\n{layer}\n90\n{len(points)}\n70\n0\n"
        for x, y in points:
            dxf += f"10\n{x}\n20\n{y}\n"
        return dxf
    
    def generate_report(self) -> str:
        """Generate text report"""
        
        report = []
        report.append("=" * 70)
        report.append("SKEW BRIDGE LONGITUDINAL SECTION REPORT")
        report.append("=" * 70)
        
        report.append(f"\nBridge Parameters:")
        report.append(f"  Span Length: {self.span_length} m")
        report.append(f"  Deck Width: {self.deck_width} m")
        report.append(f"  Skew Angle: {self.skew_angle}°")
        report.append(f"  Tan(Skew): {self.tan_skew:.4f}")
        
        report.append(f"\nGirder Positions:")
        for girder_id, offset in self.girder_offsets.items():
            report.append(f"  {girder_id}: Offset = {offset:+.2f} m")
        
        report.append(f"\nSoffit Profile: {self.profile_type}")
        if self.profile_type == SoffitProfileType.PARABOLIC:
            report.append(f"  Crown Height: {self.crown_height} m")
        
        # Calculate key elevations
        mid_chainage = self.span_length / 2
        
        report.append(f"\nElevations at Mid-Span (Chainage {mid_chainage}m):")
        center_soffit = self._calculate_center_soffit(mid_chainage)
        report.append(f"  Center Soffit: {center_soffit:.3f} m")
        
        for girder_id in sorted(self.girder_offsets.keys()):
            soffit = self.calculate_soffit_elevation(mid_chainage, girder_id=girder_id)
            formation = self._interpolate_formation(mid_chainage)
            depth = formation - soffit
            report.append(f"  {girder_id} Soffit: {soffit:.3f} m (Depth: {depth:.3f} m)")
        
        # End elevation summary
        report.append(f"\nEnd Elevation Summary:")
        end_data = self.generate_end_elevation(0)
        report.append(f"  Min Soffit (G1): {end_data['Soffit_Elevation'].min():.3f} m")
        report.append(f"  Max Soffit (G4): {end_data['Soffit_Elevation'].max():.3f} m")
        report.append(f"  Soffit Slope: {(end_data['Soffit_Elevation'].max() - end_data['Soffit_Elevation'].min()) / self.deck_width:.2%}")
        
        report.append("\n" + "=" * 70)
        
        return '\n'.join(report)


# Example Usage
if __name__ == "__main__":
    # Create skew bridge longitudinal section
    skew_section = SkewLongitudinalSection(
        span_length=30.0,
        deck_width=12.0,
        skew_angle=30.0,
        num_girders=4,
        girder_spacing=2.5,
        profile_type=SoffitProfileType.PARABOLIC,
        support_soffit_center=98.50,
        crown_height=0.300,
        formation_level_start=101.500,
        formation_level_end=102.100
    )
    
    # Generate longitudinal table
    long_table = skew_section.generate_longitudinal_table()
    print(long_table[['Chainage', 'Soffit_G1', 'Soffit_G4', 'Depth_G1', 'Depth_G4']])
    
    # Generate end elevation
    end_elev = skew_section.generate_end_elevation(support_chainage=0)
    print("\nEnd Elevation:")
    print(end_elev[['Offset_Y', 'Soffit_Elevation', 'Construction_Depth']])
    
    # Generate bearing seats
    bearing_seats = skew_section.calculate_bearing_seats(support_chainage=0)
    print("\nBearing Seats:")
    print(bearing_seats[['Girder', 'Soffit_Elevation', 'Seat_Elevation']])
    
    # Print report
    print(skew_section.generate_report())
    
    # Generate DXF
    skew_section.generate_dxf_longitudinal("skew_long_section.dxf", girder_id="G1")
```

## SECTION J: EXCEL FORMULAS REFERENCE

### J.1 Soffit Elevation Formulas

| Cell | Description | Formula |
|------|-------------|---------|
| D10 | Center Soffit @ Chainage | `=$B$5+(4*$B$7*B10/$B$4)*(1-B10/$B$4)` |
| E10 | G1 Soffit | `=D10+(-3.75)*TAN(RADIANS($B$6))` |
| F10 | G2 Soffit | `=D10+(-1.25)*TAN(RADIANS($B$6))` |
| G10 | G3 Soffit | `=D10+(1.25)*TAN(RADIANS($B$6))` |
| H10 | G4 Soffit | `=D10+(3.75)*TAN(RADIANS($B$6))` |
| I10 | Formation Level | `=$B$9+($B$10-$B$9)*B10/$B$4` |
| J10 | G1 Depth | `=I10-E10` |

### J.2 Named Ranges

| Name | Refers To | Description |
|------|-----------|-------------|
| `SPAN_LENGTH` | `=$B$4` | Bridge span |
| `SUPPORT_SOFFIT` | `=` | |

# SHEET 23: SKEW_CROSS_SECTION_GENERATOR

## Purpose
Generate cross-sections for skew bridges showing skewed girder spacing, variable deck geometry, and end diaphragm details.

## SECTION A: SKEW CROSS-SECTION GEOMETRY FUNDAMENTALS

### A.1 Skew vs Normal Cross-Section Comparison

```
NORMAL CROSS-SECTION (α=0°)          SKEW CROSS-SECTION (α=30°)
                                     
     ←——— W ———→                     
     ┌─────────┐                      ╱────────────────╲
     │ ↑       │    Deck            ╱    ↑            ╲
     │ │       │    Slab           ╱     │             ╲
     │ │  G1   │    ←———         ╱      │              ╲
     │ │  G2   │    Perpendicular    │               ╲
     │ │  G3   │    to girders      │                ╲
     │ │  G4   │                   │                 ╲
     │ ↓       │                  │                  ╲
     └─────────┘                 └──────────────────╲
     ↑↑↑↑↑↑↑↑↑                  ↑↑↑↑↑↑↑↑↑↑↑↑↑↑↑↑↑↑↑↑↑↑
     Bearings (aligned)          Bearings (skewed)
     
     Girder Spacing: Constant    Girder Spacing: Varies
     End Face: Vertical          End Face: Sloped (Skewed)
```

### A.2 Key Geometric Relationships

| Parameter | Normal Bridge | Skew Bridge | Formula |
|-----------|--------------|-------------|---------|
| Girder Spacing (perpendicular) | S | S' = S/cos(α) | `S/cos(α)` |
| End Face Width | W | W' = W/cos(α) | `W/cos(α)` |
| Deck Area | L×W | L×W/cos(α) | `L×W/cos(α)` |
| Minimum Girder Length | L | L_min = L - W×tan(α)/2 | `L - W×tan(α)/2` |
| Maximum Girder Length | L | L_max = L + W×tan(α)/2 | `L + W×tan(α)/2` |

---

## SECTION B: SKEWED GIRDER SPACING CALCULATIONS

### B.1 Girder Position Definitions

**Given:**
- Deck Width (W) = 12.0 m
- Number of Girders (N) = 4
- Nominal Spacing (S) = 2.5 m (perpendicular to girders)
- Skew Angle (α) = 30°

**Calculations:**

| Parameter | Symbol | Formula | Value |
|-----------|--------|---------|-------|
| Skew Factor | k | `1/cos(α)` | 1.155 |
| Spacing along deck | S' | `S/cos(α)` | 2.887 m |
| Half-width | W/2 | `W/2` | 6.0 m |
| First girder offset | y₁ | `-(N-1)×S/2` | -3.75 m |
| Last girder offset | yₙ | `+(N-1)×S/2` | +3.75 m |

### B.2 Girder Coordinates (Plan View)

**In Deck Coordinate System (Y perpendicular to centerline):**

| Girder | Y-Offset (perp) | X-Offset (along) | Skew Correction |
|--------|-----------------|------------------|-----------------|
| G1 | -3.75 m | 0 | Reference |
| G2 | -1.25 m | 0 | Reference |
| G3 | +1.25 m | 0 | Reference |
| G4 | +3.75 m | 0 | Reference |

**At Support Face (Skewed at angle α):**

| Girder | Chainage | Y-Position | X-Skew-Offset |
|--------|----------|------------|---------------|
| G1 | 0.0 | -3.75 | 0 (Reference) |
| G2 | 0.0 | -1.25 | +2.165 m |
| G3 | 0.0 | +1.25 | +4.330 m |
| G4 | 0.0 | +3.75 | +6.495 m |

**X-Skew-Offset Formula:**
```
ΔX = y × tan(α)

For α = 30°, tan(30°) = 0.577

G2: ΔX = -1.25 × 0.577 = -0.722 m (but at support, use absolute)
Actually: G2 is at y = -1.25, so at x=0, G2 is at chainage 0.722
```

### B.3 Girder Length Variation Table

| Girder | Y-Offset | Start Chainage | End Chainage | Length | Diff from Nominal |
|--------|----------|----------------|--------------|--------|-------------------|
| G1 | -3.75 | -2.165 | 27.835 | 30.000 | 0.000 |
| G2 | -1.25 | -0.722 | 29.278 | 30.000 | 0.000 |
| G3 | +1.25 | +0.722 | 30.722 | 30.000 | 0.000 |
| G4 | +3.75 | +2.165 | 32.165 | 30.000 | 0.000 |

**Wait - this needs correction. Let me recalculate:**

For a 30m span with 30° skew:
- The support face is at 30° to the girder
- G1 (y=-3.75) meets support at chainage = 3.75 × tan(30°) = 2.165m
- G4 (y=+3.75) meets support at chainage = -2.165m (or 27.835m from other end)

| Girder | Y-Offset | Start Chainage | End Chainage | Length |
|--------|----------|----------------|--------------|--------|
| G1 | -3.75 | 2.165 | 32.165 | 30.000 |
| G2 | -1.25 | 0.722 | 30.722 | 30.000 |
| G3 | +1.25 | -0.722 | 29.278 | 30.000 |
| G4 | +3.75 | -2.165 | 27.835 | 30.000 |

Actually, for a simple span, all girders have the same length along their centerline:
Length = Span / cos(α) for exterior? No wait...

Let me clarify: The SPAN is measured perpendicular to supports (30m).
The girders are parallel to the centerline.
At 30° skew, the girder length = 30 / cos(30°) = 34.64m

| Girder | Y-Offset | Start X | End X | Girder Length |
|--------|----------|---------|-------|---------------|
| G1 | -3.75 | 2.165 | 36.805 | 34.64 |
| G2 | -1.25 | 0.722 | 35.362 | 34.64 |
| G3 | +1.25 | -0.722 | 33.918 | 34.64 |
| G4 | +3.75 | -2.165 | 32.475 | 34.64 |

| Bearing Bottom | 99.243 | 99.214 | 99.186 | 99.157 |

## SECTION G: PYTHON CROSS-SECTION GENERATOR

```python
"""
Skew Cross-Section Generator
Generates cross-sections for skew bridges with skewed girder spacing
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import List, Dict, Tuple, Optional
import math


@dataclass
class SkewCrossSection:
    """Cross-section generator for skew bridges"""
    
    # Bridge parameters
    deck_width: float           # m
    span_length: float          # m (perpendicular to supports)
    skew_angle: float           # degrees
    
    # Girder parameters
    num_girders: int
    girder_spacing: float       # m (perpendicular to girders)
    girder_depth: float         # m
    web_thickness: float        # m
    
    # Deck parameters
    deck_thickness: float       # m
    cross_slope: float          # % (for drainage)
    
    # Vertical parameters
    formation_level: float    # m at center, mid-span
    soffit_level: float         # m at center, mid-span
    bearing_height: float       # m
    
    def __post_init__(self):
        self.skew_rad = math.radians(self.skew_angle)
        self.tan_skew = math.tan(self.skew_rad)
        self.cos_skew = math.cos(self.skew_rad)
        self.sin_skew = math.sin(self.skew_rad)
        
        # Calculate girder positions
        self.girder_data = self._calculate_girder_positions()
        
        # Calculate end face width
        self.end_face_width = self.deck_width / self.cos_skew
    
    def _calculate_girder_positions(self) -> Dict:
        """Calculate girder positions in plan"""
        
        girders = {}
        
        # Perpendicular offsets from centerline
        half_spacing = (self.num_girders - 1) * self.girder_spacing / 2
        
        for i in range(self.num_girders):
            girder_id = f"G{i+1}"
            y_offset = -half_spacing + i * self.girder_spacing
            
            # At support face, x-offset due to skew
            x_offset_support = y_offset * self.tan_skew
            
            # Girder length along centerline
            girder_length = self.span_length / self.cos_skew
            
            girders[girder_id] = {
                'index': i,
                'y_offset': y_offset,           # Perpendicular to girders
                'x_offset_support': x_offset_support,  # Along bridge at support
                'start_chainage': x_offset_support,
                'end_chainage': x_offset_support + girder_length,
                'length': girder_length,
                'start_elevation': None,  # To be calculated
                'end_elevation': None
            }
        
        return girders
    
    def calculate_elevations(self, 
                              grade: float = 2.0,
                              parabolic_soffit: bool = True,
                              crown_height: float = 0.3):
        """Calculate elevations for all girders"""
        
        for girder_id, data in self.girder_data.items():
            # Start elevation (at support)
            # Account for grade and cross-slope
            start_y = data['y_offset']
            start_elev = (self.formation_level - 
                         self.span_length/2 * grade/100 -  # Grade effect
                         start_y * self.cross_slope/100)    # Cross-slope
            
            data['start_elevation'] = {
                'formation': start_elev,
                'deck_top': start_elev,
                'deck_bottom': start_elev - self.deck_thickness,
                'girder_top': start_elev - self.deck_thickness,
                'girder_bottom': start_elev - self.deck_thickness - self.girder_depth,
                'bearing_top': start_elev - self.deck_thickness - self.girder_depth + self.bearing_height,
                'bearing_bottom': start_elev - self.deck_thickness - self.girder_depth,
                'soffit': start_elev - self.deck_thickness - self.girder_depth
            }
            
            # End elevation (at other support)
            end_elev = (self.formation_level + 
                       self.span_length/2 * grade/100 - 
                       start_y * self.cross_slope/100)
            
            data['end_elevation'] = {
                'formation': end_elev,
                'deck_top': end_elev,
                'deck_bottom': end_elev - self.deck_thickness,
                'girder_top': end_elev - self.deck_thickness,
                'girder_bottom': end_elev - self.deck_thickness - self.girder_depth,
                'bearing_top': end_elev - self.deck_thickness - self.girder_depth + self.bearing_height,
                'bearing_bottom': end_elev - self.deck_thickness - self.girder_depth,
                'soffit': end_elev - self.deck_thickness - self.girder_depth
            }
            
            # Mid-span elevation
            mid_elev = (self.formation_level - 
                       start_y * self.cross_slope/100)
            
            if parabolic_soffit:
                mid_soffit = (self.soffit_level + 
                             crown_height * 4 * 0.5 * 0.5)  # Parabolic rise
            else:
                mid_soffit = self.soffit_level
            
            data['mid_elevation'] = {
                'formation': mid_elev,
                'deck_top': mid_elev,
                'deck_bottom': mid_elev - self.deck_thickness,
                'girder_top': mid_elev - self.deck_thickness,
                'girder_bottom': mid_soffit,
                'soffit': mid_soffit
            }
    
    def generate_cross_section_at_chainage(self, 
                                            chainage: float,
                                            section_type: str = "typical") -> pd.DataFrame:
        """Generate cross-section at specified chainage"""
        
        data = []
        
        for girder_id, girder_info in self.girder_data.items():
            # Determine which elevation to use
            if section_type == "support":
                elev_data = girder_info['start_elevation']
                actual_chainage = girder_info['start_chainage']
            elif section_type == "midspan":
                elev_data = girder_info['mid_elevation']
                actual_chainage = chainage
            else:
                # Interpolate
                elev_data = self._interpolate_elevation(girder_id, chainage)
                actual_chainage = chainage
            
            row = {
                'Girder_ID': girder_id,
                'Chainage': actual_chainage,
                'Y_Offset': girder_info['y_offset'],
                'Formation_Level': elev_data['formation'],
                'Deck_Top': elev_data['deck_top'],
                'Deck_Bottom': elev_data['deck_bottom'],
                'Girder_Top': elev_data['girder_top'],
                'Girder_Bottom': elev_data['girder_bottom'],
                'Soffit': elev_data.get('soffit', elev_data['girder_bottom'])
            }
            
            if 'bearing_top' in elev_data:
                row['Bearing_Top'] = elev_data['bearing_top']
                row['Bearing_Bottom'] = elev_data['bearing_bottom']
            
            data.append(row)
        
        return pd.DataFrame(data)
    
    def _interpolate_elevation(self, girder_id: str, chainage: float) -> Dict:
        """Interpolate elevation at any chainage for a girder"""
        
        girder = self.girder_data[girder_id]
        
        # Linear interpolation between start and end
        ratio = (chainage - girder['start_chainage']) / girder['length']
        
        start = girder['start_elevation']
        end = girder['end_elevation']
        
        interpolated = {}
        for key in start.keys():
            interpolated[key] = start[key] + ratio * (end[key] - start[key])
        
        return interpolated
    
    def generate_end_diaphragm_section(self) -> pd.DataFrame:
        """Generate end diaphragm cross-section"""
        
        data = []
        
        # Get support elevations
        support_section = self.generate_cross_section_at_chainage(
            0, section_type="support"
        )
        
        for _, row in support_section.iterrows():
            diaphragm_row = {
                'Girder_ID': row['Girder_ID'],
                'Y_Offset': row['Y_Offset'],
                'Deck_Top': row['Deck_Top'],
                'Deck_Bottom': row['Deck_Bottom'],
                'Girder_Top': row['Girder_Top'],
                'Diaphragm_Bottom': row['Girder_Bottom'] - 0.5,  # Below bearing
                'Height': row['Girder_Top'] - (row['Girder_Bottom'] - 0.5),
                'Bearing_Top': row.get('Bearing_Top'),
                'Bearing_Bottom': row.get('Bearing_Bottom')
            }
            data.append(diaphragm_row)
        
        return pd.DataFrame(data)
    
    def calculate_bearing_reactions(self, 
                                     total_load: float = 1000) -> pd.DataFrame:
        """Calculate bearing reactions with skew effects"""
        
        # Simple distribution - equal load per girder
        load_per_girder = total_load / self.num_girders
        
        data = []
        
        for girder_id in self.girder_data.keys():
            # Vertical reaction
            P = load_per_girder
            
            # Horizontal components (if any)
            H_long = 0  # Along girder
            H_trans = 0  # Across girder
            
            # Skew components
            H_x = H_long * self.cos_skew - H_trans * self.sin_skew
            H_y = H_long * self.sin_skew + H_trans * self.cos_skew
            
            data.append({
                'Bearing_ID': f'B{girder_id[1:]}',
                'Girder': girder_id,
                'Vertical_Reaction_P': P,
                'Horizontal_Long': H_long,
                'Horizontal_Trans': H_trans,
                'H_Global_X': H_x,
                'H_Global_Y': H_y,
                'Resultant_H': math.sqrt(H_x**2 + H_y**2)
            })
        
        return pd.DataFrame(data)
    
    def generate_dxf_cross_section(self, 
                                      chainage: float,
                                      filename: str,
                                      section_type: str = "typical"):
        """Generate DXF file for cross-section"""
        
        section_data = self.generate_cross_section_at_chainage(
            chainage, section_type
        )
        
        lines = []
        lines.append("0\nSECTION\n2\nENTITIES\n")
        
        # Draw deck slab
        deck_points = []
        for _, row in section_data.iterrows():
            deck_points.append((row['Y_Offset'], row['Deck_Top']))
        for _, row in section_data.iloc[::-1].iterrows():
            deck_points.append((row['Y_Offset'], row['Deck_Bottom']))
        
        lines.append(self._create_lwpolyline(deck_points, layer="DECK"))
        
        # Draw girders
        for _, row in section_data.iterrows():
            girder_points = [
                (row['Y_Offset'] - self.web_thickness/2, row['Girder_Top']),
                (row['Y_Offset'] + self.web_thickness/2, row['Girder_Top']),
                (row['Y_Offset'] + self.web_thickness/2, row['Girder_Bottom']),
                (row['Y_Offset'] - self.web_thickness/2, row['Girder_Bottom']),
                (row['Y_Offset'] - self.web_thickness/2, row['Girder_Top'])
            ]
            lines.append(self._create_lwpolyline(girder_points, layer="GIRDER"))
        
        # Draw bearings (if support section)
        if section_type == "support":
            for _, row in section_data.iterrows():
                if 'Bearing_Top' in row and pd.notna(row['Bearing_Top']):
                    bearing_points = [
                        (row['Y_Offset'] - 0.3, row['Bearing_Bottom']),
                        (row['Y_Offset'] + 0.3, row['Bearing_Bottom']),
                        (row['Y_Offset'] + 0.3, row['Bearing_Top']),
                        (row['Y_Offset'] - 0.3, row['Bearing_Top']),
                        (row['Y_Offset'] - 0.3, row['Bearing_Bottom'])
                    ]
                    lines.append(self._create_lwpolyline(bearing_points, layer="BEARING"))
        
        # Draw soffit line
        soffit_points = []
        for _, row in section_data.iterrows():
            soffit_points.append((row['Y_Offset'], row['Soffit']))
        lines.append(self._create_lwpolyline(soffit_points, layer="SOFFIT"))
        
        lines.append("0\nENDSEC\n0\nEOF\n")
        
        with open(filename, 'w') as f:
            f.write('\n'.join(lines))
    
    def _create_lwpolyline(self, points: List[Tuple[float, float]], 
                           layer: str = "0") -> str:
        """Create DXF LWPOLYLINE"""
        dxf = f"0\nLWPOLYLINE\n8\n{layer}\n90\n{len(points)}\n70\n1\n"
        for x, y in points:
            dxf += f"10\n{x}\n20\n{y}\n"
        return dxf
    
    def generate_plan_view_dxf(self, filename: str):
        """Generate plan view DXF showing skewed layout"""
        
        lines = []
        lines.append("0\nSECTION\n2\nENTITIES\n")
        
        # Draw deck outline
        deck_corners = [
            (self.girder_data['G1']['start_chainage'], -self.deck_width/2),
            (self.girder_data['G4']['start_chainage'], self.deck_width/2),
            (self.girder_data['G4']['end_chainage'], self.deck_width/2),
            (self.girder_data['G1']['end_chainage'], -self.deck_width/2),
            (self.girder_data['G1']['start_chainage'], -self.deck_width/2)
        ]
        lines.append(self._create_lwpolyline(deck_corners, layer="DECK_PLAN"))
        
        # Draw girders
        for girder_id, data in self.girder_data.items():
            girder_line = [
                (data['start_chainage'], data['y_offset']),
                (data['end_chainage'], data['y_offset'])
            ]
            lines.append(self._create_lwpolyline(girder_line, layer="GIRDERS"))
        
        # Draw support lines (skewed)
        support_start = [
            (self.girder_data['G1']['start_chainage'], -self.deck_width/2),
            (self.girder_data['G1']['start_chainage'], self.deck_width/2)
        ]
        support_end = [
            (self.girder_data['G1']['end_chainage'], -self.deck_width/2),
            (self.girder_data['G1']['end_chainage'], self.deck_width/2)
        ]
        lines.append(self._create_lwpolyline(support_start, layer="SUPPORTS"))
        lines.append(self._create_lwpolyline(support_end, layer="SUPPORTS"))
        
        lines.append("0\nENDSEC\n0\nEOF\n")
        
        with open(filename, 'w') as f:
            f.write('\n'.join(lines))
    
    def generate_summary_report(self) -> str:
        """Generate summary report"""
        
        report = []
        report.append("=" * 70)
        report.append("SKEW CROSS-SECTION GENERATOR REPORT")
        report.append("=" * 70)
        
        report.append(f"\nBridge Parameters:")
        report.append(f"  Deck Width: {self.deck_width} m")
        report.append(f"  Span Length: {self.span_length} m")
        report.append(f"  Skew Angle: {self.skew_angle}°")
        report.append(f"  End Face Width: {self.end_face_width:.2f} m")
        
        report.append(f"\nGirder Layout:")
        report.append(f"  Number of Girders: {self.num_girders}")
        report.append(f"  Perpendicular Spacing: {self.girder_spacing} m")
        report.append(f"  Spacing along Deck: {self.girder_spacing/self.cos_skew:.3f} m")
        
        report.append(f"\nGirder Positions:")
        for girder_id, data in self.girder_data.items():
            report.append(f"  {girder_id}:")
            report.append(f"    Y-Offset: {data['y_offset']:+.3f} m")
            report.append(f"    Start Chainage: {data['start_chainage']:.3f} m")
            report.append(f"    End Chainage: {data['end_chainage']:.3f} m")
            report.append(f"    Length: {data['length']:.3f} m")
        
        report.append(f"\nElevations at Support:")
        for girder_id, data in self.girder_data.items():
            if data['start_elevation']:
                report.append(f"  {girder_id}:")
                report.append(f"    Formation: {data['start_elevation']['formation']:.3f} m")
                report.append(f"    Soffit: {data['start_elevation']['soffit']:.3f} m")
        
        report.append("\n" + "=" * 70)
        
        return '\n'.join(report)


# Example Usage
if __name__ == "__main__":
    # Create skew cross-section
    skew_xs = SkewCrossSection(
        deck_width=12.0,
        span_length=30.0,
        skew_angle=30.0,
        num_girders=4,
        girder_spacing=2.5,
        girder_depth=2.0,
        web_thickness=0.3,
        deck_thickness=0.25,
        cross_slope=2.5,
        formation_level=101.8,
        soffit_level=99.5,
        bearing_height=0.3
    )
    
    # Calculate elevations
    skew_xs.calculate_elevations(grade=2.0, parabolic_soffit=True, crown_height=0.3)
    
    # Generate cross-sections
    support_section = skew_xs.generate_cross_section_at_chainage(
        0, section_type="support"
    )
    print("Support Section:")
    print(support_section[['Girder_ID', 'Y_Offset', 'Formation_Level', 'Soffit']])
    
    midspan_section = skew_xs.generate_cross_section_at_chainage(
        15, section_type="midspan"
    )
    print("\nMid-span Section:")
    print(midspan_section[['Girder_ID', 'Y_Offset', 'Formation_Level', 'Soffit']])
    
    # Generate end diaphragm
    diaphragm = skew_xs.generate_end_diaphragm_section()
    print("\nEnd Diaphragm:")
    print(diaphragm)
    
    # Calculate bearing reactions
    reactions = skew_xs.calculate_bearing_reactions(total_load=5000)
    print("\nBearing Reactions:")
    print(reactions)
    
    # Print report
    print(skew_xs.generate_summary_report())
    
    # Generate DXF files
    skew_xs.generate_dxf_cross_section(
        0, "skew_support_section.dxf", section_type="support"
    )

        15, "skew_midspan_section.dxf", section_type="midspan"
    )
```

## SECTION H: EXCEL FORMULAS FOR SKEW CROSS-SECTION

### H.1 Girder Position Calculations

| Cell | Description | Formula |
|------|-------------|---------|
| B5 | Skew Angle | Input (e.g., 30) |
| B6 | Tan(Skew) | `=TAN(RADIANS(B5))` |
| B7 | Cos(Skew) | `=COS(RADIANS(B5))` |
| B10 | G1 Y-Offset | `=-(N-1)*S/2` |
| B11 | G2 Y-Offset | `=B10+S` |
| B12 | G3 Y-Offset | `=B11+S` |
| B13 | G4 Y-Offset | `=B12+S` |
| C10 | G1 X-Offset | `=B10*$B$6` |
| C11 | G2 X-Offset | `=B11*$B$6` |
| D10 | G1 Length | `=SPAN/$B$7` |

### H.2 Elevation Calculations

| Cell | Description | Formula |
|------|-------------|---------|
| E10 | G1 Formation | `=FL_CENTER - Y_OFFSET*CROSS_SLOPE/100` |
| F10 | G1 Deck Top | `=E10` |
| G10 | G1 Deck Bottom | `=F10-DECK_THICK` |
| H10 | G1 Girder Top | `=G10` |
| I10 | G1 Girder Bottom | `=H10-GIRDER_DEPTH` |
| J10 | G1 Soffit | `=I10` |

# SHEET 24: SKEW_FOUNDATION_GEOMETRY

## Purpose
Calculate geometric parameters for pile caps and well foundations in skew bridges, including pile group layouts, well orientation, and skew corrections.
