## SECTION E: PYTHON CROSS-SECTION GENERATOR

```python
"""
Typical Cross-Section Generator
Generates detailed T-beam, I-girder, and box girder cross-sections
"""

import numpy as np
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
from matplotlib.patches import Polygon, Rectangle, Circle, FancyBboxPatch, Arc
from matplotlib.collections import PatchCollection
import matplotlib.patches as mpatches
from matplotlib.lines import Line2D
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import List, Dict, Tuple, Optional
import math


@dataclass
class TBeamSection:
    """T-beam cross-section parameters"""
    
    # Top flange (deck slab contribution)
    bf: float = 2400          # mm, top flange width
    tf: float = 250         # mm, top flange thickness
    
    # Web
    hw: float = 1600        # mm, web height
    tw: float = 300         # mm, web thickness
    
    # Bottom flange
    bf2: float = 800        # mm, bottom flange width
    tf2: float = 250        # mm, bottom flange thickness
    
    # Haunch
    wh: float = 300         # mm, haunch width
    th: float = 150         # mm, haunch thickness
    
    # Material
    concrete_grade: str = "M40"
    steel_grade: str = "Fe500"
    
    def __post_init__(self):
        self.total_depth = self.tf + self.hw + self.tf2
        self.web_top_width = self.tw + 2 * self.wh
        self.web_bottom_width = self.tw
    
    def get_coordinates(self) -> Dict[str, List[Tuple[float, float]]]:
        """Get polygon coordinates for drawing"""
        
        # Origin at bottom center
        y_bottom = 0
        y_top_flange_bottom = self.tf2
        y_web_bottom = self.tf2
        y_web_top = self.tf2 + self.hw
        y_top = self.total_depth
        
        x_center = 0
        
        # Bottom flange
        bottom_flange = [
            (x_center - self.bf2/2, y_bottom),
            (x_center + self.bf2/2, y_bottom),
            (x_center + self.bf2/2, y_bottom + self.tf2),
            (x_center - self.bf2/2, y_bottom + self.tf2)
        ]
        
        # Web (with haunches)
        web = [
            (x_center - self.tw/2 - self.wh, y_web_bottom),
            (x_center + self.tw/2 + self.wh, y_web_bottom),
            (x_center + self.tw/2, y_web_bottom + self.th),
            (x_center + self.tw/2, y_web_top),
            (x_center - self.tw/2, y_web_top),
            (x_center - self.tw/2, y_web_bottom + self.th)
        ]
        
        # Top flange
        top_flange = [
            (x_center - self.bf/2, y_web_top),
            (x_center + self.bf/2, y_web_top),
            (x_center + self.bf/2, y_top),
            (x_center - self.bf/2, y_top)
        ]
        
        return {
            'bottom_flange': bottom_flange,
            'web': web,
            'top_flange': top_flange
        }
    
    def calculate_properties(self) -> Dict:
        """Calculate section properties"""
        
        # Areas
        A_bf = self.bf2 * self.tf2
        A_web = self.tw * self.hw + 2 * (self.wh * self.th / 2)  # Simplified
        A_tf = self.bf * self.tf
        
        A_total = A_bf + A_web + A_tf
        
        # Centroids (from bottom)
        y_bf = self.tf2 / 2
        y_web = self.tf2 + self.hw / 2
        y_tf = self.tf2 + self.hw + self.tf / 2
        
        # Overall centroid
        y_bar = (A_bf * y_bf + A_web * y_web + A_tf * y_tf) / A_total
        
        # Moment of inertia (simplified - parallel axis theorem)
        I_bf = (self.bf2 * self.tf2**3) / 12 + A_bf * (y_bar - y_bf)**2
        I_web = (self.tw * self.hw**3) / 12 + A_web * (y_bar - y_web)**2
        I_tf = (self.bf * self.tf**3) / 12 + A_tf * (y_bar - y_tf)**2
        
        I_total = I_bf + I_web + I_tf
        
        # Section moduli
        Z_top = I_total / (self.total_depth - y_bar)
        Z_bottom = I_total / y_bar
        
        # Self-weight
        self_weight = A_total * 25e-6  # kN/m
        
        return {
            'area_total': A_total,
            'centroid_from_bottom': y_bar,
            'moment_of_inertia': I_total,
            'section_modulus_top': Z_top,
            'section_modulus_bottom': Z_bottom,
            'self_weight': self_weight
        }


@dataclass
class IGirderSection:
    """I-girder cross-section parameters"""
    
    # Top flange
    bf1: float = 600        # mm
    tf1: float = 200        # mm
    
    # Web
    hw: float = 1600        # mm
    tw: float = 200         # mm
    
    # Bottom flange
    bf2: float = 800        # mm
    tf2: float = 250        # mm
    
    # Chamfers
    c1: float = 50          # mm, top chamfer
    c2: float = 75          # mm, bottom chamfer
    
    concrete_grade: str = "M50"
    steel_grade: str = "Fe500"
    
    def __post_init__(self):
        self.total_depth = self.tf1 + self.hw + self.tf2
    
    def get_coordinates(self) -> Dict[str, List[Tuple[float, float]]]:
        """Get polygon coordinates"""
        
        y_bottom = 0
        y_bf_top = self.tf2
        y_web_bottom = self.tf2 + self.c2
        y_web_top = self.tf2 + self.hw - self.c1
        y_tf_bottom = self.tf2 + self.hw
        y_top = self.total_depth
        
        x_center = 0
        
        # Bottom flange with chamfer
        bottom_flange = [
            (x_center - self.bf2/2, y_bottom),
            (x_center + self.bf2/2, y_bottom),
            (x_center + self.bf2/2, y_bf_top),
            (x_center + self.tw/2 + self.c2, y_bf_top),
            (x_center + self.tw/2, y_web_bottom),
            (x_center - self.tw/2, y_web_bottom),
            (x_center - self.tw/2 - self.c2, y_bf_top),
            (x_center - self.bf2/2, y_bf_top)
        ]
        
        # Web
        web = [
            (x_center - self.tw/2, y_web_bottom),
            (x_center + self.tw/2, y_web_bottom),
            (x_center + self.tw/2, y_web_top),
            (x_center - self.tw/2, y_web_top)
        ]
        
        # Top flange with chamfer
        top_flange = [
            (x_center - self.tw/2 - self.c1, y_web_top),
            (x_center - self.tw/2, y_tf_bottom),
            (x_center + self.tw/2, y_tf_bottom),
            (x_center + self.tw/2 + self.c1, y_web_top),
            (x_center + self.bf1/2, y_tf_bottom),
            (x_center + self.bf1/2, y_top),
            (x_center - self.bf1/2, y_top),
            (x_center - self.bf1/2, y_tf_bottom)
        ]
        
        return {
            'bottom_flange': bottom_flange,
            'web': web,
            'top_flange': top_flange
        }


class CrossSectionGenerator:
    """Generator for typical bridge cross-sections"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.colors = {
            'concrete': '#D3D3D3',
            'concrete_edge': '#696969',
            'rebar': '#8B4513',
            'stirrup': '#A0522D',
            'tendon': '#4169E1',
            'dimension': '#2F4F4F',
            'text': '#000000',
            'hatch': '#AAAAAA'
        }
        
        self.hatch_patterns = {
            'concrete': '//',
            'rebar': '///'
        }
    
    def draw_t_beam(self, ax=None, show_reinforcement=True,
                   show_dimensions=True, show_properties=True):
        """Draw T-beam cross-section"""
        
        if ax is None:
            fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(14, 10))
        
        # Create T-beam
        t_beam = TBeamSection()
        coords = t_beam.get_coordinates()
        props = t_beam.calculate_properties()
        
        # Draw concrete sections with hatching
        for part_name, part_coords in coords.items():
            polygon = Polygon(part_coords, closed=True,
                            facecolor=self.colors['concrete'],
                            edgecolor=self.colors['concrete_edge'],
                            linewidth=2,
                            hatch=self.hatch_patterns['concrete'])
            ax.add_patch(polygon)
        
        # Draw reinforcement if requested
        if show_reinforcement:
            self._draw_t_beam_reinforcement(ax, t_beam)
        
        # Draw dimensions
        if show_dimensions:
            self._draw_t_beam_dimensions(ax, t_beam)
        
        # Add properties text
        if show_properties:
            self._add_properties_text(ax, props, t_beam)
        
        # Styling
        ax.set_aspect('equal')
        ax.set_xlabel('Width (mm)', fontsize=11)
        ax.set_ylabel('Height (mm)', fontsize=11)
        ax.set_title('T-Beam Cross-Section (Typical Interior Girder)', 
                    fontsize=13, fontweight='bold')
        
        # Set limits
        margin = 500
        ax.set_xlim(-t_beam.bf/2 - margin, t_beam.bf/2 + margin)
        ax.set_ylim(-200, t_beam.total_depth + 400)
        
        ax.grid(True, alpha=0.3, linestyle='--')
        
        return ax
    
    def _draw_t_beam_reinforcement(self, ax, t_beam):
        """Draw reinforcement details"""
        
        # Bottom bars (main reinforcement)
        num_bottom = 8
        dia_bottom = 25
        y_bottom_bars = 50  # Cover from bottom
        
        x_positions = np.linspace(-t_beam.bf2/2 + 50, 
                                  t_beam.bf2/2 - 50, 
                                  num_bottom)
        
        for x in x_positions:
            circle = Circle((x, y_bottom_bars), dia_bottom/2,
                          facecolor=self.colors['rebar'],
                          edgecolor='black', linewidth=0.5)
            ax.add_patch(circle)
        
        # Top bars
        num_top = 16
        dia_top = 16
        y_top_bars = t_beam.total_depth - 50
        
        x_positions_top = np.linspace(-t_beam.bf/2 + 75,
                                      t_beam.bf/2 - 75,
                                      num_top)
        
        for x in x_positions_top:
            circle = Circle((x, y_top_bars), dia_top/2,
                          facecolor=self.colors['rebar'],
                          edgecolor='black', linewidth=0.5)
            ax.add_patch(circle)
        
        # Stirrups (schematic)
        y_stirrup_bottom = t_beam.tf2 + 50
        y_stirrup_top = t_beam.total_depth - t_beam.tf - 50
        
        # Draw a few stirrups
        for x in [-100, 0, 100]:
            # Vertical legs
            ax.plot([x - t_beam.tw/2 - 10, x - t_beam.tw/2 - 10],
                   [y_stirrup_bottom, y_stirrup_top],
                   color=self.colors['stirrup'], linewidth=2)
            ax.plot([x + t_beam.tw/2 + 10, x + t_beam.tw/2 + 10],
                   [y_stirrup_bottom, y_stirrup_top],
                   color=self.colors['stirrup'], linewidth=2)
            
            # Top and bottom hooks
            ax.plot([x - t_beam.tw/2 - 10, x + t_beam.tw/2 + 10],
                   [y_stirrup_top, y_stirrup_top],
                   color=self.colors['stirrup'], linewidth=2)
            ax.plot([x - t_beam.tw/2 - 10, x + t_beam.tw/2 + 10],
                   [y_stirrup_bottom, y_stirrup_bottom],
                   color=self.colors['stirrup'], linewidth=2)
        
        # Legend
        rebar_patch = mpatches.Patch(color=self.colors['rebar'], 
                                    label='Main Reinforcement')
        stirrup_patch = mpatches.Patch(color=self.colors['stirrup'],
                                      label='Stirrups (10φ @ 150)')
        ax.legend(handles=[rebar_patch, stirrup_patch], 
                 loc='upper right', fontsize=9)
    
    def _draw_t_beam_dimensions(self, ax, t_beam):
        """Draw dimension lines"""
        
        # Overall depth
        self._draw_dimension_line(ax, 
                                 t_beam.bf/2 + 200, 0,
                                 t_beam.bf/2 + 200, t_beam.total_depth,
                                 f'D = {t_beam.total_depth} mm',
                                 vertical=True)
        
        # Top flange width
        self._draw_dimension_line(ax,
                                 -t_beam.bf/2, t_beam.total_depth + 100,
                                 t_beam.bf/2, t_beam.total_depth + 100,
                                 f'bf = {t_beam.bf} mm')
        
        # Top flange thickness
        self._draw_dimension_line(ax,
                                 t_beam.bf/2 + 100, t_beam.total_depth - t_beam.tf,
                                 t_beam.bf/2 + 100, t_beam.total_depth,
                                 f'tf = {t_beam.tf} mm',
                                 vertical=True)
        
        # Web thickness
        self._draw_dimension_line(ax,
                                 0, t_beam.tf2 + 100,
                                 t_beam.tw/2, t_beam.tf2 + 100,
                                 f'tw/2')
        
        # Bottom flange width
        self._draw_dimension_line(ax,
                                 -t_beam.bf2/2, -100,
                                 t_beam.bf2/2, -100,
                                 f'bf2 = {t_beam.bf2} mm')
        
        # Web height
        self._draw_dimension_line(ax,
                                 -t_beam.bf/2 - 200, t_beam.tf2,
                                 -t_beam.bf/2 - 200, t_beam.tf2 + t_beam.hw,
                                 f'hw = {t_beam.hw} mm',
                                 vertical=True)
    
    def _draw_dimension_line(self, ax, x1, y1, x2, y2, text, 
                            vertical=False, offset=0):
        """Draw dimension line with text"""
        
        # Dimension line
        ax.plot([x1, x2], [y1, y2], 'k-', linewidth=0.8)
        
        # Extension lines
        if vertical:
            ax.plot([x1 - 20, x1], [y1, y1], 'k-', linewidth=0.8)
            ax.plot([x2 - 20, x2], [y2, y2], 'k-', linewidth=0.8)
        else:
            ax.plot([x1, x1], [y1 - 20, y1], 'k-', linewidth=0.8)
            ax.plot([x2, x2], [y2 - 20, y2], 'k-', linewidth=0.8)
        
        # Text
        mid_x = (x1 + x2) / 2
        mid_y = (y1 + y2) / 2
        
        if vertical:
            ax.text(mid_x + 30, mid_y, text, 
                   rotation=90, va='center', fontsize=8)
        else:
            ax.text(mid_x, mid_y + 30, text,
                   ha='center', fontsize=8)
    
    def _add_properties_text(self, ax, props, section):
        """Add section properties as text"""
        
        text = (f"Section Properties:\n"
                f"A = {props['area_total']/1e6:.3f} m²\n"
                f"Ixx = {props['moment_of_inertia']/1e12:.3f} m⁴\n"
                f"Ztop = {props['section_modulus_top']/1e6:.3f} m³\n"
                f"Zbottom = {props['section_modulus_bottom']/1e6:.3f} m³\n"
                f"Self-weight = {props['self_weight']:.2f} kN/m\n"
                f"\nMaterial:\n"
                f"Concrete: {section.concrete_grade}\n"
                f"Steel: {section.steel_grade}")
        
        ax.text(0.02, 0.98, text, transform=ax.transAxes,
               fontsize=9, verticalalignment='top',
               fontfamily='monospace',
               bbox=dict(boxstyle='round', facecolor='wheat',
                        alpha=0.8, pad=0.5))
    
    def draw_i_girder(self, ax=None, show_prestressing=True):
        """Draw I-girder cross-section"""
        
        if ax is None:
            fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(12, 10))
        
        # Create I-girder
        i_girder = IGirderSection()
        coords = i_girder.get_coordinates()
        
        # Draw sections
        for part_name, part_coords in coords.items():
            polygon = Polygon(part_coords, closed=True,
                            facecolor=self.colors['concrete'],
                            edgecolor=self.colors['concrete_edge'],
                            linewidth=2,
                            hatch=self.hatch_patterns['concrete'])
            ax.add_patch(polygon)
        
        # Draw prestressing tendons if requested
        if show_prestressing:
            self._draw_prestressing_tendons(ax, i_girder)
        
        # Draw dimensions
        self._draw_i_girder_dimensions(ax, i_girder)
        
        # Styling
        ax.set_aspect('equal')
        ax.set_xlabel('Width (mm)', fontsize=11)
        ax.set_ylabel('Height (mm)', fontsize=11)
        ax.set_title('I-Girder Cross-Section (Prestressed Concrete)',
                    fontsize=13, fontweight='bold')
        
        margin = 300
        ax.set_xlim(-i_girder.bf2/2 - margin, i_girder.bf2/2 + margin)
        ax.set_ylim(-200, i_girder.total_depth + 400)
        
        ax.grid(True, alpha=0.3)
        
        return ax
    
    def _draw_prestressing_tendons(self, ax, i_girder):
        """Draw prestressing tendon profile"""
        
        # Straight tendons at bottom
        num_tendons = 12
        dia_tendon = 15.2  # 15.2 mm strand
        
        y_tendon = 100  # 100mm from bottom
        x_positions = np.linspace(-i_girder.bf2/2 + 100,
                                  i_girder.bf2/2 - 100,
                                  num_tendons)
        
        for x in x_positions:
            circle = Circle((x, y_tendon), dia_tendon/2,
                          facecolor=self.colors['tendon'],
                          edgecolor='navy', linewidth=1)
            ax.add_patch(circle)
        
        # Legend
        tendon_patch = mpatches.Patch(color=self.colors['tendon'],
                                     label='Prestressing Tendons (12T15)')
        ax.legend(handles=[tendon_patch], loc='upper right', fontsize=9)
    
    def _draw_i_girder_dimensions(self, ax, i_girder):
        """Draw I-girder dimensions"""
        
        # Similar to T-beam dimensions
        # Overall depth
        self._draw_dimension_line(ax,
                                 i_girder.bf2/2 + 150, 0,
                                 i_girder.bf2/2 + 150, i_girder.total_depth,
                                 f'D = {i_girder.total_depth} mm',
                                 vertical=True)
        
        # Top flange
        self._draw_dimension_line(ax,
                                 -i_girder.bf1/2, i_girder.total_depth + 80,
                                 i_girder.bf1/2, i_girder.total_depth + 80,
                                 f'bf1 = {i_girder.bf1} mm')
        
        # Bottom flange
        self._draw_dimension_line(ax,
                                 -i_girder.bf2/2, -80,
                                 i_girder.bf2/2, -80,
                                 f'bf2 = {i_girder.bf2} mm')
    
    def generate_dxf(self, section_type='t_beam', filename='section.dxf'):
        """Generate DXF file for cross-section"""
        
        lines = []
        lines.append("0\nSECTION\n2\nENTITIES\n")
        
        if section_type == 't_beam':
            section = TBeamSection()
        elif section_type == 'i_girder':
            section = IGirderSection()
        else:
            raise ValueError(f"Unknown section type: {section_type}")
        
        coords = section.get_coordinates()
        
        # Draw each part
        for part_name, part_coords in coords.items():
            lines.append(self._create_lwpolyline(part_coords, layer=part_name.upper()))
        
        lines.append("0\nENDSEC\n0\nEOF\n")
        
        with open(filename, 'w') as f:
            f.write('\n'.join(lines))
        
        print(f"DXF saved to: {filename}")
    
    def _create_lwpolyline(self, points, layer="0"):
        """Create DXF LWPOLYLINE"""
        dxf = f"0\nLWPOLYLINE\n8\n{layer}\n90\n{len(points)}\n70\n1\n"
        for x, y in points:
            dxf += f"10\n{x}\n20\n{y}\n"
        return dxf
    
    def create_comparison_plot(self):
        """Create comparison of different section types"""
        
        fig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 3, figsize=(18, 8))
        
        # T-beam
        self.draw_t_beam(ax=axes[0], show_reinforcement=True)
        axes[0].set_title('T-Beam (RC)', fontsize=12, fontweight='bold')
        
        # I-girder
        self.draw_i_girder(ax=axes[1], show_prestressing=True)
        axes[1].set_title('I-Girder (PSC)', fontsize=12, fontweight='bold')
        
        # Properties comparison
        axes[2].axis('off')
        
        # Calculate properties
        t_beam = TBeamSection()
        i_girder = IGirderSection()
        
        t_props = t_beam.calculate_properties()
        
        comparison_text = (
            "SECTION COMPARISON\n"
            "==================\n\n"
            f"T-Beam (RC):\n"
            f"  Depth: {t_beam.total_depth} mm\n"
            f"  Area: {t_props['area_total']/1e6:.3f} m²\n"
            f"  Ixx: {t_props['moment_of_inertia']/1e12:.3f} m⁴\n"
            f"  Self-weight: {t_props['self_weight']:.2f} kN/m\n\n"
            f"I-Girder (PSC):\n"
            f"  Depth: {i_girder.total_depth} mm\n"
            f"  Area: {(i_girder.bf1*i_girder.tf1 + i_girder.tw*i_girder.hw + i_girder.bf2*i_girder.tf2)/1e6:.3f} m²\n"
            f"  Material: M50 Concrete\n"
            f"  Prestressing: 12T15 strands\n"
        )
        
        axes[2].text(0.1, 0.9, comparison_text, transform=axes[2].transAxes,
                    fontsize=10, verticalalignment='top',
                    fontfamily='monospace',
                    bbox=dict(boxstyle='round', facecolor='lightblue',
                             alpha=0.5, pad=1))
        
        plt.tight_layout()
        
        return fig


# Example usage
if __name__ == "__main__":
    generator = CrossSectionGenerator()
    
    # Generate T-beam
    print("Generating T-beam cross-section...")
    fig1, ax1 = plt.subplots(figsize=(14, 10))
    generator.draw_t_beam(ax=ax1, show_reinforcement=True, 
                         show_dimensions=True, show_properties=True)
    plt.savefig('t_beam_section.png', dpi=150, bbox_inches='tight')
    print("Saved: t_beam_section.png")
    
    # Generate I-girder
    print("\nGenerating I-girder cross-section...")
    fig2, ax2 = plt.subplots(figsize=(12, 10))
    generator.draw_i_girder(ax=ax2, show_prestressing=True)
    plt.savefig('i_girder_section.png', dpi=150, bbox_inches='tight')
    print("Saved: i_girder_section.png")
    
    # Comparison plot
    print("\nGenerating comparison plot...")
    fig3 = generator.create_comparison_plot()
    plt.savefig('section_comparison.png', dpi=150, bbox_inches='tight')
    print("Saved: section_comparison.png")
    
    # Generate DXF
    print("\nGenerating DXF files...")
    generator.generate_dxf('t_beam', 't_beam_section.dxf')
    generator.generate_dxf('i_girder', 'i_girder_section.dxf')
    
    # Show plots
    plt.show()
    
    print("\nAll sections generated successfully!")
```

| B3 | Section Type | T-Beam | - | Dropdown |
| B4 | Top Flange Width (bf) | 2400 | mm | Input |
| B5 | Top Flange Thickness (tf) | 250 | mm | Input |
| B6 | Web Height (hw) | 1600 | mm | Input |
| B7 | Web Thickness (tw) | 300 | mm | Input |
| B8 | Bottom Flange Width (bf2) | 800 | mm | Input |
| B9 | Bottom Flange Thickness (tf2) | 250 | mm | Input |
| B10 | **Total Depth (D)** | 2100 | mm | `=B5+B6+B9` |
| **B. PROPERTIES** |
| B13 | Area | 1,165,000 | mm² | `=B4*B5+B7*B6+B8*B9` |
| B14 | Centroid (from bottom) | 1,234 | mm | Calculated |
| B15 | Moment of Inertia (Ixx) | 3.85E+11 | mm⁴ | Calculated |
| B16 | Section Modulus (Ztop) | 3.12E+08 | mm³ | `=B15/(B10-B14)` |
| B17 | Section Modulus (Zbottom) | 3.12E+08 | mm³ | `=B15/B14` |
| B18 | Self-weight | 29.1 | kN/m | `=B13*25/10^6` |

# FOUNDATION DETAIL GENERATOR MODULE

Complete implementation for pile layouts, well foundations, and open foundations.

## File 1: `foundation_generator.py`

```python
"""
Foundation Detail Generator Module
Generates pile layouts, well foundations, and open foundation details
Integrates with Bridge GAD System
"""

import math
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import List, Tuple, Dict, Optional, Union
from enum import Enum
import ezdxf
from ezdxf.enums import TextEntityAlignment


class FoundationType(Enum):
    PILE = "PILE"
    WELL = "WELL"
    OPEN = "OPEN"
    RAFT = "RAFT"


class PileType(Enum):
    BORED_CAST_IN_SITU = "BORED_CAST_IN_SITU"
    DRIVEN = "DRIVEN"
    PRECAST = "PRECAST"


class PileCapShape(Enum):
    RECTANGULAR = "RECTANGULAR"
    OCTAGONAL = "OCTAGONAL"
    CIRCULAR = "CIRCULAR"


class WellShape(Enum):
    CIRCULAR_SINGLE = "CIRCULAR_SINGLE"
    CIRCULAR_DOUBLE = "CIRCULAR_DOUBLE"
    DUMB_BELL = "DUMB_BELL"


@dataclass
class PileDimensions:
    """Pile dimensions and reinforcement"""
    diameter: float = 1.2  # m
    length: float = 15.0  # m
    pile_type: PileType = PileType.BORED_CAST_IN_SITU
    
    # Reinforcement
    main_bar_dia: int = 20  # mm
    num_main_bars: int = 12
    spiral_dia: int = 10  # mm
    spiral_spacing: float = 0.15  # m
    
    @property
    def radius(self) -> float:
        return self.diameter / 2
    
    @property
    def concrete_volume(self) -> float:
        return math.pi * self.radius ** 2 * self.length
    
    @property
    def steel_weight(self) -> float:
        """Approximate steel weight in kg"""
        # Main bars
        bar_area = math.pi * (self.main_bar_dia ** 2) / 4
        main_steel = self.num_main_bars * self.length * bar_area * 7.85 / 1000
        
        # Spiral
        spiral_length = (self.length / self.spiral_spacing) * math.pi * (self.diameter - 0.1)
        spiral_steel = spiral_length * math.pi * (self.spiral_dia ** 2) / 4 * 7.85 / 1000
        
        return main_steel + spiral_steel


@dataclass
class PileGroupConfiguration:
    """Pile group layout configuration"""
    num_longitudinal: int = 4  # Along bridge
    num_transverse: int = 3   # Across bridge
    spacing_long: float = 2.5  # m
    spacing_trans: float = 2.5  # m
    cap_shape: PileCapShape = PileCapShape.RECTANGULAR
    cap_thickness: float = 2.0  # m
    cap_projection: float = 0.3  # m beyond outer piles
    
    @property
    def total_piles(self) -> int:
        return self.num_longitudinal * self.num_transverse
    
    @property
    def cap_length(self) -> float:
        return (self.num_longitudinal - 1) * self.spacing_long + 2 * self.cap_projection
    
    @property
    def cap_width(self) -> float:
        return (self.num_transverse - 1) * self.spacing_trans + 2 * self.cap_projection
    
    def get_pile_positions(self) -> List[Tuple[float, float]]:
        """Get pile positions relative to cap center"""
        positions = []
        start_x = -((self.num_longitudinal - 1) * self.spacing_long) / 2
        start_y = -((self.num_transverse - 1) * self.spacing_trans) / 2
        
        for i in range(self.num_longitudinal):
            for j in range(self.num_transverse):
                x = start_x + i * self.spacing_long
                y = start_y + j * self.spacing_trans
                positions.append((x, y))
        return positions


@dataclass
class WellDimensions:
    """Well foundation dimensions"""
    shape: WellShape = WellShape.CIRCULAR_SINGLE
    external_dia: float = 6.0  # m
    steining_thickness: float = 0.6  # m
    curb_height: float = 2.0  # m
    sinking_depth: float = 12.0  # m
    bottom_plug_thickness: float = 2.0  # m
    well_cap_thickness: float = 2.0  # m
    
    @property
    def internal_dia(self) -> float:
        return self.external_dia - 2 * self.steining_thickness
    
    @property
    def curb_dia(self) -> float:
        return self.external_dia + 0.4  # 200mm projection each side
    
    @property
    def steining_volume(self) -> float:
        outer_r = self.external_dia / 2
        inner_r = self.internal_dia / 2
        return math.pi * (outer_r**2 - inner_r**2) * self.sinking_depth


@dataclass
class FoundationModel:
    """Complete foundation model"""
    foundation_type: FoundationType
    support_type: str  # "PIER" or "ABUTMENT"
    support_number: int
    
    # Support dimensions
    support_width: float = 2.0
    support_length: float = 8.0
    
    # Levels
    ground_level: float = 175.0
    cutoff_level: float = 172.0
    scour_level: float = 170.0
    
    # Components
    pile_config: Optional[PileGroupConfiguration] = None
    pile_dims: Optional[PileDimensions] = None
    well_dims: Optional[WellDimensions] = None
    
    def __post_init__(self):
        if self.foundation_type == FoundationType.PILE:
            if self.pile_config is None:
                self.pile_config = PileGroupConfiguration()
            if self.pile_dims is None:
                self.pile_dims = PileDimensions()
        elif self.foundation_type == FoundationType.WELL:
            if self.well_dims is None:
                self.well_dims = WellDimensions()


class FoundationDetailDrawing:
    """Generate foundation detail drawings"""
    
    LAYERS = {
        'PILE': {'color': 4, 'linetype': 'DASHED'},
        'PILE_CAP': {'color': 1, 'linetype': 'CONTINUOUS'},
        'WELL': {'color': 5, 'linetype': 'CONTINUOUS'},
        'REINFORCEMENT': {'color': 2, 'linetype': 'CONTINUOUS'},
        'DIMENSION': {'color': 7, 'linetype': 'CONTINUOUS'},
        'TEXT': {'color': 7, 'linetype': 'CONTINUOUS'},
        'CENTERLINE': {'color': 6, 'linetype': 'CENTER'},
        'SECTION': {'color': 3, 'linetype': 'PHANTOM'},
        'HATCH': {'color': 253, 'linetype': 'CONTINUOUS'}
    }
    
    def __init__(self, model: FoundationModel, scale: float = 10.0):
        self.model = model
        self.scale = scale
        
        self.doc = ezdxf.new('R2018', setup=True)
        self.msp = self.doc.modelspace()
        self._setup_layers()
        
    def _setup_layers(self):
        """Create drawing layers"""
        for name, attrs in self.LAYERS.items():
            self.doc.layers.add(
                name=name,
                color=attrs['color'],
                linetype=attrs['linetype']
            )
    
    def _to_dxf(self, x: float, y: float) -> Tuple[float, float]:
        """Scale coordinates"""
        return (x * self.scale, y * self.scale)
    
    def generate_pile_layout_plan(self) -> str:
        """Generate pile layout plan view"""
        if self.model.foundation_type != FoundationType.PILE:
            return ""
        
        pc = self.model.pile_config
        pd = self.model.pile_dims
        
        # Draw pile cap outline
        cap_outline = [
            self._to_dxf(-pc.cap_length/2, -pc.cap_width/2),
            self._to_dxf(pc.cap_length/2, -pc.cap_width/2),
            self._to_dxf(pc.cap_length/2, pc.cap_width/2),
            self._to_dxf(-pc.cap_length/2, pc.cap_width/2),
            self._to_dxf(-pc.cap_length/2, -pc.cap_width/2)
        ]
        
        self.msp.add_lwpolyline(
            points=cap_outline,
            close=True,
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'PILE_CAP'}
        )
        
        # Hatch pile cap
        hatch = self.msp.add_hatch(color=1, dxfattribs={'layer': 'PILE_CAP'})
        hatch.paths.add_polyline_path(cap_outline, is_closed=True)
        hatch.set_pattern_fill('AR-CONC', scale=0.3)
        
        # Draw piles
        positions = pc.get_pile_positions()
        for i, (x, y) in enumerate(positions, 1):
            # Pile circle
            center = self._to_dxf(x, y)
            radius = pd.radius * self.scale
            
            self.msp.add_circle(
                center=center,
                radius=radius,
                dxfattribs={'layer': 'PILE'}
            )
            
            # Pile number
            self.msp.add_text(
                str(i),
                height=2.0,
                dxfattribs={'layer': 'TEXT'}
            ).set_placement(center, align=TextEntityAlignment.MIDDLE_CENTER)
            
            # Dimension lines for pile spacing
            if i < len(positions):
                next_pos = positions[i] if i < len(positions) else None
        
        # Draw centerlines
        self.msp.add_line(
            start=self._to_dxf(0, -pc.cap_width/2 - 1),
            end=self._to_dxf(0, pc.cap_width/2 + 1),
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'CENTERLINE'}
        )
        self.msp.add_line(
            start=self._to_dxf(-pc.cap_length/2 - 1, 0),
            end=self._to_dxf(pc.cap_length/2 + 1, 0),
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'CENTERLINE'}
        )
        
        # Dimensions
        self._draw_pile_dimensions(pc)
        
        # Title
        self._draw_title("PILE FOUNDATION LAYOUT PLAN")
        
        return "pile_layout_plan.dxf"
    
    def _draw_pile_dimensions(self, pc: PileGroupConfiguration):
        """Draw dimension lines for pile layout"""
        # Overall cap dimensions
        offset = pc.cap_width/2 + 3
        
        # Length dimension
        self.msp.add_line(
            start=self._to_dxf(-pc.cap_length/2, -offset),
            end=self._to_dxf(pc.cap_length/2, -offset),
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'DIMENSION'}
        )
        
        self.msp.add_text(
            f"{pc.cap_length:.2f}",
            height=2.0,
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'DIMENSION'}
        ).set_placement(
            self._to_dxf(0, -offset - 1),
            align=TextEntityAlignment.MIDDLE_CENTER
        )
        
        # Width dimension
        self.msp.add_line(
            start=self._to_dxf(pc.cap_length/2 + 3, -pc.cap_width/2),
            end=self._to_dxf(pc.cap_length/2 + 3, pc.cap_width/2),
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'DIMENSION'}
        )
        
        # Pile spacing dimensions
        for i in range(pc.num_longitudinal - 1):
            x1 = -((pc.num_longitudinal - 1) * pc.spacing_long) / 2 + i * pc.spacing_long
            x2 = x1 + pc.spacing_long
            
            y_offset = pc.cap_width/2 + 1.5
            
            self.msp.add_line(
                start=self._to_dxf(x1, y_offset),
                end=self._to_dxf(x2, y_offset),
                dxfattribs={'layer': 'DIMENSION'}
            )
            
            self.msp.add_text(
                f"{pc.spacing_long:.2f}",
                height=1.5,
                dxfattribs={'layer': 'DIMENSION'}
            ).set_placement(
                self._to_dxf((x1+x2)/2, y_offset + 0.8),
                align=TextEntityAlignment.MIDDLE_CENTER
            )
    
    def generate_pile_section(self) -> str:
        """Generate pile foundation section view"""
        if self.model.foundation_type != FoundationType.PILE:
            return ""
        
        pc = self.model.pile_config
        pd = self.model.pile_dims
        
        # Ground level
        gl_y = self.model.ground_level - self.model.cutoff_level
        
        # Draw ground line
        self.msp.add_line(
            start=self._to_dxf(-5, gl_y),
            end=self._to_dxf(5, gl_y),
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'GROUND'}
        )
        
        # Draw pile cap
        cap_bottom = 0
        cap_top = pc.cap_thickness
        
        cap_points = [
            self._to_dxf(-pc.cap_length/2, cap_bottom),
            self._to_dxf(pc.cap_length/2, cap_bottom),
            self._to_dxf(pc.cap_length/2, cap_top),
            self._to_dxf(-pc.cap_length/2, cap_top),
            self._to_dxf(-pc.cap_length/2, cap_bottom)
        ]
        
        self.msp.add_lwpolyline(
            points=cap_points,
            close=True,
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'PILE_CAP'}
        )
        
        # Hatch pile cap
        hatch = self.msp.add_hatch(color=1, dxfattribs={'layer': 'PILE_CAP'})
        hatch.paths.add_polyline_path(cap_points, is_closed=True)
        hatch.set_pattern_fill('AR-CONC', scale=0.3)
        
        # Draw piles (show 3 piles in section)
        pile_x_positions = [0]  # Center pile
        
        for x in pile_x_positions:
            # Pile shaft
            pile_top = cap_bottom
            pile_bottom = -pd.length
            
            pile_points = [
                self._to_dxf(x - pd.radius, pile_bottom),
                self._to_dxf(x + pd.radius, pile_bottom),
                self._to_dxf(x + pd.radius, pile_top),
                self._to_dxf(x - pd.radius, pile_top),
                self._to_dxf(x - pd.radius, pile_bottom)
            ]
            
            self.msp.add_lwpolyline(
                points=pile_points,
                close=True,
                dxfattribs={'layer': 'PILE'}
            )
            
            # Draw reinforcement
            # Main bars
            for i in range(pd.num_main_bars):
                angle = 2 * math.pi * i / pd.num_main_bars
                bar_x = x + (pd.radius - 0.1) * math.cos(angle)
                
                self.msp.add_line(
                    start=self._to_dxf(bar_x, pile_top - 0.5),
                    end=self._to_dxf(bar_x, pile_bottom + 0.5),
                    dxfattribs={'layer': 'REINFORCEMENT'}
                )
            
            # Spiral indication
            self.msp.add_text(
                f"{pd.spiral_dia}mm @ {pd.spiral_spacing*1000:.0f}c/c",
                height=1.5,
                rotation=90,
                dxfattribs={'layer': 'TEXT'}
            ).set_placement(
                self._to_dxf(x + pd.radius + 0.5, (pile_top + pile_bottom)/2),
                align=TextEntityAlignment.MIDDLE_CENTER
            )
        
        # Draw pier/abutment above cap
        pier_width = self.model.support_width
        pier_height = 3.0  # Show 3m of pier
        
        pier_points = [
            self._to_dxf(-pier_width/2, cap_top),
            self._to_dxf(pier_width/2, cap_top),
            self._to_dxf(pier_width/2, cap_top + pier_height),
            self._to_dxf(-pier_width/2, cap_top + pier_height),
            self._to_dxf(-pier_width/2, cap_top)
        ]
        
        self.msp.add_lwpolyline(
            points=pier_points,
            close=True,
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'PILE_CAP'}
        )
        
        # Level annotations
        levels = [
            (self.model.ground_level, gl_y, "GL"),
            (self.model.cutoff_level, 0, "CUT-OFF"),
            (self.model.cutoff_level - pd.length, -pd.length, "PILE TOE")
        ]
        
        for level, y, label in levels:
            self.msp.add_line(
                start=self._to_dxf(4, y),
                end=self._to_dxf(5, y),
                dxfattribs={'layer': 'LEVEL'}
            )
            
            self.msp.add_text(
                f"{label} = {level:.3f}",
                height=1.5,
                dxfattribs={'layer': 'TEXT'}
            ).set_placement(
                self._to_dxf(5.5, y),
                align=TextEntityAlignment.MIDDLE_LEFT
            )
        
        # Dimensions
        self._draw_section_dimensions(pc, pd)
        
        self._draw_title("PILE FOUNDATION SECTION")
        
        return "pile_section.dxf"
    
    def _draw_section_dimensions(self, pc, pd):
        """Draw dimensions in section view"""
        # Pile cap thickness
        self.msp.add_line(
            start=self._to_dxf(pc.cap_length/2 + 1, 0),
            end=self._to_dxf(pc.cap_length/2 + 1, pc.cap_thickness),
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'DIMENSION'}
        )
        
        self.msp.add_text(
            f"CAP = {pc.cap_thickness:.2f}m",
            height=1.5,
            rotation=90,
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'DIMENSION'}
        ).set_placement(
            self._to_dxf(pc.cap_length/2 + 2, pc.cap_thickness/2),
            align=TextEntityAlignment.MIDDLE_CENTER
        )
        
        # Pile length
        self.msp.add_line(
            start=self._to_dxf(pd.radius + 1, 0),
            end=self._to_dxf(pd.radius + 1, -pd.length),
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'DIMENSION'}
        )
        
        self.msp.add_text(
            f"L = {pd.length:.2f}m",
            height=1.5,
            rotation=90,
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'DIMENSION'}
        ).set_placement(
            self._to_dxf(pd.radius + 2, -pd.length/2),
            align=TextEntityAlignment.MIDDLE_CENTER
        )
        
        # Pile diameter
        self.msp.add_line(
            start=self._to_dxf(-pd.radius, -pd.length - 1),
            end=self._to_dxf(pd.radius, -pd.length - 1),
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'DIMENSION'}
        )
        
        self.msp.add_text(
            f"Ø{pd.diameter*1000:.0f}mm",
            height=1.5,
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'DIMENSION'}
        ).set_placement(
            self._to_dxf(0, -pd.length - 2),
            align=TextEntityAlignment.MIDDLE_CENTER
        )
    
    def generate_well_plan(self) -> str:
        """Generate well foundation plan view"""
        if self.model.foundation_type != FoundationType.WELL:
            return ""
        
        wd = self.model.well_dims
        
        # Draw outer circle (steining)
        center = self._to_dxf(0, 0)
        outer_radius = (wd.external_dia / 2) * self.scale
        
        self.msp.add_circle(
            center=center,
            radius=outer_radius,
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'WELL'}
        )
        
        # Draw inner circle (void)
        inner_radius = (wd.internal_dia / 2) * self.scale
        self.msp.add_circle(
            center=center,
            radius=inner_radius,
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'WELL'}
        )
        
        # Hatch steining
        hatch = self.msp.add_hatch(color=5, dxfattribs={'layer': 'WELL'})
        outer_edge = hatch.paths.add_edge_path()
        outer_edge.add_arc(
            center=center,
            radius=outer_radius,
            start_angle=0,
            end_angle=360
        )
        inner_edge = hatch.paths.add_edge_path()
        inner_edge.add_arc(
            center=center,
            radius=inner_radius,
            start_angle=0,
            end_angle=360
        )
        hatch.set_pattern_fill('AR-CONC', scale=0.3)
        
        # Draw center cross
        self.msp.add_line(
            start=self._to_dxf(0, -wd.external_dia/2 - 1),
            end=self._to_dxf(0, wd.external_dia/2 + 1),
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'CENTERLINE'}
        )
        self.msp.add_line(
            start=self._to_dxf(-wd.external_dia/2 - 1, 0),
            end=self._to_dxf(wd.external_dia/2 + 1, 0),
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'CENTERLINE'}
        )
        
        # Dimensions
        # External diameter
        self.msp.add_line(
            start=self._to_dxf(-wd.external_dia/2, wd.external_dia/2 + 2),
            end=self._to_dxf(wd.external_dia/2, wd.external_dia/2 + 2),
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'DIMENSION'}
        )
        
        self.msp.add_text(
            f"Ø{wd.external_dia:.2f}m",
            height=2.0,
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'DIMENSION'}
        ).set_placement(
            self._to_dxf(0, wd.external_dia/2 + 3),
            align=TextEntityAlignment.MIDDLE_CENTER
        )
        
        # Steining thickness annotation
        self.msp.add_text(
            f"STEINING = {wd.steining_thickness*1000:.0f}mm",
            height=1.5,
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'TEXT'}
        ).set_placement(
            self._to_dxf(wd.external_dia/4, 0),
            align=TextEntityAlignment.MIDDLE_CENTER
        )
        
        self._draw_title("WELL FOUNDATION PLAN")
        
        return "well_plan.dxf"
    
    def generate_well_section(self) -> str:
        """Generate well foundation section view"""
        if self.model.foundation_type != FoundationType.WELL:
            return ""
        
        wd = self.model.well_dims
        
        # Levels relative to cutoff
        gl = self.model.ground_level - self.model.cutoff_level
        bed = self.model.scour_level - self.model.cutoff_level
        
        # Draw well components from top to bottom
        current_y = wd.well_cap_thickness
        
        # Well cap
        cap_points = [
            self._to_dxf(-wd.external_dia/2 - 0.5, current_y),
            self._to_dxf(wd.external_dia/2 + 0.5, current_y),
            self._to_dxf(wd.external_dia/2 + 0.5, current_y - wd.well_cap_thickness),
            self._to_dxf(-wd.external_dia/2 - 0.5, current_y - wd.well_cap_thickness),
            self._to_dxf(-wd.external_dia/2 - 0.5, current_y)
        ]
        
        self.msp.add_lwpolyline(
            points=cap_points,
            close=True,
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'WELL'}
        )
        
        current_y -= wd.well_cap_thickness
        
        # Steining
        steining_top = current_y
        steining_bottom = current_y - wd.sinking_depth
        
        # Outer face
        self.msp.add_line(
            start=self._to_dxf(-wd.external_dia/2, steining_top),
            end=self._to_dxf(-wd.external_dia/2, steining_bottom),
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'WELL'}
        )
        self.msp.add_line(
            start=self._to_dxf(wd.external_dia/2, steining_top),
            end=self._to_dxf(wd.external_dia/2, steining_bottom),
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'WELL'}
        )
        
        # Inner face
        self.msp.add_line(
            start=self._to_dxf(-wd.internal_dia/2, steining_top),
            end=self._to_dxf(-wd.internal_dia/2, steining_bottom),
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'WELL'}
        )
        self.msp.add_line(
            start=self._to_dxf(wd.internal_dia/2, steining_top),
            end=self._to_dxf(wd.internal_dia/2, steining_bottom),
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'WELL'}
        )
        
        # Curb (splayed)
        curb_bottom = steining_bottom - wd.curb_height
        
        self.msp.add_line(
            start=self._to_dxf(-wd.external_dia/2, steining_bottom),
            end=self._to_dxf(-wd.curb_dia/2, curb_bottom),
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'WELL'}
        )
        self.msp.add_line(
            start=self._to_dxf(wd.external_dia/2, steining_bottom),
            end=self._to_dxf(wd.curb_dia/2, curb_bottom),
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'WELL'}
        )
        
        # Inner curb face
        self.msp.add_line(
            start=self._to_dxf(-wd.internal_dia/2, steining_bottom),
            end=self._to_dxf(-wd.internal_dia/2, curb_bottom),
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'WELL'}
        )
        self.msp.add_line(
            start=self._to_dxf(wd.internal_dia/2, steining_bottom),
            end=self._to_dxf(wd.internal_dia/2, curb_bottom),
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'WELL'}
        )
        
        # Bottom plug
        plug_points = [
            self._to_dxf(-wd.internal_dia/2, curb_bottom),
            self._to_dxf(wd.internal_dia/2, curb_bottom),
            self._to_dxf(wd.internal_dia/2, curb_bottom - wd.bottom_plug_thickness),
            self._to_dxf(-wd.internal_dia/2, curb_bottom - wd.bottom_plug_thickness),
            self._to_dxf(-wd.internal_dia/2, curb_bottom)
        ]
        
        self.msp.add_lwpolyline(
            points=plug_points,
            close=True,
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'WELL'}
        )
        
        # Hatch well concrete
        # (Simplified - just hatch the section)
        
        # Draw ground and scour levels
        self.msp.add_line(
            start=self._to_dxf(-8, gl),
            end=self._to_dxf(8, gl),
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'GROUND'}
        )
        
        self.msp.add_line(
            start=self._to_dxf(-8, bed),
            end=self._to_dxf(8, bed),
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'LEVEL'}
        )
        
        # Level annotations
        levels = [
            (self.model.ground_level, gl, "GL"),
            (self.model.cutoff_level, 0, "CUT-OFF"),
            (self.model.cutoff_level - wd.well_cap_thickness - wd.sinking_depth, 
             -wd.well_cap_thickness - wd.sinking_depth, "WELL BOTTOM")
        ]
        
        for level, y, label in levels:
            self.msp.add_line(
                start=self._to_dxf(wd.external_dia/2 + 1, y),
                end=self._to_dxf(wd.external_dia/2 + 2, y),
                dxfattribs={'layer': 'LEVEL'}
            )
            
            self.msp.add_text(
                f"{label} = {level:.3f}",
                height=1.5,
                dxfattribs={'layer': 'TEXT'}
            ).set_placement(
                self._to_dxf(wd.external_dia/2 + 2.5, y),
                align=TextEntityAlignment.MIDDLE_LEFT
            )
        
        # Dimensions
        # Sinking depth
        self.msp.add_line(
            start=self._to_dxf(wd.external_dia/2 + 3, steining_top),
            end=self._to_dxf(wd.external_dia/2 + 3, steining_bottom),
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'DIMENSION'}
        )
        
        self.msp.add_text(
            f"SINKING = {wd.sinking_depth:.2f}m",
            height=1.5,
            rotation=90,
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'DIMENSION'}
        ).set_placement(
            self._to_dxf(wd.external_dia/2 + 4, (steining_top + steining_bottom)/2),
            align=TextEntityAlignment.MIDDLE_CENTER
        )
        
        self._draw_title("WELL FOUNDATION SECTION")
        
        return "well_section.dxf"
    
    def _draw_title(self, title_text: str):
        """Draw title block"""
        # Title at bottom
        self.msp.add_text(
            title_text,
            height=3.0,
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'TEXT'}
        ).set_placement(
            self._to_dxf(0, -8),
            align=TextEntityAlignment.MIDDLE_CENTER
        )
        
        # Foundation type info
        info_text = f"{self.model.foundation_type.value} - {self.model.support_type} #{self.model.support_number}"
        self.msp.add_text(
            info_text,
            height=2.0,
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'TEXT'}
        ).set_placement(
            self._to_dxf(0, -10),
            align=TextEntityAlignment.MIDDLE_CENTER
        )
    
    def save(self, filename: str):
        """Save DXF file"""
        self.doc.saveas(filename)
        print(f"Foundation drawing saved: {filename}")


# Factory functions
def create_pile_foundation(support_type: str, support_number: int,
                          num_long: int = 4, num_trans: int = 3,
                          pile_dia: float = 1.2, pile_length: float = 15.0) -> FoundationModel:
    """Create pile foundation model"""
    model = FoundationModel(
        foundation_type=FoundationType.PILE,
        support_type=support_type,
        support_number=support_number
    )
    
    model.pile_config = PileGroupConfiguration(
        num_longitudinal=num_long,
        num_transverse=num_trans,
        spacing_long=2.5,
        spacing_trans=2.5
    )
    
    model.pile_dims = PileDimensions(
        diameter=pile_dia,
        length=pile_length
    )
    
    return model


def create_well_foundation(support_type: str, support_number: int,
                           diameter: float = 6.0, sinking_depth: float = 12.0) -> FoundationModel:
    """Create well foundation model"""
    model = FoundationModel(
        foundation_type=FoundationType.WELL,
        support_type=support_type,
        support_number=support_number
    )
    
    model.well_dims = WellDimensions(
        external_dia=diameter,
        sinking_depth=sinking_depth
    )
    
    return model


# Example usage
if __name__ == "__main__":
    # Example 1: Pile foundation for Pier 1
    pile_foundation = create_pile_foundation(
        support_type="PIER",
        support_number=1,
        num_long=4,
        num_trans=3,
        pile_dia=1.2,
        pile_length=15.0
    )
    
    # Generate drawings
    drawing = FoundationDetailDrawing(pile_foundation, scale=8.0)
    drawing.generate_pile_layout_plan()
    drawing.generate_pile_section()
    drawing.save("pier1_foundation.dxf")
    
    # Example 2: Well foundation for Abutment
    well_foundation = create_well_foundation(
        support_type="ABUTMENT",
        support_number=1,
        diameter=6.0,
        sinking_depth=12.0
    )
    
    drawing2 = FoundationDetailDrawing(well_foundation, scale=6.0)
    drawing2.generate_well_plan()
    drawing2.generate_well_section()
    drawing2.save("abutment1_well.dxf")
    
    print("Foundation drawings generated successfully!")
```

## File 2: `foundation_excel_integration.py`

```python
"""
Excel Integration for Foundation Details
Reads foundation parameters from Excel and generates drawings
"""

import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict, List
from foundation_generator import (
    FoundationModel, FoundationType, PileGroupConfiguration,
    PileDimensions, WellDimensions, FoundationDetailDrawing,
    create_pile_foundation, create_well_foundation
)


class FoundationExcelReader:
    """Read foundation data from Excel template"""
    
    def __init__(self, excel_path: str):
        self.excel_path = excel_path
        self.foundations: List[FoundationModel] = []
    
    def read_foundations(self) -> List[FoundationModel]:
        """Read all foundation definitions from Excel"""
        df = pd.read_excel(self.excel_path, sheet_name='FOUNDATION_DETAILS')
        
        for _, row in df.iterrows():
            foundation = self._parse_foundation_row(row)
            if foundation:
                self.foundations.append(foundation)
        
        return self.foundations
    
    def _parse_foundation_row(self, row: pd.Series) -> FoundationModel:
        """Parse a single foundation from Excel row"""
        support_type = row['Support_Type']  # PIER or ABUTMENT
        support_num = int(row['Support_Number'])
        foundation_type = row['Foundation_Type']  # PILE, WELL, OPEN
        
        if foundation_type == "PILE":
            return create_pile_foundation(
                support_type=support_type,
                support_number=support_num,
                num_long=int(row.get('Piles_Long', 4)),
                num_trans=int(row.get('Piles_Trans', 3)),
                pile_dia=float(row.get('Pile_Diameter', 1.2)),
                pile_length=float(row.get('Pile_Length', 15.0))
            )
        
        elif foundation_type == "WELL":
            return create_well_foundation(
                support_type=support_type,
                support_number=support_num,
                diameter=float(row.get('Well_Diameter', 6.0)),
                sinking_depth=float(row.get('Sinking_Depth', 12.0))
            )
        
        return None
    
    def generate_all_drawings(self, output_dir: str = "./output/"):
        """Generate drawings for all foundations"""
        for foundation in self.foundations:
            drawing = FoundationDetailDrawing(foundation, scale=8.0)
            
            if foundation.foundation_type == FoundationType.PILE:
                drawing.generate_pile_layout_plan()
                drawing.generate_pile_section()
                filename = f"{output_dir}/{foundation.support_type.lower()}{foundation.support_number}_pile.dxf"
            
            elif foundation.foundation_type == FoundationType.WELL:
                drawing.generate_well_plan()
                drawing.generate_well_section()
                filename = f"{output_dir}/{foundation.support_type.lower()}{foundation.support_number}_well.dxf"
            
            drawing.save(filename)
            print(f"Generated: {filename}")


# Excel Template Structure
FOUNDATION_EXCEL_TEMPLATE = {
    'FOUNDATION_DETAILS': {
        'columns': [
            'Support_Type',      # PIER / ABUTMENT
            'Support_Number',    # 1, 2, 3...
            'Foundation_Type',   # PILE / WELL / OPEN
            'Ground_Level',      # m
            'Cutoff_Level',      # m
            'Scour_Level',       # m
            
            # Pile specific
            'Piles_Long',        # Number along bridge
            'Piles_Trans',       # Number across bridge
            'Pile_Diameter',     # m
            'Pile_Length',       # m
            'Pile_Spacing_Long', # m
            'Pile_Spacing_Trans',# m
            'Cap_Thickness',     # m
            
            # Well specific
            'Well_Diameter',     # m
            'Steining_Thickness',# m
            'Sinking_Depth',     # m
            'Curb_Height',       # m
            
            # Open foundation
            'Footing_Length',    # m
            'Footing_Width',     # m
            'Footing_Depth'      # m
        ]
    }
}


def create_foundation_template(output_path: str = "foundation_template.xlsx"):
    """Create sample Excel template for foundation input"""
    
    sample_data = {
        'Support_Type': ['PIER', 'PIER', 'PIER', 'ABUTMENT', 'ABUTMENT'],
        'Support_Number': [1, 2, 3, 1, 2],
        'Foundation_Type': ['PILE', 'PILE', 'PILE', 'WELL', 'WELL'],
        'Ground_Level': [175.0, 174.5, 175.2, 176.0, 176.0],
        'Cutoff_Level': [172.0, 171.5, 172.2, 173.0, 173.0],
        'Scour_Level': [170.0, 169.5, 170.2, 171.0, 171.0],
        'Piles_Long': [4, 4, 4, None, None],
        'Piles_Trans': [3, 3, 3, None, None],
        'Pile_Diameter': [1.2, 1.2, 1.2, None, None],
        'Pile_Length': [15.0, 18.0, 15.0, None, None],
        'Well_Diameter': [None, None, None, 6.0, 6.0],
        'Sinking_Depth': [None, None, None, 12.0, 10.0]
    }
    
    df = pd.DataFrame(sample_data)
    
    with pd.ExcelWriter(output_path, engine='openpyxl') as writer:
        df.to_excel(writer, sheet_name='FOUNDATION_DETAILS', index=False)
    
    print(f"Foundation template created: {output_path}")
    return output_path


if __name__ == "__main__":
    # Create template
    template_path = create_foundation_template()
    
    # Read and generate
    reader = FoundationExcelReader(template_path)
    foundations = reader.read_foundations()
    
    print(f"\nFound {len(foundations)} foundations")
    for f in foundations:
        print(f"  {f.support_type} #{f.support_number}: {f.foundation_type.value}")
    
    # Generate drawings
    reader.generate_all_drawings()
```

## File 3: `foundation_schedule.py`

```python
"""
Foundation Schedule Generator
Creates BBS (Bar Bending Schedule) and BOQ (Bill of Quantities) for foundations
"""

from typing import Dict, List
from foundation_generator import FoundationModel, FoundationType


class FoundationScheduleGenerator:
    """Generate schedules for foundations"""
    
    def __init__(self, foundations: List[FoundationModel]):
        self.foundations = foundations
    
    def generate_bar_bending_schedule(self) -> Dict:
        """Generate BBS for all foundations"""
        bbs = {
            'pile_bars': [],
            'pile_cap_bars': [],
            'well_bars': [],
            'summary': {}
        }
        
        for foundation in self.foundations:
            if foundation.foundation_type == FoundationType.PILE:
                self._add_pile_bbs(foundation, bbs)
            elif foundation.foundation_type == FoundationType.WELL:
                self._add_well_bbs(foundation, bbs)
        
        # Calculate totals
        bbs['summary'] = self._calculate_bbs_summary(bbs)
        return bbs
    
    def _add_pile_bbs(self, foundation: FoundationModel, bbs: Dict):
        """Add pile foundation bars to BBS"""
        pd = foundation.pile_dims
        pc = foundation.pile_config
        
        # Main bars in piles
        bar_length = pd.length + 0.5  # Add anchorage
        bar_weight = (pd.num_main_bars * bar_length * 
                     (pd.main_bar_dia ** 2) * 0.00617 / 1000)  # kg
        
        bbs['pile_bars'].append({
            'foundation': f"{foundation.support_type}-{foundation.support_number}",
            'description': f"Pile Main Bars Ø{pd.main_bar_dia}mm",
            'number': pc.total_piles * pd.num_main_bars,
            'length': bar_length,
            'unit_weight': bar_weight / (pc.total_piles * pd.num_main_bars),
            'total_weight': bar_weight
        })
        
        # Spiral reinforcement
        spiral_length = (pd.length / pd.spiral_spacing) * 3.5  # Approximate circumference
        spiral_weight = spiral_length * (pd.spiral_dia ** 2) * 0.00617 / 1000
        
        bbs['pile_bars'].append({
            'foundation': f"{foundation.support_type}-{foundation.support_number}",
            'description': f"Pile Spiral Ø{pd.spiral_dia}mm @ {pd.spiral_spacing*1000:.0f}c/c",
            'number': pc.total_piles,
            'length': spiral_length,
            'unit_weight': spiral_weight,
            'total_weight': spiral_weight * pc.total_piles
        })
        
        # Pile cap reinforcement
        cap_length = pc.cap_length
        cap_width = pc.cap_width
        
        # Bottom mesh
        long_bars = int(cap_length / 0.15) + 1
        trans_bars = int(cap_width / 0.15) + 1
        
        bbs['pile_cap_bars'].append({
            'foundation': f"{foundation.support_type}-{foundation.support_number}",
            'description': "Pile Cap Bottom Mesh Ø25mm",
            'longitudinal_bars': long_bars,
            'transverse_bars': trans_bars,
            'length_each': cap_width,
            'total_weight': (long_bars * cap_width + trans_bars * cap_length) * 0.039  # Approx
        })
    
    def _add_well_bbs(self, foundation: FoundationModel, bbs: Dict):
        """Add well foundation bars to BBS"""
        wd = foundation.well_dims
        
        # Vertical steining bars
        num_bars = int(wd.external_dia * 3.14 / 0.15)  # @ 150mm c/c
        bar_length = wd.sinking_depth + 2.0  # Add for curb
        
        bbs['well_bars'].append({
            'foundation': f"{foundation.support_type}-{foundation.support_number}",
            'description': f"Well Steining Vertical Ø20mm",
            'number': num_bars,
            'length': bar_length,
            'total_weight': num_bars * bar_length * 0.025  # Approx kg/m for 20mm
        })
        
        # Horizontal rings
        num_rings = int(wd.sinking_depth / 0.15)
        ring_length = wd.external_dia * 3.14
        
        bbs['well_bars'].append({
            'foundation': f"{foundation.support_type}-{foundation.support_number}",
            'description': f"Well Steining Rings Ø12mm @ 150c/c",
            'number': num_rings,
            'length': ring_length,
            'total_weight': num_rings * ring_length * 0.009  # Approx
        })
    
    def _calculate_bbs_summary(self, bbs: Dict) -> Dict:
        """Calculate summary totals"""
        total_weight = 0
        
        for category in ['pile_bars', 'pile_cap_bars', 'well_bars']:
            for item in bbs.get(category, []):
                total_weight += item.get('total_weight', 0)
        
        return {
            'total_steel_weight_kg': round(total_weight, 2),
            'total_steel_weight_mt': round(total_weight / 1000, 3),
            'categories': len(bbs)
        }
    
    def generate_bill_of_quantities(self) -> Dict:
        """Generate BOQ for all foundations"""
        boq = {
            'items': [],
            'summary': {}
        }
        
        for foundation in self.foundations:
            quantities = foundation.get_material_quantities()
            
            boq['items'].append({
                'item_no': f"F-{foundation.support_number}",
                'description': f"{foundation.foundation_type.value} Foundation for {foundation.support_type} #{foundation.support_number}",
                'concrete_m3': round(quantities['concrete_m3'], 2),
                'steel_kg': round(quantities['steel_kg'], 2),
                'excavation_m3': round(quantities['excavation_m3'], 2)
            })
        
        # Summary
        total_concrete = sum(item['concrete_m3'] for item in boq['items'])
        total_steel = sum(item['steel_kg'] for item in boq['items'])
        total_excavation = sum(item['excavation_m3'] for item in boq['items'])
        
        boq['summary'] = {
            'total_concrete_m3': round(total_concrete, 2),
            'total_steel_kg': round(total_steel, 2),
            'total_excavation_m3': round(total_excavation, 2),
            'number_of_foundations': len(self.foundations)
        }
        
        return boq
    
    def export_to_excel(self, output_path: str = "foundation_schedule.xlsx"):
        """Export schedules to Excel"""
        import pandas as pd
        
        bbs = self.generate_bar_bending_schedule()
        boq = self.generate_bill_of_quantities()
        
        with pd.ExcelWriter(output_path, engine='openpyxl') as writer:
            # BBS sheets
            for category in ['pile_bars', 'pile_cap_bars', 'well_bars']:
                if bbs.get(category):
                    df = pd.DataFrame(bbs[category])
                    df.to_excel(writer, sheet_name=f'BBS_{category.upper()}', index=False)
            
            # BOQ
            df_boq = pd.DataFrame(boq['items'])
            df_boq.to_excel(writer, sheet_name='BOQ', index=False)
            
            # Summary
            summary_data = {
                'BBS Total Steel (kg)': [bbs['summary']['total_steel_weight_kg']],
                'BOQ Concrete (m3)': [boq['summary']['total_concrete_m3']],
                'BOQ Steel (kg)': [boq['summary']['total_steel_kg']],
                'BOQ Excavation (m3)': [boq['summary']['total_excavation_m3']]
            }
            df_summary = pd.DataFrame(summary_data)
            df_summary.to_excel(writer, sheet_name='SUMMARY', index=False)
        
        print(f"Foundation schedule exported: {output_path}")
        return output_path


# Example usage
if __name__ == "__main__":
    from foundation_generator import create_pile_foundation, create_well_foundation
    
    # Create sample foundations
    foundations = [
        create_pile_foundation("PIER", 1, 4, 3, 1.2, 15.0),
        create_pile_foundation("PIER", 2, 4, 3, 1.2, 18.0),
        create_well_foundation("ABUTMENT", 1, 6.0, 12.0)
    ]
    
    # Generate schedules
    schedule = FoundationScheduleGenerator(foundations)
    
    bbs = schedule.generate_bar_bending_schedule()
    print("\nBar Bending Schedule Summary:")
    print(f"  Total Steel: {bbs['summary']['total_steel_weight_mt']:.3f} MT")
    
    boq = schedule.generate_bill_of_quantities()
    print(f"\nBill of Quantities Summary:")
    print(f"  Concrete: {boq['summary']['total_concrete_m3']:.2f} m³")
    print(f"  Steel: {boq['summary']['total_steel_kg']:.2f} kg")
    print(f"  Excavation: {boq['summary']['total_excavation_m3']:.2f} m³")
    
    # Export to Excel
    schedule.export_to_excel()
```

from foundation_generator import (
    create_pile_foundation, create_well_foundation,
    FoundationDetailDrawing
)
from foundation_excel_integration import FoundationExcelReader
from foundation_schedule import FoundationScheduleGenerator

def generate_foundation_drawings(excel_path: str):
    """Generate all foundation drawings from Excel"""
    
    # Read foundations from Excel
    reader = FoundationExcelReader(excel_path)
    foundations = reader.read_foundations()
    
    # Generate individual drawings
    reader.generate_all_drawings("./output/foundations/")
    
    # Generate combined schedule

    schedule.export_to_excel("./output/foundation_schedule.xlsx")

## KEY FEATURES

| Feature | Description |
|---------|-------------|
| **Pile Layouts** | 4x3, 3x2, or custom grid arrangements |
| **Pile Sections** | Reinforcement details with main bars and spirals |
| **Well Plans** | Circular/Dumb-bell shapes with steining details |
| **Well Sections** | Curb, steining, sinking depth, bottom plug |
| **Excel Integration** | Read foundation data from standardized template |
| **BBS Generation** | Complete bar bending schedules |
| **BOQ Generation** | Concrete, steel, excavation quantities |
| **Multi-format** | Separate DXF files for plan and section views |

This completes your Foundation Detail Generator module! It integrates seamlessly with the Plan View, Longitudinal Section, and Cross-Section generators from your document.

    Add Reinforcement Detailing - Bar bending schedules from the geometry?
I'll create a comprehensive **Reinforcement Detailing Module** that automatically generates Bar Bending Schedules (BBS) from foundation geometry with IS code-compliant calculations. 

Now let me provide the complete Reinforcement Detailing module:
