# REINFORCEMENT DETAILING MODULE

Complete Bar Bending Schedule (BBS) generation with IS code calculations.

## File 1: `reinforcement_calculator.py`

```python
"""
Reinforcement Calculator Module
Calculates bar lengths, shapes, and quantities per IS:2502 and IS:456
"""

import math
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import List, Tuple, Dict, Optional
from enum import Enum


class ConcreteGrade(Enum):
    """IS 456 concrete grades"""
    M15 = 15
    M20 = 20
    M25 = 25
    M30 = 30
    M35 = 35
    M40 = 40


class SteelGrade(Enum):
    """IS 1786 steel grades"""
    FE250 = 250  # Mild steel
    FE415 = 415  # HYSD
    FE500 = 500  # HYSD
    FE550 = 550  # HYSD


class BarShape(Enum):
    """Standard bar shapes per IS:2502"""
    STRAIGHT = 1
    L_HOOK_90 = 2      # 90° hook
    L_HOOK_135 = 3     # 135° hook (seismic)
    L_HOOK_180 = 4     # 180° hook
    STIRRUP_RECT = 5   # Rectangular stirrup
    STIRRUP_SQUARE = 6 # Square stirrup
    LINK_SQUARE = 7    # Square link
    LINK_TRIANGULAR = 8
    U_SHAPE = 9
    CRANKED = 10       # Cranked/bent bar
    SPIRAL = 11


@dataclass
class BarProperties:
    """Physical properties of reinforcement bars"""
    diameter_mm: int = 20
    
    @property
    def area_mm2(self) -> float:
        """Cross-sectional area in mm²"""
        return math.pi * (self.diameter_mm ** 2) / 4
    
    @property
    def perimeter_mm(self) -> float:
        """Perimeter in mm"""
        return math.pi * self.diameter_mm
    
    @property
    def unit_weight_kg_per_m(self) -> float:
        """Weight per meter in kg/m"""
        return self.diameter_mm ** 2 * 0.00617  # d² × 0.00617
    
    @property
    def radius_mm(self) -> float:
        """Bend radius (4d for mild steel, 6d for HYSD)"""
        if self.diameter_mm <= 12:
            return 2 * self.diameter_mm
        return 4 * self.diameter_mm  # Standard bend radius


class ISCodeCalculator:
    """IS Code compliant reinforcement calculations"""
    
    # IS:2502 - Bend deductions
    BEND_DEDUCTIONS = {
        45: 1.0,   # d
        90: 2.0,   # 2d
        135: 2.5,  # 2.5d
        180: 3.0   # 3d (approximate)
    }
    
    # IS:456 - Development length factors
    TAU_BD = {
        (ConcreteGrade.M20, SteelGrade.FE250): 1.2,
        (ConcreteGrade.M20, SteelGrade.FE415): 1.6,
        (ConcreteGrade.M20, SteelGrade.FE500): 1.9,
        (ConcreteGrade.M25, SteelGrade.FE250): 1.4,
        (ConcreteGrade.M25, SteelGrade.FE415): 1.7,
        (ConcreteGrade.M25, SteelGrade.FE500): 2.0,
        (ConcreteGrade.M30, SteelGrade.FE250): 1.5,
        (ConcreteGrade.M30, SteelGrade.FE415): 1.9,
        (ConcreteGrade.M30, SteelGrade.FE500): 2.2,
    }
    
    # Hook lengths (straight portion)
    HOOK_LENGTH_90 = 12  # 12d for 90° hook
    HOOK_LENGTH_135 = 16  # 16d for 135° hook
    HOOK_LENGTH_180 = 8   # 8d for 180° hook (plus semicircle)
    
    @classmethod
    def get_tau_bd(cls, concrete: ConcreteGrade, steel: SteelGrade) -> float:
        """Get design bond stress (τbd) in MPa"""
        key = (concrete, steel)
        if key in cls.TAU_BD:
            return cls.TAU_BD[key]
        # Interpolate or return default
        return 1.6
    
    @classmethod
    def calculate_development_length(
        cls,
        bar_dia_mm: int,
        steel_grade: SteelGrade,
        concrete_grade: ConcreteGrade,
        tension: bool = True,
        deformed_bars: bool = True
    ) -> float:
        """
        Calculate development length (Ld) per IS:456 Clause 26.2.1
        
        Ld = (φ × σs) / (4 × τbd)
        
        Where:
        φ = bar diameter
        σs = stress in bar (0.87fy for full development)
        τbd = design bond stress
        """
        tau_bd = cls.get_tau_bd(concrete_grade, steel_grade)
        
        # Increase for deformed bars (1.6 times)
        if deformed_bars:
            tau_bd *= 1.6
        
        # Stress in bar (0.87fy for tension, 0.67fy for compression)
        if tension:
            stress = 0.87 * steel_grade.value
        else:
            stress = 0.67 * steel_grade.value
        
        # Basic development length
        ld = (bar_dia_mm * stress) / (4 * tau_bd)
        
        # Return in meters
        return ld / 1000
    
    @classmethod
    def calculate_hook_length(
        cls,
        bar_dia_mm: int,
        hook_type: int = 90,
        seismic: bool = False
    ) -> float:
        """
        Calculate hook length per IS:456
        
        90° hook: 12d + bend deduction
        135° hook: 16d + bend deduction (seismic)
        180° hook: 8d + semicircle (πd/2)
        """
        if hook_type == 90:
            if seismic:
                return (16 * bar_dia_mm) / 1000  # 16d for seismic
            return (12 * bar_dia_mm) / 1000  # 12d
        
        elif hook_type == 135:
            return (16 * bar_dia_mm) / 1000  # 16d
        
        elif hook_type == 180:
            # 8d straight + semicircular bend
            straight = 8 * bar_dia_mm
            bend = math.pi * bar_dia_mm / 2
            return (straight + bend) / 1000
        
        return 0
    
    @classmethod
    def calculate_bend_deduction(
        cls,
        bar_dia_mm: int,
        bend_angle: int
    ) -> float:
        """Calculate bend deduction for bar cutting length"""
        if bend_angle in cls.BEND_DEDUCTIONS:
            return (cls.BEND_DEDUCTIONS[bend_angle] * bar_dia_mm) / 1000
        return 0
    
    @classmethod
    def calculate_lap_length(
        cls,
        bar_dia_mm: int,
        steel_grade: SteelGrade,
        concrete_grade: ConcreteGrade,
        tension: bool = True
    ) -> float:
        """
        Calculate lap length per IS:456
        
        Lap length = development length (minimum 30φ or 24φ for compression)
        """
        ld = cls.calculate_development_length(
            bar_dia_mm, steel_grade, concrete_grade, tension
        )
        
        # Minimum lap length
        min_lap = 30 * bar_dia_mm / 1000 if tension else 24 * bar_dia_mm / 1000
        
        return max(ld, min_lap)
    
    @classmethod
    def calculate_stirrup_spacing(
        cls,
        beam_depth_mm: float,
        bar_dia_mm: int,
        min_spacing: float = 0.15
    ) -> float:
        """
        Calculate maximum stirrup spacing per IS:456
        
        Minimum of:
        - 0.75d (for vertical stirrups)
        - 300mm
        - Asv calculated spacing
        """
        d = beam_depth_mm - 50  # Effective depth (assuming 50mm cover)
        
        max_spacing = min(0.75 * d / 1000, 0.3)  # 0.75d or 300mm
        
        return min(max_spacing, min_spacing)


@dataclass
class BarSegment:
    """Individual segment of a bar"""
    length_m: float
    angle_degrees: float = 0  # 0 = straight, bend angle for turns
    
    @property
    def is_bend(self) -> bool:
        return self.angle_degrees != 0


@dataclass
class ReinforcementBar:
    """Complete reinforcement bar definition"""
    mark: str  # Bar mark (P1, S1, etc.)
    description: str
    shape: BarShape
    diameter_mm: int
    num_bars: int
    
    # Geometry
    segments: List[BarSegment] = field(default_factory=list)
    
    # Properties
    concrete_grade: ConcreteGrade = ConcreteGrade.M25
    steel_grade: SteelGrade = SteelGrade.FE500
    
    # Additional info
    location: str = ""  # "Pile Cap Bottom", "Pile Main", etc.
    remarks: str = ""
    
    @property
    def cutting_length_m(self) -> float:
        """Calculate cutting length with bend deductions"""
        total = sum(seg.length_m for seg in self.segments)
        
        # Apply bend deductions
        deductions = 0
        for seg in self.segments:
            if seg.is_bend:
                deductions += ISCodeCalculator.calculate_bend_deduction(
                    self.diameter_mm, seg.angle_degrees
                )
        
        return max(0, total - deductions)
    
    @property
    def development_length_m(self) -> float:
        """Calculate required development length"""
        return ISCodeCalculator.calculate_development_length(
            self.diameter_mm,
            self.steel_grade,
            self.concrete_grade
        )
    
    @property
    def weight_per_bar_kg(self) -> float:
        """Weight of one bar in kg"""
        props = BarProperties(self.diameter_mm)
        return props.unit_weight_kg_per_m * self.cutting_length_m
    
    @property
    def total_weight_kg(self) -> float:
        """Total weight for all bars"""
        return self.weight_per_bar_kg * self.num_bars
    
    @property
    def total_length_m(self) -> float:
        """Total length of all bars"""
        return self.cutting_length_m * self.num_bars
    
    def to_bbs_row(self) -> Dict:
        """Convert to BBS row format"""
        return {
            'Bar Mark': self.mark,
            'Description': self.description,
            'Shape': self.shape.name,
            'Dia (mm)': self.diameter_mm,
            'No. of Bars': self.num_bars,
            'Cutting Length (m)': round(self.cutting_length_m, 3),
            'Total Length (m)': round(self.total_length_m, 2),
            'Unit Weight (kg/m)': round(BarProperties(self.diameter_mm).unit_weight_kg_per_m, 3),
            'Weight per Bar (kg)': round(self.weight_per_bar_kg, 2),
            'Total Weight (kg)': round(self.total_weight_kg, 2),
            'Location': self.location,
            'Remarks': self.remarks
        }


class BarShapeFactory:
    """Factory for creating standard bar shapes"""
    
    @staticmethod
    def create_straight_bar(
        mark: str,
        diameter_mm: int,
        length_m: float,
        num_bars: int = 1,
        location: str = ""
    ) -> ReinforcementBar:
        """Create straight bar"""
        return ReinforcementBar(
            mark=mark,
            description=f"Straight Bar Ø{diameter_mm}mm",
            shape=BarShape.STRAIGHT,
            diameter_mm=diameter_mm,
            num_bars=num_bars,
            segments=[BarSegment(length_m, 0)],
            location=location
        )
    
    @staticmethod
    def create_l_bar_with_hook(
        mark: str,
        diameter_mm: int,
        long_leg_m: float,
        short_leg_m: float,
        hook_type: int = 90,
        num_bars: int = 1,
        location: str = ""
    ) -> ReinforcementBar:
        """Create L-shaped bar with hook"""
        hook_length = ISCodeCalculator.calculate_hook_length(diameter_mm, hook_type)
        
        segments = [
            BarSegment(long_leg_m, 0),
            BarSegment(0, 90),  # Bend
            BarSegment(short_leg_m + hook_length, 0)
        ]
        
        return ReinforcementBar(
            mark=mark,
            description=f"L-Bar with {hook_type}° Hook Ø{diameter_mm}mm",
            shape=BarShape.L_HOOK_90 if hook_type == 90 else BarShape.L_HOOK_135,
            diameter_mm=diameter_mm,
            num_bars=num_bars,
            segments=segments,
            location=location
        )
    
    @staticmethod
    def create_rectangular_stirrup(
        mark: str,
        diameter_mm: int,
        width_m: float,
        depth_m: float,
        cover_m: float = 0.05,
        hook_type: int = 135,  # Seismic
        num_bars: int = 1,
        location: str = ""
    ) -> ReinforcementBar:
        """Create rectangular stirrup with hooks"""
        # Inside dimensions
        w = width_m - 2 * cover_m
        d = depth_m - 2 * cover_m
        
        # Hook length
        hook_length = ISCodeCalculator.calculate_hook_length(diameter_mm, hook_type)
        
        # Perimeter + hooks
        perimeter = 2 * (w + d)
        total_length = perimeter + 2 * hook_length
        
        segments = [
            BarSegment(w, 0),
            BarSegment(0, 90),
            BarSegment(d, 0),
            BarSegment(0, 90),
            BarSegment(w, 0),
            BarSegment(0, 90),
            BarSegment(d, 0),
            BarSegment(0, 135),  # Hook bend
            BarSegment(hook_length, 0),
            BarSegment(0, 135),  # Hook bend
            BarSegment(hook_length, 0)
        ]
        
        return ReinforcementBar(
            mark=mark,
            description=f"Rectangular Stirrup {width_m*1000:.0f}x{depth_m*1000:.0f} Ø{diameter_mm}mm",
            shape=BarShape.STIRRUP_RECT,
            diameter_mm=diameter_mm,
            num_bars=num_bars,
            segments=segments,
            location=location
        )
    
    @staticmethod
    def create_spiral(
        mark: str,
        diameter_mm: int,
        pile_dia_m: float,
        pitch_m: float,
        pile_length_m: float,
        num_bars: int = 1,
        location: str = ""
    ) -> ReinforcementBar:
        """Create spiral reinforcement for pile"""
        # Mean diameter (inside pile - cover)
        mean_dia = pile_dia_m - 0.1  # 50mm cover each side
        
        # Number of turns
        num_turns = pile_length_m / pitch_m
        
        # Length of one turn (helix)
        turn_length = math.sqrt((math.pi * mean_dia) ** 2 + pitch_m ** 2)
        
        total_length = num_turns * turn_length
        
        return ReinforcementBar(
            mark=mark,
            description=f"Spiral Ø{diameter_mm}mm @ {pitch_m*1000:.0f}c/c",
            shape=BarShape.SPIRAL,
            diameter_mm=diameter_mm,
            num_bars=num_bars,
            segments=[BarSegment(total_length, 0)],
            location=location
        )
    
    @staticmethod
    def create_u_bar(
        mark: str,
        diameter_mm: int,
        width_m: float,
        depth_m: float,
        cover_m: float = 0.05,
        num_bars: int = 1,
        location: str = ""
    ) -> ReinforcementBar:
        """Create U-shaped bar"""
        w = width_m - 2 * cover_m
        d = depth_m - 2 * cover_m
        
        segments = [
            BarSegment(d, 0),
            BarSegment(0, 90),
            BarSegment(w, 0),
            BarSegment(0, 90),
            BarSegment(d, 0)
        ]
        
        return ReinforcementBar(
            mark=mark,
            description=f"U-Bar {width_m*1000:.0f}x{depth_m*1000:.0f} Ø{diameter_mm}mm",
            shape=BarShape.U_SHAPE,
            diameter_mm=diameter_mm,
            num_bars=num_bars,
            segments=segments,
            location=location
        )
    
    @staticmethod
    def create_cranked_bar(
        mark: str,
        diameter_mm: int,
        total_span_m: float,
        crank_height_m: float,
        crank_location_m: float,
        num_bars: int = 1,
        location: str = ""
    ) -> ReinforcementBar:
        """Create cranked/bent bar for slab/beam"""
        # Simplified crank calculation
        # Actual crank has slope 1:10
        
        straight1 = crank_location_m - crank_height_m / 2
        straight2 = total_span_m - crank_location_m - crank_height_m / 2
        
        crank_slope_length = math.sqrt(crank_height_m ** 2 + (crank_height_m * 10) ** 2)
        
        segments = [
            BarSegment(straight1, 0),
            BarSegment(0, 10),  # Small angle for crank
            BarSegment(crank_slope_length, 0),
            BarSegment(0, -10),  # Return to horizontal
            BarSegment(straight2, 0)
        ]
        
        return ReinforcementBar(
            mark=mark,
            description=f"Cranked Bar Ø{diameter_mm}mm",
            shape=BarShape.CRANKED,
            diameter_mm=diameter_mm,
            num_bars=num_bars,
            segments=segments,
            location=location
        )


class FoundationReinforcementCalculator:
    """Calculate reinforcement for foundation types"""
    
    def __init__(self, concrete_grade: ConcreteGrade = ConcreteGrade.M25,
                 steel_grade: SteelGrade = SteelGrade.FE500):
        self.concrete_grade = concrete_grade
        self.steel_grade = steel_grade
    
    def calculate_pile_cap_reinforcement(
        self,
        cap_length_m: float,
        cap_width_m: float,
        cap_thickness_m: float,
        pile_dia_m: float,
        num_piles: int
    ) -> List[ReinforcementBar]:
        """Calculate reinforcement for pile cap"""
        bars = []
        
        # Cover
        cover = 0.075  # 75mm
        
        # Bottom mesh - Longitudinal
        long_span = cap_length_m - 2 * cover
        num_long_bars = int(long_span / 0.15) + 1  # @ 150mm c/c
        long_bar_length = cap_width_m - 2 * cover
        
        bars.append(BarShapeFactory.create_straight_bar(
            mark="PC1",
            diameter_mm=25,
            length_m=long_bar_length,
            num_bars=num_long_bars,
            location="Pile Cap Bottom - Longitudinal"
        ))
        
        # Bottom mesh - Transverse
        trans_span = cap_width_m - 2 * cover
        num_trans_bars = int(trans_span / 0.15) + 1
        trans_bar_length = cap_length_m - 2 * cover
        
        bars.append(BarShapeFactory.create_straight_bar(
            mark="PC2",
            diameter_mm=25,
            length_m=trans_bar_length,
            num_bars=num_trans_bars,
            location="Pile Cap Bottom - Transverse"
        ))
        
        # Top mesh (if cantilever > 1.5x thickness)
        cantilever = (cap_length_m - pile_dia_m) / 2
        if cantilever > 1.5 * cap_thickness_m:
            bars.append(BarShapeFactory.create_straight_bar(
                mark="PC3",
                diameter_mm=16,
                length_m=long_bar_length,
                num_bars=num_long_bars // 2,
                location="Pile Cap Top - Longitudinal"
            ))
            
            bars.append(BarShapeFactory.create_straight_bar(
                mark="PC4",
                diameter_mm=16,
                length_m=trans_bar_length,
                num_bars=num_trans_bars // 2,
                location="Pile Cap Top - Transverse"
            ))
        
        # Side face reinforcement (if thickness > 750mm)
        if cap_thickness_m > 0.75:
            side_bars_per_face = int(cap_length_m / 0.30) + 1
            
            bars.append(BarShapeFactory.create_straight_bar(
                mark="PC5",
                diameter_mm=12,
                length_m=cap_thickness_m - 2 * cover,
                num_bars=side_bars_per_face * 2,  # Both faces
                location="Pile Cap Side Face"
            ))
        
        # Pile anchorage bars (into cap)
        anchorage_length = ISCodeCalculator.calculate_development_length(
            25, self.steel_grade, self.concrete_grade
        )
        
        bars.append(BarShapeFactory.create_l_bar_with_hook(
            mark="PC6",
            diameter_mm=25,
            long_leg_m=cap_thickness_m - cover,
            short_leg_m=0.5,
            hook_type=90,
            num_bars=num_piles * 8,  # 8 bars per pile into cap
            location="Pile Anchorage into Cap"
        ))
        
        return bars
    
    def calculate_pile_reinforcement(
        self,
        pile_dia_m: float,
        pile_length_m: float,
        main_bar_dia: int = 20,
        num_main_bars: int = 12,
        spiral_dia: int = 10,
        spiral_pitch: float = 0.15
    ) -> List[ReinforcementBar]:
        """Calculate reinforcement for bored cast-in-situ pile"""
        bars = []
        
        # Main bars
        # Length = pile length + projection into cap + development length at bottom
        anchorage_top = 0.5  # 500mm into cap
        anchorage_bottom = ISCodeCalculator.calculate_development_length(
            main_bar_dia, self.steel_grade, self.concrete_grade
        )
        
        main_bar_length = pile_length_m + anchorage_top + anchorage_bottom
        
        bars.append(BarShapeFactory.create_straight_bar(
            mark="P1",
            diameter_mm=main_bar_dia,
            length_m=main_bar_length,
            num_bars=num_main_bars,
            location="Pile Main Bars"
        ))
        
        # Spiral reinforcement
        bars.append(BarShapeFactory.create_spiral(
            mark="P2",
            diameter_mm=spiral_dia,
            pile_dia_m=pile_dia_m,
            pitch_m=spiral_pitch,
            pile_length_m=pile_length_m + 0.5,  # Include cap projection
            num_bars=num_main_bars,  # One spiral per pile
            location="Pile Spiral"
        ))
        
        # Centralizer bars (3 nos for alignment)
        bars.append(BarShapeFactory.create_straight_bar(
            mark="P3",
            diameter_mm=12,
            length_m=pile_length_m,
            num_bars=3,
            location="Pile Centralizer"
        ))
        
        return bars
    
    def calculate_well_reinforcement(
        self,
        external_dia_m: float,
        steining_thickness_m: float,
        sinking_depth_m: float,
        well_cap_thickness_m: float
    ) -> List[ReinforcementBar]:
        """Calculate reinforcement for well foundation"""
        bars = []
        
        internal_dia = external_dia_m - 2 * steining_thickness_m
        
        # Vertical steining bars
        num_vertical = int(external_dia_m * 3.14159 / 0.15)  # @ 150mm c/c
        
        # Full height including curb and cap
        bar_height = sinking_depth_m + 2.0 + well_cap_thickness_m
        
        bars.append(BarShapeFactory.create_straight_bar(
            mark="W1",
            diameter_mm=20,
            length_m=bar_height,
            num_bars=num_vertical,
            location="Well Steining Vertical"
        ))
        
        # Horizontal rings in steining
        num_rings = int(sinking_depth_m / 0.15)
        ring_length = external_dia_m * 3.14159
        
        bars.append(BarShapeFactory.create_straight_bar(
            mark="W2",
            diameter_mm=12,
            length_m=ring_length,
            num_bars=num_rings,
            location="Well Steining Rings"
        ))
        
        # Curb reinforcement (heavier)
        curb_bars = int(external_dia_m * 3.14159 / 0.10)  # @ 100mm c/c
        
        bars.append(BarShapeFactory.create_straight_bar(
            mark="W3",
            diameter_mm=25,
            length_m=2.5,  # Curb height + splay
            num_bars=curb_bars,
            location="Well Curb Reinforcement"
        ))
        
        # Bottom plug reinforcement
        plug_dia = internal_dia
        num_plug_bars = int(plug_dia * 3.14159 / 0.20)  # Radial @ 200mm c/c
        
        bars.append(BarShapeFactory.create_straight_bar(
            mark="W4",
            diameter_mm=16,
            length_m=plug_dia / 2 - 0.05,
            num_bars=num_plug_bars,
            location="Well Bottom Plug - Radial"
        ))
        
        # Well cap reinforcement
        cap_bars = int(external_dia_m * 3.14159 / 0.15)
        
        bars.append(BarShapeFactory.create_straight_bar(
            mark="W5",
            diameter_mm=25,
            length_m=external_dia_m + 1.0,  # Project beyond well
            num_bars=cap_bars,
            location="Well Cap Bottom"
        ))
        
        return bars
    
    def calculate_open_foundation_reinforcement(
        self,
        footing_length_m: float,
        footing_width_m: float,
        footing_depth_m: float,
        pedestal_length_m: float,
        pedestal_width_m: float,
        pedestal_height_m: float
    ) -> List[ReinforcementBar]:
        """Calculate reinforcement for open foundation"""
        bars = []
        
        cover = 0.075  # 75mm
        
        # Footing bottom mesh - Longitudinal
        long_span = footing_length_m - 2 * cover
        num_long = int(long_span / 0.15) + 1
        long_bar_len = footing_width_m - 2 * cover
        
        bars.append(BarShapeFactory.create_straight_bar(
            mark="OF1",
            diameter_mm=20,
            length_m=long_bar_len,
            num_bars=num_long,
            location="Footing Bottom - Longitudinal"
        ))
        
        # Footing bottom mesh - Transverse
        trans_span = footing_width_m - 2 * cover
        num_trans = int(trans_span / 0.15) + 1
        trans_bar_len = footing_length_m - 2 * cover
        
        bars.append(BarShapeFactory.create_straight_bar(
            mark="OF2",
            diameter_mm=20,
            length_m=trans_bar_len,
            num_bars=num_trans,
            location="Footing Bottom - Transverse"
        ))
        
        # Pedestal vertical bars
        num_ped_vertical = int(2 * (pedestal_length_m + pedestal_width_m) / 0.15)
        
        bars.append(BarShapeFactory.create_straight_bar(
            mark="OF3",
            diameter_mm=25,
            length_m=pedestal_height_m + 1.0,  # Development into footing
            num_bars=num_ped_vertical,
            location="Pedestal Vertical Bars"
        ))
        
        # Pedestal ties
        num_ties = int(pedestal_height_m / 0.15)
        
        bars.append(BarShapeFactory.create_rectangular_stirrup(
            mark="OF4",
            diameter_mm=10,
            width_m=pedestal_width_m,
            depth_m=pedestal_length_m,
            num_bars=num_ties,
            location="Pedestal Ties"
        ))
        
        return bars


# Example usage
if __name__ == "__main__":
    calc = FoundationReinforcementCalculator()
    
    # Pile cap reinforcement
    pile_cap_bars = calc.calculate_pile_cap_reinforcement(
        cap_length_m=10.0,
        cap_width_m=7.5,
        cap_thickness_m=2.0,
        pile_dia_m=1.2,
        num_piles=12
    )
    
    print("PILE CAP REINFORCEMENT")
    print("=" * 80)
    for bar in pile_cap_bars:
        row = bar.to_bbs_row()
        print(f"{row['Bar Mark']}: {row['Description']}")
        print(f"  {row['No. of Bars']} nos × {row['Cutting Length (m)']}m = {row['Total Length (m)']}m")
        print(f"  Weight: {row['Total Weight (kg)']} kg")
        print()
    
    # Pile reinforcement
    pile_bars = calc.calculate_pile_reinforcement(
        pile_dia_m=1.2,
        pile_length_m=15.0
    )
    
    print("\nPILE REINFORCEMENT")
    print("=" * 80)
    for bar in pile_bars:
        row = bar.to_bbs_row()
        print(f"{row['Bar Mark']}: {row['Description']}")
        print(f"  {row['No. of Bars']} nos × {row['Cutting Length (m)']}m = {row['Total Length (m)']}m")
        print(f"  Weight: {row['Total Weight (kg)']} kg")
        print()
```

## File 2: `reinforcement_drawing_generator.py`

```python
"""
Reinforcement Drawing Generator
Creates detailed reinforcement drawings with bar shapes and BBS tables
"""

import ezdxf
from ezdxf.enums import TextEntityAlignment
from ezdxf.math import Vec2
import math
from typing import List, Dict, Tuple
from reinforcement_calculator import ReinforcementBar, BarShape, BarProperties


class ReinforcementDrawingGenerator:
    """Generate reinforcement detail drawings"""
    
    LAYERS = {
        'REINFORCEMENT': {'color': 2, 'linetype': 'CONTINUOUS'},
        'CONCRETE': {'color': 1, 'linetype': 'CONTINUOUS'},
        'DIMENSION': {'color': 7, 'linetype': 'CONTINUOUS'},
        'TEXT': {'color': 7, 'linetype': 'CONTINUOUS'},
        'CENTERLINE': {'color': 6, 'linetype': 'CENTER'},
        'HATCH': {'color': 253, 'linetype': 'CONTINUOUS'},
        'TABLE': {'color': 5, 'linetype': 'CONTINUOUS'}
    }
    
    def __init__(self, scale: float = 10.0):
        self.scale = scale
        
        self.doc = ezdxf.new('R2018', setup=True)
        self.msp = self.doc.modelspace()
        self._setup_layers()
        
    def _setup_layers(self):
        """Create drawing layers"""
        for name, attrs in self.LAYERS.items():
            self.doc.layers.add(
                name=name,
                color=attrs['color'],
                linetype=attrs['linetype']
            )
    
    def _to_dxf(self, x: float, y: float) -> Tuple[float, float]:
        """Scale coordinates"""
        return (x * self.scale, y * self.scale)
    
    def draw_bar_shape(
        self,
        bar: ReinforcementBar,
        insert_point: Tuple[float, float],
        scale_factor: float = 1.0
    ):
        """Draw bar shape with dimensions"""
        x0, y0 = insert_point
        
        # Bar diameter for drawing
        bar_width = max(0.5, bar.diameter_mm / 20) * scale_factor
        
        current_x, current_y = x0, y0
        angle = 0  # Current angle in degrees
        
        points = [(current_x, current_y)]
        
        for i, segment in enumerate(bar.segments):
            if segment.is_bend:
                # Draw bend arc
                angle += segment.angle_degrees
            else:
                # Draw straight segment
                rad = math.radians(angle)
                end_x = current_x + segment.length_m * self.scale * scale_factor * math.cos(rad)
                end_y = current_y + segment.length_m * self.scale * scale_factor * math.sin(rad)
                
                # Draw bar line
                self.msp.add_line(
                    start=self._to_dxf(current_x, current_y),
                    end=self._to_dxf(end_x, end_y),
                    dxfattribs={
                        'layer': 'REINFORCEMENT',
                        'lineweight': bar_width * 100
                    }
                )
                
                # Add dimension
                if segment.length_m > 0.3:  # Only dimension significant lengths
                    mid_x = (current_x + end_x) / 2
                    mid_y = (current_y + end_y) / 2
                    
                    # Offset dimension text
                    offset = 1.5 * scale_factor
                    dim_x = mid_x + offset * math.sin(rad)
                    dim_y = mid_y - offset * math.cos(rad)
                    
                    self.msp.add_text(
                        f"{segment.length_m:.3f}",
                        height=1.5 * scale_factor,
                        rotation=angle,
                        dxfattribs={'layer': 'DIMENSION'}
                    ).set_placement(
                        self._to_dxf(dim_x, dim_y),
                        align=TextEntityAlignment.MIDDLE_CENTER
                    )
                
                current_x, current_y = end_x, end_y
                points.append((current_x, current_y))
        
        # Draw bar mark
        self.msp.add_text(
            bar.mark,
            height=3.0 * scale_factor,
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'TEXT', 'color': 4}
        ).set_placement(
            self._to_dxf(x0, y0 - 3),
            align=TextEntityAlignment.MIDDLE_CENTER
        )
        
        # Draw diameter symbol
        self.msp.add_text(
            f"Ø{bar.diameter_mm}",
            height=2.0 * scale_factor,
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'TEXT'}
        ).set_placement(
            self._to_dxf(x0, y0 - 5),
            align=TextEntityAlignment.MIDDLE_CENTER
        )
    
    def draw_pile_cap_reinforcement_plan(
        self,
        cap_length_m: float,
        cap_width_m: float,
        cap_thickness_m: float,
        bars: List[ReinforcementBar],
        pile_positions: List[Tuple[float, float]],
        pile_dia_m: float
    ):
        """Draw pile cap reinforcement plan"""
        
        # Draw cap outline
        cap_outline = [
            self._to_dxf(-cap_length_m/2, -cap_width_m/2),
            self._to_dxf(cap_length_m/2, -cap_width_m/2),
            self._to_dxf(cap_length_m/2, cap_width_m/2),
            self._to_dxf(-cap_length_m/2, cap_width_m/2),
            self._to_dxf(-cap_length_m/2, -cap_width_m/2)
        ]
        
        self.msp.add_lwpolyline(
            points=cap_outline,
            close=True,
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'CONCRETE'}
        )
        
        # Hatch concrete
        hatch = self.msp.add_hatch(color=253, dxfattribs={'layer': 'HATCH'})
        hatch.paths.add_polyline_path(cap_outline, is_closed=True)
        hatch.set_pattern_fill('AR-CONC', scale=0.2)
        
        # Draw piles
        for x, y in pile_positions:
            self.msp.add_circle(
                center=self._to_dxf(x, y),
                radius=pile_dia_m/2 * self.scale,
                dxfattribs={'layer': 'CONCRETE', 'color': 1}
            )
        
        # Draw reinforcement bars
        cover = 0.075
        
        # Group bars by location
        bottom_long = [b for b in bars if "Bottom" in b.location and "Long" in b.location]
        bottom_trans = [b for b in bars if "Bottom" in b.location and "Trans" in b.location]
        
        # Draw bottom longitudinal bars (simplified - show representative bars)
        for bar in bottom_long:
            num_bars = bar.num_bars
            spacing = (cap_width_m - 2 * cover) / (num_bars - 1) if num_bars > 1 else 0
            
            # Draw every 5th bar for clarity
            for i in range(0, num_bars, max(1, num_bars // 5)):
                y_pos = -cap_width_m/2 + cover + i * spacing
                self.msp.add_line(
                    start=self._to_dxf(-cap_length_m/2 + cover, y_pos),
                    end=self._to_dxf(cap_length_m/2 - cover, y_pos),
                    dxfattribs={'layer': 'REINFORCEMENT', 'color': 2}
                )
            
            # Add label
            self.msp.add_text(
                f"{bar.mark}: {num_bars}Ø{bar.diameter_mm} @ {spacing*1000:.0f}c/c",
                height=2.0,
                dxfattribs={'layer': 'TEXT'}
            ).set_placement(
                self._to_dxf(0, cap_width_m/2 + 3),
                align=TextEntityAlignment.MIDDLE_CENTER
            )
            break  # Only label first set
        
        # Draw bottom transverse bars
        for bar in bottom_trans:
            num_bars = bar.num_bars
            spacing = (cap_length_m - 2 * cover) / (num_bars - 1) if num_bars > 1 else 0
            
            for i in range(0, num_bars, max(1, num_bars // 5)):
                x_pos = -cap_length_m/2 + cover + i * spacing
                self.msp.add_line(
                    start=self._to_dxf(x_pos, -cap_width_m/2 + cover),
                    end=self._to_dxf(x_pos, cap_width_m/2 - cover),
                    dxfattribs={'layer': 'REINFORCEMENT', 'color': 2}
                )
            
            self.msp.add_text(
                f"{bar.mark}: {num_bars}Ø{bar.diameter_mm} @ {spacing*1000:.0f}c/c",
                height=2.0,
                rotation=90,
                dxfattribs={'layer': 'TEXT'}
            ).set_placement(
                self._to_dxf(cap_length_m/2 + 4, 0),
                align=TextEntityAlignment.MIDDLE_CENTER
            )
            break
        
        # Draw centerlines
        self.msp.add_line(
            start=self._to_dxf(0, -cap_width_m/2 - 2),
            end=self._to_dxf(0, cap_width_m/2 + 2),
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'CENTERLINE'}
        )
        self.msp.add_line(
            start=self._to_dxf(-cap_length_m/2 - 2, 0),
            end=self._to_dxf(cap_length_m/2 + 2, 0),
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'CENTERLINE'}
        )
        
        # Title
        self._draw_title("PILE CAP REINFORCEMENT PLAN")
    
    def draw_pile_cap_section(
        self,
        cap_thickness_m: float,
        cap_width_m: float,
        bars: List[ReinforcementBar],
        pile_dia_m: float
    ):
        """Draw pile cap section showing reinforcement"""
        
        cover = 0.075
        
        # Draw section outline
        section_points = [
            self._to_dxf(-cap_width_m/2, 0),
            self._to_dxf(cap_width_m/2, 0),
            self._to_dxf(cap_width_m/2, cap_thickness_m),
            self._to_dxf(-cap_width_m/2, cap_thickness_m),
            self._to_dxf(-cap_width_m/2, 0)
        ]
        
        self.msp.add_lwpolyline(
            points=section_points,
            close=True,
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'CONCRETE'}
        )
        
        # Hatch
        hatch = self.msp.add_hatch(color=253, dxfattribs={'layer': 'HATCH'})
        hatch.paths.add_polyline_path(section_points, is_closed=True)
        hatch.set_pattern_fill('AR-CONC', scale=0.2)
        
        # Draw bottom reinforcement
        bottom_bars = [b for b in bars if "Bottom" in b.location]
        
        for bar_group in bottom_bars:
            y_pos = cover + bar_group.diameter_mm / 2000  # Center of bar
            
            # Representative bars
            for x in [-cap_width_m/4, 0, cap_width_m/4]:
                self.msp.add_circle(
                    center=self._to_dxf(x, y_pos),
                    radius=bar_group.diameter_mm / 2000 * self.scale,
                    dxfattribs={'layer': 'REINFORCEMENT'}
                )
        
        # Draw top reinforcement if present
        top_bars = [b for b in bars if "Top" in b.location]
        
        for bar_group in top_bars:
            y_pos = cap_thickness_m - cover - bar_group.diameter_mm / 2000
            
            for x in [-cap_width_m/4, 0, cap_width_m/4]:
                self.msp.add_circle(
                    center=self._to_dxf(x, y_pos),
                    radius=bar_group.diameter_mm / 2000 * self.scale,
                    dxfattribs={'layer': 'REINFORCEMENT'}
                )
        
        # Draw side face reinforcement if present
        side_bars = [b for b in bars if "Side" in b.location]
        
        for bar_group in side_bars:
            x_pos = cover + bar_group.diameter_mm / 2000
            
            self.msp.add_line(
                start=self._to_dxf(x_pos, cover),
                end=self._to_dxf(x_pos, cap_thickness_m - cover),
                dxfattribs={'layer': 'REINFORCEMENT'}
            )
            
            self.msp.add_line(
                start=self._to_dxf(-x_pos, cover),
                end=self._to_dxf(-x_pos, cap_thickness_m - cover),
                dxfattribs={'layer': 'REINFORCEMENT'}
            )
        
        # Section label
        self.msp.add_text(
            "SECTION A-A",
            height=2.5,
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'TEXT'}
        ).set_placement(
            self._to_dxf(0, -3),
            align=TextEntityAlignment.MIDDLE_CENTER
        )
        
        # Title
        self._draw_title("PILE CAP SECTION")
    
    def draw_bar_bending_schedule_table(
        self,
        bars: List[ReinforcementBar],
        insert_point: Tuple[float, float] = (0, -15)
    ):
        """Draw BBS table on drawing"""
        x0, y0 = insert_point
        
        # Table columns
        col_widths = [8, 25, 8, 10, 12, 12, 12]  # Approximate widths
        headers = ['Mark', 'Description', 'Dia', 'No.', 'Cut Len', 'Tot Len', 'Weight']
        
        row_height = 3
        header_height = 4
        
        # Draw table header
        current_x = x0
        for i, header in enumerate(headers):
            # Header cell
            cell_points = [
                self._to_dxf(current_x, y0),
                self._to_dxf(current_x + col_widths[i], y0),
                self._to_dxf(current_x + col_widths[i], y0 + header_height),
                self._to_dxf(current_x, y0 + header_height),
                self._to_dxf(current_x, y0)
            ]
            
            self.msp.add_lwpolyline(
                points=cell_points,
                close=True,
                dxfattribs={'layer': 'TABLE'}
            )
            
            # Header text
            self.msp.add_text(
                header,
                height=2.0,
                dxfattribs={'layer': 'TEXT'}
            ).set_placement(
                self._to_dxf(current_x + col_widths[i]/2, y0 + header_height/2),
                align=TextEntityAlignment.MIDDLE_CENTER
            )
            
            current_x += col_widths[i]
        
        # Draw data rows
        y = y0 - row_height
        
        for bar in bars:
            row = bar.to_bbs_row()
            current_x = x0
            
            values = [
                row['Bar Mark'],
                row['Description'][:20],
                f"Ø{row['Dia (mm)']}",
                str(row['No. of Bars']),
                f"{row['Cutting Length (m)']:.2f}",
                f"{row['Total Length (m)']:.1f}",
                f"{row['Total Weight (kg)']:.1f}"
            ]
            
            for i, value in enumerate(values):
                # Cell border
                cell_points = [
                    self._to_dxf(current_x, y),
                    self._to_dxf(current_x + col_widths[i], y),
                    self._to_dxf(current_x + col_widths[i], y + row_height),
                    self._to_dxf(current_x, y + row_height),
                    self._to_dxf(current_x, y)
                ]
                
                self.msp.add_lwpolyline(
                    points=cell_points,
                    close=True,
                    dxfattribs={'layer': 'TABLE'}
                )
                
                # Cell text
                self.msp.add_text(
                    str(value),
                    height=1.8,
                    dxfattribs={'layer': 'TEXT'}
                ).set_placement(
                    self._to_dxf(current_x + col_widths[i]/2, y + row_height/2),
                    align=TextEntityAlignment.MIDDLE_CENTER
                )
                
                current_x += col_widths[i]
            
            y -= row_height
        
        # Total row
        total_weight = sum(b.total_weight_kg for b in bars)
        
        current_x = x0
        for i, col_width in enumerate(col_widths):
            if i == len(col_widths) - 1:
                # Last column - total weight
                self.msp.add_text(
                    f"TOTAL: {total_weight:.1f} kg",
                    height=2.0,
                    dxfattribs={'layer': 'TEXT', 'color': 1}
                ).set_placement(
                    self._to_dxf(current_x + col_width/2, y + row_height/2),
                    align=TextEntityAlignment.MIDDLE_CENTER
                )
            elif i == 0:
                self.msp.add_text(
                    "TOTAL",
                    height=2.0,
                    dxfattribs={'layer': 'TEXT', 'color': 1}
                ).set_placement(
                    self._to_dxf(current_x + col_width/2, y + row_height/2),
                    align=TextEntityAlignment.MIDDLE_CENTER
                )
            
            current_x += col_width
    
    def draw_bar_shapes_legend(
        self,
        bars: List[ReinforcementBar],
        insert_point: Tuple[float, float] = (20, 10)
    ):
        """Draw bar shapes legend"""
        x0, y0 = insert_point
        
        self.msp.add_text(
            "BAR SHAPES",
            height=3.0,
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'TEXT', 'color': 5}
        ).set_placement(
            self._to_dxf(x0, y0 + 5),
            align=TextEntityAlignment.MIDDLE_LEFT
        )
        
        y = y0
        
        unique_shapes = {}
        for bar in bars:
            if bar.shape not in unique_shapes:
                unique_shapes[bar.shape] = bar
        
        for shape, bar in unique_shapes.items():
            # Draw bar shape
            self.draw_bar_shape(bar, (x0, y), scale_factor=0.5)
            
            # Description
            self.msp.add_text(
                f"{bar.mark}: {bar.description}",
                height=2.0,
                dxfattribs={'layer': 'TEXT'}
            ).set_placement(
                self._to_dxf(x0 + 10, y),
                align=TextEntityAlignment.MIDDLE_LEFT
            )
            
            y -= 8
    
    def _draw_title(self, title_text: str):
        """Draw title"""
        self.msp.add_text(
            title_text,
            height=3.0,
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'TEXT', 'color': 5}
        ).set_placement(
            self._to_dxf(0, -12),
            align=TextEntityAlignment.MIDDLE_CENTER
        )
    
    def save(self, filename: str):
        """Save DXF file"""
        self.doc.saveas(filename)
        print(f"Reinforcement drawing saved: {filename}")


# Example usage
if __name__ == "__main__":
    from reinforcement_calculator import FoundationReinforcementCalculator
    
    # Calculate reinforcement
    calc = FoundationReinforcementCalculator()
    
    bars = calc.calculate_pile_cap_reinforcement(
        cap_length_m=10.0,
        cap_width_m=7.5,
        cap_thickness_m=2.0,
        pile_dia_m=1.2,
        num_piles=12
    )
    
    # Generate drawing
    drawing = ReinforcementDrawingGenerator(scale=8.0)
    
    pile_positions = []
    for i in range(4):
        for j in range(3):
            x = -3.75 + i * 2.5
            y = -2.5 + j * 2.5
            pile_positions.append((x, y))
    
    drawing.draw_pile_cap_reinforcement_plan(
        cap_length_m=10.0,
        cap_width_m=7.5,
        cap_thickness_m=2.0,
        bars=bars,
        pile_positions=pile_positions,
        pile_dia_m=1.2
    )
    
    drawing.draw_pile_cap_section(
        cap_thickness_m=2.0,
        cap_width_m=7.5,
        bars=bars,
        pile_dia_m=1.2
    )
    
    drawing.draw_bar_bending_schedule_table(bars)
    drawing.draw_bar_shapes_legend(bars)
    
    drawing.save("pile_cap_reinforcement.dxf")
```

## File 3: `bbs_exporter.py`

```python
"""
Bar Bending Schedule Excel Exporter
Exports BBS to formatted Excel with IS:2502 compliance
"""

import pandas as pd
from typing import List, Dict
from openpyxl import Workbook
from openpyxl.styles import Font, Alignment, Border, Side, PatternFill
from openpyxl.utils.dataframe import dataframe_to_rows
from reinforcement_calculator import ReinforcementBar


class BBSExporter:
    """Export Bar Bending Schedule to Excel"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.wb = Workbook()
        self.ws = self.wb.active
        self.ws.title = "BBS"
        
        # Styles
        self.header_font = Font(bold=True, size=11, color="FFFFFF")
        self.header_fill = PatternFill(start_color="366092", end_color="366092", fill_type="solid")
        self.subheader_fill = PatternFill(start_color="95B3D7", end_color="95B3D7", fill_type="solid")
        self.total_fill = PatternFill(start_color="FFFF00", end_color="FFFF00", fill_type="solid")
        self.border = Border(
            left=Side(style='thin'),
            right=Side(style='thin'),
            top=Side(style='thin'),
            bottom=Side(style='thin')
        )
        self.center_align = Alignment(horizontal='center', vertical='center')
    
    def export_bbs(
        self,
        bars: List[ReinforcementBar],
        project_info: Dict,
        output_path: str = "bar_bending_schedule.xlsx"
    ):
        """Export complete BBS to Excel"""
        
        # Project header
        self._add_project_header(project_info)
        
        # BBS table
        self._add_bbs_table(bars)
        
        # Summary
        self._add_summary(bars)
        
        # Notes
        self._add_notes()
        
        # Save
        self.wb.save(output_path)
        print(f"BBS exported: {output_path}")
        return output_path
    
    def _add_project_header(self, info: Dict):
        """Add project information header"""
        row = 1
        
        # Title
        self.ws.merge_cells('A1:I1')
        cell = self.ws['A1']
        cell.value = "BAR BENDING SCHEDULE"
        cell.font = Font(bold=True, size=16)
        cell.alignment = Alignment(horizontal='center')
        
        # Project info
        row = 3
        self.ws[f'A{row}'] = "Project:"
        self.ws[f'B{row}'] = info.get('project_name', '')
        self.ws[f'D{row}'] = "Drawing No:"
        self.ws[f'E{row}'] = info.get('drawing_no', '')
        
        row += 1
        self.ws[f'A{row}'] = "Structure:"
        self.ws[f'B{row}'] = info.get('structure', '')
        self.ws[f'D{row}'] = "Revision:"
        self.ws[f'E{row}'] = info.get('revision', 'A')
        
        row += 1
        self.ws[f'A{row}'] = "Date:"
        self.ws[f'B{row}'] = info.get('date', '')
        self.ws[f'D{row}'] = "Prepared By:"
        self.ws[f'E{row}'] = info.get('prepared_by', '')
        
        self.ws[f'G{row}'] = "Checked By:"
        self.ws[f'H{row}'] = info.get('checked_by', '')
        
        self._blank_row = row + 2
    
    def _add_bbs_table(self, bars: List[ReinforcementBar]):
        """Add main BBS table"""
        start_row = self._blank_row
        
        # Column headers per IS:2502
        headers = [
            'S.No.',
            'Bar Mark',
            'Description',
            'Shape',
            'Dia\n(mm)',
            'No. of\nBars',
            'Cutting\nLength\n(m)',
            'Total\nLength\n(m)',
            'Unit\nWeight\n(kg/m)',
            'Weight per\nBar (kg)',
            'Total\nWeight\n(kg)',
            'Location',
            'Remarks'
        ]
        
        # Write headers
        for col, header in enumerate(headers, 1):
            cell = self.ws.cell(row=start_row, column=col, value=header)
            cell.font = self.header_font
            cell.fill = self.header_fill
            cell.alignment = Alignment(horizontal='center', vertical='center', wrap_text=True)
            cell.border = self.border
        
        # Write data
        for i, bar in enumerate(bars, 1):
            row = start_row + i
            data = bar.to_bbs_row()
            
            values = [
                i,
                data['Bar Mark'],
                data['Description'],
                data['Shape'],
                data['Dia (mm)'],
                data['No. of Bars'],
                data['Cutting Length (m)'],
                data['Total Length (m)'],
                data['Unit Weight (kg/m)'],
                data['Weight per Bar (kg)'],
                data['Total Weight (kg)'],
                data['Location'],
                data['Remarks']
            ]
            
            for col, value in enumerate(values, 1):
                cell = self.ws.cell(row=row, column=col, value=value)
                cell.border = self.border
                cell.alignment = self.center_align if col <= 11 else Alignment(horizontal='left')
        
        self._data_end_row = start_row + len(bars)
    
    def _add_summary(self, bars: List[ReinforcementBar]):
        """Add summary section"""
        row = self._data_end_row + 2
        
        # Group by diameter
        dia_summary = {}
        for bar in bars:
            dia = bar.diameter_mm
            if dia not in dia_summary:
                dia_summary[dia] = {'length': 0, 'weight': 0}
            dia_summary[dia]['length'] += bar.total_length_m
            dia_summary[dia]['weight'] += bar.total_weight_kg
        
        # Summary header
        self.ws.merge_cells(f'A{row}:D{row}')
        cell = self.ws[f'A{row}']
        cell.value = "SUMMARY BY DIAMETER"
        cell.font = Font(bold=True, size=12)
        cell.fill = self.subheader_fill
        
        row += 1
        
        # Summary headers
        summary_headers = ['Diameter (mm)', 'Total Length (m)', 'Total Weight (kg)', '% of Total']
        total_weight = sum(d['weight'] for d in dia_summary.values())
        
        for col, header in enumerate(summary_headers, 1):
            cell = self.ws.cell(row=row, column=col, value=header)
            cell.font = Font(bold=True)
            cell.fill = self.subheader_fill
            cell.border = self.border
        
        # Summary data
        for dia in sorted(dia_summary.keys()):
            row += 1
            data = dia_summary[dia]
            pct = (data['weight'] / total_weight * 100) if total_weight > 0 else 0
            
            values = [f"Ø{dia}", round(data['length'], 2), round(data['weight'], 2), f"{pct:.1f}%"]
            
            for col, value in enumerate(values, 1):
                cell = self.ws.cell(row=row, column=col, value=value)
                cell.border = self.border
                cell.alignment = self.center_align
        
        # Grand total
        row += 1
        self.ws.cell(row=row, column=1, value="GRAND TOTAL").font = Font(bold=True)
        self.ws.cell(row=row, column=3, value=round(total_weight, 2)).font = Font(bold=True)
        self.ws.cell(row=row, column=3).fill = self.total_fill
        
        for col in range(1, 5):
            self.ws.cell(row=row, column=col).border = self.border
        
        # Add to main table
        row += 2
        self.ws.merge_cells(f'A{row}:I{row}')
        self.ws[f'A{row}'] = f"TOTAL STEEL: {round(total_weight/1000, 3)} MT"
        self.ws[f'A{row}'].font = Font(bold=True, size=12)
        self.ws[f'A{row}'].alignment = Alignment(horizontal='right')
    
    def _add_notes(self):
        """Add standard notes"""
        row = self.ws.max_row + 3
        
        notes = [
            "NOTES:",
            "1. All dimensions are in millimeters unless otherwise specified.",
            "2. Cutting lengths calculated as per IS:2502.",
            "3. Development lengths as per IS:456.",
            "4. Bend deductions: 45°=1d, 90°=2d, 135°=2.5d, 180°=3d (approx).",
            "5. Hook lengths: 90°=12d, 135°=16d, 180°=8d+semicircle.",
            "6. Concrete grade: M25, Steel grade: Fe500.",
            "7. Cover to reinforcement: 75mm for foundations, 50mm for piles."
        ]
        
        for note in notes:
            self.ws.cell(row=row, column=1, value=note)
            if note == "NOTES:":
                self.ws.cell(row=row, column=1).font = Font(bold=True)
            row += 1
    
    def export_multiple_foundation_bbs(
        self,
        foundation_bars: Dict[str, List[ReinforcementBar]],
        project_info: Dict,
        output_path: str = "foundation_bbs.xlsx"
    ):
        """Export BBS for multiple foundations with separate sheets"""
        
        with pd.ExcelWriter(output_path, engine='openpyxl') as writer:
            # Summary sheet
            summary_data = []
            
            for foundation_name, bars in foundation_bars.items():
                # Individual foundation sheet
                df = pd.DataFrame([b.to_bbs_row() for b in bars])
                df.to_excel(writer, sheet_name=foundation_name[:31], index=False)
                
                # Add to summary
                total_weight = sum(b.total_weight_kg for b in bars)
                total_length = sum(b.total_length_m for b in bars)
                
                summary_data.append({
                    'Foundation': foundation_name,
                    'No. of Bar Marks': len(bars),
                    'Total Length (m)': round(total_length, 2),
                    'Total Weight (kg)': round(total_weight, 2),
                    'Weight (MT)': round(total_weight/1000, 3)
                })
            
            # Summary sheet
            df_summary = pd.DataFrame(summary_data)
            df_summary.to_excel(writer, sheet_name='SUMMARY', index=False)
            
            # Grand total
            grand_total = df_summary['Weight (MT)'].sum()
            
            # Add grand total row
            summary_total = pd.DataFrame([{
                'Foundation': 'GRAND TOTAL',
                'No. of Bar Marks': '',
                'Total Length (m)': '',
                'Total Weight (kg)': '',
                'Weight (MT)': round(grand_total, 3)
            }])
            
            # Append to summary
            start_row = len(df_summary) + 2
            summary_total.to_excel(writer, sheet_name='SUMMARY', 
                                  startrow=start_row, index=False, header=False)
        
        print(f"Multi-foundation BBS exported: {output_path}")
        return output_path


# Example usage
if __name__ == "__main__":
    from reinforcement_calculator import FoundationReinforcementCalculator
    
    calc = FoundationReinforcementCalculator()
    
    # Calculate for multiple foundations
    foundations = {
        'PIER1_PILE_CAP': calc.calculate_pile_cap_reinforcement(10.0, 7.5, 2.0, 1.2, 12),
        'PIER2_PILE_CAP': calc.calculate_pile_cap_reinforcement(10.0, 7.5, 2.0, 1.2, 12),
        'ABUT1_WELL': calc.calculate_well_reinforcement(6.0, 0.6, 12.0, 2.0)
    }
    
    # Export combined
    exporter = BBSExporter()
    
    project_info = {
        'project_name': 'Bridge over River XYZ',
        'structure': 'Multi-Span Bridge',
        'drawing_no': 'BR-01-FOUND-01',
        'revision': 'A',
        'date': '2024-01-15',
        'prepared_by': 'Engr. ABC',
        'checked_by': 'Engr. XYZ'
    }
    
    # Export individual foundation
    all_bars = []
    for bars in foundations.values():
        all_bars.extend(bars)
    
    exporter.export_bbs(all_bars, project_info, "complete_bbs.xlsx")
    
    # Export multiple foundations
    exporter.export_multiple_foundation_bbs(foundations, project_info, "foundation_bbs.xlsx")
```

## File 4: `reinforcement_integration.py`

```python
"""
Integration module - connects reinforcement with foundation drawings
"""

from typing import List, Dict
from foundation_generator import FoundationModel, FoundationType
from reinforcement_calculator import (
    FoundationReinforcementCalculator, ReinforcementBar
)
from reinforcement_drawing_generator import ReinforcementDrawingGenerator
from bbs_exporter import BBSExporter


class ReinforcementIntegration:
    """Integrate reinforcement with foundation drawings"""
    
    def __init__(self, concrete_grade=None, steel_grade=None):
        self.calculator = FoundationReinforcementCalculator(concrete_grade, steel_grade)
    
    def generate_complete_foundation_package(
        self,
        foundation: FoundationModel,
        output_prefix: str = "foundation"
    ) -> Dict:
        """
        Generate complete foundation package:
        - Geometry drawings
        - Reinforcement drawings
        - Bar bending schedule
        """
        
        results = {
            'foundation_type': foundation.foundation_type.value,
            'support': f"{foundation.support_type}_{foundation.support_number}",
            'files': []
        }
        
        # Calculate reinforcement
        bars = self._calculate_reinforcement(foundation)
        results['total_bars'] = len(bars)
        results['total_steel_kg'] = sum(b.total_weight_kg for b in bars)
        
        # Generate geometry drawing
        from foundation_generator import FoundationDetailDrawing
        
        geom_drawing = FoundationDetailDrawing(foundation, scale=8.0)
        
        if foundation.foundation_type == FoundationType.PILE:
            geom_drawing.generate_pile_layout_plan()
            geom_drawing.generate_pile_section()
            geom_file = f"{output_prefix}_geometry.dxf"
        
        elif foundation.foundation_type == FoundationType.WELL:
            geom_drawing.generate_well_plan()
            geom_drawing.generate_well_section()
            geom_file = f"{output_prefix}_geometry.dxf"
        
        geom_drawing.save(geom_file)
        results['files'].append(geom_file)
        
        # Generate reinforcement drawing
        reinf_drawing = ReinforcementDrawingGenerator(scale=8.0)
        
        if foundation.foundation_type == FoundationType.PILE:
            pc = foundation.pile_config
            
            # Get pile positions
            pile_positions = pc.get_pile_positions()
            
            # Pile cap reinforcement plan
            cap_bars = [b for b in bars if "Cap" in b.location]
            
            reinf_drawing.draw_pile_cap_reinforcement_plan(
                cap_length_m=pc.cap_length,
                cap_width_m=pc.cap_width,
                cap_thickness_m=pc.cap_thickness,
                bars=cap_bars,
                pile_positions=pile_positions,
                pile_dia_m=foundation.pile_dims.diameter
            )
            
            # Section
            reinf_drawing.draw_pile_cap_section(
                cap_thickness_m=pc.cap_thickness,
                cap_width_m=pc.cap_width,
                bars=cap_bars,
                pile_dia_m=foundation.pile_dims.diameter
            )
            
            # BBS table
            reinf_drawing.draw_bar_bending_schedule_table(cap_bars)
            reinf_drawing.draw_bar_shapes_legend(cap_bars)
            
            reinf_file = f"{output_prefix}_reinforcement.dxf"
            reinf_drawing.save(reinf_file)
            results['files'].append(reinf_file)
        
        # Export BBS to Excel
        exporter = BBSExporter()
        
        project_info = {
            'project_name': 'Bridge Foundation',
            'structure': f"{foundation.support_type} #{foundation.support_number}",
            'drawing_no': f"{output_prefix.upper()}-01",
            'date': '2024-01-15'
        }
        
        bbs_file = f"{output_prefix}_bbs.xlsx"
        exporter.export_bbs(bars, project_info, bbs_file)
        results['files'].append(bbs_file)
        
        return results
    
    def _calculate_reinforcement(
        self,
        foundation: FoundationModel
    ) -> List[ReinforcementBar]:
        """Calculate all reinforcement for foundation"""
        
        all_bars = []
        
        if foundation.foundation_type == FoundationType.PILE:
            pc = foundation.pile_config
            pd = foundation.pile_dims
            
            # Pile cap reinforcement
            cap_bars = self.calculator.calculate_pile_cap_reinforcement(
                cap_length_m=pc.cap_length,
                cap_width_m=pc.cap_width,
                cap_thickness_m=pc.cap_thickness,
                pile_dia_m=pd.diameter,
                num_piles=pc.total_piles
            )
            all_bars.extend(cap_bars)
            
            # Pile reinforcement (per pile, multiply by number)
            pile_bars = self.calculator.calculate_pile_reinforcement(
                pile_dia_m=pd.diameter,
                pile_length_m=pd.length,
                main_bar_dia=pd.main_bar_dia,
                num_main_bars=pd.num_main_bars,
                spiral_dia=pd.spiral_dia,
                spiral_pitch=pd.spiral_spacing
            )
            
            # Multiply pile bars by number of piles
            for bar in pile_bars:
                bar.num_bars *= pc.total_piles
            
            all_bars.extend(pile_bars)
        
        elif foundation.foundation_type == FoundationType.WELL:
            wd = foundation.well_dims
            
            well_bars = self.calculator.calculate_well_reinforcement(
                external_dia_m=wd.external_dia,
                steining_thickness_m=wd.steining_thickness,
                sinking_depth_m=wd.sinking_depth,
                well_cap_thickness_m=wd.well_cap_thickness
            )
            all_bars.extend(well_bars)
        
        return all_bars
    
    def generate_project_bbs(
        self,
        foundations: List[FoundationModel],
        output_path: str = "project_bbs.xlsx"
    ):
        """Generate combined BBS for entire project"""
        
        foundation_bars = {}
        
        for foundation in foundations:
            name = f"{foundation.support_type}{foundation.support_number}_{foundation.foundation_type.value}"
            bars = self._calculate_reinforcement(foundation)
            foundation_bars[name] = bars
        
        exporter = BBSExporter()
        
        project_info = {
            'project_name': 'Bridge Project',
            'structure': 'Multi-Span Bridge',
            'drawing_no': 'BR-ALL-FOUND',
            'revision': 'A'
        }
        
        exporter.export_multiple_foundation_bbs(
            foundation_bars,
            project_info,
            output_path
        )
        
        # Calculate totals
        total_steel = sum(
            sum(b.total_weight_kg for b in bars)
            for bars in foundation_bars.values()
        )
        
        print(f"\nProject BBS Summary:")
        print(f"  Foundations: {len(foundations)}")
        print(f"  Total Steel: {total_steel/1000:.3f} MT")
        print(f"  Exported to: {output_path}")


# Example usage
if __name__ == "__main__":
    from foundation_generator import create_pile_foundation, create_well_foundation
    
    # Create foundations
    foundations = [
        create_pile_foundation("PIER", 1, 4, 3, 1.2, 15.0),
        create_pile_foundation("PIER", 2, 4, 3, 1.2, 18.0),
        create_well_foundation("ABUTMENT", 1, 6.0, 12.0)
    ]
    
    # Generate complete packages
    integration = ReinforcementIntegration()
    
    for foundation in foundations:
        print(f"\nGenerating package for {foundation.support_type} #{foundation.support_number}...")
        
        results = integration.generate_complete_foundation_package(
            foundation,
            output_prefix=f"{foundation.support_type.lower()}{foundation.support_number}"
        )
        
        print(f"  Generated {len(results['files'])} files")
        print(f"  Total steel: {results['total_steel_kg']:.1f} kg")
    
    # Generate project-wide BBS
    integration.generate_project_bbs(foundations, "bridge_project_bbs.xlsx")
```

---

## KEY FEATURES

| Feature | IS Code Reference | Description |
|---------|------------------|-------------|
| **Development Length** | IS:456 Clause 26.2.1 | Ld = (φ × σs) / (4 × τbd) |
| **Hook Lengths** | IS:456 Clause 26.2.2.1 | 90°=12d, 135°=16d, 180°=8d+πd/2 |
| **Bend Deductions** | IS:2502 | 45°=1d, 90°=2d, 135°=2.5d |
| **Lap Length** | IS:456 | 30d (tension), 24d (compression) |
| **Stirrup Spacing** | IS:456 | 0.75d or 300mm max |
| **Cover** | IS:456 | 75mm foundations, 50mm piles |
