# 3D BRIDGE MODEL GENERATOR

Complete 3D visualization with multi-format export capabilities.

## File 1: `bridge_3d_geometry.py`

```python
"""
3D Bridge Geometry Module
Defines 3D primitives and bridge component generators
"""

import numpy as np
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import List, Tuple, Dict, Optional, Union
from enum import Enum
import math


class MaterialType(Enum):
    """Bridge materials for visualization"""
    CONCRETE = "concrete"
    STEEL = "steel"
    ASPHALT = "asphalt"
    REINFORCEMENT = "reinforcement"
    SOIL = "soil"
    WATER = "water"
    BEARING = "bearing"


@dataclass
class Vertex:
    """3D vertex with normal and UV coordinates"""
    x: float
    y: float
    z: float
    nx: float = 0.0  # Normal X
    ny: float = 0.0  # Normal Y
    nz: float = 1.0  # Normal Z
    u: float = 0.0   # Texture U
    v: float = 0.0   # Texture V
    
    def to_tuple(self) -> Tuple[float, float, float]:
        return (self.x, self.y, self.z)
    
    def to_array(self) -> np.ndarray:
        return np.array([self.x, self.y, self.z])


@dataclass
class Triangle:
    """Triangle face with material"""
    v1: int  # Vertex indices
    v2: int
    v3: int
    material: MaterialType = MaterialType.CONCRETE
    normal: Optional[Tuple[float, float, float]] = None


@dataclass
class Mesh:
    """3D mesh with vertices and faces"""
    vertices: List[Vertex] = field(default_factory=list)
    triangles: List[Triangle] = field(default_factory=list)
    material: MaterialType = MaterialType.CONCRETE
    name: str = "mesh"
    
    def add_vertex(self, x: float, y: float, z: float, 
                   nx: float = 0, ny: float = 0, nz: float = 1,
                   u: float = 0, v: float = 0) -> int:
        """Add vertex and return index"""
        self.vertices.append(Vertex(x, y, z, nx, ny, nz, u, v))
        return len(self.vertices) - 1
    
    def add_triangle(self, v1: int, v2: int, v3: int, 
                     material: MaterialType = None):
        """Add triangle face"""
        if material is None:
            material = self.material
        self.triangles.append(Triangle(v1, v2, v3, material))
    
    def add_quad(self, v1: int, v2: int, v3: int, v4: int,
                 material: MaterialType = None):
        """Add quad as two triangles"""
        self.add_triangle(v1, v2, v3, material)
        self.add_triangle(v1, v3, v4, material)
    
    def translate(self, dx: float, dy: float, dz: float):
        """Translate mesh"""
        for v in self.vertices:
            v.x += dx
            v.y += dy
            v.z += dz
    
    def rotate_z(self, angle_deg: float, cx: float = 0, cy: float = 0):
        """Rotate around Z axis"""
        angle = math.radians(angle_deg)
        cos_a = math.cos(angle)
        sin_a = math.sin(angle)
        
        for v in self.vertices:
            # Translate to origin
            x = v.x - cx
            y = v.y - cy
            
            # Rotate
            v.x = x * cos_a - y * sin_a + cx
            v.y = x * sin_a + y * cos_a + cy
    
    def get_bounds(self) -> Tuple[Tuple[float, float, float], Tuple[float, float, float]]:
        """Get bounding box (min, max)"""
        if not self.vertices:
            return ((0, 0, 0), (0, 0, 0))
        
        xs = [v.x for v in self.vertices]
        ys = [v.y for v in self.vertices]
        zs = [v.z for v in self.vertices]
        
        return ((min(xs), min(ys), min(zs)), (max(xs), max(ys), max(zs)))
    
    def merge(self, other: 'Mesh'):
        """Merge another mesh into this one"""
        offset = len(self.vertices)
        self.vertices.extend(other.vertices)
        
        for tri in other.triangles:
            self.triangles.append(Triangle(
                tri.v1 + offset,
                tri.v2 + offset,
                tri.v3 + offset,
                tri.material
            ))


class PrimitiveFactory:
    """Factory for 3D primitives"""
    
    @staticmethod
    def create_box(length: float, width: float, height: float,
                   material: MaterialType = MaterialType.CONCRETE) -> Mesh:
        """Create rectangular box"""
        mesh = Mesh(material=material, name="box")
        
        # Half dimensions
        hl, hw, hh = length / 2, width / 2, height / 2
        
        # Vertices (8 corners)
        vertices = [
            (-hl, -hw, -hh), (hl, -hw, -hh), (hl, hw, -hh), (-hl, hw, -hh),  # Bottom
            (-hl, -hw, hh), (hl, -hw, hh), (hl, hw, hh), (-hl, hw, hh)        # Top
        ]
        
        # Add vertices
        indices = []
        for v in vertices:
            idx = mesh.add_vertex(v[0], v[1], v[2])
            indices.append(idx)
        
        # Faces (6 sides, 2 triangles each)
        # Bottom
        mesh.add_triangle(indices[0], indices[2], indices[1], material)
        mesh.add_triangle(indices[0], indices[3], indices[2], material)
        
        # Top
        mesh.add_triangle(indices[4], indices[5], indices[6], material)
        mesh.add_triangle(indices[4], indices[6], indices[7], material)
        
        # Front
        mesh.add_triangle(indices[0], indices[1], indices[5], material)
        mesh.add_triangle(indices[0], indices[5], indices[4], material)
        
        # Back
        mesh.add_triangle(indices[2], indices[3], indices[7], material)
        mesh.add_triangle(indices[2], indices[7], indices[6], material)
        
        # Left
        mesh.add_triangle(indices[0], indices[4], indices[7], material)
        mesh.add_triangle(indices[0], indices[7], indices[3], material)
        
        # Right
        mesh.add_triangle(indices[1], indices[2], indices[6], material)
        mesh.add_triangle(indices[1], indices[6], indices[5], material)
        
        return mesh
    
    @staticmethod
    def create_cylinder(radius: float, height: float, segments: int = 32,
                        material: MaterialType = MaterialType.CONCRETE) -> Mesh:
        """Create cylinder"""
        mesh = Mesh(material=material, name="cylinder")
        
        # Center vertices
        bottom_center = mesh.add_vertex(0, 0, 0)
        top_center = mesh.add_vertex(0, 0, height)
        
        # Ring vertices
        bottom_ring = []
        top_ring = []
        
        for i in range(segments):
            angle = 2 * math.pi * i / segments
            x = radius * math.cos(angle)
            y = radius * math.sin(angle)
            
            bottom_ring.append(mesh.add_vertex(x, y, 0))
            top_ring.append(mesh.add_vertex(x, y, height))
        
        # Side faces
        for i in range(segments):
            next_i = (i + 1) % segments
            
            # Side quad
            mesh.add_quad(bottom_ring[i], bottom_ring[next_i], 
                         top_ring[next_i], top_ring[i], material)
            
            # Bottom cap
            mesh.add_triangle(bottom_center, bottom_ring[next_i], 
                            bottom_ring[i], material)
            
            # Top cap
            mesh.add_triangle(top_center, top_ring[i], 
                            top_ring[next_i], material)
        
        return mesh
    
    @staticmethod
    def create_circular_well(external_dia: float, internal_dia: float,
                            height: float, segments: int = 64,
                            material: MaterialType = MaterialType.CONCRETE) -> Mesh:
        """Create hollow cylinder (well steining)"""
        mesh = Mesh(material=material, name="well")
        
        re = external_dia / 2
        ri = internal_dia / 2
        
        # Generate vertices
        bottom_outer = []
        bottom_inner = []
        top_outer = []
        top_inner = []
        
        for i in range(segments):
            angle = 2 * math.pi * i / segments
            cos_a = math.cos(angle)
            sin_a = math.sin(angle)
            
            bottom_outer.append(mesh.add_vertex(re * cos_a, re * sin_a, 0))
            bottom_inner.append(mesh.add_vertex(ri * cos_a, ri * sin_a, 0))
            top_outer.append(mesh.add_vertex(re * cos_a, re * sin_a, height))
            top_inner.append(mesh.add_vertex(ri * cos_a, ri * sin_a, height))
        
        # Create faces
        for i in range(segments):
            next_i = (i + 1) % segments
            
            # Outer wall
            mesh.add_quad(bottom_outer[i], bottom_outer[next_i],
                         top_outer[next_i], top_outer[i], material)
            
            # Inner wall
            mesh.add_quad(bottom_inner[next_i], bottom_inner[i],
                         top_inner[i], top_inner[next_i], material)
            
            # Bottom ring
            mesh.add_quad(bottom_outer[i], bottom_inner[i],
                         bottom_inner[next_i], bottom_outer[next_i], material)
            
            # Top ring
            mesh.add_quad(top_outer[next_i], top_inner[next_i],
                         top_inner[i], top_outer[i], material)
        
        return mesh
    
    @staticmethod
    def create_pile(diameter: float, length: float, segments: int = 24,
                   material: MaterialType = MaterialType.CONCRETE) -> Mesh:
        """Create pile (cylinder extending downward)"""
        mesh = PrimitiveFactory.create_cylinder(
            diameter / 2, length, segments, material
        )
        mesh.name = "pile"
        
        # Pile extends downward (negative Z)
        mesh.translate(0, 0, -length)
        
        return mesh
    
    @staticmethod
    def create_pile_cap(length: float, width: float, thickness: float,
                       material: MaterialType = MaterialType.CONCRETE) -> Mesh:
        """Create pile cap"""
        mesh = PrimitiveFactory.create_box(length, width, thickness, material)
        mesh.name = "pile_cap"
        return mesh
    
    @staticmethod
    def create_pier_circular(diameter: float, height: float, segments: int = 48,
                            material: MaterialType = MaterialType.CONCRETE) -> Mesh:
        """Create circular pier"""
        mesh = PrimitiveFactory.create_cylinder(
            diameter / 2, height, segments, material
        )
        mesh.name = "circular_pier"
        return mesh
    
    @staticmethod
    def create_pier_rectangular(length: float, width: float, height: float,
                               material: MaterialType = MaterialType.CONCRETE) -> Mesh:
        """Create rectangular pier"""
        mesh = PrimitiveFactory.create_box(length, width, height, material)
        mesh.name = "rectangular_pier"
        return mesh
    
    @staticmethod
    def create_pier_hammerhead(cap_length: float, cap_width: float, cap_depth: float,
                               shaft_dia: float, shaft_height: float,
                               material: MaterialType = MaterialType.CONCRETE) -> Mesh:
        """Create hammerhead pier with shaft and cap"""
        mesh = Mesh(material=material, name="hammerhead_pier")
        
        # Shaft
        shaft = PrimitiveFactory.create_cylinder(shaft_dia / 2, shaft_height, 48, material)
        mesh.merge(shaft)
        
        # Cap (on top of shaft)
        cap = PrimitiveFactory.create_box(cap_length, cap_width, cap_depth, material)
        cap.translate(0, 0, shaft_height)
        mesh.merge(cap)
        
        return mesh
    
    @staticmethod
    def create_abutment_wall(height: float, width: float, thickness: float,
                             wing_length: float = 0,
                             material: MaterialType = MaterialType.CONCRETE) -> Mesh:
        """Create abutment with optional wing walls"""
        mesh = Mesh(material=material, name="abutment")
        
        # Main wall
        wall = PrimitiveFactory.create_box(thickness, width, height, material)
        mesh.merge(wall)
        
        # Wing walls
        if wing_length > 0:
            wing_thickness = thickness * 0.6
            wing_height = height * 0.7
            
            # Left wing
            left_wing = PrimitiveFactory.create_box(wing_length, wing_thickness, 
                                                     wing_height, material)
            left_wing.translate(-wing_length/2 - thickness/2, -width/2 + wing_thickness/2, 0)
            mesh.merge(left_wing)
            
            # Right wing
            right_wing = PrimitiveFactory.create_box(wing_length, wing_thickness,
                                                      wing_height, material)
            right_wing.translate(-wing_length/2 - thickness/2, width/2 - wing_thickness/2, 0)
            mesh.merge(right_wing)
        
        return mesh
    
    @staticmethod
    def create_bearing(length: float, width: float, height: float,
                       material: MaterialType = MaterialType.BEARING) -> Mesh:
        """Create elastomeric bearing"""
        mesh = PrimitiveFactory.create_box(length, width, height, material)
        mesh.name = "bearing"
        return mesh
    
    @staticmethod
    def create_deck_segment(length: float, width: float, depth: float,
                          girder_type: str = "box",
                          material: MaterialType = MaterialType.CONCRETE) -> Mesh:
        """Create bridge deck segment"""
        mesh = Mesh(material=material, name=f"{girder_type}_deck")
        
        if girder_type == "box":
            # Box girder - hollow section
            # Top slab
            top_slab = PrimitiveFactory.create_box(length, width, 0.25, material)
            top_slab.translate(0, 0, depth - 0.125)
            mesh.merge(top_slab)
            
            # Bottom slab
            bottom_slab = PrimitiveFactory.create_box(length, width, 0.25, material)
            bottom_slab.translate(0, 0, 0.125)
            mesh.merge(bottom_slab)
            
            # Webs
            web_thickness = 0.4
            web_height = depth - 0.5
            
            # Left web
            left_web = PrimitiveFactory.create_box(length, web_thickness, web_height, material)
            left_web.translate(0, -width/2 + web_thickness/2 + 0.5, depth/2)
            mesh.merge(left_web)
            
            # Right web
            right_web = PrimitiveFactory.create_box(length, web_thickness, web_height, material)
            right_web.translate(0, width/2 - web_thickness/2 - 0.5, depth/2)
            mesh.merge(right_web)
            
            # Cross diaphragms at ends
            diaphragm_thickness = 0.3
            for x in [-length/2 + 0.5, length/2 - 0.5]:
                diaphragm = PrimitiveFactory.create_box(diaphragm_thickness, width - 1.0, web_height, material)
                diaphragm.translate(x, 0, depth/2)
                mesh.merge(diaphragm)
        
        elif girder_type == "I":
            # I-girder
            # Top flange
            top_flange = PrimitiveFactory.create_box(length, width, 0.3, material)
            top_flange.translate(0, 0, depth - 0.15)
            mesh.merge(top_flange)
            
            # Bottom flange
            bottom_flange = PrimitiveFactory.create_box(length, width * 0.7, 0.4, material)
            bottom_flange.translate(0, 0, 0.2)
            mesh.merge(bottom_flange)
            
            # Web
            web_thickness = 0.3
            web = PrimitiveFactory.create_box(length, web_thickness, depth - 0.7, material)
            web.translate(0, 0, depth/2)
            mesh.merge(web)
        
        else:
            # Solid slab
            slab = PrimitiveFactory.create_box(length, width, depth, material)
            mesh.merge(slab)
        
        # Wearing course (asphalt)
        asphalt = PrimitiveFactory.create_box(length, width, 0.065, MaterialType.ASPHALT)
        asphalt.translate(0, 0, depth + 0.0325)
        mesh.merge(asphalt)
        
        return mesh
    
    @staticmethod
    def create_parapet(length: float, height: float = 1.1,
                       material: MaterialType = MaterialType.CONCRETE) -> Mesh:
        """Create parapet wall"""
        mesh = Mesh(material=material, name="parapet")
        
        # Base
        base = PrimitiveFactory.create_box(length, 0.3, 0.25, material)
        mesh.merge(base)
        
        # Wall
        wall = PrimitiveFactory.create_box(length, 0.2, height - 0.25, material)
        wall.translate(0, 0, 0.25 + (height - 0.25)/2)
        mesh.merge(wall)
        
        # Coping
        coping = PrimitiveFactory.create_box(length, 0.25, 0.1, material)
        coping.translate(0, 0, height - 0.05)
        mesh.merge(coping)
        
        return mesh
    
    @staticmethod
    def create_crash_barrier(length: float, material: MaterialType = MaterialType.STEEL) -> Mesh:
        """Create metal crash barrier"""
        mesh = Mesh(material=material, name="crash_barrier")
        
        # W-beam profile (simplified as angled plate)
        beam = PrimitiveFactory.create_box(length, 0.31, 0.08, material)
        beam.translate(0, 0, 0.7)
        # Rotate to typical W-beam angle
        for v in beam.vertices:
            v.y += (v.z - 0.7) * 0.3  # Slight angle
        
        mesh.merge(beam)
        
        # Posts
        post_spacing = 2.0
        num_posts = int(length / post_spacing) + 1
        
        for i in range(num_posts):
            x = -length/2 + i * post_spacing
            post = PrimitiveFactory.create_box(0.15, 0.15, 0.75, material)
            post.translate(x, 0, 0.375)
            mesh.merge(post)
        
        return mesh
    
    @staticmethod
    def create_ground_plane(size: float = 200, level: float = 0,
                           material: MaterialType = MaterialType.SOIL) -> Mesh:
        """Create ground plane"""
        mesh = Mesh(material=material, name="ground")
        
        # Large flat box for ground
        ground = PrimitiveFactory.create_box(size, size, 1, material)
        ground.translate(0, 0, level - 0.5)
        mesh.merge(ground)
        
        return mesh


class Bridge3DAssembler:
    """Assemble complete 3D bridge model from components"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.components: List[Mesh] = []
        self.bridge_data = {}
    
    def add_component(self, mesh: Mesh, position: Tuple[float, float, float] = (0, 0, 0),
                      rotation_z: float = 0):
        """Add component to bridge"""
        mesh_copy = self._copy_mesh(mesh)
        mesh_copy.translate(*position)
        
        if rotation_z != 0:
            mesh_copy.rotate_z(rotation_z, position[0], position[1])
        
        self.components.append(mesh_copy)
    
    def _copy_mesh(self, mesh: Mesh) -> Mesh:
        """Create deep copy of mesh"""
        new_mesh = Mesh(material=mesh.material, name=mesh.name)
        
        for v in mesh.vertices:
            new_mesh.vertices.append(Vertex(
                v.x, v.y, v.z, v.nx, v.ny, v.nz, v.u, v.v
            ))
        
        for t in mesh.triangles:
            new_mesh.triangles.append(Triangle(
                t.v1, t.v2, t.v3, t.material, t.normal
            ))
        
        return new_mesh
    
    def merge_all(self) -> Mesh:
        """Merge all components into single mesh"""
        if not self.components:
            return Mesh()
        
        merged = Mesh(name="bridge_complete")
        
        for comp in self.components:
            merged.merge(comp)
        
        return merged
    
    def get_mesh_by_material(self, material: MaterialType) -> Mesh:
        """Extract mesh with specific material"""
        filtered = Mesh(material=material, name=f"{material.value}_mesh")
        
        for comp in self.components:
            for tri in comp.triangles:
                if tri.material == material:
                    # Add vertices and create new triangle
                    v1 = comp.vertices[tri.v1]
                    v2 = comp.vertices[tri.v2]
                    v3 = comp.vertices[tri.v3]
                    
                    i1 = filtered.add_vertex(v1.x, v1.y, v1.z, v1.nx, v1.ny, v1.nz)
                    i2 = filtered.add_vertex(v2.x, v2.y, v2.z, v2.nx, v2.ny, v2.nz)
                    i3 = filtered.add_vertex(v3.x, v3.y, v3.z, v3.nx, v3.ny, v3.nz)
                    
                    filtered.add_triangle(i1, i2, i3, material)
        
        return filtered
```

## File 2: `bridge_3d_exporter.py`

```python
"""
3D Bridge Model Exporter
Exports to OBJ, STL, GLTF, DXF 3D formats
"""

import json
import struct
import base64
from typing import List, Dict, Tuple, BinaryIO
from pathlib import Path
import numpy as np
from bridge_3d_geometry import Mesh, Vertex, Triangle, MaterialType


class OBJExporter:
    """Export to Wavefront OBJ format"""
    
    MATERIAL_COLORS = {
        MaterialType.CONCRETE: (0.7, 0.7, 0.7),
        MaterialType.STEEL: (0.6, 0.6, 0.7),
        MaterialType.ASPHALT: (0.2, 0.2, 0.2),
        MaterialType.REINFORCEMENT: (0.4, 0.3, 0.2),
        MaterialType.SOIL: (0.5, 0.4, 0.3),
        MaterialType.WATER: (0.3, 0.5, 0.8),
        MaterialType.BEARING: (0.2, 0.2, 0.2)
    }
    
    def export(self, mesh: Mesh, filepath: str, 
               separate_materials: bool = True) -> str:
        """Export mesh to OBJ file"""
        
        path = Path(filepath)
        
        # Main OBJ file
        with open(path, 'w') as f:
            f.write(f"# Bridge 3D Model\n")
            f.write(f"# Vertices: {len(mesh.vertices)}\n")
            f.write(f"# Faces: {len(mesh.triangles)}\n\n")
            
            # Material library
            mtl_path = path.with_suffix('.mtl')
            f.write(f"mtllib {mtl_path.name}\n\n")
            
            # Group by material
            materials_used = set(t.material for t in mesh.triangles)
            
            # Write vertices
            for v in mesh.vertices:
                f.write(f"v {v.x:.6f} {v.y:.6f} {v.z:.6f}\n")
            
            # Write normals
            for v in mesh.vertices:
                f.write(f"vn {v.nx:.6f} {v.ny:.6f} {v.nz:.6f}\n")
            
            # Write texture coordinates
            for v in mesh.vertices:
                f.write(f"vt {v.u:.6f} {v.v:.6f}\n")
            
            f.write("\n")
            
            # Write faces by material
            current_material = None
            
            for tri in mesh.triangles:
                if tri.material != current_material:
                    current_material = tri.material
                    f.write(f"usemtl {current_material.value}\n")
                    f.write(f"g {current_material.value}_group\n")
                
                # OBJ uses 1-based indexing
                v1 = tri.v1 + 1
                v2 = tri.v2 + 1
                v3 = tri.v3 + 1
                
                # Format: v/vt/vn
                f.write(f"f {v1}/{v1}/{v1} {v2}/{v2}/{v2} {v3}/{v3}/{v3}\n")
        
        # Write MTL file
        with open(mtl_path, 'w') as f:
            f.write("# Material definitions\n\n")
            
            for mat in materials_used:
                color = self.MATERIAL_COLORS.get(mat, (0.5, 0.5, 0.5))
                f.write(f"newmtl {mat.value}\n")
                f.write(f"Ka {color[0]*0.3:.3f} {color[1]*0.3:.3f} {color[2]*0.3:.3f}\n")
                f.write(f"Kd {color[0]:.3f} {color[1]:.3f} {color[2]:.3f}\n")
                f.write(f"Ks {color[0]*0.5:.3f} {color[1]*0.5:.3f} {color[2]*0.5:.3f}\n")
                f.write(f"Ns 50.0\n")
                f.write(f"d 1.0\n")
                f.write(f"illum 2\n\n")
        
        return str(path)
    
    def export_multi_mesh(self, meshes: Dict[str, Mesh], filepath: str) -> str:
        """Export multiple meshes as separate objects"""
        
        path = Path(filepath)
        
        with open(path, 'w') as f:
            f.write(f"# Bridge 3D Model - Multi-Object\n\n")
            
            # Material library
            mtl_path = path.with_suffix('.mtl')
            f.write(f"mtllib {mtl_path.name}\n\n")
            
            vertex_offset = 0
            
            for name, mesh in meshes.items():
                f.write(f"\n# Object: {name}\n")
                f.write(f"o {name}\n")
                
                # Write vertices
                for v in mesh.vertices:
                    f.write(f"v {v.x:.6f} {v.y:.6f} {v.z:.6f}\n")
                
                # Write normals
                for v in mesh.vertices:
                    f.write(f"vn {v.nx:.6f} {v.ny:.6f} {v.nz:.6f}\n")
                
                # Write faces
                for tri in mesh.triangles:
                    v1 = tri.v1 + 1 + vertex_offset
                    v2 = tri.v2 + 1 + vertex_offset
                    v3 = tri.v3 + 1 + vertex_offset
                    
                    f.write(f"f {v1}//{v1} {v2}//{v2} {v3}//{v3}\n")
                
                vertex_offset += len(mesh.vertices)
        
        # Write MTL
        all_materials = set()
        for mesh in meshes.values():
            all_materials.update(t.material for t in mesh.triangles)
        
        with open(mtl_path, 'w') as f:
            for mat in all_materials:
                color = self.MATERIAL_COLORS.get(mat, (0.5, 0.5, 0.5))
                f.write(f"newmtl {mat.value}\n")
                f.write(f"Kd {color[0]:.3f} {color[1]:.3f} {color[2]:.3f}\n\n")
        
        return str(path)


class STLExporter:
    """Export to STL format (for 3D printing)"""
    
    def export(self, mesh: Mesh, filepath: str, binary: bool = True) -> str:
        """Export mesh to STL file"""
        
        if binary:
            return self._export_binary(mesh, filepath)
        else:
            return self._export_ascii(mesh, filepath)
    
    def _export_ascii(self, mesh: Mesh, filepath: str) -> str:
        """Export ASCII STL"""
        
        with open(filepath, 'w') as f:
            f.write(f"solid bridge_model\n")
            
            for tri in mesh.triangles:
                v1 = mesh.vertices[tri.v1]
                v2 = mesh.vertices[tri.v2]
                v3 = mesh.vertices[tri.v3]
                
                # Calculate normal
                normal = self._calculate_normal(v1, v2, v3)
                
                f.write(f"  facet normal {normal[0]:.6f} {normal[1]:.6f} {normal[2]:.6f}\n")
                f.write(f"    outer loop\n")
                f.write(f"      vertex {v1.x:.6f} {v1.y:.6f} {v1.z:.6f}\n")
                f.write(f"      vertex {v2.x:.6f} {v2.y:.6f} {v2.z:.6f}\n")
                f.write(f"      vertex {v3.x:.6f} {v3.y:.6f} {v3.z:.6f}\n")
                f.write(f"    endloop\n")
                f.write(f"  endfacet\n")
            
            f.write(f"endsolid bridge_model\n")
        
        return filepath
    
    def _export_binary(self, mesh: Mesh, filepath: str) -> str:
        """Export binary STL"""
        
        with open(filepath, 'wb') as f:
            # 80 byte header
            header = b"Bridge 3D Model" + b'\x00' * (80 - 15)
            f.write(header)
            
            # Number of triangles
            num_triangles = len(mesh.triangles)
            f.write(struct.pack('<I', num_triangles))
            
            for tri in mesh.triangles:
                v1 = mesh.vertices[tri.v1]
                v2 = mesh.vertices[tri.v2]
                v3 = mesh.vertices[tri.v3]
                
                # Normal
                normal = self._calculate_normal(v1, v2, v3)
                f.write(struct.pack('<fff', *normal))
                
                # Vertices
                f.write(struct.pack('<fff', v1.x, v1.y, v1.z))
                f.write(struct.pack('<fff', v2.x, v2.y, v2.z))
                f.write(struct.pack('<fff', v3.x, v3.y, v3.z))
                
                # Attribute byte count
                f.write(struct.pack('<H', 0))
        
        return filepath
    
    def _calculate_normal(self, v1: Vertex, v2: Vertex, v3: Vertex) -> Tuple[float, float, float]:
        """Calculate triangle normal"""
        # Vectors from v1
        a = np.array([v2.x - v1.x, v2.y - v1.y, v2.z - v1.z])
        b = np.array([v3.x - v1.x, v3.y - v1.y, v3.z - v1.z])
        
        # Cross product
        normal = np.cross(a, b)
        
        # Normalize
        length = np.linalg.norm(normal)
        if length > 0:
            normal = normal / length
        
        return (normal[0], normal[1], normal[2])
    
    def export_separate_materials(self, meshes: Dict[MaterialType, Mesh], 
                                   filepath: str) -> List[str]:
        """Export each material as separate STL file"""
        
        files = []
        base_path = Path(filepath).parent
        base_name = Path(filepath).stem
        
        for material, mesh in meshes.items():
            filename = f"{base_name}_{material.value}.stl"
            full_path = base_path / filename
            self.export(mesh, str(full_path), binary=True)
            files.append(str(full_path))
        
        return files


class GLTFExporter:
    """Export to GLTF/GLB format (for web viewers)"""
    
    def export(self, meshes: Dict[str, Mesh], filepath: str, 
               embed_buffers: bool = True) -> str:
        """Export to GLTF/GLB format"""
        
        is_glb = filepath.endswith('.glb')
        
        # Build GLTF structure
        gltf = {
            "asset": {
                "version": "2.0",
                "generator": "Bridge3DGenerator"
            },
            "scene": 0,
            "scenes": [{"nodes": list(range(len(meshes)))}],
            "nodes": [],
            "meshes": [],
            "buffers": [],
            "bufferViews": [],
            "accessors": []
        }
        
        # Build nodes and meshes
        buffer_data = bytearray()
        buffer_views = []
        accessors = []
        
        for i, (name, mesh) in enumerate(meshes.items()):
            # Node
            gltf["nodes"].append({
                "name": name,
                "mesh": i
            })
            
            # Build mesh data
            vertices = []
            normals = []
            indices = []
            
            for v in mesh.vertices:
                vertices.extend([v.x, v.y, v.z])
                normals.extend([v.nx, v.ny, v.nz])
            
            for tri in mesh.triangles:
                indices.extend([tri.v1, tri.v2, tri.v3])
            
            # Create accessors
            # Vertices
            vert_offset = len(buffer_data)
            vert_bytes = struct.pack(f'<{len(vertices)}f', *vertices)
            buffer_data.extend(vert_bytes)
            
            # Normals
            norm_offset = len(buffer_data)
            norm_bytes = struct.pack(f'<{len(normals)}f', *normals)
            buffer_data.extend(norm_bytes)
            
            # Indices
            idx_offset = len(buffer_data)
            idx_bytes = struct.pack(f'<{len(indices)}H', *indices)
            buffer_data.extend(idx_bytes)
            # Pad to 4 bytes
            while len(buffer_data) % 4 != 0:
                buffer_data.append(0)
            
            # Buffer views
            vert_bv = len(buffer_views)
            buffer_views.append({
                "buffer": 0,
                "byteOffset": vert_offset,
                "byteLength": len(vert_bytes)
            })
            
            norm_bv = len(buffer_views)
            buffer_views.append({
                "buffer": 0,
                "byteOffset": norm_offset,
                "byteLength": len(norm_bytes)
            })
            
            idx_bv = len(buffer_views)
            buffer_views.append({
                "buffer": 0,
                "byteOffset": idx_offset,
                "byteLength": len(idx_bytes)
            })
            
            # Accessors
            vert_acc = len(accessors)
            accessors.append({
                "bufferView": vert_bv,
                "componentType": 5126,  # FLOAT
                "count": len(mesh.vertices),
                "type": "VEC3",
                "max": [
                    max(v.x for v in mesh.vertices),
                    max(v.y for v in mesh.vertices),
                    max(v.z for v in mesh.vertices)
                ],
                "min": [
                    min(v.x for v in mesh.vertices),
                    min(v.y for v in mesh.vertices),
                    min(v.z for v in mesh.vertices)
                ]
            })
            
            norm_acc = len(accessors)
            accessors.append({
                "bufferView": norm_bv,
                "componentType": 5126,  # FLOAT
                "count": len(mesh.vertices),
                "type": "VEC3"
            })
            
            idx_acc = len(accessors)
            accessors.append({
                "bufferView": idx_bv,
                "componentType": 5123,  # UNSIGNED_SHORT
                "count": len(indices),
                "type": "SCALAR"
            })
            
            # Mesh primitive
            gltf["meshes"].append({
                "primitives": [{
                    "attributes": {
                        "POSITION": vert_acc,
                        "NORMAL": norm_acc
                    },
                    "indices": idx_acc,
                    "mode": 4  # TRIANGLES
                }]
            })
        
        # Add buffer views and accessors
        gltf["bufferViews"] = buffer_views
        gltf["accessors"] = accessors
        
        # Buffer
        if embed_buffers:
            # Embed as base64
            encoded = base64.b64encode(buffer_data).decode('ascii')
            uri = f"data:application/octet-stream;base64,{encoded}"
        else:
            # Separate bin file
            bin_path = Path(filepath).with_suffix('.bin')
            with open(bin_path, 'wb') as f:
                f.write(buffer_data)
            uri = bin_path.name
        
        gltf["buffers"] = [{
            "uri": uri,
            "byteLength": len(buffer_data)
        }]
        
        # Write file
        if is_glb:
            self._write_glb(gltf, buffer_data, filepath)
        else:
            with open(filepath, 'w') as f:
                json.dump(gltf, f, indent=2)
        
        return filepath
    
    def _write_glb(self, gltf: dict, buffer_data: bytes, filepath: str):
        """Write GLB (binary GLTF) format"""
        
        # JSON chunk
        json_str = json.dumps(gltf)
        # Pad to 4 bytes
        while len(json_str) % 4 != 0:
            json_str += ' '
        
        json_bytes = json_str.encode('utf-8')
        json_padding = (4 - (len(json_bytes) % 4)) % 4
        
        # Buffer chunk
        buffer_padding = (4 - (len(buffer_data) % 4)) % 4
        
        # Header
        header = struct.pack('<4sII',
                            b'glTF',
                            2,  # Version
                            12 + 8 + len(json_bytes) + json_padding + 8 + len(buffer_data) + buffer_padding)
        
        # JSON chunk header
        json_chunk = struct.pack('<I', len(json_bytes)) + struct.pack('<I', 0x4E4F534A) + json_bytes
        json_chunk += b' ' * json_padding
        
        # Buffer chunk header
        buffer_chunk = struct.pack('<I', len(buffer_data)) + struct.pack('<I', 0x004E4942) + buffer_data
        buffer_chunk += b'\x00' * buffer_padding
        
        # Write
        with open(filepath, 'wb') as f:
            f.write(header)
            f.write(json_chunk)
            f.write(buffer_chunk)


class ThreeJSExporter:
    """Export to Three.js JSON format"""
    
    def export(self, meshes: Dict[str, Mesh], filepath: str) -> str:
        """Export to Three.js JSON format"""
        
        output = {
            "metadata": {
                "version": 4.5,
                "type": "Object",
                "generator": "Bridge3DGenerator"
            },
            "scene": {},
            "geometries": [],
            "materials": [],
            "object": {
                "uuid": "bridge_root",
                "type": "Group",
                "name": "Bridge",
                "children": []
            }
        }
        
        geometry_index = 0
        material_index = 0
        
        material_map = {}
        
        for name, mesh in meshes.items():
            # Create geometry
            vertices = []
            normals = []
            
            for v in mesh.vertices:
                vertices.extend([v.x, v.y, v.z])
                normals.extend([v.nx, v.ny, v.nz])
            
            faces = []
            for tri in mesh.triangles:
                faces.extend([tri.v1, tri.v2, tri.v3])
            
            geometry = {
                "uuid": f"geom_{geometry_index}",
                "type": "BufferGeometry",
                "data": {
                    "attributes": {
                        "position": {
                            "itemSize": 3,
                            "type": "Float32Array",
                            "array": vertices
                        },
                        "normal": {
                            "itemSize": 3,
                            "type": "Float32Array",
                            "array": normals
                        }
                    },
                    "index": {
                        "type": "Uint16Array",
                        "array": faces
                    }
                }
            }
            
            output["geometries"].append(geometry)
            
            # Material (use first triangle's material)
            mat = mesh.triangles[0].material if mesh.triangles else MaterialType.CONCRETE
            
            if mat not in material_map:
                material_map[mat] = material_index
                material_index += 1
            
            # Create mesh object
            mesh_obj = {
                "uuid": f"mesh_{geometry_index}",
                "type": "Mesh",
                "name": name,
                "geometry": f"geom_{geometry_index}",
                "material": f"mat_{material_map[mat]}"
            }
            
            output["object"]["children"].append(mesh_obj)
            geometry_index += 1
        
        # Add materials
        for mat, idx in material_map.items():
            color = self._material_to_color(mat)
            material = {
                "uuid": f"mat_{idx}",
                "type": "MeshStandardMaterial",
                "color": color,
                "roughness": 0.8,
                "metalness": 0.1
            }
            output["materials"].append(material)
        
        with open(filepath, 'w') as f:
            json.dump(output, f, indent=2)
        
        return filepath
    
    def _material_to_color(self, material: MaterialType) -> int:
        """Convert material to color integer"""
        colors = {
            MaterialType.CONCRETE: 0x808080,
            MaterialType.STEEL: 0xA0A0B0,
            MaterialType.ASPHALT: 0x202020,
            MaterialType.REINFORCEMENT: 0x604020,
            MaterialType.SOIL: 0x806040,
            MaterialType.WATER: 0x4080C0,
            MaterialType.BEARING: 0x101010
        }
        return colors.get(material, 0x808080)


class DXF3DExporter:
    """Export 3D mesh to DXF format"""
    
    def export(self, mesh: Mesh, filepath: str) -> str:
        """Export to DXF 3D"""
        
        import ezdxf
        from ezdxf.enums import MlineJustify
        
        doc = ezdxf.new('R2018')
        msp = doc.modelspace()
        
        # Create 3DFACE entities for each triangle
        for tri in mesh.triangles:
            v1 = mesh.vertices[tri.v1]
            v2 = mesh.vertices[tri.v2]
            v3 = mesh.vertices[tri.v3]
            
            # 3DFACE requires 4 points, duplicate last
            msp.add_3dface([
                (v1.x, v1.y, v1.z),
                (v2.x, v2.y, v2.z),
                (v3.x, v3.y, v3.z),
                (v3.x, v3.y, v3.z)  # Duplicate for triangular face
            ])
        
        doc.saveas(filepath)
        return filepath
```

## File 3: `bridge_3d_generator.py`

```python
"""
Complete 3D Bridge Generator
Generates full 3D bridge model from bridge data
"""

from typing import List, Dict, Tuple, Optional
from dataclasses import dataclass
from bridge_3d_geometry import (
    Bridge3DAssembler, PrimitiveFactory, Mesh, MaterialType
)
from bridge_3d_exporter import (
    OBJExporter, STLExporter, GLTFExporter, 
    ThreeJSExporter, DXF3DExporter
)
from foundation_generator import FoundationModel, FoundationType


@dataclass
class BridgeSpan:
    """Bridge span definition"""
    span_length: float = 30.0
    deck_width: float = 12.0
    deck_depth: float = 2.5
    girder_type: str = "box"  # box, I, slab
    
    def __post_init__(self):
        self.carriageway_width = self.deck_width - 1.0  # 0.5m each side


@dataclass
class BridgePier:
    """Pier definition"""
    pier_number: int
    chainage: float
    pier_type: str = "circular"  # circular, rectangular, hammerhead
    diameter: float = 2.0
    height: float = 15.0
    foundation: Optional[FoundationModel] = None


@dataclass
class BridgeAbutment:
    """Abutment definition"""
    abutment_type: str = "wall"  # wall, frame
    height: float = 15.0
    width: float = 12.0
    thickness: float = 2.0
    wing_length: float = 8.0
    foundation: Optional[FoundationModel] = None


@dataclass
class BridgeConfiguration:
    """Complete bridge configuration"""
    project_name: str = "Bridge Project"
    spans: List[BridgeSpan] = None
    piers: List[BridgePier] = None
    left_abutment: BridgeAbutment = None
    right_abutment: BridgeAbutment = None
    has_parapet: bool = True
    has_crash_barrier: bool = True
    
    def __post_init__(self):
        if self.spans is None:
            self.spans = [BridgeSpan()]
        if self.piers is None:
            self.piers = []
        if self.left_abutment is None:
            self.left_abutment = BridgeAbutment()
        if self.right_abutment is None:
            self.right_abutment = BridgeAbutment()


class Bridge3DGenerator:
    """Generate complete 3D bridge model"""
    
    def __init__(self, config: BridgeConfiguration):
        self.config = config
        self.assembler = Bridge3DAssembler()
    
    def generate(self) -> Bridge3DAssembler:
        """Generate complete 3D bridge"""
        
        # Calculate total bridge length
        total_length = sum(s.span_length for s in self.config.spans)
        
        # Generate components
        self._generate_ground()
        self._generate_foundations()
        self._generate_piers()
        self._generate_abutments()
        self._generate_deck()
        self._generate_parapets()
        self._generate_crash_barriers()
        self._generate_bearings()
        
        return self.assembler
    
    def _generate_ground(self):
        """Generate ground plane"""
        ground = PrimitiveFactory.create_ground_plane(size=300, level=0)
        self.assembler.add_component(ground, (0, 0, 0))
    
    def _generate_foundations(self):
        """Generate foundations (piles, wells)"""
        
        for pier in self.config.piers:
            if pier.foundation:
                self._generate_foundation(pier.foundation, pier.chainage, 0)
        
        # Abutment foundations
        if self.config.left_abutment.foundation:
            self._generate_foundation(
                self.config.left_abutment.foundation, 
                0, 0
            )
        
        if self.config.right_abutment.foundation:
            total_length = sum(s.span_length for s in self.config.spans)
            self._generate_foundation(
                self.config.right_abutment.foundation,
                total_length, 0
            )
    
    def _generate_foundation(self, foundation: FoundationModel, 
                             x: float, y: float):
        """Generate foundation 3D model"""
        
        if foundation.foundation_type == FoundationType.PILE:
            pc = foundation.pile_config
            pd = foundation.pile_dims
            
            # Pile cap
            cap = PrimitiveFactory.create_pile_cap(
                pc.cap_length, pc.cap_width, pc.cap_thickness
            )
            self.assembler.add_component(cap, (x, y, 0))
            
            # Piles
            positions = pc.get_pile_positions()
            for px, py in positions:
                pile = PrimitiveFactory.create_pile(
                    pd.diameter, pd.length
                )
                self.assembler.add_component(
                    pile, 
                    (x + px, y + py, 0)
                )
        
        elif foundation.foundation_type == FoundationType.WELL:
            wd = foundation.well_dims
            
            # Well steining
            well = PrimitiveFactory.create_circular_well(
                wd.external_dia, 
                wd.internal_dia,
                wd.sinking_depth
            )
            self.assembler.add_component(well, (x, y, -wd.sinking_depth))
            
            # Well cap
            cap = PrimitiveFactory.create_pile_cap(
                wd.external_dia + 1.0,
                wd.external_dia + 1.0,
                wd.well_cap_thickness
            )
            self.assembler.add_component(cap, (x, y, 0))
            
            # Bottom plug
            plug = PrimitiveFactory.create_cylinder(
                wd.internal_dia / 2, 
                wd.bottom_plug_thickness,
                32,
                MaterialType.CONCRETE
            )
            self.assembler.add_component(
                plug, 
                (x, y, -wd.sinking_depth)
            )
    
    def _generate_piers(self):
        """Generate piers"""
        
        for pier in self.config.piers:
            pier_height = pier.height
            
            if pier.pier_type == "circular":
                pier_mesh = PrimitiveFactory.create_pier_circular(
                    pier.diameter, pier_height
                )
            elif pier.pier_type == "rectangular":
                pier_mesh = PrimitiveFactory.create_pier_rectangular(
                    pier.diameter, pier.diameter, pier_height
                )
            elif pier.pier_type == "hammerhead":
                pier_mesh = PrimitiveFactory.create_pier_hammerhead(
                    cap_length=4.0,
                    cap_width=12.0,
                    cap_depth=2.0,
                    shaft_dia=pier.diameter,
                    shaft_height=pier_height - 2.0
                )
            else:
                pier_mesh = PrimitiveFactory.create_pier_circular(
                    pier.diameter, pier_height
                )
            
            self.assembler.add_component(
                pier_mesh, 
                (pier.chainage, 0, 0)
            )
    
    def _generate_abutments(self):
        """Generate abutments"""
        
        # Left abutment
        left_abut = self.config.left_abutment
        left_mesh = PrimitiveFactory.create_abutment_wall(
            left_abut.height,
            left_abut.width,
            left_abut.thickness,
            left_abut.wing_length
        )
        self.assembler.add_component(left_mesh, (0, 0, 0))
        
        # Right abutment
        right_abut = self.config.right_abutment
        total_length = sum(s.span_length for s in self.config.spans)
        
        right_mesh = PrimitiveFactory.create_abutment_wall(
            right_abut.height,
            right_abut.width,
            right_abut.thickness,
            right_abut.wing_length
        )
        self.assembler.add_component(right_mesh, (total_length, 0, 0))
    
    def _generate_deck(self):
        """Generate bridge deck"""
        
        current_x = 0
        
        for i, span in enumerate(self.config.spans):
            # Deck segment
            deck = PrimitiveFactory.create_deck_segment(
                span.span_length,
                span.deck_width,
                span.deck_depth,
                span.girder_type
            )
            
            # Position at center of span
            segment_center = current_x + span.span_length / 2
            
            self.assembler.add_component(
                deck,
                (segment_center, 0, self._get_deck_elevation())
            )
            
            current_x += span.span_length
    
    def _get_deck_elevation(self) -> float:
        """Get deck soffit elevation"""
        # Assuming pier height is to bearing level
        # Deck sits on bearings
        return 15.0  # Simplified
    
    def _generate_parapets(self):
        """Generate parapet walls"""
        
        if not self.config.has_parapet:
            return
        
        current_x = 0
        
        for span in self.config.spans:
            # Left parapet
            left_parapet = PrimitiveFactory.create_parapet(span.span_length)
            self.assembler.add_component(
                left_parapet,
                (current_x + span.span_length/2, 
                 -span.deck_width/2 + 0.15,
                 self._get_deck_elevation() + span.deck_depth)
            )
            
            # Right parapet
            right_parapet = PrimitiveFactory.create_parapet(span.span_length)
            self.assembler.add_component(
                right_parapet,
                (current_x + span.span_length/2,
                 span.deck_width/2 - 0.15,
                 self._get_deck_elevation() + span.deck_depth)
            )
            
            current_x += span.span_length
    
    def _generate_crash_barriers(self):
        """Generate crash barriers"""
        
        if not self.config.has_crash_barrier:
            return
        
        current_x = 0
        
        for span in self.config.spans:
            # Left barrier
            left_barrier = PrimitiveFactory.create_crash_barrier(span.span_length)
            self.assembler.add_component(
                left_barrier,
                (current_x + span.span_length/2,
                 -span.deck_width/2 + 0.5,
                 self._get_deck_elevation() + span.deck_depth)
            )
            
            # Right barrier
            right_barrier = PrimitiveFactory.create_crash_barrier(span.span_length)
            self.assembler.add_component(
                right_barrier,
                (current_x + span.span_length/2,
                 span.deck_width/2 - 0.5,
                 self._get_deck_elevation() + span.deck_depth)
            )
            
            current_x += span.span_length
    
    def _generate_bearings(self):
        """Generate bearings at pier/abutment locations"""
        
        bearing_width = 0.6
        bearing_length = 0.6
        bearing_height = 0.1
        
        # At piers
        for pier in self.config.piers:
            # Multiple bearings per pier
            num_bearings = 4
            spacing = 3.0
            
            for i in range(num_bearings):
                y = (i - 1.5) * spacing
                
                bearing = PrimitiveFactory.create_bearing(
                    bearing_length, bearing_width, bearing_height
                )
                
                self.assembler.add_component(
                    bearing,
                    (pier.chainage, y, 15.0)  # At pier top
                )
        
        # At abutments
        # (Simplified - similar to piers)
    
    def export(self, output_dir: str, base_name: str = "bridge") -> Dict[str, str]:
        """Export to all formats"""
        
        # Merge all components
        complete_mesh = self.assembler.merge_all()
        
        # Separate by material for some formats
        material_meshes = {}
        for mat in MaterialType:
            mesh = self.assembler.get_mesh_by_material(mat)
            if mesh.vertices:
                material_meshes[mat] = mesh
        
        # Component meshes
        component_meshes = {}
        for i, comp in enumerate(self.assembler.components):
            component_meshes[f"{comp.name}_{i}"] = comp
        
        results = {}
        
        # Export OBJ
        obj_exp = OBJExporter()
        results['obj'] = obj_exp.export_multi_mesh(
            component_meshes,
            f"{output_dir}/{base_name}.obj"
        )
        
        # Export STL (combined)
        stl_exp = STLExporter()
        results['stl'] = stl_exp.export(
            complete_mesh,
            f"{output_dir}/{base_name}.stl",
            binary=True
        )
        
        # Export STL by material
        stl_files = stl_exp.export_separate_materials(
            material_meshes,
            f"{output_dir}/{base_name}_material.stl"
        )
        results['stl_materials'] = stl_files
        
        # Export GLB
        gltf_exp = GLTFExporter()
        results['glb'] = gltf_exp.export(
            component_meshes,
            f"{output_dir}/{base_name}.glb",
            embed_buffers=True
        )
        
        # Export Three.js JSON
        three_exp = ThreeJSExporter()
        results['threejs'] = three_exp.export(
            component_meshes,
            f"{output_dir}/{base_name}.json"
        )
        
        # Export DXF 3D
        dxf_exp = DXF3DExporter()
        results['dxf3d'] = dxf_exp.export(
            complete_mesh,
            f"{output_dir}/{base_name}_3d.dxf"
        )
        
        return results


# Example usage
if __name__ == "__main__":
    from foundation_generator import create_pile_foundation, create_well_foundation
    
    # Create bridge configuration
    config = BridgeConfiguration(
        project_name="Multi-Span Bridge Example",
        spans=[
            BridgeSpan(span_length=30.0, deck_width=12.0, deck_depth=2.5, girder_type="box"),
            BridgeSpan(span_length=35.0, deck_width=12.0, deck_depth=2.5, girder_type="box"),
            BridgeSpan(span_length=30.0, deck_width=12.0, deck_depth=2.5, girder_type="box")
        ],
        piers=[
            BridgePier(1, 30.0, "circular", 2.0, 15.0, 
                      create_pile_foundation("PIER", 1)),
            BridgePier(2, 65.0, "circular", 2.0, 15.0,
                      create_pile_foundation("PIER", 2))
        ],
        left_abutment=BridgeAbutment("wall", 15.0, 12.0, 2.0, 8.0,
                                     create_well_foundation("ABUTMENT", 1)),
        right_abutment=BridgeAbutment("wall", 15.0, 12.0, 2.0, 8.0,
                                      create_well_foundation("ABUTMENT", 2)),
        has_parapet=True,
        has_crash_barrier=True
    )
    
    # Generate 3D model
    print("Generating 3D bridge model...")
    generator = Bridge3DGenerator(config)
    generator.generate()
    
    # Export to all formats
    print("\nExporting to multiple formats...")
    results = generator.export("./output_3d", "multi_span_bridge")
    
    print("\nExport complete!")
    print(f"  OBJ: {results['obj']}")
    print(f"  STL: {results['stl']}")
    print(f"  GLB: {results['glb']}")
    print(f"  Three.js: {results['threejs']}")
    print(f"  DXF 3D: {results['dxf3d']}")
    print(f"\nMaterial STLs: {len(results['stl_materials'])} files")
```

## File 4: `bridge_3d_viewer.py`

```python
"""
3D Bridge Viewer
Creates HTML/Three.js viewer for browser-based 3D visualization
"""

import json
from typing import Dict, List


class Bridge3DViewer:
    """Generate HTML viewer for 3D bridge model"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.template = """
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html>
<head>
    <meta charset="utf-8">
    <title>{title}</title>
    <style>
        body {{ margin: 0; overflow: hidden; font-family: Arial, sans-serif; }}
        #container {{ width: 100vw; height: 100vh; }}
        #info {{
            position: absolute; top: 10px; left: 10px;
            background: rgba(0,0,0,0.7); color: white;
            padding: 10px; border-radius: 5px;
            font-size: 12px;
        }}
        #controls {{
            position: absolute; bottom: 10px; left: 10px;
            background: rgba(0,0,0,0.7); color: white;
            padding: 10px; border-radius: 5px;
        }}
        button {{
            margin: 2px; padding: 5px 10px;
            cursor: pointer;
        }}
    </style>
</head>
<body>
    <div id="container"></div>
    <div id="info">
        <h3>{title}</h3>
        <p>Vertices: {vertex_count}</p>
        <p>Triangles: {triangle_count}</p>
        <p>Materials: {material_count}</p>
    </div>
    <div id="controls">
        <button onclick="resetView()">Reset View</button>
        <button onclick="toggleWireframe()">Wireframe</button>
        <button onclick="toggleMaterials()">Toggle Materials</button>
    </div>
    
    <script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/three.js/r128/three.min.js"></script>
    <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/three@0.128.0/examples/js/controls/OrbitControls.js"></script>
    
    <script>
        // Scene setup
        const scene = new THREE.Scene();
        scene.background = new THREE.Color(0x87CEEB); // Sky blue
        
        const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(60, window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight, 0.1, 1000);
        camera.position.set(50, 50, 50);
        
        const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ antialias: true });
        renderer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);
        renderer.shadowMap.enabled = true;
        document.getElementById('container').appendChild(renderer.domElement);
        
        // Controls
        const controls = new THREE.OrbitControls(camera, renderer.domElement);
        controls.enableDamping = true;
        controls.dampingFactor = 0.05;
        
        // Lighting
        const ambientLight = new THREE.AmbientLight(0xffffff, 0.4);
        scene.add(ambientLight);
        
        const directionalLight = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff, 0.8);
        directionalLight.position.set(50, 100, 50);
        directionalLight.castShadow = true;
        scene.add(directionalLight);
        
        // Ground
        const groundGeometry = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(200, 200);
        const groundMaterial = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ 
            color: 0x3d5c3d,
            roughness: 0.8
        });
        const ground = new THREE.Mesh(groundGeometry, groundMaterial);
        ground.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;
        ground.position.y = -0.1;
        ground.receiveShadow = true;
        scene.add(ground);
        
        // Grid
        const gridHelper = new THREE.GridHelper(200, 40, 0x000000, 0x444444);
        gridHelper.position.y = 0.01;
        scene.add(gridHelper);
        
        // Bridge model data
        const bridgeData = {bridge_data};
        
        // Material colors
        const materialColors = {{
            'concrete': 0x808080,
            'steel': 0xA0A0B0,
            'asphalt': 0x202020,
            'reinforcement': 0x604020,
            'soil': 0x806040,
            'water': 0x4080C0,
            'bearing': 0x101010
        }};
        
        // Load and display bridge
        let bridgeGroup = new THREE.Group();
        let wireframeMode = false;
        
        function loadBridge() {{
            const loader = new THREE.ObjectLoader();
            const object = loader.parse(bridgeData);
            bridgeGroup.add(object);
            scene.add(bridgeGroup);
            
            // Center camera
            const box = new THREE.Box3().setFromObject(bridgeGroup);
            const center = box.getCenter(new THREE.Vector3());
            controls.target.copy(center);
        }}
        
        loadBridge();
        
        // View functions
        function resetView() {{
            camera.position.set(50, 50, 50);
            controls.target.set(0, 0, 0);
            controls.update();
        }}
        
        function toggleWireframe() {{
            wireframeMode = !wireframeMode;
            bridgeGroup.traverse((child) => {{
                if (child.isMesh) {{
                    child.material.wireframe = wireframeMode;
                }}
            }});
        }}
        
        let materialsVisible = true;
        function toggleMaterials() {{
            materialsVisible = !materialsVisible;
            bridgeGroup.traverse((child) => {{
                if (child.isMesh) {{
                    child.visible = materialsVisible;
                }}
            }});
        }}
        
        // Animation loop
        function animate() {{
            requestAnimationFrame(animate);
            controls.update();
            renderer.render(scene, camera);
        }}
        animate();
        
        // Handle resize
        window.addEventListener('resize', () => {{
            camera.aspect = window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight;
            camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
            renderer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);
        }});
    </script>
</body>
</html>
"""
    
    def generate_viewer(self, threejs_data: Dict, 
                        output_path: str,
                        title: str = "3D Bridge Viewer") -> str:
        """Generate HTML viewer file"""
        
        # Count statistics
        vertex_count = 0
        triangle_count = 0
        materials = set()
        
        for geom in threejs_data.get('geometries', []):
            data = geom.get('data', {})
            attrs = data.get('attributes', {})
            if 'position' in attrs:
                pos = attrs['position']
                vertex_count += pos.get('array', [])
            
            if 'index' in data:
                idx = data['index']
                triangle_count += len(idx.get('array', [])) // 3
        
        for mat in threejs_data.get('materials', []):
            materials.add(mat.get('name', 'unknown'))
        
        # Generate HTML
        html = self.template.format(
            title=title,
            vertex_count=vertex_count,
            triangle_count=triangle_count,
            material_count=len(materials),
            bridge_data=json.dumps(threejs_data)
        )
        
        with open(output_path, 'w') as f:
            f.write(html)
        
        return output_path
    
    def generate_glb_viewer(self, glb_path: str, 
                            output_path: str,
                            title: str = "3D Bridge Viewer") -> str:
        """Generate HTML viewer for GLB file"""
        
        template = f"""
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html>
<head>
    <meta charset="utf-8">
    <title>{title}</title>
    <style>
        body {{ margin: 0; overflow: hidden; }}
        #container {{ width: 100vw; height: 100vh; }}
    </style>
</head>
<body>
    <div id="container"></div>
    
    <script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/three.js/r128/three.min.js"></script>
    <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/three@0.128.0/examples/js/controls/OrbitControls.js"></script>
    <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/three@0.128.0/examples/js/loaders/GLTFLoader.js"></script>
    
    <script>
        const scene = new THREE.Scene();
        scene.background = new THREE.Color(0x87CEEB);
        
        const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(60, window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight, 0.1, 1000);
        camera.position.set(50, 50, 50);
        
        const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({{ antialias: true }});
        renderer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);
        renderer.shadowMap.enabled = true;
        document.getElementById('container').appendChild(renderer.domElement);
        
        const controls = new THREE.OrbitControls(camera, renderer.domElement);
        controls.enableDamping = true;
        
        // Lighting
        const ambient = new THREE.AmbientLight(0xffffff, 0.4);
        scene.add(ambient);
        
        const directional = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff, 0.8);
        directional.position.set(50, 100, 50);
        scene.add(directional);
        
        // Ground
        const ground = new THREE.Mesh(
            new THREE.PlaneGeometry(200, 200),
            new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({{ color: 0x3d5c3d }})
        );
        ground.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;
        ground.position.y = -0.1;
        scene.add(ground);
        
        // Grid
        scene.add(new THREE.GridHelper(200, 40));
        
        // Load GLB
        const loader = new THREE.GLTFLoader();
        loader.load('{glb_path}', (gltf) => {{
            const model = gltf.scene;
            scene.add(model);
            
            // Center camera
            const box = new THREE.Box3().setFromObject(model);
            const center = box.getCenter(new THREE.Vector3());
            controls.target.copy(center);
            
            console.log('Bridge loaded successfully');
        }}, undefined, (error) => {{
            console.error('Error loading model:', error);
        }});
        
        // Animation
        function animate() {{
            requestAnimationFrame(animate);
            controls.update();
            renderer.render(scene, camera);
        }}
        animate();
        
        // Resize
        window.addEventListener('resize', () => {{
            camera.aspect = window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight;
            camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
            renderer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);
        }});
    </script>
</body>
</html>
"""
        
        with open(output_path, 'w') as f:
            f.write(template)
        
        return output_path


# Example usage
if __name__ == "__main__":
    viewer = Bridge3DViewer()
    
    # Generate viewer for GLB
    viewer.generate_glb_viewer(
        "multi_span_bridge.glb",
        "bridge_viewer.html",
        "Multi-Span Bridge 3D Viewer"
    )
    
    print("Viewer generated: bridge_viewer.html")
    print("Open in browser to view 3D bridge model")
```

## OUTPUT FORMATS

| Format | Extension | Use Case |
|--------|-----------|----------|
| **OBJ** | .obj | Universal 3D format, import to Blender/3ds Max |
| **STL** | .stl | 3D printing, CNC machining |
| **GLB** | .glb | Web viewers, Unity, Unreal Engine |
| **Three.js** | .json | Browser-based interactive viewing |
| **DXF 3D** | .dxf | AutoCAD, Civil 3D integration |

   1. **Add Cost Estimation Module** - Rate analysis and cost breakdown from quantities?
