# COST ESTIMATION MODULE

Complete rate analysis and cost estimation system with Schedule of Rates (SOR).

## File 1: `schedule_of_rates.py`

```python
"""
Schedule of Rates (SOR) Module
Manages unit rates for construction items per IS / CPWD / State PWD standards
"""

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Tuple
from enum import Enum
import json


class WorkCategory(Enum):
    """Work categories per standard classification"""
    EARTHWORK = "Earthwork"
    CONCRETE = "Concrete Work"
    REINFORCEMENT = "Reinforcement"
    FORMWORK = "Formwork"
    PILE_WORK = "Pile Foundation"
    WELL_WORK = "Well Foundation"
    MASONRY = "Masonry"
    STEEL_WORK = "Structural Steel"
    ROAD_WORK = "Road Work"
    PROTECTION_WORK = "Protection Work"
    MISCELLANEOUS = "Miscellaneous"


class Unit(Enum):
    """Standard units of measurement"""
    CUM = "cum"           # Cubic meter
    SQ_M = "sqm"          # Square meter
    M = "m"               # Meter
    KG = "kg"             # Kilogram
    MT = "mt"             # Metric ton
    NOS = "nos"           # Numbers
    HOUR = "hour"         # Hour
    DAY = "day"           # Day
    L_S = "LS"            # Lump sum
    KL = "kl"             # Kiloliter


@dataclass
class RateItem:
    """Individual rate item"""
    item_code: str
    description: str
    category: WorkCategory
    unit: Unit
    basic_rate: float      # Basic rate in currency units
    taxes_pct: float = 18.0  # GST/VAT percentage
    overhead_pct: float = 0.0  # Overhead percentage
    profit_pct: float = 10.0   # Profit percentage
    
    @property
    def total_rate(self) -> float:
        """Calculate total rate including taxes and overheads"""
        subtotal = self.basic_rate * (1 + self.overhead_pct / 100)
        subtotal *= (1 + self.profit_pct / 100)
        subtotal *= (1 + self.taxes_pct / 100)
        return round(subtotal, 2)
    
    @property
    def rate_without_tax(self) -> float:
        """Rate before taxes"""
        subtotal = self.basic_rate * (1 + self.overhead_pct / 100)
        subtotal *= (1 + self.profit_pct / 100)
        return round(subtotal, 2)
    
    def calculate_cost(self, quantity: float) -> Dict:
        """Calculate cost breakdown for given quantity"""
        basic_cost = quantity * self.basic_rate
        overhead = basic_cost * self.overhead_pct / 100
        profit = (basic_cost + overhead) * self.profit_pct / 100
        subtotal = basic_cost + overhead + profit
        tax = subtotal * self.taxes_pct / 100
        total = subtotal + tax
        
        return {
            'item_code': self.item_code,
            'description': self.description,
            'category': self.category.value,
            'unit': self.unit.value,
            'quantity': quantity,
            'basic_rate': self.basic_rate,
            'basic_cost': round(basic_cost, 2),
            'overhead': round(overhead, 2),
            'profit': round(profit, 2),
            'tax': round(tax, 2),
            'total_cost': round(total, 2),
            'rate_analysis': self.get_rate_analysis()
        }
    
    def get_rate_analysis(self) -> Dict:
        """Get detailed rate analysis"""
        return {
            'materials': [],
            'labour': [],
            'equipment': [],
            'overhead': self.overhead_pct,
            'profit': self.profit_pct,
            'tax': self.taxes_pct
        }


class ScheduleOfRates:
    """Complete Schedule of Rates"""
    
    def __init__(self, name: str = "Standard SOR", 
                 currency: str = "INR",
                 currency_symbol: str = "₹"):
        self.name = name
        self.currency = currency
        self.currency_symbol = currency_symbol
        self.items: Dict[str, RateItem] = {}
        self._initialize_standard_rates()
    
    def _initialize_standard_rates(self):
        """Initialize standard CPWD/State PWD rates"""
        
        # EARTHWORK
        self.add_item(RateItem(
            "E-001", "Excavation in soil by manual means",
            WorkCategory.EARTHWORK, Unit.CUM, 250.00
        ))
        self.add_item(RateItem(
            "E-002", "Excavation in hard soil including rock",
            WorkCategory.EARTHWORK, Unit.CUM, 450.00
        ))
        self.add_item(RateItem(
            "E-003", "Backfilling with selected soil",
            WorkCategory.EARTHWORK, Unit.CUM, 180.00
        ))
        self.add_item(RateItem(
            "E-004", "Disposal of surplus soil",
            WorkCategory.EARTHWORK, Unit.CUM, 120.00
        ))
        self.add_item(RateItem(
            "E-005", "Dewatering",
            WorkCategory.EARTHWORK, Unit.HOUR, 500.00
        ))
        
        # CONCRETE WORK
        self.add_item(RateItem(
            "C-001", "M15 grade concrete (1:2:4)",
            WorkCategory.CONCRETE, Unit.CUM, 5200.00
        ))
        self.add_item(RateItem(
            "C-002", "M20 grade concrete (1:1.5:3)",
            WorkCategory.CONCRETE, Unit.CUM, 5800.00
        ))
        self.add_item(RateItem(
            "C-003", "M25 grade concrete (1:1:2)",
            WorkCategory.CONCRETE, Unit.CUM, 6500.00
        ))
        self.add_item(RateItem(
            "C-004", "M30 grade concrete with admixture",
            WorkCategory.CONCRETE, Unit.CUM, 7200.00
        ))
        self.add_item(RateItem(
            "C-005", "M35 grade concrete for piles",
            WorkCategory.CONCRETE, Unit.CUM, 7800.00
        ))
        self.add_item(RateItem(
            "C-006", "Underwater concrete (M35)",
            WorkCategory.CONCRETE, Unit.CUM, 9500.00
        ))
        
        # REINFORCEMENT
        self.add_item(RateItem(
            "R-001", "Mild steel reinforcement (Fe250)",
            WorkCategory.REINFORCEMENT, Unit.KG, 65.00
        ))
        self.add_item(RateItem(
            "R-002", "HYSD reinforcement (Fe415)",
            WorkCategory.REINFORCEMENT, Unit.KG, 68.00
        ))
        self.add_item(RateItem(
            "R-003", "HYSD reinforcement (Fe500)",
            WorkCategory.REINFORCEMENT, Unit.KG, 72.00
        ))
        self.add_item(RateItem(
            "R-004", "Binding wire",
            WorkCategory.REINFORCEMENT, Unit.KG, 85.00
        ))
        self.add_item(RateItem(
            "R-005", "Bar bending and placing",
            WorkCategory.REINFORCEMENT, Unit.KG, 12.00
        ))
        
        # FORMWORK
        self.add_item(RateItem(
            "F-001", "Formwork for foundation",
            WorkCategory.FORMWORK, Unit.SQ_M, 450.00
        ))
        self.add_item(RateItem(
            "F-002", "Formwork for column/pier",
            WorkCategory.FORMWORK, Unit.SQ_M, 650.00
        ))
        self.add_item(RateItem(
            "F-003", "Formwork for cap/beam",
            WorkCategory.FORMWORK, Unit.SQ_M, 750.00
        ))
        self.add_item(RateItem(
            "F-004", "Formwork for pile cap",
            WorkCategory.FORMWORK, Unit.SQ_M, 550.00
        ))
        
        # PILE FOUNDATION
        self.add_item(RateItem(
            "P-001", "Boring for cast-in-situ pile",
            WorkCategory.PILE_WORK, Unit.M, 3500.00
        ))
        self.add_item(RateItem(
            "P-002", "Boring through hard rock",
            WorkCategory.PILE_WORK, Unit.M, 8500.00
        ))
        self.add_item(RateItem(
            "P-003", "Pile load test",
            WorkCategory.PILE_WORK, Unit.NOS, 150000.00
        ))
        self.add_item(RateItem(
            "P-004", "Pile integrity test",
            WorkCategory.PILE_WORK, Unit.NOS, 25000.00
        ))
        
        # WELL FOUNDATION
        self.add_item(RateItem(
            "W-001", "Well sinking in soil",
            WorkCategory.WELL_WORK, Unit.M, 45000.00
        ))
        self.add_item(RateItem(
            "W-002", "Well sinking in hard soil/rock",
            WorkCategory.WELL_WORK, Unit.M, 85000.00
        ))
        self.add_item(RateItem(
            "W-003", "Well curb concreting",
            WorkCategory.WELL_WORK, Unit.CUM, 8200.00
        ))
        self.add_item(RateItem(
            "W-004", "Bottom plug concreting",
            WorkCategory.WELL_WORK, Unit.CUM, 9000.00
        ))
        self.add_item(RateItem(
            "W-005", "Well cap concreting",
            WorkCategory.WELL_WORK, Unit.CUM, 7500.00
        ))
        self.add_item(RateItem(
            "W-006", "Pneumatic sinking (if required)",
            WorkCategory.WELL_WORK, Unit.HOUR, 15000.00
        ))
        
        # STEEL WORK
        self.add_item(RateItem(
            "S-001", "Structural steel fabrication",
            WorkCategory.STEEL_WORK, Unit.KG, 85.00
        ))
        self.add_item(RateItem(
            "S-002", "Structural steel erection",
            WorkCategory.STEEL_WORK, Unit.KG, 25.00
        ))
        
        # PROTECTION WORK
        self.add_item(RateItem(
            "PR-001", "Anti-corrosive treatment",
            WorkCategory.PROTECTION_WORK, Unit.SQ_M, 450.00
        ))
        self.add_item(RateItem(
            "PR-002", "Guniting/Shotcreting",
            WorkCategory.PROTECTION_WORK, Unit.SQ_M, 850.00
        ))
    
    def add_item(self, item: RateItem):
        """Add item to SOR"""
        self.items[item.item_code] = item
    
    def get_item(self, item_code: str) -> Optional[RateItem]:
        """Get rate item by code"""
        return self.items.get(item_code)
    
    def get_items_by_category(self, category: WorkCategory) -> List[RateItem]:
        """Get all items in a category"""
        return [item for item in self.items.values() 
                if item.category == category]
    
    def update_rate(self, item_code: str, new_rate: float):
        """Update basic rate for an item"""
        if item_code in self.items:
            self.items[item_code].basic_rate = new_rate
    
    def apply_escalation(self, percentage: float):
        """Apply escalation to all rates"""
        for item in self.items.values():
            item.basic_rate *= (1 + percentage / 100)
    
    def export_to_json(self, filepath: str):
        """Export SOR to JSON"""
        data = {
            'name': self.name,
            'currency': self.currency,
            'items': {
                code: {
                    'description': item.description,
                    'category': item.category.value,
                    'unit': item.unit.value,
                    'basic_rate': item.basic_rate,
                    'total_rate': item.total_rate
                }
                for code, item in self.items.items()
            }
        }
        
        with open(filepath, 'w') as f:
            json.dump(data, f, indent=2)
    
    def import_from_json(self, filepath: str):
        """Import SOR from JSON"""
        with open(filepath, 'r') as f:
            data = json.load(f)
        
        self.name = data.get('name', self.name)
        self.currency = data.get('currency', self.currency)
        
        # Clear existing and load new
        self.items.clear()
        
        for code, item_data in data.get('items', {}).items():
            # Map category and unit
            category = WorkCategory(item_data['category'])
            unit = Unit(item_data['unit'])
            
            item = RateItem(
                item_code=code,
                description=item_data['description'],
                category=category,
                unit=unit,
                basic_rate=item_data['basic_rate']
            )
            self.add_item(item)


class RateAnalysis:
    """Detailed rate analysis for an item"""
    
    def __init__(self, item_code: str, sor: ScheduleOfRates):
        self.item_code = item_code
        self.sor = sor
        self.item = sor.get_item(item_code)
        
        self.materials: List[Dict] = []
        self.labour: List[Dict] = []
        self.equipment: List[Dict] = []
        self.overhead_pct: float = 0.0
        self.profit_pct: float = 10.0
        self.tax_pct: float = 18.0
    
    def add_material(self, description: str, unit: Unit, 
                     quantity: float, rate: float):
        """Add material component"""
        self.materials.append({
            'description': description,
            'unit': unit.value,
            'quantity': quantity,
            'rate': rate,
            'amount': quantity * rate
        })
    
    def add_labour(self, description: str, unit: Unit,
                   quantity: float, rate: float):
        """Add labour component"""
        self.labour.append({
            'description': description,
            'unit': unit.value,
            'quantity': quantity,
            'rate': rate,
            'amount': quantity * rate
        })
    
    def add_equipment(self, description: str, unit: Unit,
                      quantity: float, rate: float):
        """Add equipment component"""
        self.equipment.append({
            'description': description,
            'unit': unit.value,
            'quantity': quantity,
            'rate': rate,
            'amount': quantity * rate
        })
    
    def calculate(self) -> Dict:
        """Calculate complete rate analysis"""
        
        material_cost = sum(m['amount'] for m in self.materials)
        labour_cost = sum(l['amount'] for l in self.labour)
        equipment_cost = sum(e['amount'] for e in self.equipment)
        
        subtotal = material_cost + labour_cost + equipment_cost
        
        overhead = subtotal * self.overhead_pct / 100
        profit = (subtotal + overhead) * self.profit_pct / 100
        tax = (subtotal + overhead + profit) * self.tax_pct / 100
        
        total = subtotal + overhead + profit + tax
        
        return {
            'item_code': self.item_code,
            'description': self.item.description if self.item else '',
            'unit': self.item.unit.value if self.item else '',
            
            'materials': self.materials,
            'material_cost': material_cost,
            
            'labour': self.labour,
            'labour_cost': labour_cost,
            
            'equipment': self.equipment,
            'equipment_cost': equipment_cost,
            
            'subtotal': subtotal,
            'overhead': overhead,
            'profit': profit,
            'tax': tax,
            'total': total,
            
            'rate_per_unit': total
        }
    
    def generate_detailed_report(self) -> str:
        """Generate text report"""
        analysis = self.calculate()
        
        lines = [
            "=" * 80,
            f"RATE ANALYSIS: {analysis['description']}",
            "=" * 80,
            f"Item Code: {analysis['item_code']}",
            f"Unit: {analysis['unit']}",
            "",
            "MATERIALS:",
            "-" * 40
        ]
        
        for mat in analysis['materials']:
            lines.append(f"  {mat['description']}: {mat['quantity']} {mat['unit']} × "
                        f"{self.sor.currency_symbol}{mat['rate']:.2f} = "
                        f"{self.sor.currency_symbol}{mat['amount']:.2f}")
        
        lines.extend([
            f"  Total Materials: {self.sor.currency_symbol}{analysis['material_cost']:.2f}",
            "",
            "LABOUR:",
            "-" * 40
        ])
        
        for lab in analysis['labour']:
            lines.append(f"  {lab['description']}: {lab['quantity']} {lab['unit']} × "
                        f"{self.sor.currency_symbol}{lab['rate']:.2f} = "
                        f"{self.sor.currency_symbol}{lab['amount']:.2f}")
        
        lines.extend([
            f"  Total Labour: {self.sor.currency_symbol}{analysis['labour_cost']:.2f}",
            "",
            "EQUIPMENT:",
            "-" * 40
        ])
        
        for eq in analysis['equipment']:
            lines.append(f"  {eq['description']}: {eq['quantity']} {eq['unit']} × "
                        f"{self.sor.currency_symbol}{eq['rate']:.2f} = "
                        f"{self.sor.currency_symbol}{eq['amount']:.2f}")
        
        lines.extend([
            f"  Total Equipment: {self.sor.currency_symbol}{analysis['equipment_cost']:.2f}",
            "",
            "SUMMARY:",
            "-" * 40,
            f"  Subtotal:        {self.sor.currency_symbol}{analysis['subtotal']:>12.2f}",
            f"  Overhead ({self.overhead_pct}%): {self.sor.currency_symbol}{analysis['overhead']:>12.2f}",
            f"  Profit ({self.profit_pct}%):   {self.sor.currency_symbol}{analysis['profit']:>12.2f}",
            f"  Tax ({self.tax_pct}%):      {self.sor.currency_symbol}{analysis['tax']:>12.2f}",
            "-" * 40,
            f"  TOTAL:           {self.sor.currency_symbol}{analysis['total']:>12.2f}",
            f"  Rate per Unit:   {self.sor.currency_symbol}{analysis['rate_per_unit']:>12.2f}",
            "=" * 80
        ])
        
        return "\n".join(lines)


# Example usage
if __name__ == "__main__":
    sor = ScheduleOfRates("CPWD 2024", "INR", "₹")
    
    # Create rate analysis for M25 concrete
    analysis = RateAnalysis("C-003", sor)
    
    # Materials for 1 cum of M25 concrete
    analysis.add_material("Cement", Unit.KG, 400, 8.50)
    analysis.add_material("Fine Aggregate", Unit.CUM, 0.45, 1200)
    analysis.add_material("Coarse Aggregate", Unit.CUM, 0.90, 1100)
    analysis.add_material("Water", Unit.KL, 0.18, 50)
    
    # Labour
    analysis.add_labour("Mason", Unit.DAY, 0.2, 800)
    analysis.add_labour("Mazdoor", Unit.DAY, 2.0, 600)
    analysis.add_labour("Bhisti", Unit.DAY, 0.4, 650)
    
    # Equipment
    analysis.add_equipment("Concrete Mixer", Unit.HOUR, 1, 300)
    analysis.add_equipment("Vibrator", Unit.HOUR, 1, 150)
    
    # Generate report
    print(analysis.generate_detailed_report())
    
    # Export SOR
    sor.export_to_json("schedule_of_rates.json")
```

## File 2: `cost_estimator.py`

```python
"""
Cost Estimator Module
Generates detailed cost estimates from BOQ/BBS quantities
"""

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import List, Dict, Optional, Tuple
from datetime import datetime
import pandas as pd

from schedule_of_rates import ScheduleOfRates, RateItem, WorkCategory, Unit
from foundation_generator import FoundationModel, FoundationType


@dataclass
class CostItem:
    """Cost item for estimate"""
    item_code: str
    description: str
    category: WorkCategory
    unit: Unit
    quantity: float
    rate: float
    amount: float
    remarks: str = ""
    
    @property
    def formatted_amount(self) -> str:
        return f"{self.amount:,.2f}"


class FoundationCostEstimator:
    """Estimate costs for foundation types"""
    
    def __init__(self, sor: ScheduleOfRates):
        self.sor = sor
    
    def estimate_pile_foundation(self, foundation: FoundationModel) -> List[CostItem]:
        """Generate cost estimate for pile foundation"""
        
        pc = foundation.pile_config
        pd = foundation.pile_dims
        
        cost_items = []
        
        # 1. Excavation for pile cap
        excavation_vol = pc.cap_length * pc.cap_width * 2.0  # 2m depth
        cost_items.append(self._create_cost_item(
            "E-001", "Excavation for pile cap", 
            WorkCategory.EARTHWORK, Unit.CUM,
            excavation_vol, "E-001"
        ))
        
        # 2. Pile boring
        boring_length = pd.length * pc.total_piles
        cost_items.append(self._create_cost_item(
            "P-001", f"Boring for {pc.total_piles} piles",
            WorkCategory.PILE_WORK, Unit.M,
            boring_length, "P-001"
        ))
        
        # 3. Pile concreting (M35)
        pile_concrete = pd.concrete_volume * pc.total_piles
        cost_items.append(self._create_cost_item(
            "C-005", "Pile concreting M35",
            WorkCategory.CONCRETE, Unit.CUM,
            pile_concrete, "C-005"
        ))
        
        # 4. Pile reinforcement
        pile_steel = pd.steel_weight * pc.total_piles
        cost_items.append(self._create_cost_item(
            "R-003", "Pile reinforcement Fe500",
            WorkCategory.REINFORCEMENT, Unit.KG,
            pile_steel, "R-003"
        ))
        
        # 5. Pile cap concreting (M25)
        cap_concrete = pc.cap_length * pc.cap_width * pc.cap_thickness
        cost_items.append(self._create_cost_item(
            "C-003", "Pile cap concreting M25",
            WorkCategory.CONCRETE, Unit.CUM,
            cap_concrete, "C-003"
        ))
        
        # 6. Pile cap reinforcement
        cap_steel = self._estimate_cap_steel(pc)
        cost_items.append(self._create_cost_item(
            "R-003", "Pile cap reinforcement Fe500",
            WorkCategory.REINFORCEMENT, Unit.KG,
            cap_steel, "R-003"
        ))
        
        # 7. Pile cap formwork
        cap_formwork = self._calculate_formwork_area(pc)
        cost_items.append(self._create_cost_item(
            "F-004", "Pile cap formwork",
            WorkCategory.FORMWORK, Unit.SQ_M,
            cap_formwork, "F-004"
        ))
        
        # 8. Backfilling
        backfill = excavation_vol - cap_concrete
        if backfill > 0:
            cost_items.append(self._create_cost_item(
                "E-003", "Backfilling around pile cap",
                WorkCategory.EARTHWORK, Unit.CUM,
                backfill, "E-003"
            ))
        
        # 9. Pile testing (5% of piles)
        test_piles = max(1, int(pc.total_piles * 0.05))
        cost_items.append(self._create_cost_item(
            "P-003", f"Pile load test ({test_piles} nos)",
            WorkCategory.PILE_WORK, Unit.NOS,
            test_piles, "P-003"
        ))
        
        # 10. Pile integrity test (all piles)
        cost_items.append(self._create_cost_item(
            "P-004", f"Pile integrity test ({pc.total_piles} nos)",
            WorkCategory.PILE_WORK, Unit.NOS,
            pc.total_piles, "P-004"
        ))
        
        return cost_items
    
    def estimate_well_foundation(self, foundation: FoundationModel) -> List[CostItem]:
        """Generate cost estimate for well foundation"""
        
        wd = foundation.well_dims
        
        cost_items = []
        
        # 1. Well sinking
        cost_items.append(self._create_cost_item(
            "W-001", f"Well sinking ({wd.sinking_depth:.1f}m)",
            WorkCategory.WELL_WORK, Unit.M,
            wd.sinking_depth, "W-001"
        ))
        
        # 2. Well curb concreting
        curb_volume = (wd.curb_dia ** 2 - wd.external_dia ** 2) * 0.785 * wd.curb_height
        cost_items.append(self._create_cost_item(
            "W-003", "Well curb concreting",
            WorkCategory.WELL_WORK, Unit.CUM,
            curb_volume, "W-003"
        ))
        
        # 3. Well steining concreting
        steining_volume = wd.steining_volume
        cost_items.append(self._create_cost_item(
            "C-003", "Well steining concreting M25",
            WorkCategory.CONCRETE, Unit.CUM,
            steining_volume, "C-003"
        ))
        
        # 4. Bottom plug concreting
        plug_volume = (wd.internal_dia ** 2) * 0.785 * wd.bottom_plug_thickness
        cost_items.append(self._create_cost_item(
            "W-004", "Bottom plug concreting",
            WorkCategory.WELL_WORK, Unit.CUM,
            plug_volume, "W-004"
        ))
        
        # 5. Well cap concreting
        cap_volume = ((wd.external_dia + 1.0) ** 2) * 0.785 * wd.well_cap_thickness
        cost_items.append(self._create_cost_item(
            "W-005", "Well cap concreting",
            WorkCategory.WELL_WORK, Unit.CUM,
            cap_volume, "W-005"
        ))
        
        # 6. Well reinforcement
        well_steel = self._estimate_well_steel(wd)
        cost_items.append(self._create_cost_item(
            "R-003", "Well reinforcement Fe500",
            WorkCategory.REINFORCEMENT, Unit.KG,
            well_steel, "R-003"
        ))
        
        # 7. Well formwork
        well_surface = wd.external_dia * 3.14159 * wd.sinking_depth
        cost_items.append(self._create_cost_item(
            "F-001", "Well formwork",
            WorkCategory.FORMWORK, Unit.SQ_M,
            well_surface, "F-001"
        ))
        
        # 8. Dewatering (estimated)
        dewatering_hours = wd.sinking_depth * 24 * 10  # 10 days per meter
        cost_items.append(self._create_cost_item(
            "E-005", "Dewatering during sinking",
            WorkCategory.EARTHWORK, Unit.HOUR,
            dewatering_hours, "E-005"
        ))
        
        return cost_items
    
    def estimate_open_foundation(self, foundation: FoundationModel) -> List[CostItem]:
        """Generate cost estimate for open foundation"""
        
        # This would be implemented based on open foundation dimensions
        cost_items = []
        
        # Placeholder - would need open foundation dimensions
        cost_items.append(self._create_cost_item(
            "E-001", "Excavation for open foundation",
            WorkCategory.EARTHWORK, Unit.CUM,
            100, "E-001"
        ))
        
        cost_items.append(self._create_cost_item(
            "C-003", "Footing concreting M25",
            WorkCategory.CONCRETE, Unit.CUM,
            50, "C-003"
        ))
        
        return cost_items
    
    def _create_cost_item(self, item_code: str, description: str,
                         category: WorkCategory, unit: Unit,
                         quantity: float, sor_code: str) -> CostItem:
        """Create cost item from SOR"""
        
        rate_item = self.sor.get_item(sor_code)
        rate = rate_item.total_rate if rate_item else 0
        
        return CostItem(
            item_code=item_code,
            description=description,
            category=category,
            unit=unit,
            quantity=round(quantity, 3),
            rate=rate,
            amount=round(quantity * rate, 2)
        )
    
    def _estimate_cap_steel(self, pc) -> float:
        """Estimate pile cap steel quantity"""
        # Approximate formula: 80-120 kg/cum depending on size
        cap_volume = pc.cap_length * pc.cap_width * pc.cap_thickness
        return cap_volume * 100  # 100 kg/cum
    
    def _calculate_formwork_area(self, pc) -> float:
        """Calculate pile cap formwork area"""
        # Sides + bottom (if required)
        side_area = 2 * (pc.cap_length + pc.cap_width) * pc.cap_thickness
        bottom_area = pc.cap_length * pc.cap_width
        return side_area + bottom_area * 0.3  # 30% of bottom
    
    def _estimate_well_steel(self, wd) -> float:
        """Estimate well reinforcement quantity"""
        # Approximate: 50-80 kg/cum of concrete
        total_concrete = wd.steining_volume + \
                        (wd.curb_dia ** 2 * 0.785 * wd.curb_height) + \
                        (wd.internal_dia ** 2 * 0.785 * wd.bottom_plug_thickness)
        return total_concrete * 60  # 60 kg/cum average


class ProjectCostEstimator:
    """Complete project cost estimation"""
    
    def __init__(self, sor: ScheduleOfRates, 
                 project_name: str = "Bridge Project"):
        self.sor = sor
        self.project_name = project_name
        self.foundation_estimator = FoundationCostEstimator(sor)
        
        self.cost_items: List[CostItem] = []
        self.overhead_pct: float = 5.0
        self.contingency_pct: float = 3.0
    
    def add_foundation(self, foundation: FoundationModel):
        """Add foundation to estimate"""
        
        if foundation.foundation_type == FoundationType.PILE:
            items = self.foundation_estimator.estimate_pile_foundation(foundation)
        elif foundation.foundation_type == FoundationType.WELL:
            items = self.foundation_estimator.estimate_well_foundation(foundation)
        else:
            items = self.foundation_estimator.estimate_open_foundation(foundation)
        
        self.cost_items.extend(items)
    
    def add_custom_item(self, item_code: str, description: str,
                        category: WorkCategory, unit: Unit,
                        quantity: float, sor_code: str):
        """Add custom cost item"""
        
        rate_item = self.sor.get_item(sor_code)
        rate = rate_item.total_rate if rate_item else 0
        
        item = CostItem(
            item_code=item_code,
            description=description,
            category=category,
            unit=unit,
            quantity=quantity,
            rate=rate,
            amount=quantity * rate
        )
        self.cost_items.append(item)
    
    def calculate_summary(self) -> Dict:
        """Calculate cost summary"""
        
        # Group by category
        category_totals = {}
        for item in self.cost_items:
            cat = item.category.value
            if cat not in category_totals:
                category_totals[cat] = 0
            category_totals[cat] += item.amount
        
        # Subtotal
        subtotal = sum(item.amount for item in self.cost_items)
        
        # Overhead
        overhead = subtotal * self.overhead_pct / 100
        
        # Contingency
        contingency = (subtotal + overhead) * self.contingency_pct / 100
        
        # Grand total
        grand_total = subtotal + overhead + contingency
        
        return {
            'category_breakdown': category_totals,
            'subtotal': subtotal,
            'overhead': overhead,
            'contingency': contingency,
            'grand_total': grand_total,
            'total_items': len(self.cost_items)
        }
    
    def generate_abstract(self) -> pd.DataFrame:
        """Generate abstract of cost"""
        
        data = []
        for item in self.cost_items:
            data.append({
                'Item Code': item.item_code,
                'Description': item.description,
                'Category': item.category.value,
                'Unit': item.unit.value,
                'Quantity': item.quantity,
                'Rate': item.rate,
                'Amount': item.amount
            })
        
        df = pd.DataFrame(data)
        
        # Add totals by category
        summary = df.groupby('Category')['Amount'].sum().reset_index()
        summary['Item Code'] = ''
        summary['Description'] = 'SUBTOTAL'
        summary['Unit'] = ''
        summary['Quantity'] = ''
        summary['Rate'] = ''
        
        # Reorder columns
        summary = summary[['Item Code', 'Description', 'Category', 'Unit', 
                          'Quantity', 'Rate', 'Amount']]
        
        # Combine
        df = pd.concat([df, summary], ignore_index=True)
        
        # Sort by category
        df = df.sort_values(['Category', 'Item Code'])
        
        return df
    
    def export_to_excel(self, filepath: str):
        """Export complete estimate to Excel"""
        
        summary = self.calculate_summary()
        
        with pd.ExcelWriter(filepath, engine='openpyxl') as writer:
            # 1. Detailed Estimate
            df_detailed = self.generate_abstract()
            df_detailed.to_excel(writer, sheet_name='Detailed Estimate', index=False)
            
            # 2. Category Summary
            category_data = []
            for cat, amount in summary['category_breakdown'].items():
                category_data.append({
                    'Category': cat,
                    'Amount': amount,
                    'Percentage': amount / summary['subtotal'] * 100
                })
            df_category = pd.DataFrame(category_data)
            df_category.to_excel(writer, sheet_name='Category Summary', index=False)
            
            # 3. Project Summary
            summary_data = {
                'Description': [
                    'Subtotal',
                    f'Overhead ({self.overhead_pct}%)',
                    f'Contingency ({self.contingency_pct}%)',
                    'GRAND TOTAL'
                ],
                'Amount': [
                    summary['subtotal'],
                    summary['overhead'],
                    summary['contingency'],
                    summary['grand_total']
                ]
            }
            df_summary = pd.DataFrame(summary_data)
            df_summary.to_excel(writer, sheet_name='Project Summary', index=False)
            
            # 4. Rate Reference
            rate_data = []
            for item in self.cost_items:
                rate_item = self.sor.get_item(item.item_code)
                if rate_item:
                    rate_data.append({
                        'Item Code': item.item_code,
                        'Description': rate_item.description,
                        'Unit': rate_item.unit.value,
                        'Basic Rate': rate_item.basic_rate,
                        'Total Rate': rate_item.total_rate
                    })
            df_rates = pd.DataFrame(rate_data)
            df_rates.to_excel(writer, sheet_name='Rate Reference', index=False)
        
        print(f"Cost estimate exported: {filepath}")
        return filepath
    
    def print_estimate(self):
        """Print formatted estimate to console"""
        
        summary = self.calculate_summary()
        
        print("\n" + "=" * 100)
        print(f"COST ESTIMATE: {self.project_name}")
        print("=" * 100)
        print(f"{'Item':<10} {'Description':<40} {'Unit':<8} {'Qty':>10} "
              f"{'Rate':>12} {'Amount':>15}")
        print("-" * 100)
        
        current_category = None
        
        for item in self.cost_items:
            if item.category.value != current_category:
                current_category = item.category.value
                print(f"\n{current_category.upper()}")
                print("-" * 100)
            
            print(f"{item.item_code:<10} {item.description[:38]:<40} "
                  f"{item.unit.value:<8} {item.quantity:>10.3f} "
                  f"{self.sor.currency_symbol}{item.rate:>10.2f} "
                  f"{self.sor.currency_symbol}{item.amount:>12,.2f}")
        
        print("=" * 100)
        print(f"{'SUBTOTAL':<90} {self.sor.currency_symbol}{summary['subtotal']:>12,.2f}")
        print(f"{'Overhead (' + str(self.overhead_pct) + '%)':<90} "
              f"{self.sor.currency_symbol}{summary['overhead']:>12,.2f}")
        print(f"{'Contingency (' + str(self.contingency_pct) + '%)':<90} "
              f"{self.sor.currency_symbol}{summary['contingency']:>12,.2f}")
        print("=" * 100)
        print(f"{'GRAND TOTAL':<90} {self.sor.currency_symbol}{summary['grand_total']:>12,.2f}")
        print("=" * 100)


# Example usage
if __name__ == "__main__":
    from foundation_generator import create_pile_foundation, create_well_foundation
    
    # Create SOR
    sor = ScheduleOfRates("CPWD 2024", "INR", "₹")
    
    # Create estimator
    estimator = ProjectCostEstimator(sor, "Bridge Foundation Works")
    
    # Add foundations
    pile_foundation = create_pile_foundation("PIER", 1, 4, 3, 1.2, 15.0)
    well_foundation = create_well_foundation("ABUTMENT", 1, 6.0, 12.0)
    
    estimator.add_foundation(pile_foundation)
    estimator.add_foundation(well_foundation)
    
    # Print estimate
    estimator.print_estimate()
    
    # Export to Excel
    estimator.export_to_excel("foundation_cost_estimate.xlsx")
```

## File 3: `cost_comparison.py`

```python
"""
Cost Comparison Module
Compares costs between different foundation alternatives
"""

from typing import List, Dict, Tuple
import pandas as pd
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt

from schedule_of_rates import ScheduleOfRates
from cost_estimator import ProjectCostEstimator, FoundationCostEstimator
from foundation_generator import FoundationModel


class FoundationAlternatives:
    """Define foundation alternatives for comparison"""
    
    def __init__(self, sor: ScheduleOfRates):
        self.sor = sor
        self.alternatives: Dict[str, FoundationModel] = {}
        self.estimates: Dict[str, Dict] = {}
    
    def add_alternative(self, name: str, foundation: FoundationModel):
        """Add foundation alternative"""
        self.alternatives[name] = foundation
    
    def compare(self) -> pd.DataFrame:
        """Compare all alternatives"""
        
        estimator = FoundationCostEstimator(self.sor)
        
        results = []
        
        for name, foundation in self.alternatives.items():
            # Generate cost items
            if foundation.foundation_type.value == "PILE":
                items = estimator.estimate_pile_foundation(foundation)
            elif foundation.foundation_type.value == "WELL":
                items = estimator.estimate_well_foundation(foundation)
            else:
                items = estimator.estimate_open_foundation(foundation)
            
            # Calculate totals
            total_cost = sum(item.amount for item in items)
            
            # Calculate quantities
            concrete_qty = sum(item.quantity for item in items 
                             if item.category.value == "Concrete Work")
            steel_qty = sum(item.quantity for item in items 
                           if item.category.value == "Reinforcement")
            
            results.append({
                'Alternative': name,
                'Type': foundation.foundation_type.value,
                'Total Cost': total_cost,
                'Concrete (cum)': concrete_qty,
                'Steel (kg)': steel_qty,
                'Cost per cum concrete': total_cost / concrete_qty if concrete_qty > 0 else 0,
                'Cost per kg steel': total_cost / steel_qty if steel_qty > 0 else 0
            })
            
            self.estimates[name] = {
                'items': items,
                'total': total_cost
            }
        
        return pd.DataFrame(results)
    
    def generate_comparison_chart(self, output_path: str = "cost_comparison.png"):
        """Generate comparison chart"""
        
        df = self.compare()
        
        fig, axes = plt.subplots(2, 2, figsize=(14, 10))
        
        # Total cost comparison
        ax1 = axes[0, 0]
        bars = ax1.bar(df['Alternative'], df['Total Cost'] / 100000, 
                       color=['#1f77b4', '#ff7f0e', '#2ca02c'])
        ax1.set_ylabel('Cost (Lakhs)')
        ax1.set_title('Total Cost Comparison')
        ax1.tick_params(axis='x', rotation=45)
        
        # Add value labels
        for bar in bars:
            height = bar.get_height()
            ax1.text(bar.get_x() + bar.get_width()/2., height,
                    f'₹{height:.1f}L',
                    ha='center', va='bottom')
        
        # Concrete quantity
        ax2 = axes[0, 1]
        ax2.bar(df['Alternative'], df['Concrete (cum)'], 
                color=['#1f77b4', '#ff7f0e', '#2ca02c'])
        ax2.set_ylabel('Concrete (cum)')
        ax2.set_title('Concrete Quantity')
        ax2.tick_params(axis='x', rotation=45)
        
        # Steel quantity
        ax3 = axes[1, 0]
        ax3.bar(df['Alternative'], df['Steel (kg)'] / 1000, 
                color=['#1f77b4', '#ff7f0e', '#2ca02c'])
        ax3.set_ylabel('Steel (MT)')
        ax3.set_title('Steel Quantity')
        ax3.tick_params(axis='x', rotation=45)
        
        # Cost efficiency
        ax4 = axes[1, 1]
        efficiency = df['Total Cost'] / df['Total Cost'].max() * 100
        ax4.bar(df['Alternative'], efficiency, 
                color=['#1f77b4', '#ff7f0e', '#2ca02c'])
        ax4.set_ylabel('Relative Cost (%)')
        ax4.set_title('Cost Efficiency (Lower is Better)')
        ax4.axhline(y=100, color='r', linestyle='--', label='Baseline')
        ax4.tick_params(axis='x', rotation=45)
        
        plt.tight_layout()
        plt.savefig(output_path, dpi=150, bbox_inches='tight')
        print(f"Comparison chart saved: {output_path}")
        
        return output_path
    
    def export_detailed_comparison(self, output_path: str = "foundation_comparison.xlsx"):
        """Export detailed comparison to Excel"""
        
        with pd.ExcelWriter(output_path, engine='openpyxl') as writer:
            # Summary comparison
            df_summary = self.compare()
            df_summary.to_excel(writer, sheet_name='Summary', index=False)
            
            # Detailed breakdown for each alternative
            for name, data in self.estimates.items():
                items_data = []
                for item in data['items']:
                    items_data.append({
                        'Item Code': item.item_code,
                        'Description': item.description,
                        'Category': item.category.value,
                        'Unit': item.unit.value,
                        'Quantity': item.quantity,
                        'Rate': item.rate,
                        'Amount': item.amount
                    })
                
                df_detail = pd.DataFrame(items_data)
                
                # Sheet name limited to 31 chars
                sheet_name = name[:31]
                df_detail.to_excel(writer, sheet_name=sheet_name, index=False)
        
        print(f"Detailed comparison exported: {output_path}")
        return output_path


class SensitivityAnalysis:
    """Sensitivity analysis for cost factors"""
    
    def __init__(self, base_estimate: ProjectCostEstimator):
        self.base = base_estimate
        self.base_summary = base_estimate.calculate_summary()
    
    def analyze_material_cost_variation(self, 
                                        variation_range: Tuple[float, float] = (-20, 20),
                                        step: float = 5) -> pd.DataFrame:
        """Analyze sensitivity to material cost changes"""
        
        results = []
        
        for pct in range(int(variation_range[0]), int(variation_range[1]) + 1, int(step)):
            # Calculate new total with material cost variation
            # (Assuming materials are 60% of total cost)
            material_factor = 0.6
            variation = self.base_summary['grand_total'] * material_factor * (pct / 100)
            new_total = self.base_summary['grand_total'] + variation
            
            results.append({
                'Material Cost Change (%)': pct,
                'Grand Total': new_total,
                'Change from Base': variation,
                'Change %': pct * material_factor
            })
        
        return pd.DataFrame(results)
    
    def analyze_quantity_variation(self,
                                   variation_range: Tuple[float, float] = (-10, 10),
                                   step: float = 2) -> pd.DataFrame:
        """Analyze sensitivity to quantity changes"""
        
        results = []
        
        for pct in range(int(variation_range[0]), int(variation_range[1]) + 1, int(step)):
            new_total = self.base_summary['grand_total'] * (1 + pct / 100)
            
            results.append({
                'Quantity Change (%)': pct,
                'Grand Total': new_total,
                'Change from Base': new_total - self.base_summary['grand_total'],
                'Change %': pct
            })
        
        return pd.DataFrame(results)
    
    def generate_tornado_diagram(self, output_path: str = "sensitivity_analysis.png"):
        """Generate tornado diagram for sensitivity analysis"""
        
        # Define factors and their impact
        factors = {
            'Concrete Cost (+20%)': self.base_summary['grand_total'] * 0.25 * 0.2,
            'Steel Cost (+20%)': self.base_summary['grand_total'] * 0.20 * 0.2,
            'Labour Cost (+20%)': self.base_summary['grand_total'] * 0.30 * 0.2,
            'Equipment Cost (+20%)': self.base_summary['grand_total'] * 0.15 * 0.2,
            'Quantity Overrun (+10%)': self.base_summary['grand_total'] * 0.10,
        }
        
        fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(10, 6))
        
        # Sort by absolute impact
        sorted_factors = sorted(factors.items(), key=lambda x: abs(x[1]), reverse=True)
        
        y_pos = range(len(sorted_factors))
        impacts = [f[1] / 100000 for f in sorted_factors]  # Convert to lakhs
        
        colors = ['red' if i < 0 else 'green' for i in impacts]
        
        ax.barh(y_pos, impacts, color=colors, alpha=0.7)
        ax.set_yticks(y_pos)
        ax.set_yticklabels([f[0] for f in sorted_factors])
        ax.set_xlabel('Cost Impact (₹ Lakhs)')
        ax.set_title('Sensitivity Analysis - Cost Factor Impact')
        ax.axvline(x=0, color='black', linewidth=0.5)
        
        plt.tight_layout()
        plt.savefig(output_path, dpi=150, bbox_inches='tight')
        print(f"Tornado diagram saved: {output_path}")
        
        return output_path


# Example usage
if __name__ == "__main__":
    from foundation_generator import create_pile_foundation, create_well_foundation
    
    sor = ScheduleOfRates("CPWD 2024", "INR", "₹")
    
    # Create alternatives
    alternatives = FoundationAlternatives(sor)
    
    # Alternative 1: Pile foundation
    alternatives.add_alternative(
        "Pile Foundation (1.2m dia)",
        create_pile_foundation("PIER", 1, 4, 3, 1.2, 15.0)
    )
    
    # Alternative 2: Larger piles
    alternatives.add_alternative(
        "Pile Foundation (1.5m dia)",
        create_pile_foundation("PIER", 1, 3, 2, 1.5, 18.0)
    )
    
    # Alternative 3: Well foundation
    alternatives.add_alternative(
        "Well Foundation (6m dia)",
        create_well_foundation("PIER", 1, 6.0, 12.0)
    )
    
    # Compare
    print("\nFOUNDATION ALTERNATIVES COMPARISON")
    print("=" * 80)
    df = alternatives.compare()
    print(df.to_string(index=False))
    
    # Export
    alternatives.export_detailed_comparison("foundation_alternatives.xlsx")
    alternatives.generate_comparison_chart("foundation_comparison.png")
    
    # Sensitivity analysis
    print("\nSENSITIVITY ANALYSIS")
    print("=" * 80)
    
    base_estimator = ProjectCostEstimator(sor, "Base")
    base_estimator.add_foundation(create_pile_foundation("PIER", 1, 4, 3, 1.2, 15.0))
    
    sensitivity = SensitivityAnalysis(base_estimator)
    
    df_material = sensitivity.analyze_material_cost_variation()
    print("\nMaterial Cost Sensitivity:")
    print(df_material.to_string(index=False))
    
    sensitivity.generate_tornado_diagram("sensitivity_analysis.png")
```

## File 4: `cost_integration.py`

```python
"""
Cost Integration Module
Integrates cost estimation with BOQ/BBS generation
"""

from typing import List, Dict
from pathlib import Path

from schedule_of_rates import ScheduleOfRates
from cost_estimator import ProjectCostEstimator, FoundationCostEstimator
from bbs_exporter import BBSExporter
from foundation_generator import FoundationModel


class IntegratedCostSystem:
    """Integrated cost estimation system"""
    
    def __init__(self, sor_path: str = None):
        self.sor = ScheduleOfRates("Project SOR", "INR", "₹")
        
        if sor_path and Path(sor_path).exists():
            self.sor.import_from_json(sor_path)
        
        self.estimators: Dict[str, ProjectCostEstimator] = {}
    
    def create_project_estimate(self, project_name: str) -> ProjectCostEstimator:
        """Create new project estimate"""
        estimator = ProjectCostEstimator(self.sor, project_name)
        self.estimators[project_name] = estimator
        return estimator
    
    def generate_complete_package(self,
                                   foundations: List[FoundationModel],
                                   project_name: str,
                                   output_dir: str = "./output") -> Dict[str, str]:
        """Generate complete cost estimation package"""
        
        output_path = Path(output_dir)
        output_path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        
        results = {}
        
        # 1. Create project estimate
        estimator = self.create_project_estimate(project_name)
        
        for foundation in foundations:
            estimator.add_foundation(foundation)
        
        # 2. Export cost estimate
        cost_file = output_path / f"{project_name}_cost_estimate.xlsx"
        estimator.export_to_excel(str(cost_file))
        results['cost_estimate'] = str(cost_file)
        
        # 3. Generate BBS (if reinforcement module available)
        try:
            from reinforcement_calculator import FoundationReinforcementCalculator
            from bbs_exporter import BBSExporter
            
            calc = FoundationReinforcementCalculator()
            exporter = BBSExporter()
            
            all_bars = []
            for foundation in foundations:
                bars = self._get_reinforcement(calc, foundation)
                all_bars.extend(bars)
            
            bbs_file = output_path / f"{project_name}_bbs.xlsx"
            exporter.export_bbs(all_bars, {
                'project_name': project_name,
                'structure': 'Bridge Foundations'
            }, str(bbs_file))
            results['bbs'] = str(bbs_file)
            
        except ImportError:
            print("Reinforcement module not available, skipping BBS")
        
        # 4. Generate summary report
        summary_file = output_path / f"{project_name}_cost_summary.txt"
        self._generate_summary_report(estimator, str(summary_file))
        results['summary'] = str(summary_file)
        
        # 5. Print to console
        estimator.print_estimate()
        
        return results
    
    def _get_reinforcement(self, calc, foundation: FoundationModel):
        """Get reinforcement bars for foundation"""
        # This would integrate with reinforcement module
        return []
    
    def _generate_summary_report(self, estimator: ProjectCostEstimator, 
                                  filepath: str):
        """Generate text summary report"""
        
        summary = estimator.calculate_summary()
        
        lines = [
            "=" * 80,
            f"COST ESTIMATE SUMMARY",
            f"Project: {estimator.project_name}",
            f"Date: {pd.Timestamp.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M')}",
            "=" * 80,
            "",
            "CATEGORY BREAKDOWN:",
            "-" * 40
        ]
        
        for category, amount in summary['category_breakdown'].items():
            lines.append(f"  {category:<30} ₹{amount:>15,.2f}")
        
        lines.extend([
            "-" * 40,
            f"  {'SUBTOTAL':<30} ₹{summary['subtotal']:>15,.2f}",
            f"  {'Overhead':<30} ₹{summary['overhead']:>15,.2f}",
            f"  {'Contingency':<30} ₹{summary['contingency']:>15,.2f}",
            "=" * 40,
            f"  {'GRAND TOTAL':<30} ₹{summary['grand_total']:>15,.2f}",
            "=" * 80,
            "",
            f"Total Items: {summary['total_items']}",
            "",
            "NOTE: This estimate is based on Schedule of Rates and",
            "      standard quantities. Actual costs may vary.",
            "=" * 80
        ])
        
        with open(filepath, 'w') as f:
            f.write('\n'.join(lines))
        
        print(f"Summary report saved: {filepath}")
    
    def update_rates_from_market(self, escalation_pct: float):
        """Update all rates based on market escalation"""
        self.sor.apply_escalation(escalation_pct)
        print(f"Rates updated with {escalation_pct}% escalation")
    
    def export_sor_template(self, filepath: str = "schedule_of_rates_template.xlsx"):
        """Export SOR as Excel template for editing"""
        
        data = []
        for code, item in self.sor.items.items():
            data.append({
                'Item Code': code,
                'Description': item.description,
                'Category': item.category.value,
                'Unit': item.unit.value,
                'Basic Rate': item.basic_rate,
                'Overhead %': item.overhead_pct,
                'Profit %': item.profit_pct,
                'Tax %': item.taxes_pct,
                'Total Rate': item.total_rate
            })
        
        df = pd.DataFrame(data)
        df.to_excel(filepath, index=False)
        print(f"SOR template exported: {filepath}")
        return filepath


# Example usage
if __name__ == "__main__":
    from foundation_generator import create_pile_foundation, create_well_foundation
    
    # Create integrated system
    system = IntegratedCostSystem()
    
    # Define foundations
    foundations = [
        create_pile_foundation("PIER", 1, 4, 3, 1.2, 15.0),
        create_pile_foundation("PIER", 2, 4, 3, 1.2, 18.0),
        create_well_foundation("ABUTMENT", 1, 6.0, 12.0),
        create_well_foundation("ABUTMENT", 2, 6.0, 10.0)
    ]
    
    # Generate complete package
    results = system.generate_complete_package(
        foundations,
        "Multi_Span_Bridge",
        "./output_cost"
    )
    
    print("\nGenerated files:")
    for key, path in results.items():
        print(f"  {key}: {path}")
    
    # Export SOR template
    system.export_sor_template("./schedule_of_rates.xlsx")
```

## KEY FEATURES

| Feature | Description |
|---------|-------------|
| **Schedule of Rates** | CPWD/State PWD compliant rate database |
| **Rate Analysis** | Detailed material/labour/equipment breakdown |
| **Foundation Types** | Pile, Well, Open foundation cost models |
| **Cost Categories** | Earthwork, Concrete, Steel, Formwork, etc. |
| **Overheads** | Configurable overhead and profit percentages |
| **Taxes** | GST/VAT calculations |
| **Alternatives** | Compare different foundation options |
| **Sensitivity** | Tornado diagrams for risk analysis |
| **Export** | Excel with multiple sheets, charts |
