## File 1: `reinforcement_schedule_core.py`

```python
"""
Core Reinforcement Schedule Module
Complete bar bending schedule system for all bridge elements
"""

import math
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import List, Tuple, Dict, Optional, Union
from enum import Enum
from datetime import datetime


class ElementType(Enum):
    """Bridge structural elements"""
    PILE = "Pile"
    PILE_CAP = "Pile Cap"
    WELL = "Well Foundation"
    WELL_CAP = "Well Cap"
    PIER_SHAFT = "Pier Shaft"
    PIER_CAP = "Pier Cap"
    ABUTMENT_WALL = "Abutment Wall"
    ABUTMENT_CAP = "Abutment Cap"
    DECK_SLAB = "Deck Slab"
    GIRDER = "Girder"
    CROSS_GIRDER = "Cross Girder"
    DIAPHRAGM = "Diaphragm"
    BEARING_PEDESTAL = "Bearing Pedestal"
    APPROACH_SLAB = "Approach Slab"
    PARAPET = "Parapet"


class BarShapeCode(Enum):
    """IS:2502 Bar Shape Codes"""
    A = "A"  # Straight bar
    B = "B"  # L-bar with 90° hook
    C = "C"  # L-bar with 135° hook
    D = "D"  # L-bar with 180° hook
    E = "E"  # U-bar
    F = "F"  # Rectangular stirrup
    G = "G"  # Square stirrup
    H = "H"  # Circular stirrup
    I = "I"  # Triangular stirrup
    J = "J"  # Cranked bar
    K = "K"  # Double cranked bar
    L = "L"  # Spiral/Circular
    M = "M"  # Straight with hooks both ends
    N = "N"  # L-bar with unequal legs


@dataclass
class BarSegment:
    """Individual segment of a bar"""
    length_mm: float
    angle_degrees: float = 0  # 0 = straight, bend angle
    
    @property
    def is_bend(self) -> bool:
        return self.angle_degrees != 0


@dataclass
class BarShape:
    """Complete bar shape definition"""
    shape_code: BarShapeCode
    description: str
    segments: List[BarSegment]
    hook_type: Optional[int] = None  # 90, 135, 180
    
    def calculate_cutting_length(self, bar_dia_mm: int) -> float:
        """Calculate cutting length with bend deductions"""
        total = sum(seg.length_mm for seg in self.segments)
        
        # IS:2502 bend deductions
        deductions = {
            45: 1.0 * bar_dia_mm,
            90: 2.0 * bar_dia_mm,
            135: 2.5 * bar_dia_mm,
            180: 3.0 * bar_dia_mm
        }
        
        for seg in self.segments:
            if seg.is_bend and seg.angle_degrees in deductions:
                total -= deductions[seg.angle_degrees]
        
        return max(0, total)
    
    def to_dict(self) -> Dict:
        return {
            'code': self.shape_code.value,
            'description': self.description,
            'segments': len(self.segments)
        }


@dataclass
class ReinforcementBar:
    """Complete reinforcement bar entry"""
    # Identification
    bar_mark: str
    element_type: ElementType
    element_id: str  # e.g., "Pier-1", "Cap-2"
    
    # Bar properties
    shape: BarShape
    diameter_mm: int
    grade: str = "Fe500"
    
    # Quantities
    num_bars: int = 1
    spacing_mm: Optional[int] = None  # For distributed bars
    
    # Lengths
    cutting_length_mm: float = 0
    total_length_m: float = 0
    
    # Location
    location: str = ""  # "Bottom", "Top", "Side", etc.
    layer: str = ""     # "Outer", "Inner", etc.
    
    # Additional info
    lap_length_mm: Optional[float] = None
    development_length_mm: Optional[float] = None
    remarks: str = ""
    
    def __post_init__(self):
        if self.cutting_length_mm == 0:
            self.cutting_length_mm = self.shape.calculate_cutting_length(self.diameter_mm)
        if self.total_length_m == 0:
            self.total_length_m = (self.cutting_length_mm * self.num_bars) / 1000
    
    @property
    def unit_weight_kg_per_m(self) -> float:
        """Weight per meter (d² × 0.00617)"""
        return (self.diameter_mm ** 2) * 0.00617 / 1000
    
    @property
    def weight_per_bar_kg(self) -> float:
        """Weight of one bar"""
        return self.unit_weight_kg_per_m * (self.cutting_length_mm / 1000)
    
    @property
    def total_weight_kg(self) -> float:
        """Total weight for all bars"""
        return self.weight_per_bar_kg * self.num_bars
    
    def to_bbs_row(self) -> Dict:
        """Convert to BBS table row"""
        return {
            'bar_mark': self.bar_mark,
            'element_type': self.element_type.value,
            'element_id': self.element_id,
            'shape_code': self.shape.shape_code.value,
            'description': self.shape.description,
            'diameter_mm': self.diameter_mm,
            'grade': self.grade,
            'num_bars': self.num_bars,
            'spacing_mm': self.spacing_mm,
            'cutting_length_mm': round(self.cutting_length_mm, 0),
            'total_length_m': round(self.total_length_m, 2),
            'unit_weight': round(self.unit_weight_kg_per_m * 1000, 3),
            'weight_per_bar': round(self.weight_per_bar_kg, 2),
            'total_weight_kg': round(self.total_weight_kg, 2),
            'location': self.location,
            'remarks': self.remarks
        }


class BarShapeLibrary:
    """Library of standard bar shapes"""
    
    @staticmethod
    def straight_bar(total_length_mm: float) -> BarShape:
        """Shape A: Straight bar"""
        return BarShape(
            shape_code=BarShapeCode.A,
            description="Straight Bar",
            segments=[BarSegment(total_length_mm, 0)]
        )
    
    @staticmethod
    def l_bar_90(long_leg_mm: float, short_leg_mm: float,
                 hook_length_mm: float = 0) -> BarShape:
        """Shape B: L-bar with 90° hook"""
        total_short = short_leg_mm + hook_length_mm
        
        segments = [
            BarSegment(long_leg_mm, 0),
            BarSegment(0, 90),
            BarSegment(total_short, 0)
        ]
        
        return BarShape(
            shape_code=BarShapeCode.B,
            description="L-Bar with 90° Hook",
            segments=segments,
            hook_type=90
        )
    
    @staticmethod
    def l_bar_135(long_leg_mm: float, short_leg_mm: float,
                  hook_length_mm: float = 0) -> BarShape:
        """Shape C: L-bar with 135° hook (seismic)"""
        total_short = short_leg_mm + hook_length_mm
        
        segments = [
            BarSegment(long_leg_mm, 0),
            BarSegment(0, 135),
            BarSegment(total_short, 0)
        ]
        
        return BarShape(
            shape_code=BarShapeCode.C,
            description="L-Bar with 135° Hook",
            segments=segments,
            hook_type=135
        )
    
    @staticmethod
    def u_bar(width_mm: float, height_mm: float) -> BarShape:
        """Shape E: U-bar"""
        segments = [
            BarSegment(height_mm, 0),
            BarSegment(0, 90),
            BarSegment(width_mm, 0),
            BarSegment(0, 90),
            BarSegment(height_mm, 0)
        ]
        
        return BarShape(
            shape_code=BarShapeCode.E,
            description="U-Bar",
            segments=segments
        )
    
    @staticmethod
    def rectangular_stirrup(width_mm: float, height_mm: float,
                          hook_length_mm: float = 0) -> BarShape:
        """Shape F: Rectangular stirrup with hooks"""
        # Inside dimensions
        w = width_mm
        h = height_mm
        
        segments = [
            BarSegment(w, 0),
            BarSegment(0, 90),
            BarSegment(h, 0),
            BarSegment(0, 90),
            BarSegment(w, 0),
            BarSegment(0, 135),
            BarSegment(hook_length_mm, 0),
            BarSegment(0, 135),
            BarSegment(hook_length_mm, 0)
        ]
        
        return BarShape(
            shape_code=BarShapeCode.F,
            description="Rectangular Stirrup",
            segments=segments,
            hook_type=135
        )
    
    @staticmethod
    def cranked_bar(span_mm: float, crank_height_mm: float,
                    crank_pos_mm: float) -> BarShape:
        """Shape J: Cranked bar"""
        # Simplified - assumes crank at center
        straight1 = crank_pos_mm - crank_height_mm * 5  # 1:10 slope
        straight2 = span_mm - crank_pos_mm - crank_height_mm * 5
        
        # Crank length (hypotenuse of 1:10 slope)
        crank_len = math.sqrt(crank_height_mm**2 + (crank_height_mm*10)**2)
        
        segments = [
            BarSegment(straight1, 0),
            BarSegment(crank_len, 0),
            BarSegment(straight2, 0)
        ]
        
        return BarShape(
            shape_code=BarShapeCode.J,
            description="Cranked Bar",
            segments=segments
        )
    
    @staticmethod
    def spiral(pitch_mm: float, diameter_mm: float,
               total_length_mm: float) -> BarShape:
        """Shape L: Spiral reinforcement"""
        # Approximate as straight for BBS
        return BarShape(
            shape_code=BarShapeCode.L,
            description="Spiral Reinforcement",
            segments=[BarSegment(total_length_mm, 0)]
        )
    
    @staticmethod
    def straight_with_hooks(length_mm: float, hook_length_mm: float,
                           hook_type: int = 90) -> BarShape:
        """Shape M: Straight bar with hooks both ends"""
        total_length = length_mm + 2 * hook_length_mm
        
        if hook_type == 90:
            segments = [
                BarSegment(hook_length_mm, 0),
                BarSegment(0, 90),
                BarSegment(length_mm, 0),
                BarSegment(0, 90),
                BarSegment(hook_length_mm, 0)
            ]
        else:  # 135 or 180
            segments = [BarSegment(total_length, 0)]
        
        return BarShape(
            shape_code=BarShapeCode.M,
            description=f"Bar with {hook_type}° Hooks",
            segments=segments,
            hook_type=hook_type
        )


class ISCodeCalculations:
    """IS Code compliant calculations"""
    
    @staticmethod
    def development_length(bar_dia_mm: int, concrete_grade: str = "M25",
                          steel_grade: str = "Fe500") -> float:
        """Calculate development length per IS:456"""
        # Simplified formula
        # Ld = (φ × σs) / (4 × τbd)
        
        tau_bd = {
            "M20": 1.6, "M25": 1.9, "M30": 2.2,
            "M35": 2.4, "M40": 2.6
        }.get(concrete_grade, 1.9)
        
        fy = {"Fe250": 250, "Fe415": 415, "Fe500": 500}.get(steel_grade, 500)
        
        # 0.87 fy for tension
        sigma_s = 0.87 * fy
        
        ld = (bar_dia_mm * sigma_s) / (4 * tau_bd)
        
        return ld  # in mm
    
    @staticmethod
    def hook_length(bar_dia_mm: int, hook_type: int = 90) -> float:
        """Calculate hook length per IS:456"""
        if hook_type == 90:
            return 12 * bar_dia_mm
        elif hook_type == 135:
            return 16 * bar_dia_mm
        elif hook_type == 180:
            return 8 * bar_dia_mm + math.pi * bar_dia_mm / 2
        return 12 * bar_dia_mm
    
    @staticmethod
    def lap_length(bar_dia_mm: int, concrete_grade: str = "M25",
                   steel_grade: str = "Fe500") -> float:
        """Calculate lap length per IS:456"""
        ld = ISCodeCalculations.development_length(bar_dia_mm, concrete_grade, steel_grade)
        return max(ld, 30 * bar_dia_mm)  # Minimum 30φ
    
    @staticmethod
    def bend_deduction(bar_dia_mm: int, angle: int) -> float:
        """Calculate bend deduction per IS:2502"""
        deductions = {45: 1.0, 90: 2.0, 135: 2.5, 180: 3.0}
        return deductions.get(angle, 2.0) * bar_dia_mm


class ReinforcementSchedule:
    """Complete reinforcement schedule for a project"""
    
    def __init__(self, project_name: str = "Bridge Project"):
        self.project_name = project_name
        self.bars: List[ReinforcementBar] = []
        self.created_date = datetime.now()
    
    def add_bar(self, bar: ReinforcementBar):
        """Add bar to schedule"""
        self.bars.append(bar)
    
    def add_bars(self, bars: List[ReinforcementBar]):
        """Add multiple bars"""
        self.bars.extend(bars)
    
    def get_by_element(self, element_type: ElementType) -> List[ReinforcementBar]:
        """Get bars by element type"""
        return [b for b in self.bars if b.element_type == element_type]
    
    def get_by_bar_mark(self, bar_mark: str) -> Optional[ReinforcementBar]:
        """Get bar by mark"""
        for bar in self.bars:
            if bar.bar_mark == bar_mark:
                return bar
        return None
    
    def get_summary_by_diameter(self) -> Dict[int, Dict]:
        """Get summary grouped by diameter"""
        summary = {}
        
        for bar in self.bars:
            dia = bar.diameter_mm
            
            if dia not in summary:
                summary[dia] = {
                    'diameter_mm': dia,
                    'total_bars': 0,
                    'total_length_m': 0,
                    'total_weight_kg': 0
                }
            
            summary[dia]['total_bars'] += bar.num_bars
            summary[dia]['total_length_m'] += bar.total_length_m
            summary[dia]['total_weight_kg'] += bar.total_weight_kg
        
        return summary
    
    def get_summary_by_element(self) -> Dict[str, Dict]:
        """Get summary grouped by element"""
        summary = {}
        
        for bar in self.bars:
            key = f"{bar.element_type.value}_{bar.element_id}"
            
            if key not in summary:
                summary[key] = {
                    'element_type': bar.element_type.value,
                    'element_id': bar.element_id,
                    'total_weight_kg': 0,
                    'num_bar_marks': 0
                }
            
            summary[key]['total_weight_kg'] += bar.total_weight_kg
            summary[key]['num_bar_marks'] += 1
        
        return summary
    
    def calculate_totals(self) -> Dict:
        """Calculate schedule totals"""
        total_bars = sum(b.num_bars for b in self.bars)
        total_length = sum(b.total_length_m for b in self.bars)
        total_weight = sum(b.total_weight_kg for b in self.bars)
        
        # Add 5% wastage
        weight_with_wastage = total_weight * 1.05
        
        return {
            'total_bar_marks': len(self.bars),
            'total_bars': total_bars,
            'total_length_m': round(total_length, 2),
            'total_weight_kg': round(total_weight, 2),
            'total_weight_mt': round(total_weight / 1000, 3),
            'weight_with_wastage_kg': round(weight_with_wastage, 2),
            'weight_with_wastage_mt': round(weight_with_wastage / 1000, 3),
            'wastage_percent': 5
        }
    
    def to_dataframe(self):
        """Convert to pandas DataFrame"""
        import pandas as pd
        
        data = [bar.to_bbs_row() for bar in self.bars]
        return pd.DataFrame(data)
    
    def validate(self) -> List[str]:
        """Validate schedule for errors"""
        errors = []
        
        # Check for duplicate bar marks
        marks = [b.bar_mark for b in self.bars]
        duplicates = set([m for m in marks if marks.count(m) > 1])
        
        if duplicates:
            errors.append(f"Duplicate bar marks: {duplicates}")
        
        # Check for zero quantities
        for bar in self.bars:
            if bar.num_bars <= 0:
                errors.append(f"{bar.bar_mark}: Zero or negative quantity")
            if bar.cutting_length_mm <= 0:
                errors.append(f"{bar.bar_mark}: Zero or negative length")
        
        # Check total weight
        totals = self.calculate_totals()
        if totals['total_weight_kg'] <= 0:
            errors.append("Total weight is zero")
        
        return errors
    
    def merge(self, other: 'ReinforcementSchedule'):
        """Merge another schedule into this one"""
        self.bars.extend(other.bars)


# Example usage
if __name__ == "__main__":
    # Create schedule
    schedule = ReinforcementSchedule("Bridge Over River")
    
    # Create bar shapes
    shapes = BarShapeLibrary()
    
    # Add pile cap bottom mesh
    bar1 = ReinforcementBar(
        bar_mark="PC1-B1",
        element_type=ElementType.PILE_CAP,
        element_id="PC-1",
        shape=shapes.straight_bar(9750),
        diameter_mm=25,
        num_bars=25,
        spacing_mm=150,
        location="Bottom - Longitudinal"
    )
    schedule.add_bar(bar1)
    
    # Add stirrups
    bar2 = ReinforcementBar(
        bar_mark="PC1-S1",
        element_type=ElementType.PILE_CAP,
        element_id="PC-1",
        shape=shapes.rectangular_stirrup(7250, 1950, 150),
        diameter_mm=10,
        num_bars=65,
        spacing_mm=150,
        location="Stirrups"
    )
    schedule.add_bar(bar2)
    
    # Print summary
    print("REINFORCEMENT SCHEDULE")
    print("=" * 80)
    print(f"Project: {schedule.project_name}")
    print(f"Date: {schedule.created_date}")
    print("=" * 80)
    
    totals = schedule.calculate_totals()
    print(f"\nTotal Bar Marks: {totals['total_bar_marks']}")
    print(f"Total Bars: {totals['total_bars']}")
    print(f"Total Length: {totals['total_length_m']:.2f} m")
    print(f"Total Weight: {totals['total_weight_mt']:.3f} MT")
    print(f"With 5% Wastage: {totals['weight_with_wastage_mt']:.3f} MT")
    
    # Diameter summary
    print("\nBy Diameter:")
    for dia, data in sorted(schedule.get_summary_by_diameter().items()):
        print(f"  Ø{dia}mm: {data['total_bars']} bars, "
              f"{data['total_weight_kg']:.2f} kg")
    
    # Validation
    errors = schedule.validate()
    if errors:
        print("\nValidation Errors:")
        for e in errors:
            print(f"  - {e}")
    else:
        print("\nValidation: PASSED")
```

## File 2: `bridge_element_schedules.py`

```python
"""
Complete Schedules for All Bridge Elements
Pre-configured schedule generators for each element type
"""

from typing import List, Dict, Tuple
import math

from reinforcement_schedule_core import (
    ReinforcementSchedule, ReinforcementBar, ElementType,
    BarShapeLibrary, ISCodeCalculations
)


class PileCapScheduleGenerator:
    """Generate reinforcement schedule for pile cap"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.shapes = BarShapeLibrary()
    
    def generate(self, cap_length_m: float, cap_width_m: float,
                 cap_thickness_m: float, pile_dia_m: float,
                 num_piles: int, cap_id: str = "PC-1") -> List[ReinforcementBar]:
        """Generate complete pile cap schedule"""
        
        bars = []
        cover_mm = 75
        
        # 1. Bottom mesh - Longitudinal
        long_span_mm = (cap_length_m - 2 * cover_mm/1000) * 1000
        num_long_bars = int(long_span_mm / 150) + 1
        long_bar_length = (cap_width_m - 2 * cover_mm/1000) * 1000
        
        bars.append(ReinforcementBar(
            bar_mark=f"{cap_id}-B1",
            element_type=ElementType.PILE_CAP,
            element_id=cap_id,
            shape=self.shapes.straight_bar(long_bar_length),
            diameter_mm=25,
            num_bars=num_long_bars,
            spacing_mm=150,
            location="Bottom - Longitudinal",
            remarks="Main bottom reinforcement"
        ))
        
        # 2. Bottom mesh - Transverse
        trans_span_mm = (cap_width_m - 2 * cover_mm/1000) * 1000
        num_trans_bars = int(trans_span_mm / 150) + 1
        trans_bar_length = (cap_length_m - 2 * cover_mm/1000) * 1000
        
        bars.append(ReinforcementBar(
            bar_mark=f"{cap_id}-B2",
            element_type=ElementType.PILE_CAP,
            element_id=cap_id,
            shape=self.shapes.straight_bar(trans_bar_length),
            diameter_mm=25,
            num_bars=num_trans_bars,
            spacing_mm=150,
            location="Bottom - Transverse",
            remarks="Distribution bars"
        ))
        
        # 3. Top mesh (if cantilever > 1.5x thickness)
        cantilever = (cap_length_m - pile_dia_m) / 2
        if cantilever > 1.5 * cap_thickness_m:
            bars.append(ReinforcementBar(
                bar_mark=f"{cap_id}-T1",
                element_type=ElementType.PILE_CAP,
                element_id=cap_id,
                shape=self.shapes.straight_bar(long_bar_length),
                diameter_mm=16,
                num_bars=num_long_bars // 2,
                spacing_mm=300,
                location="Top - Longitudinal",
                remarks="Top mesh for cantilever"
            ))
            
            bars.append(ReinforcementBar(
                bar_mark=f"{cap_id}-T2",
                element_type=ElementType.PILE_CAP,
                element_id=cap_id,
                shape=self.shapes.straight_bar(trans_bar_length),
                diameter_mm=16,
                num_bars=num_trans_bars // 2,
                spacing_mm=300,
                location="Top - Transverse",
                remarks="Top distribution"
            ))
        
        # 4. Side face reinforcement (if thickness > 750mm)
        if cap_thickness_m > 0.75:
            side_face_length = cap_thickness_m * 1000 - 2 * cover_mm
            side_bars_per_face = int(cap_length_m / 0.30) + 1
            
            bars.append(ReinforcementBar(
                bar_mark=f"{cap_id}-S1",
                element_type=ElementType.PILE_CAP,
                element_id=cap_id,
                shape=self.shapes.straight_bar(side_face_length),
                diameter_mm=12,
                num_bars=side_bars_per_face * 2,  # Both faces
                spacing_mm=300,
                location="Side Face",
                remarks="Face reinforcement"
            ))
        
        # 5. Stirrups around piles
        stirrup_width = pile_dia_m * 1000 + 300  # 150mm each side
        stirrup_height = cap_thickness_m * 1000 - 2 * cover_mm
        
        bars.append(ReinforcementBar(
            bar_mark=f"{cap_id}-ST1",
            element_type=ElementType.PILE_CAP,
            element_id=cap_id,
            shape=self.shapes.rectangular_stirrup(
                stirrup_width, stirrup_height, 150
            ),
            diameter_mm=10,
            num_bars=num_piles * 8,  # 8 stirrups per pile
            location="Pile Zone",
            remarks="Shear reinforcement around piles"
        ))
        
        # 6. Pile anchorage bars
        anchorage_length = ISCodeCalculations.development_length(25, "M35", "Fe500")
        
        for i in range(num_piles):
            # 8 bars per pile into cap
            for j in range(8):
                bars.append(ReinforcementBar(
                    bar_mark=f"{cap_id}-A{i+1}-{j+1}",
                    element_type=ElementType.PILE_CAP,
                    element_id=cap_id,
                    shape=self.shapes.l_bar_135(
                        cap_thickness_m * 1000 - cover_mm,
                        500,
                        16 * 25  # 16d hook
                    ),
                    diameter_mm=25,
                    num_bars=1,
                    location="Pile Anchorage",
                    development_length_mm=anchorage_length,
                    remarks=f"Pile {i+1} anchorage"
                ))
        
        return bars


class PileScheduleGenerator:
    """Generate reinforcement schedule for piles"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.shapes = BarShapeLibrary()
    
    def generate(self, pile_dia_m: float, pile_length_m: float,
                 num_piles: int, pile_ids: List[str] = None) -> List[ReinforcementBar]:
        """Generate pile reinforcement schedule"""
        
        bars = []
        
        # Main bars - full length + projection + development
        anchorage_bottom = ISCodeCalculations.development_length(20, "M35", "Fe500")
        projection_top = 500  # Into cap
        main_bar_length = (pile_length_m * 1000 + projection_top + 
                          anchorage_bottom)
        
        # 12 bars per pile
        num_main_bars = 12
        
        for i in range(num_piles):
            pile_id = pile_ids[i] if pile_ids else f"P-{i+1}"
            
            bars.append(ReinforcementBar(
                bar_mark=f"{pile_id}-MB",
                element_type=ElementType.PILE,
                element_id=pile_id,
                shape=self.shapes.straight_bar(main_bar_length),
                diameter_mm=20,
                num_bars=num_main_bars,
                location="Main Bars",
                development_length_mm=anchorage_bottom,
                remarks="Vertical reinforcement"
            ))
            
            # Spiral reinforcement
            spiral_length = (pile_length_m + 0.5) / 0.15  # @ 150mm
            mean_dia = (pile_dia_m - 0.1) * 1000  # 50mm cover
            spiral_total_length = spiral_length * math.pi * mean_dia
            
            bars.append(ReinforcementBar(
                bar_mark=f"{pile_id}-SP",
                element_type=ElementType.PILE,
                element_id=pile_id,
                shape=self.shapes.spiral(150, mean_dia, spiral_total_length),
                diameter_mm=10,
                num_bars=1,
                location="Spiral",
                remarks="@ 150mm c/c"
            ))
            
            # Centralizer bars (3 nos)
            bars.append(ReinforcementBar(
                bar_mark=f"{pile_id}-CB",
                element_type=ElementType.PILE,
                element_id=pile_id,
                shape=self.shapes.straight_bar(pile_length_m * 1000),
                diameter_mm=12,
                num_bars=3,
                location="Centralizer",
                remarks="For alignment"
            ))
        
        return bars


class PierScheduleGenerator:
    """Generate reinforcement schedule for piers"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.shapes = BarShapeLibrary()
    
    def generate_circular_pier(self, diameter_m: float, height_m: float,
                                pier_id: str = "P-1") -> List[ReinforcementBar]:
        """Generate circular pier schedule"""
        
        bars = []
        cover_mm = 50
        
        # Vertical bars
        num_vertical = int(diameter_m * 3.14159 / 0.15)  # @ 150mm c/c
        bar_height = height_m * 1000 + 1000  # Extra for lap
        
        bars.append(ReinforcementBar(
            bar_mark=f"{pier_id}-V",
            element_type=ElementType.PIER_SHAFT,
            element_id=pier_id,
            shape=self.shapes.straight_bar(bar_height),
            diameter_mm=25,
            num_bars=num_vertical,
            spacing_mm=150,
            location="Vertical",
            remarks="Main vertical bars"
        ))
        
        # Helical rings
        num_rings = int(height_m / 0.15)
        ring_length = diameter_m * 3.14159 * 1000
        
        bars.append(ReinforcementBar(
            bar_mark=f"{pier_id}-H",
            element_type=ElementType.PIER_SHAFT,
            element_id=pier_id,
            shape=self.shapes.spiral(150, diameter_m * 1000, ring_length * num_rings),
            diameter_mm=12,
            num_bars=num_rings,
            spacing_mm=150,
            location="Helical",
            remarks="@ 150mm c/c"
        ))
        
        return bars
    
    def generate_pier_cap(self, cap_length_m: float, cap_width_m: float,
                         cap_depth_m: float, pier_id: str = "P-1") -> List[ReinforcementBar]:
        """Generate pier cap schedule"""
        
        bars = []
        cover_mm = 50
        
        # Bottom mesh
        bars.append(ReinforcementBar(
            bar_mark=f"{pier_id}C-B1",
            element_type=ElementType.PIER_CAP,
            element_id=f"{pier_id}-CAP",
            shape=self.shapes.straight_bar((cap_width_m - 0.1) * 1000),
            diameter_mm=32,
            num_bars=int(cap_length_m / 0.15),
            spacing_mm=150,
            location="Bottom - Longitudinal",
            remarks="Main bottom bars"
        ))
        
        # Top bars (negative moment)
        bars.append(ReinforcementBar(
            bar_mark=f"{pier_id}C-T1",
            element_type=ElementType.PIER_CAP,
            element_id=f"{pier_id}-CAP",
            shape=self.shapes.straight_bar((cap_width_m - 0.1) * 1000),
            diameter_mm=25,
            num_bars=int(cap_length_m / 0.20),
            spacing_mm=200,
            location="Top - Longitudinal",
            remarks="Negative moment reinforcement"
        ))
        
        # Stirrups
        bars.append(ReinforcementBar(
            bar_mark=f"{pier_id}C-S1",
            element_type=ElementType.PIER_CAP,
            element_id=f"{pier_id}-CAP",
            shape=self.shapes.rectangular_stirrup(
                (cap_width_m - 0.1) * 1000,
                (cap_depth_m - 0.1) * 1000,
                150
            ),
            diameter_mm=12,
            num_bars=int(cap_length_m / 0.15),
            spacing_mm=150,
            location="Stirrups",
            remarks="Shear reinforcement"
        ))
        
        return bars


class WellScheduleGenerator:
    """Generate reinforcement schedule for well foundations"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.shapes = BarShapeLibrary()
    
    def generate(self, external_dia_m: float, steining_thickness_m: float,
                 sinking_depth_m: float, well_cap_thickness_m: float,
                 well_id: str = "W-1") -> List[ReinforcementBar]:
        """Generate complete well schedule"""
        
        bars = []
        internal_dia = external_dia_m - 2 * steining_thickness_m
        
        # 1. Vertical steining bars
        num_vertical = int(external_dia_m * 3.14159 / 0.15)
        bar_height = sinking_depth_m * 1000 + 2000  # Extra for curb and cap
        
        bars.append(ReinforcementBar(
            bar_mark=f"{well_id}-SV",
            element_type=ElementType.WELL,
            element_id=well_id,
            shape=self.shapes.straight_bar(bar_height),
            diameter_mm=20,
            num_bars=num_vertical,
            spacing_mm=150,
            location="Steining Vertical",
            remarks="Main vertical bars"
        ))
        
        # 2. Horizontal rings in steining
        num_rings = int(sinking_depth_m / 0.15)
        ring_length = external_dia_m * 3.14159 * 1000
        
        bars.append(ReinforcementBar(
            bar_mark=f"{well_id}-SH",
            element_type=ElementType.WELL,
            element_id=well_id,
            shape=self.shapes.straight_bar(ring_length),
            diameter_mm=12,
            num_bars=num_rings,
            spacing_mm=150,
            location="Steining Rings",
            remarks="@ 150mm c/c"
        ))
        
        # 3. Curb reinforcement (heavier)
        curb_bars = int(external_dia_m * 3.14159 / 0.10)
        
        bars.append(ReinforcementBar(
            bar_mark=f"{well_id}-CR",
            element_type=ElementType.WELL,
            element_id=well_id,
            shape=self.shapes.l_bar_90(2500, 1500, 200),
            diameter_mm=25,
            num_bars=curb_bars,
            location="Curb",
            remarks="Curb reinforcement"
        ))
        
        # 4. Bottom plug - radial bars
        num_radial = int(internal_dia * 3.14159 / 0.20)
        
        bars.append(ReinforcementBar(
            bar_mark=f"{well_id}-BP-R",
            element_type=ElementType.WELL,
            element_id=well_id,
            shape=self.shapes.straight_bar(internal_dia / 2 * 1000),
            diameter_mm=16,
            num_bars=num_radial,
            location="Bottom Plug - Radial",
            remarks="Radial bars"
        ))
        
        # 5. Bottom plug - circular bars
        bars.append(ReinforcementBar(
            bar_mark=f"{well_id}-BP-C",
            element_type=ElementType.WELL,
            element_id=well_id,
            shape=self.shapes.spiral(200, internal_dia * 1000, 
                                     internal_dia * 3.14159 * 1000 * 5),
            diameter_mm=12,
            num_bars=5,
            location="Bottom Plug - Circular",
            remarks="@ 200mm c/c"
        ))
        
        # 6. Well cap reinforcement
        cap_size = external_dia_m + 1.0
        
        bars.append(ReinforcementBar(
            bar_mark=f"{well_id}C-B1",
            element_type=ElementType.WELL_CAP,
            element_id=f"{well_id}-CAP",
            shape=self.shapes.straight_bar(cap_size * 1000),
            diameter_mm=25,
            num_bars=int(cap_size / 0.15),
            spacing_mm=150,
            location="Cap Bottom",
            remarks="Main bottom bars"
        ))
        
        return bars


class DeckScheduleGenerator:
    """Generate reinforcement schedule for deck"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.shapes = BarShapeLibrary()
    
    def generate_box_girder(self, span_length_m: float, deck_width_m: float,
                            girder_depth_m: float, span_id: str = "S-1") -> List[ReinforcementBar]:
        """Generate box girder deck schedule"""
        
        bars = []
        
        # Top slab reinforcement
        bars.append(ReinforcementBar(
            bar_mark=f"{span_id}-TS1",
            element_type=ElementType.DECK_SLAB,
            element_id=span_id,
            shape=self.shapes.straight_bar(deck_width_m * 1000),
            diameter_mm=16,
            num_bars=int(span_length_m / 0.15),
            spacing_mm=150,
            location="Top Slab - Transverse",
            remarks="Top face"
        ))
        
        # Bottom slab reinforcement
        bars.append(ReinforcementBar(
            bar_mark=f"{span_id}-BS1",
            element_type=ElementType.DECK_SLAB,
            element_id=span_id,
            shape=self.shapes.straight_bar(deck_width_m * 1000),
            diameter_mm=20,
            num_bars=int(span_length_m / 0.15),
            spacing_mm=150,
            location="Bottom Slab - Transverse",
            remarks="Bottom face"
        ))
        
        # Web reinforcement
        web_height = (girder_depth_m - 0.5) * 1000
        num_stirrups = int(span_length_m / 0.15)
        
        bars.append(ReinforcementBar(
            bar_mark=f"{span_id}-W-S",
            element_type=ElementType.GIRDER,
            element_id=span_id,
            shape=self.shapes.rectangular_stirrup(400, web_height, 150),
            diameter_mm=12,
            num_bars=num_stirrups * 2,  # 2 webs
            spacing_mm=150,
            location="Web Stirrups",
            remarks="Shear reinforcement"
        ))
        
        # Longitudinal girder bars
        bars.append(ReinforcementBar(
            bar_mark=f"{span_id}-G-T",
            element_type=ElementType.GIRDER,
            element_id=span_id,
            shape=self.shapes.straight_bar(span_length_m * 1000),
            diameter_mm=32,
            num_bars=20,  # 10 bars per girder × 2 girders
            location="Girder Top",
            remarks="Top longitudinal"
        ))
        
        bars.append(ReinforcementBar(
            bar_mark=f"{span_id}-G-B",
            element_type=ElementType.GIRDER,
            element_id=span_id,
            shape=self.shapes.straight_bar(span_length_m * 1000),
            diameter_mm=32,
            num_bars=24,
            location="Girder Bottom",
            remarks="Bottom longitudinal"
        ))
        
        # Cross girders at supports
        bars.append(ReinforcementBar(
            bar_mark=f"{span_id}-CG",
            element_type=ElementType.CROSS_GIRDER,
            element_id=span_id,
            shape=self.shapes.straight_bar(deck_width_m * 1000),
            diameter_mm=25,
            num_bars=12,
            location="Cross Girder",
            remarks="At supports"
        ))
        
        # Diaphragms
        bars.append(ReinforcementBar(
            bar_mark=f"{span_id}-DP",
            element_type=ElementType.DIAPHRAGM,
            element_id=span_id,
            shape=self.shapes.rectangular_stirrup(
                (deck_width_m - 1) * 1000,
                (girder_depth_m - 0.5) * 1000,
                150
            ),
            diameter_mm=12,
            num_bars=40,
            location="Diaphragm",
            remarks="End diaphragms"
        ))
        
        return bars


class CompleteBridgeScheduleGenerator:
    """Generate complete reinforcement schedule for entire bridge"""
    
    def __init__(self, project_name: str = "Bridge Project"):
        self.schedule = ReinforcementSchedule(project_name)
        self.generators = {
            'pile_cap': PileCapScheduleGenerator(),
            'pile': PileScheduleGenerator(),
            'pier': PierScheduleGenerator(),
            'well': WellScheduleGenerator(),
            'deck': DeckScheduleGenerator()
        }
    
    def add_pile_cap(self, cap_length_m: float, cap_width_m: float,
                    cap_thickness_m: float, pile_dia_m: float,
                    num_piles: int, cap_id: str = "PC-1"):
        """Add pile cap to schedule"""
        bars = self.generators['pile_cap'].generate(
            cap_length_m, cap_width_m, cap_thickness_m,
            pile_dia_m, num_piles, cap_id
        )
        self.schedule.add_bars(bars)
    
    def add_piles(self, pile_dia_m: float, pile_length_m: float,
                  num_piles: int, pile_ids: List[str] = None):
        """Add piles to schedule"""
        bars = self.generators['pile'].generate(
            pile_dia_m, pile_length_m, num_piles, pile_ids
        )
        self.schedule.add_bars(bars)
    
    def add_well(self, external_dia_m: float, steining_thickness_m: float,
                 sinking_depth_m: float, well_cap_thickness_m: float,
                 well_id: str = "W-1"):
        """Add well foundation to schedule"""
        bars = self.generators['well'].generate(
            external_dia_m, steining_thickness_m,
            sinking_depth_m, well_cap_thickness_m, well_id
        )
        self.schedule.add_bars(bars)
    
    def add_pier(self, pier_type: str, diameter_m: float, height_m: float,
                 pier_id: str = "P-1"):
        """Add pier to schedule"""
        if pier_type == "circular":
            bars = self.generators['pier'].generate_circular_pier(
                diameter_m, height_m, pier_id
            )
            self.schedule.add_bars(bars)
    
    def add_pier_cap(self, cap_length_m: float, cap_width_m: float,
                    cap_depth_m: float, pier_id: str = "P-1"):
        """Add pier cap to schedule"""
        bars = self.generators['pier'].generate_pier_cap(
            cap_length_m, cap_width_m, cap_depth_m, pier_id
        )
        self.schedule.add_bars(bars)
    
    def add_deck(self, span_length_m: float, deck_width_m: float,
                 girder_depth_m: float, span_id: str = "S-1"):
        """Add deck to schedule"""
        bars = self.generators['deck'].generate_box_girder(
            span_length_m, deck_width_m, girder_depth_m, span_id
        )
        self.schedule.add_bars(bars)
    
    def get_schedule(self) -> ReinforcementSchedule:
        """Get complete schedule"""
        return self.schedule
    
    def export_to_excel(self, filepath: str):
        """Export to Excel"""
        from reinforcement_schedule_exporter import ScheduleExporter
        
        exporter = ScheduleExporter()
        exporter.export(self.schedule, filepath)
    
    def print_summary(self):
        """Print schedule summary"""
        print(f"\n{'='*80}")
        print(f"REINFORCEMENT SCHEDULE: {self.schedule.project_name}")
        print(f"{'='*80}")
        
        totals = self.schedule.calculate_totals()
        print(f"\nTotal Bar Marks: {totals['total_bar_marks']}")
        print(f"Total Bars: {totals['total_bars']:,}")
        print(f"Total Length: {totals['total_length_m']:,.2f} m")
        print(f"Total Weight: {totals['total_weight_mt']:.3f} MT")
        print(f"With Wastage (5%): {totals['weight_with_wastage_mt']:.3f} MT")
        
        print(f"\n{'-'*40}")
        print("BY ELEMENT TYPE:")
        print(f"{'-'*40}")
        
        by_element = self.schedule.get_summary_by_element()
        for key, data in sorted(by_element.items()):
            print(f"  {data['element_type']} {data['element_id']}: "
                  f"{data['total_weight_kg']:.2f} kg")
        
        print(f"\n{'-'*40}")
        print("BY DIAMETER:")
        print(f"{'-'*40}")
        
        by_dia = self.schedule.get_summary_by_diameter()
        for dia, data in sorted(by_dia.items()):
            print(f"  Ø{dia}mm: {data['total_bars']:,} bars, "
                  f"{data['total_weight_kg']:.2f} kg")


# Example usage
if __name__ == "__main__":
    # Create complete bridge schedule
    generator = CompleteBridgeScheduleGenerator("Multi-Span Bridge")
    
    # Add foundations
    generator.add_pile_cap(10.0, 7.5, 2.0, 1.2, 12, "PC-1")
    generator.add_piles(1.2, 15.0, 12, [f"P{i}" for i in range(1, 13)])
    
    generator.add_well(6.0, 0.6, 12.0, 2.0, "W-1")
    
    # Add piers
    generator.add_pier("circular", 2.0, 15.0, "P1")
    generator.add_pier_cap(4.0, 12.0, 2.0, "P1")
    
    generator.add_pier("circular", 2.0, 15.0, "P2")
    generator.add_pier_cap(4.0, 12.0, 2.0, "P2")
    
    # Add deck
    generator.add_deck(30.0, 12.0, 2.5, "S1")
    generator.add_deck(35.0, 12.0, 2.5, "S2")
    generator.add_deck(30.0, 12.0, 2.5, "S3")
    
    # Print summary
    generator.print_summary()
    
    # Validate
    errors = generator.schedule.validate()
    if errors:
        print("\nVALIDATION ERRORS:")
        for e in errors:
            print(f"  - {e}")
    else:
        print("\n✓ Schedule validated successfully")
    
    # Export
    # generator.export_to_excel("bridge_reinforcement_schedule.xlsx")
```

## File 3: `reinforcement_schedule_exporter.py`

```python
"""
Reinforcement Schedule Exporter
Export to Excel, PDF, and other formats
"""

import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict, List
from openpyxl import Workbook
from openpyxl.styles import Font, Alignment, PatternFill, Border, Side
from openpyxl.utils.dataframe import dataframe_to_rows
from openpyxl.drawing.image import Image as XLImage

from reinforcement_schedule_core import ReinforcementSchedule, ReinforcementBar


class ScheduleExporter:
    """Export reinforcement schedule to various formats"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.header_fill = PatternFill(start_color="366092", end_color="366092", fill_type="solid")
        self.subheader_fill = PatternFill(start_color="95B3D7", end_color="95B3D7", fill_type="solid")
        self.total_fill = PatternFill(start_color="FFFF00", end_color="FFFF00", fill_type="solid")
        self.alt_row_fill = PatternFill(start_color="E7E6E6", end_color="E7E6E6", fill_type="solid")
        
        self.border = Border(
            left=Side(style='thin'),
            right=Side(style='thin'),
            top=Side(style='thin'),
            bottom=Side(style='thin')
        )
        
        self.center_align = Alignment(horizontal='center', vertical='center', wrap_text=True)
        self.left_align = Alignment(horizontal='left', vertical='center', wrap_text=True)
    
    def export(self, schedule: ReinforcementSchedule, filepath: str):
        """Export complete schedule to Excel"""
        
        with pd.ExcelWriter(filepath, engine='openpyxl') as writer:
            # 1. Main BBS Sheet
            self._export_main_bbs(schedule, writer)
            
            # 2. Summary by Element
            self._export_element_summary(schedule, writer)
            
            # 3. Summary by Diameter
            self._export_diameter_summary(schedule, writer)
            
            # 4. Bar Shape Reference
            self._export_shape_reference(schedule, writer)
            
            # 5. Project Summary
            self._export_project_summary(schedule, writer)
        
        print(f"Schedule exported: {filepath}")
        return filepath
    
    def _export_main_bbs(self, schedule: ReinforcementSchedule, writer):
        """Export main BBS table"""
        
        df = schedule.to_dataframe()
        
        # Reorder columns
        column_order = [
            'bar_mark', 'element_type', 'element_id', 'shape_code',
            'description', 'diameter_mm', 'grade', 'num_bars', 'spacing_mm',
            'cutting_length_mm', 'total_length_m', 'unit_weight',
            'weight_per_bar', 'total_weight_kg', 'location', 'remarks'
        ]
        
        df = df[column_order]
        
        # Rename columns
        df.columns = [
            'Bar Mark', 'Element Type', 'Element ID', 'Shape',
            'Description', 'Dia (mm)', 'Grade', 'No. of Bars', 'Spacing (mm)',
            'Cutting Length (mm)', 'Total Length (m)', 'Unit Wt (kg/m)',
            'Wt per Bar (kg)', 'Total Wt (kg)', 'Location', 'Remarks'
        ]
        
        df.to_excel(writer, sheet_name='Bar Bending Schedule', index=False)
        
        # Format worksheet
        ws = writer.sheets['Bar Bending Schedule']
        self._format_bbs_sheet(ws)
    
    def _format_bbs_sheet(self, ws):
        """Format BBS worksheet"""
        
        # Header row
        for cell in ws[1]:
            cell.font = Font(bold=True, color="FFFFFF", size=11)
            cell.fill = self.header_fill
            cell.alignment = self.center_align
            cell.border = self.border
        
        # Data rows with alternating colors
        for idx, row in enumerate(ws.iter_rows(min_row=2, max_row=ws.max_row), start=2):
            for cell in row:
                cell.border = self.border
                cell.alignment = self.left_align if cell.column in [1, 2, 5, 15, 16] else self.center_align
                
                # Alternating row colors
                if idx % 2 == 0:
                    cell.fill = self.alt_row_fill
        
        # Adjust column widths
        widths = [12, 15, 12, 8, 25, 10, 10, 12, 12, 15, 15, 12, 12, 12, 15, 20]
        for i, width in enumerate(widths, 1):
            ws.column_dimensions[chr(64 + i)].width = width
    
    def _export_element_summary(self, schedule: ReinforcementSchedule, writer):
        """Export summary by element"""
        
        summary = schedule.get_summary_by_element()
        
        data = []
        for key, info in sorted(summary.items()):
            data.append({
                'Element Type': info['element_type'],
                'Element ID': info['element_id'],
                'Number of Bar Marks': info['num_bar_marks'],
                'Total Weight (kg)': round(info['total_weight_kg'], 2),
                'Total Weight (MT)': round(info['total_weight_kg'] / 1000, 3)
            })
        
        df = pd.DataFrame(data)
        df.to_excel(writer, sheet_name='Summary by Element', index=False)
        
        ws = writer.sheets['Summary by Element']
        self._format_summary_sheet(ws)
    
    def _export_diameter_summary(self, schedule: ReinforcementSchedule, writer):
        """Export summary by diameter"""
        
        summary = schedule.get_summary_by_diameter()
        
        data = []
        for dia, info in sorted(summary.items()):
            data.append({
                'Diameter (mm)': dia,
                'Total Bars': info['total_bars'],
                'Total Length (m)': round(info['total_length_m'], 2),
                'Total Weight (kg)': round(info['total_weight_kg'], 2),
                'Total Weight (MT)': round(info['total_weight_kg'] / 1000, 3),
                'Percentage': 0  # Will calculate
            })
        
        # Calculate percentages
        total_weight = sum(d['Total Weight (kg)'] for d in data)
        for d in data:
            d['Percentage'] = round(d['Total Weight (kg)'] / total_weight * 100, 2) if total_weight > 0 else 0
        
        df = pd.DataFrame(data)
        df.to_excel(writer, sheet_name='Summary by Diameter', index=False)
        
        ws = writer.sheets['Summary by Diameter']
        self._format_summary_sheet(ws)
    
    def _export_shape_reference(self, schedule: ReinforcementSchedule, writer):
        """Export bar shape reference"""
        
        from reinforcement_schedule_core import BarShapeCode
        
        data = []
        for shape in BarShapeCode:
            data.append({
                'Shape Code': shape.value,
                'Description': shape.name,
                'Typical Use': self._get_typical_use(shape)
            })
        
        df = pd.DataFrame(data)
        df.to_excel(writer, sheet_name='Shape Reference', index=False)
    
    def _get_typical_use(self, shape_code: BarShapeCode) -> str:
        """Get typical use for shape"""
        uses = {
            BarShapeCode.A: "Main bars, distribution bars",
            BarShapeCode.B: "L-bars, pile anchorage",
            BarShapeCode.C: "Seismic hooks, pile anchorage",
            BarShapeCode.E: "U-bars, stirrups",
            BarShapeCode.F: "Rectangular stirrups, ties",
            BarShapeCode.J: "Cranked bars for slabs",
            BarShapeCode.L: "Spiral reinforcement"
        }
        return uses.get(shape_code, "General use")
    
    def _export_project_summary(self, schedule: ReinforcementSchedule, writer):
        """Export project summary"""
        
        totals = schedule.calculate_totals()
        
        data = [
            {'Description': 'Project Name', 'Value': schedule.project_name},
            {'Description': 'Created Date', 'Value': schedule.created_date.strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M')},
            {'Description': '', 'Value': ''},
            {'Description': 'Total Bar Marks', 'Value': totals['total_bar_marks']},
            {'Description': 'Total Number of Bars', 'Value': f"{totals['total_bars']:,}"},
            {'Description': 'Total Length (m)', 'Value': f"{totals['total_length_m']:,.2f}"},
            {'Description': '', 'Value': ''},
            {'Description': 'Total Steel Weight (kg)', 'Value': f"{totals['total_weight_kg']:,.2f}"},
            {'Description': 'Total Steel Weight (MT)', 'Value': f"{totals['total_weight_mt']:,.3f}"},
            {'Description': '', 'Value': ''},
            {'Description': f'Wastage ({totals["wastage_percent"]}%)', 'Value': f"{totals['total_weight_kg'] * 0.05:,.2f} kg"},
            {'Description': 'Total with Wastage (MT)', 'Value': f"{totals['weight_with_wastage_mt']:,.3f}"},
        ]
        
        df = pd.DataFrame(data)
        df.to_excel(writer, sheet_name='Project Summary', index=False)
        
        ws = writer.sheets['Project Summary']
        
        # Format
        for row in ws.iter_rows(min_row=2, max_row=ws.max_row):
            for cell in row:
                cell.border = self.border
                if cell.column == 1:
                    cell.font = Font(bold=True)
        
        ws.column_dimensions['A'].width = 30
        ws.column_dimensions['B'].width = 25
    
    def _format_summary_sheet(self, ws):
        """Format summary worksheet"""
        
        # Header
        for cell in ws[1]:
            cell.font = Font(bold=True, color="FFFFFF")
            cell.fill = self.header_fill
            cell.alignment = self.center_align
            cell.border = self.border
        
        # Data
        for row in ws.iter_rows(min_row=2, max_row=ws.max_row):
            for cell in row:
                cell.border = self.border
                cell.alignment = self.center_align
    
    def export_to_csv(self, schedule: ReinforcementSchedule, filepath: str):
        """Export to CSV format"""
        
        df = schedule.to_dataframe()
        df.to_csv(filepath, index=False)
        print(f"CSV exported: {filepath}")
        return filepath
    
    def generate_bar_mark_labels(self, schedule: ReinforcementSchedule, 
                                  output_dir: str):
        """Generate printable bar mark labels"""
        
        import os
        os.makedirs(output_dir, exist_ok=True)
        
        # Create label file
        label_file = os.path.join(output_dir, "bar_mark_labels.txt")
        
        with open(label_file, 'w') as f:
            f.write("BAR MARK LABELS\n")
            f.write("=" * 60 + "\n\n")
            
            for bar in sorted(schedule.bars, key=lambda b: b.bar_mark):
                f.write(f"\n{'='*40}\n")
                f.write(f"Bar Mark: {bar.bar_mark}\n")
                f.write(f"Element: {bar.element_type.value} {bar.element_id}\n")
                f.write(f"Shape: {bar.shape.shape_code.value}\n")
                f.write(f"Diameter: Ø{bar.diameter_mm}mm\n")
                f.write(f"Quantity: {bar.num_bars}\n")
                f.write(f"Cutting Length: {bar.cutting_length_mm:.0f}mm\n")
                f.write(f"{'='*40}\n")
        
        print(f"Labels generated: {label_file}")
        return label_file


# Example usage
if __name__ == "__main__":
    from reinforcement_schedule_core import ReinforcementSchedule, BarShapeLibrary
    from bridge_element_schedules import CompleteBridgeScheduleGenerator
    
    # Create sample schedule
    generator = CompleteBridgeScheduleGenerator("Test Bridge")
    generator.add_pile_cap(10.0, 7.5, 2.0, 1.2, 12, "PC-1")
    generator.add_piles(1.2, 15.0, 12)
    
    schedule = generator.get_schedule()
    
    # Export
    exporter = ScheduleExporter()
    exporter.export(schedule, "test_reinforcement_schedule.xlsx")
    exporter.export_to_csv(schedule, "test_reinforcement_schedule.csv")
    exporter.generate_bar_mark_labels(schedule, "./labels")
```

## KEY FEATURES

| Feature | Description |
|---------|-------------|
| **IS:2502 Compliance** | Standard bar shape codes (A-N) |
| **IS:456 Calculations** | Development length, hook length, lap length |
| **All Elements** | Piles, caps, wells, piers, decks, diaphragms |
| **Complete BBS** | Cutting lengths, bend deductions, weights |
| **Multi-format Export** | Excel with multiple sheets, CSV, labels |
| **Validation** | Error checking for duplicates, zero quantities |
| **Summaries** | By element, by diameter, with wastage |
