2. **Create Construction Sequence Generator** - Stage-by-stage construction drawings?
I'll create a comprehensive **Construction Sequence Generator** module that produces stage-by-stage construction drawings showing the progressive build-up of the bridge from foundation to completion.

```python
"""
CONSTRUCTION SEQUENCE GENERATOR MODULE
Stage-by-stage construction drawings for bridge projects
"""

import math
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import List, Dict, Tuple, Optional, Union
from enum import Enum
from datetime import datetime, timedelta


class ConstructionStage(Enum):
    """Standard bridge construction stages"""
    SITE_PREPARATION = "SITE_PREPARATION"
    PILING = "PILING"
    PILE_CAP = "PILE_CAP"
    PIER_CONSTRUCTION = "PIER_CONSTRUCTION"
    PIER_CAP = "PIER_CAP"
    BEARING_INSTALLATION = "BEARING_INSTALLATION"
    GIRDER_ERECTION = "GIRDER_ERECTION"
    DECK_SLAB = "DECK_SLAB"
    PARAPET = "PARAPET"
    WEARING_COAT = "WEARING_COAT"
    APPROACH_WORK = "APPROACH_WORK"
    COMPLETION = "COMPLETION"


@dataclass
class StageDuration:
    """Duration and timing for a construction stage"""
    stage: ConstructionStage
    duration_days: int
    lag_days: int = 0  # Lag from previous stage
    crew_size: int = 10
    equipment: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)


@dataclass
class ConstructionActivity:
    """Individual activity within a stage"""
    name: str
    description: str
    start_chainage: float
    end_chainage: float
    duration_days: int
    dependencies: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    resources: Dict[str, int] = field(default_factory=dict)  # resource: quantity


@dataclass
class StageGeometry:
    """Geometric elements visible in a specific stage"""
    stage: ConstructionStage
    completed_elements: List[Dict]  # Elements already built
    active_elements: List[Dict]    # Elements being built in this stage
    temporary_works: List[Dict]   # Formwork, scaffolding, etc.
    equipment_positions: List[Dict]  # Crane, pump positions


class ConstructionSequenceEngine:
    """
    Engine to calculate construction sequence and generate stage geometries
    """
    
    def __init__(self, bridge_model, start_date: datetime = None):
        self.bridge = bridge_model
        self.start_date = start_date or datetime.now()
        self.stages: List[StageDuration] = []
        self.activities: List[ConstructionActivity] = []
        self.schedule: Dict = {}
        
    def define_standard_sequence(self, method: str = "CAST_IN_SITU"):
        """
        Define standard construction sequence based on method
        
        Methods:
        - CAST_IN_SITU: Conventional cast-in-situ construction
        - PRECAST: Precast girder launching
        - BALANCED_CANTILEVER: Segmental construction
        - PUSHING: Incremental launching
        """
        
        if method == "CAST_IN_SITU":
            self.stages = [
                StageDuration(ConstructionStage.SITE_PREPARATION, 14, 0, 8, 
                            ["Excavator", "Dumper", "Roller"]),
                StageDuration(ConstructionStage.PILING, 45, 7, 12,
                            ["Piling Rig", "Concrete Pump", "Crane"]),
                StageDuration(ConstructionStage.PILE_CAP, 30, 14, 15,
                            ["Concrete Pump", "Vibrator", "Formwork"]),
                StageDuration(ConstructionStage.PIER_CONSTRUCTION, 60, 7, 20,
                            ["Concrete Pump", "Crane", "Formwork", "Scaffolding"]),
                StageDuration(ConstructionStage.PIER_CAP, 21, 7, 15,
                            ["Concrete Pump", "Crane", "Formwork"]),
                StageDuration(ConstructionStage.BEARING_INSTALLATION, 7, 14, 8,
                            ["Crane", "Hydraulic Jack"]),
                StageDuration(ConstructionStage.GIRDER_ERECTION, 30, 7, 25,
                            ["Launching Girder", "Crane", "Prestressing Jack"]),
                StageDuration(ConstructionStage.DECK_SLAB, 45, 14, 20,
                            ["Concrete Pump", "Screed", "Vibrator", "Formwork"]),
                StageDuration(ConstructionStage.PARAPET, 21, 14, 12,
                            ["Concrete Pump", "Formwork"]),
                StageDuration(ConstructionStage.WEARING_COAT, 14, 21, 10,
                            ["Paver", "Roller"]),
                StageDuration(ConstructionStage.APPROACH_WORK, 30, 7, 15,
                            ["Excavator", "Roller", "Paver"]),
                StageDuration(ConstructionStage.COMPLETION, 7, 0, 5,
                            ["Truck", "Cleaning Equipment"])
            ]
        
        elif method == "PRECAST":
            self.stages = [
                StageDuration(ConstructionStage.SITE_PREPARATION, 14, 0, 8),
                StageDuration(ConstructionStage.PILING, 45, 7, 12),
                StageDuration(ConstructionStage.PILE_CAP, 30, 14, 15),
                StageDuration(ConstructionStage.PIER_CONSTRUCTION, 60, 7, 20),
                StageDuration(ConstructionStage.PIER_CAP, 21, 7, 15),
                StageDuration(ConstructionStage.BEARING_INSTALLATION, 7, 14, 8),
                StageDuration(ConstructionStage.GIRDER_ERECTION, 20, 7, 30,
                            ["Heavy Lift Crane", "Trailer", "Jack"]),
                StageDuration(ConstructionStage.DECK_SLAB, 30, 14, 15,
                            ["Concrete Pump", "Screed"]),
                StageDuration(ConstructionStage.PARAPET, 21, 14, 12),
                StageDuration(ConstructionStage.WEARING_COAT, 14, 21, 10),
                StageDuration(ConstructionStage.COMPLETION, 7, 0, 5)
            ]
        
        self._calculate_schedule()
        return self.schedule
    
    def _calculate_schedule(self):
        """Calculate dates for each stage"""
        current_date = self.start_date
        
        for stage in self.stages:
            start = current_date + timedelta(days=stage.lag_days)
            end = start + timedelta(days=stage.duration_days)
            
            self.schedule[stage.stage.value] = {
                'start': start,
                'end': end,
                'duration': stage.duration_days,
                'crew': stage.crew_size,
                'equipment': stage.equipment
            }
            
            current_date = end
    
    def get_stage_geometry(self, stage: ConstructionStage, 
                          progress_percent: float = 100) -> StageGeometry:
        """
        Get geometric elements for a specific construction stage
        
        Args:
            stage: The construction stage
            progress_percent: Progress within current stage (0-100)
        """
        
        completed = []
        active = []
        temporary = []
        equipment = []
        
        # Get all supports (piers and abutments)
        supports = self._get_supports()
        
        if stage == ConstructionStage.SITE_PREPARATION:
            # Show cleared area, access roads, temporary facilities
            active.append({
                'type': 'SITE_CLEARING',
                'area': self._get_site_area(),
                'progress': progress_percent
            })
            temporary.append({
                'type': 'SITE_OFFICE',
                'position': (self.bridge.chainage - 50, 20)
            })
            temporary.append({
                'type': 'LABOR_CAMP',
                'position': (self.bridge.chainage - 80, 30)
            })
            equipment.append({
                'type': 'EXCAVATOR',
                'position': (self.bridge.chainage + 20, 10),
                'rotation': 45
            })
        
        elif stage == ConstructionStage.PILING:
            # Completed: Site prep
            completed.append({'type': 'SITE_PREPARATION'})
            
            # Active: Piling at specific locations
            num_supports = len(supports)
            supports_to_pile = int(num_supports * progress_percent / 100)
            
            for i, support in enumerate(supports):
                if i < supports_to_pile:
                    # Completed piles
                    completed.append({
                        'type': 'PILE_GROUP',
                        'support': support,
                        'piles': self._get_pile_positions(support)
                    })
                elif i == supports_to_pile:
                    # Active piling
                    active.append({
                        'type': 'PILE_DRILLING',
                        'support': support,
                        'progress': progress_percent % (100/num_supports) * num_supports
                    })
                    equipment.append({
                        'type': 'PILING_RIG',
                        'position': (support['chainage'], 0),
                        'height': 15
                    })
            
            # Temporary: Bore pile casing, bentonite plant
            temporary.append({
                'type': 'BENTONITE_PLANT',
                'position': (self.bridge.chainage - 30, 25)
            })
        
        elif stage == ConstructionStage.PILE_CAP:
            completed.append({'type': 'SITE_PREPARATION'})
            completed.append({'type': 'ALL_PILES'})
            
            num_supports = len(supports)
            caps_to_cast = int(num_supports * progress_percent / 100)
            
            for i, support in enumerate(supports):
                if i < caps_to_cast:
                    completed.append({
                        'type': 'PILE_CAP',
                        'support': support,
                        'dimensions': self._get_pile_cap_dimensions(support)
                    })
                elif i == caps_to_cast:
                    active.append({
                        'type': 'PILE_CAP_CONCRETING',
                        'support': support,
                        'formwork': True,
                        'reinforcement': True,
                        'progress': progress_percent % (100/num_supports) * num_supports
                    })
                    temporary.append({
                        'type': 'FORMWORK_PILE_CAP',
                        'support': support
                    })
                    temporary.append({
                        'type': 'REINFORCEMENT_PILE_CAP',
                        'support': support
                    })
                    equipment.append({
                        'type': 'CONCRETE_PUMP',
                        'position': (support['chainage'] - 10, 15)
                    })
        
        elif stage == ConstructionStage.PIER_CONSTRUCTION:
            completed.append({'type': 'SITE_PREPARATION'})
            completed.append({'type': 'ALL_PILES'})
            completed.append({'type': 'ALL_PILE_CAPS'})
            
            num_piers = len([s for s in supports if s['type'] == 'PIER'])
            piers_to_build = int(num_piers * progress_percent / 100)
            
            pier_count = 0
            for support in supports:
                if support['type'] == 'PIER':
                    if pier_count < piers_to_build:
                        completed.append({
                            'type': 'PIER_SHAFT',
                            'support': support,
                            'height': support['height']
                        })
                    elif pier_count == piers_to_build:
                        active.append({
                            'type': 'PIER_CONSTRUCTION',
                            'support': support,
                            'height_built': support['height'] * (progress_percent % (100/num_piers)) * num_piers / 100,
                            'formwork': True
                        })
                        temporary.append({
                            'type': 'FORMWORK_PIER',
                            'support': support,
                            'height': support['height']
                        })
                        temporary.append({
                            'type': 'SCAFFOLDING_PIER',
                            'support': support,
                            'height': support['height']
                        })
                        equipment.append({
                            'type': 'CONCRETE_PUMP',
                            'position': (support['chainage'], 5)
                        })
                        equipment.append({
                            'type': 'CRANE',
                            'position': (support['chainage'] - 15, 10),
                            'height': support['height'] + 5
                        })
                    pier_count += 1
                else:
                    # Abutments
                    completed.append({
                        'type': 'ABUTMENT',
                        'support': support
                    })
        
        elif stage == ConstructionStage.PIER_CAP:
            completed.append({'type': 'ALL_PIER_SHAFTS'})
            
            num_piers = len([s for s in supports if s['type'] == 'PIER'])
            caps_to_cast = int(num_piers * progress_percent / 100)
            
            pier_count = 0
            for support in supports:
                if support['type'] == 'PIER':
                    if pier_count < caps_to_cast:
                        completed.append({
                            'type': 'PIER_CAP',
                            'support': support
                        })
                    elif pier_count == caps_to_cast:
                        active.append({
                            'type': 'PIER_CAP_CONCRETING',
                            'support': support,
                            'formwork': True,
                            'reinforcement': True
                        })
                        temporary.append({
                            'type': 'FORMWORK_PIER_CAP',
                            'support': support
                        })
                        equipment.append({
                            'type': 'CONCRETE_PUMP',
                            'position': (support['chainage'], support['height'] + 2)
                        })
                    pier_count += 1
        
        elif stage == ConstructionStage.BEARING_INSTALLATION:
            completed.append({'type': 'ALL_PIER_CAPS'})
            
            num_supports = len(supports)
            bearings_installed = int(num_supports * 3 * progress_percent / 100)  # 3 bearings per support
            
            count = 0
            for support in supports:
                support_bearings = []
                for j in range(3):  # 3 bearings per support
                    if count < bearings_installed:
                        completed.append({
                            'type': 'BEARING',
                            'support': support,
                            'position': j
                        })
                    elif count == bearings_installed:
                        active.append({
                            'type': 'BEARING_INSTALLATION',
                            'support': support,
                            'position': j
                        })
                        equipment.append({
                            'type': 'HYDRAULIC_JACK',
                            'position': (support['chainage'], support.get('height', 0) + 1)
                        })
                    count += 1
        
        elif stage == ConstructionStage.GIRDER_ERECTION:
            completed.append({'type': 'ALL_BEARINGS'})
            
            num_spans = len(self.bridge.spans)
            spans_erected = int(num_spans * progress_percent / 100)
            
            for i, span in enumerate(self.bridge.spans):
                if i < spans_erected:
                    completed.append({
                        'type': 'GIRDERS',
                        'span': i + 1,
                        'length': span.length,
                        'num_girders': self.bridge.get_num_girders()
                    })
                elif i == spans_erected:
                    active.append({
                        'type': 'GIRDER_LAUNCHING',
                        'span': i + 1,
                        'progress': progress_percent % (100/num_spans) * num_spans,
                        'girder_number': int(self.bridge.get_num_girders() * 
                                           (progress_percent % (100/num_spans)) * num_spans / 100) + 1
                    })
                    equipment.append({
                        'type': 'LAUNCHING_GIRDER' if self.bridge.total_length > 60 else 'CRANE',
                        'position': (span.start_chainage + span.length/2, 5),
                        'height': self.bridge.deck.soffit_level + 5
                    })
        
        elif stage == ConstructionStage.DECK_SLAB:
            completed.append({'type': 'ALL_GIRDERS'})
            
            total_length = self.bridge.total_length
            cast_length = total_length * progress_percent / 100
            
            active.append({
                'type': 'DECK_CONCRETING',
                'start_chainage': self.bridge.chainage,
                'end_chainage': self.bridge.chainage + cast_length,
                'formwork': True
            })
            
            temporary.append({
                'type': 'DECK_FORMWORK',
                'start_chainage': self.bridge.chainage,
                'end_chainage': self.bridge.chainage + cast_length + 10
            })
            
            equipment.append({
                'type': 'CONCRETE_PUMP',
                'position': (self.bridge.chainage + cast_length, 0)
            })
            equipment.append({
                'type': 'SCREED',
                'position': (self.bridge.chainage + cast_length - 5, 0)
            })
        
        elif stage == ConstructionStage.PARAPET:
            completed.append({'type': 'DECK_SLAB'})
            
            total_length = self.bridge.total_length
            completed_length = total_length * progress_percent / 100
            
            active.append({
                'type': 'PARAPET_CONSTRUCTION',
                'start_chainage': self.bridge.chainage,
                'end_chainage': self.bridge.chainage + completed_length,
                'side': 'LEFT'
            })
            active.append({
                'type': 'PARAPET_CONSTRUCTION',
                'start_chainage': self.bridge.chainage,
                'end_chainage': self.bridge.chainage + completed_length,
                'side': 'RIGHT'
            })
            
            temporary.append({
                'type': 'PARAPET_FORMWORK',
                'start_chainage': self.bridge.chainage,
                'end_chainage': self.bridge.chainage + completed_length
            })
        
        elif stage == ConstructionStage.WEARING_COAT:
            completed.append({'type': 'PARAPETS'})
            
            active.append({
                'type': 'WEARING_COAT',
                'start_chainage': self.bridge.chainage,
                'end_chainage': self.bridge.chainage + 
                              (self.bridge.total_length * progress_percent / 100)
            })
            
            equipment.append({
                'type': 'PAVER',
                'position': (self.bridge.chainage + 20, 0)
            })
            equipment.append({
                'type': 'ROLLER',
                'position': (self.bridge.chainage + 10, 0)
            })
        
        elif stage == ConstructionStage.COMPLETION:
            completed.append({'type': 'ALL_ELEMENTS'})
            active.append({
                'type': 'FINISHING',
                'progress': progress_percent
            })
            equipment.append({
                'type': 'MARKING_MACHINE',
                'position': (self.bridge.chainage + 10, 0)
            })
        
        return StageGeometry(
            stage=stage,
            completed_elements=completed,
            active_elements=active,
            temporary_works=temporary,
            equipment_positions=equipment
        )
    
    def _get_supports(self) -> List[Dict]:
        """Get list of all supports with their properties"""
        supports = []
        
        # Left abutment
        supports.append({
            'number': 1,
            'type': 'ABUTMENT',
            'chainage': self.bridge.chainage,
            'height': getattr(self.bridge, 'abutment_height', 8.0),
            'width': getattr(self.bridge, 'abutment_width', 1.5)
        })
        
        # Piers
        current_ch = self.bridge.chainage
        for i, span in enumerate(self.bridge.spans[:-1]):
            current_ch += span.length
            supports.append({
                'number': i + 2,
                'type': 'PIER',
                'chainage': current_ch,
                'height': getattr(self.bridge, 'pier_height', 10.0),
                'width': getattr(self.bridge, 'pier_width', 2.0)
            })
        
        # Right abutment
        current_ch += self.bridge.spans[-1].length if self.bridge.spans else 0
        supports.append({
            'number': len(supports) + 1,
            'type': 'ABUTMENT',
            'chainage': current_ch,
            'height': getattr(self.bridge, 'abutment_height', 8.0),
            'width': getattr(self.bridge, 'abutment_width', 1.5)
        })
        
        return supports
    
    def _get_site_area(self) -> Dict:
        """Get site preparation area"""
        return {
            'center_chainage': self.bridge.chainage + self.bridge.total_length / 2,
            'width': self.bridge.total_length + 100,  # 50m each side
            'length': 40  # 20m each side of bridge
        }
    
    def _get_pile_positions(self, support: Dict) -> List[Tuple[float, float]]:
        """Get pile positions for a support"""
        # Standard 3x2 pile group
        spacing = 1.5  # m
        positions = []
        
        for i in range(3):  # Longitudinal
            for j in range(2):  # Transverse
                x = support['chainage'] + (i - 1) * spacing
                y = (j - 0.5) * spacing
                positions.append((x, y))
        
        return positions
    
    def _get_pile_cap_dimensions(self, support: Dict) -> Dict:
        """Get pile cap dimensions"""
        return {
            'length': 6.0,  # m
            'width': 4.0,   # m
            'thickness': 2.0  # m
        }
    
    def generate_methodology_statement(self) -> str:
        """Generate construction methodology statement"""
        methodology = f"""
CONSTRUCTION METHODOLOGY STATEMENT
{'='*60}

Project: {self.bridge.name}
Total Length: {self.bridge.total_length:.2f}m
Number of Spans: {len(self.bridge.spans)}

CONSTRUCTION APPROACH:
1. CAST-IN-SITU METHOD

SEQUENCE OF OPERATIONS:

Stage 1 - Site Preparation (Days 1-14):
- Clearing and grubbing of site
- Setting up site office and labor camp
- Establishing access roads
- Setting out and leveling

Stage 2 - Piling (Days 22-67):
- Bored cast-in-situ piles using hydraulic piling rig
- Pile diameter: 1.2m, typical depth: 15m
- Bentonite slurry method for pile stabilization
- {len([s for s in self._get_supports() if s['type'] == 'PIER'])} piers + 2 abutments

Stage 3 - Pile Cap Construction (Days 74-104):
- Excavation and dewatering
- Reinforcement placement
- Concrete pouring in single operation
- Curing for 14 days

Stage 4 - Pier Construction (Days 111-171):
- Erection of formwork and scaffolding
- Concreting in lifts of 3m height
- Curing between lifts

Stage 5 - Pier Cap (Days 178-199):
- Formwork erection
- Reinforcement and concreting

Stage 6 - Bearing Installation (Days 213-220):
- Elastomeric bearings placed using hydraulic jacks

Stage 7 - Girder Erection (Days 227-257):
- {'Launching girder method' if self.bridge.total_length > 60 else 'Mobile crane erection'}
- Sequential placement from one end

Stage 8 - Deck Slab (Days 271-316):
- Formwork over girders
- Reinforcement placement
- Concreting with screed finish

Stage 9 - Parapet (Days 330-351):
- Cast-in-situ RCC crash barriers

Stage 10 - Wearing Coat (Days 372-386):
- 65mm thick asphaltic concrete

Stage 11 - Approach Work (Days 393-423):
- Approach embankment and paving

Stage 12 - Completion (Days 423-430):
- Marking and signage
- Final inspection

TOTAL CONSTRUCTION PERIOD: {sum(s.duration_days for s in self.stages)} days
"""
        return methodology


class ConstructionSequenceDrawing:
    """
    Generate DXF drawings for construction stages
    """
    
    def __init__(self, sequence_engine: ConstructionSequenceEngine):
        self.engine = sequence_engine
        self.stage_geometries: Dict[ConstructionStage, StageGeometry] = {}
        
        # Pre-calculate all stages
        for stage in ConstructionStage:
            self.stage_geometries[stage] = self.engine.get_stage_geometry(stage, 100)
    
    def generate_stage_drawing(self, stage: ConstructionStage, 
                                output_path: str = None) -> str:
        """
        Generate drawing for a specific construction stage
        
        Args:
            stage: Construction stage to draw
            output_path: Output file path
            
        Returns:
            Path to generated DXF file
        """
        import ezdxf
        from ezdxf.enums import TextEntityAlignment
        
        doc = ezdxf.new('R2018', setup=True)
        msp = doc.modelspace()
        
        # Setup layers
        layers = {
            'COMPLETED': {'color': 3},    # Green - completed work
            'ACTIVE': {'color': 1},       # Red - active work
            'TEMPORARY': {'color': 4},    # Cyan - temporary works
            'EQUIPMENT': {'color': 2},      # Yellow - equipment
            'DIMENSION': {'color': 7},
            'TEXT': {'color': 7},
            'TITLE': {'color': 7}
        }
        
        for name, attrs in layers.items():
            doc.layers.add(name=name, color=attrs['color'])
        
        geom = self.stage_geometries[stage]
        
        # Draw completed elements (green)
        for elem in geom.completed_elements:
            self._draw_element(msp, elem, 'COMPLETED')
        
        # Draw active elements (red)
        for elem in geom.active_elements:
            self._draw_element(msp, elem, 'ACTIVE')
        
        # Draw temporary works (cyan)
        for temp in geom.temporary_works:
            self._draw_temporary(msp, temp, 'TEMPORARY')
        
        # Draw equipment (yellow symbols)
        for equip in geom.equipment_positions:
            self._draw_equipment(msp, equip, 'EQUIPMENT')
        
        # Add title block
        schedule = self.engine.schedule.get(stage.value, {})
        
        title_y = -30
        msp.add_text(
            f"CONSTRUCTION STAGE: {stage.value.replace('_', ' ')}",
            height=5,
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'TITLE'}
        ).set_placement((50, title_y), align=TextEntityAlignment.MIDDLE_CENTER)
        
        if schedule:
            msp.add_text(
                f"Duration: {schedule.get('duration', 'N/A')} days | "
                f"Crew: {schedule.get('crew', 'N/A')} | "
                f"Equipment: {', '.join(schedule.get('equipment', []))}",
                height=2.5,
                dxfattribs={'layer': 'TITLE'}
            ).set_placement((50, title_y - 8), align=TextEntityAlignment.MIDDLE_CENTER)
        
        # Save
        if not output_path:
            output_path = f"construction_stage_{stage.value.lower()}.dxf"
        
        doc.saveas(output_path)
        return output_path
    
    def _draw_element(self, msp, element: Dict, layer: str):
        """Draw a construction element"""
        elem_type = element.get('type', '')
        
        if elem_type == 'PILE_GROUP':
            # Draw piles as circles
            support = element.get('support', {})
            piles = element.get('piles', [])
            for px, py in piles:
                msp.add_circle(center=(px, py), radius=0.6, 
                           dxfattribs={'layer': layer})
        
        elif elem_type in ['PILE_CAP', 'PIER_SHAFT', 'PIER_CAP', 'ABUTMENT']:
            # Draw as rectangle
            support = element.get('support', {})
            ch = support.get('chainage', 0)
            width = support.get('width', 2.0)
            height = element.get('height', 8.0) if elem_type == 'PIER_SHAFT' else 2.0
            
            points = [
                (ch - width/2, 0),
                (ch + width/2, 0),
                (ch + width/2, height),
                (ch - width/2, height),
                (ch - width/2, 0)
            ]
            msp.add_lwpolyline(points=points, close=True, 
                            dxfattribs={'layer': layer})
        
        elif elem_type == 'GIRDERS':
            # Draw girders as lines
            span = element.get('span', 1)
            length = element.get('length', 30.0)
            num_girders = element.get('num_girders', 4)
            
            # Get span start chainage
            supports = self.engine._get_supports()
            start_ch = supports[span-1]['chainage'] if span <= len(supports) else 0
            
            spacing = 3.0  # Typical girder spacing
            for i in range(num_girders):
                y = -6 + i * spacing
                msp.add_line(
                    start=(start_ch, y),
                    end=(start_ch + length, y),
                    dxfattribs={'layer': layer, 'lineweight': 0.5}
                )
        
        elif elem_type == 'DECK_SLAB':
            # Draw deck outline
            bridge = self.engine.bridge
            start = bridge.chainage
            end = start + bridge.total_length
            width = bridge.deck.square_width / 2
            
            points = [
                (start, -width),
                (end, -width),
                (end, width),
                (start, width),
                (start, -width)
            ]
            msp.add_lwpolyline(points=points, close=True, 
                            dxfattribs={'layer': layer})
    
    def _draw_temporary(self, msp, temp: Dict, layer: str):
        """Draw temporary works"""
        temp_type = temp.get('type', '')
        
        if 'FORMWORK' in temp_type:
            # Draw formwork as dashed lines
            support = temp.get('support', {})
            ch = support.get('chainage', 0)
            height = support.get('height', 8.0)
            width = support.get('width', 2.0) + 0.2  # Slightly larger
            
            points = [
                (ch - width/2, 0),
                (ch + width/2, 0),
                (ch + width/2, height + 0.2),
                (ch - width/2, height + 0.2),
                (ch - width/2, 0)
            ]
            msp.add_lwpolyline(points=points, close=True,
                            dxfattribs={'layer': layer, 'linetype': 'DASHED'})
        
        elif 'SCAFFOLDING' in temp_type:
            # Draw scaffolding as grid
            support = temp.get('support', {})
            ch = support.get('chainage', 0)
            height = support.get('height', 8.0)
            
            # Vertical lines
            for offset in [-1, 0, 1]:
                msp.add_line(
                    start=(ch + offset, 0),
                    end=(ch + offset, height),
                    dxfattribs={'layer': layer, 'linetype': 'DASHED'}
                )
            
            # Horizontal lines every 2m
            for h in range(0, int(height) + 1, 2):
                msp.add_line(
                    start=(ch - 1.5, h),
                    end=(ch + 1.5, h),
                    dxfattribs={'layer': layer, 'linetype': 'DASHED'}
                )
    
    def _draw_equipment(self, msp, equip: Dict, layer: str):
        """Draw equipment symbols"""
        equip_type = equip.get('type', '')
        pos = equip.get('position', (0, 0))
        x, y = pos
        
        if equip_type == 'PILING_RIG':
            # Draw piling rig as tall rectangle with drill
            # Base
            msp.add_rectangle(
                insert=(x-2, y),
                width=4, height=3,
                dxfattribs={'layer': layer}
            )
            # Mast
            height = equip.get('height', 15)
            msp.add_line(
                start=(x, y+3),
                end=(x, y+height),
                dxfattribs={'layer': layer, 'lineweight': 0.5}
            )
            # Drill
            msp.add_line(
                start=(x, y+height),
                end=(x, y),
                dxfattribs={'layer': layer, 'linetype': 'DASHED'}
            )
        
        elif equip_type == 'CRANE':
            # Draw crane symbol
            # Body
            msp.add_rectangle(
                insert=(x-2, y),
                width=4, height=2,
                dxfattribs={'layer': layer}
            )
            # Boom
            height = equip.get('height', 10)
            msp.add_line(
                start=(x, y+2),
                end=(x+3, y+height),
                dxfattribs={'layer': layer, 'lineweight': 0.5}
            )
            # Counterweight
            msp.add_line(
                start=(x, y+2),
                end=(x-2, y+height*0.6),
                dxfattribs={'layer': layer}
            )
        
        elif equip_type == 'CONCRETE_PUMP':
            # Draw pump truck
            msp.add_rectangle(
                insert=(x-1.5, y),
                width=3, height=2,
                dxfattribs={'layer': layer}
            )
            # Boom
            msp.add_line(
                start=(x, y+2),
                end=(x+2, y+5),
                dxfattribs={'layer': layer}
            )
        
        elif equip_type == 'EXCAVATOR':
            # Simple excavator symbol
            msp.add_circle(center=(x, y+1), radius=1.5, 
                        dxfattribs={'layer': layer})
            # Arm
            rotation = equip.get('rotation', 0)
            rad = math.radians(rotation)
            arm_end_x = x + 3 * math.cos(rad)
            arm_end_y = y + 1 + 3 * math.sin(rad)
            msp.add_line(
                start=(x, y+1),
                end=(arm_end_x, arm_end_y),
                dxfattribs={'layer': layer}
            )
        
        # Label
        msp.add_text(
            equip_type.replace('_', '\n'),
            height=1.5,
            dxfattribs={'layer': 'TEXT'}
        ).set_placement((x, y-2), align=TextEntityAlignment.MIDDLE_CENTER)
    
    def generate_all_stages(self, output_dir: str = "./construction_stages/"):
        """Generate drawings for all construction stages"""
        import os
        os.makedirs(output_dir, exist_ok=True)
        
        generated_files = []
        
        for stage in ConstructionStage:
            output_path = os.path.join(output_dir, 
                                      f"stage_{stage.value.lower()}.dxf")
            file_path = self.generate_stage_drawing(stage, output_path)
            generated_files.append(file_path)
            print(f"Generated: {file_path}")
        
        return generated_files
    
    def generate_animated_sequence(self, output_dir: str = "./construction_animation/",
                                  frames_per_stage: int = 10) -> List[str]:
        """
        Generate multiple frames per stage for animation
        
        Args:
            output_dir: Output directory
            frames_per_stage: Number of frames to generate per stage
            
        Returns:
            List of generated file paths
        """
        import os
        os.makedirs(output_dir, exist_ok=True)
        
        generated_files = []
        frame_count = 0
        
        for stage in ConstructionStage:
            for progress in range(0, 101, int(100/frames_per_stage)):
                # Update geometry for this progress
                stage_geom = self.engine.get_stage_geometry(stage, progress)
                self.stage_geometries[stage] = stage_geom
                
                output_path = os.path.join(output_dir,
                                          f"frame_{frame_count:04d}.dxf")
                file_path = self.generate_stage_drawing(stage, output_path)
                generated_files.append(file_path)
                frame_count += 1
        
        print(f"Generated {len(generated_files)} animation frames")
        return generated_files


# INTEGRATION EXAMPLE
def create_construction_sequence(bridge_model, method: str = "CAST_IN_SITU"):
    """
    Complete construction sequence generation for a bridge
    
    Args:
        bridge_model: The bridge model
        method: Construction method
        
    Returns:
        ConstructionSequenceEngine and ConstructionSequenceDrawing instances
    """
    # Create sequence engine
    sequence = ConstructionSequenceEngine(bridge_model)
    sequence.define_standard_sequence(method)
    
    # Create drawing generator
    drawings = ConstructionSequenceDrawing(sequence)
    
    # Generate methodology statement
    methodology = sequence.generate_methodology_statement()
    print(methodology)
    
    # Generate all stage drawings
    files = drawings.generate_all_stages()
    
    print(f"\nGenerated {len(files)} construction stage drawings")
    
    return sequence, drawings


# USAGE EXAMPLE
if __name__ == "__main__":
    # Assuming you have a bridge model from previous modules
    from skew_bridge_engine import create_skew_bridge
    
    # Create example bridge
    bridge = create_skew_bridge(
        name="Chambakkara Bridge",
        chainage=600.0,
        span_lengths=[25.0, 30.0, 25.0],
        deck_width=12.0,
        skew_angle=15.0
    )
    
    # Generate construction sequence
    sequence, drawings = create_construction_sequence(bridge, "CAST_IN_SITU")
    
    # Generate animated sequence (optional)
    # animation_frames = drawings.generate_animated_sequence(frames_per_stage=5)
    
    print("\nConstruction sequence generation complete!")
```

This **Construction Sequence Generator** module provides:

## Key Features

| Feature | Description |
|---------|-------------|
| **12 Standard Stages** | From site prep to completion with realistic durations |
| **Stage-by-Stage Drawings** | Separate DXF for each construction phase |
| **Visual Progression** | Color-coded: Green (completed), Red (active), Cyan (temporary) |
| **Equipment Symbols** | Cranes, piling rigs, concrete pumps, launching girders |
| **Methodology Statement** | Auto-generated construction methodology document |
| **Animation Support** | Generate frame sequences for construction animation |
| **Resource Planning** | Crew sizes, equipment lists, and schedules |

## Output Files

The generator creates:
- `stage_site_preparation.dxf` - Site clearing, access roads
- `stage_piling.dxf` - Pile drilling and concreting
- `stage_pile_cap.dxf` - Pile cap construction
- `stage_pier_construction.dxf` - Pier shaft construction with scaffolding
- `stage_pier_cap.dxf` - Pier cap concreting
- `stage_bearing_installation.dxf` - Bearing placement
- `stage_girder_erection.dxf` - Girder launching/crane erection
- `stage_deck_slab.dxf` - Deck concreting
- `stage_parapet.dxf` - Parapet construction
- `stage_wearing_coat.dxf` - Road surfacing
- `stage_approach_work.dxf` - Approach embankments
- `stage_completion.dxf` - Final finishing

Each drawing shows:
- ✅ **Completed work** (green)
- 🔴 **Active construction** (red)
- 🔵 **Temporary works** (formwork, scaffolding)
- 🟡 **Equipment positions**
- 📋 **Stage duration and crew info**
