```python
"""
3D VISUALIZATION MODULE
Complete 3D bridge model generation with all elements
"""

import numpy as np
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import List, Tuple, Dict, Optional, Union
from enum import Enum
import json
import struct
from pathlib import Path


class MaterialType(Enum):
    """Bridge material types with properties"""
    CONCRETE = {
        'color': (0.75, 0.75, 0.75),
        'roughness': 0.9,
        'metallic': 0.0,
        'name': 'Concrete'
    }
    STEEL = {
        'color': (0.6, 0.6, 0.7),
        'roughness': 0.3,
        'metallic': 0.8,
        'name': 'Steel'
    }
    ASPHALT = {
        'color': (0.2, 0.2, 0.2),
        'roughness': 0.95,
        'metallic': 0.0,
        'name': 'Asphalt'
    }
    REINFORCEMENT = {
        'color': (0.4, 0.4, 0.4),
        'roughness': 0.7,
        'metallic': 0.9,
        'name': 'Reinforcement'
    }
    BEARING_PAD = {
        'color': (0.1, 0.1, 0.1),
        'roughness': 0.8,
        'metallic': 0.0,
        'name': 'Elastomer'
    }
    PARAPET = {
        'color': (0.85, 0.85, 0.85),
        'roughness': 0.9,
        'metallic': 0.0,
        'name': 'Parapet Concrete'
    }
    WATER = {
        'color': (0.2, 0.4, 0.8),
        'roughness': 0.1,
        'metallic': 0.0,
        'transparency': 0.6,
        'name': 'Water'
    }
    SOIL = {
        'color': (0.5, 0.35, 0.2),
        'roughness': 1.0,
        'metallic': 0.0,
        'name': 'Soil'
    }


@dataclass
class Vertex:
    """3D vertex with position and normal"""
    x: float
    y: float
    z: float
    nx: float = 0.0
    ny: float = 0.0
    nz: float = 1.0
    
    def to_tuple(self) -> Tuple[float, float, float]:
        return (self.x, self.y, self.z)
    
    def to_array(self) -> np.ndarray:
        return np.array([self.x, self.y, self.z])


@dataclass
class Face:
    """3D face with vertex indices and material"""
    vertices: List[int]  # Indices into vertex list
    material: MaterialType = MaterialType.CONCRETE
    normal: Optional[Tuple[float, float, float]] = None


@dataclass
class Mesh:
    """3D mesh containing vertices and faces"""
    name: str
    vertices: List[Vertex] = field(default_factory=list)
    faces: List[Face] = field(default_factory=list)
    material: MaterialType = MaterialType.CONCRETE
    
    def add_vertex(self, x: float, y: float, z: float, 
                   nx: float = 0, ny: float = 0, nz: float = 1) -> int:
        """Add vertex and return its index"""
        self.vertices.append(Vertex(x, y, z, nx, ny, nz))
        return len(self.vertices) - 1
    
    def add_face(self, indices: List[int], material: MaterialType = None):
        """Add face with vertex indices"""
        mat = material or self.material
        self.faces.append(Face(indices, mat))
    
    def add_quad(self, v1: int, v2: int, v3: int, v4: int, 
                 material: MaterialType = None):
        """Add quadrilateral face"""
        self.add_face([v1, v2, v3, v4], material)
    
    def add_triangle(self, v1: int, v2: int, v3: int, 
                     material: MaterialType = None):
        """Add triangular face"""
        self.add_face([v1, v2, v3], material)
    
    def calculate_normals(self):
        """Calculate face normals from vertices"""
        for face in self.faces:
            if len(face.vertices) >= 3:
                v0 = self.vertices[face.vertices[0]].to_array()
                v1 = self.vertices[face.vertices[1]].to_array()
                v2 = self.vertices[face.vertices[2]].to_array()
                
                # Calculate normal using cross product
                edge1 = v1 - v0
                edge2 = v2 - v0
                normal = np.cross(edge1, edge2)
                normal = normal / (np.linalg.norm(normal) + 1e-10)
                
                face.normal = tuple(normal)
                
                # Update vertex normals
                for vi in face.vertices:
                    self.vertices[vi].nx = normal[0]
                    self.vertices[vi].ny = normal[1]
                    self.vertices[vi].nz = normal[2]
    
    def translate(self, dx: float, dy: float, dz: float):
        """Translate all vertices"""
        for v in self.vertices:
            v.x += dx
            v.y += dy
            v.z += dz
    
    def rotate_z(self, angle_deg: float, cx: float = 0, cy: float = 0):
        """Rotate around Z axis"""
        angle_rad = np.radians(angle_deg)
        cos_a = np.cos(angle_rad)
        sin_a = np.sin(angle_rad)
        
        for v in self.vertices:
            # Translate to origin
            x = v.x - cx
            y = v.y - cy
            
            # Rotate
            v.x = cx + x * cos_a - y * sin_a
            v.y = cy + x * sin_a + y * cos_a
    
    def scale(self, sx: float, sy: float, sz: float):
        """Scale mesh"""
        for v in self.vertices:
            v.x *= sx
            v.y *= sy
            v.z *= sz


class Bridge3DModel:
    """
    Complete 3D bridge model with all elements
    """
    
    def __init__(self, bridge_model):
        self.bridge = bridge_model
        self.meshes: Dict[str, Mesh] = {}
        self.supports: List[Dict] = []
        self._calculate_supports()
        
    def _calculate_supports(self):
        """Calculate support positions"""
        current_ch = self.bridge.chainage
        
        # Left abutment
        self.supports.append({
            'type': 'ABUTMENT',
            'chainage': current_ch,
            'number': 1,
            'height': getattr(self.bridge, 'abutment_height', 8.0),
            'width': getattr(self.bridge, 'abutment_width', 3.0),
            'length': getattr(self.bridge, 'abutment_length', 8.0),
            'has_piles': True
        })
        
        # Piers
        for i, span in enumerate(self.bridge.spans[:-1]):
            current_ch += span.length
            self.supports.append({
                'type': 'PIER',
                'chainage': current_ch,
                'number': i + 2,
                'height': getattr(self.bridge, 'pier_height', 10.0),
                'width': getattr(self.bridge, 'pier_width', 2.0),
                'length': getattr(self.bridge, 'pier_length', 3.0),
                'has_piles': True
            })
        
        # Right abutment
        if self.bridge.spans:
            current_ch += self.bridge.spans[-1].length
        self.supports.append({
            'type': 'ABUTMENT',
            'chainage': current_ch,
            'number': len(self.supports) + 1,
            'height': getattr(self.bridge, 'abutment_height', 8.0),
            'width': getattr(self.bridge, 'abutment_width', 3.0),
            'length': getattr(self.bridge, 'abutment_length', 8.0),
            'has_piles': True
        })
    
    def generate_full_model(self, include_foundation: bool = True,
                           include_reinforcement: bool = False):
        """
        Generate complete 3D model
        
        Args:
            include_foundation: Include piles and pile caps
            include_reinforcement: Show reinforcement bars
        """
        # Generate each component
        self._generate_deck()
        self._generate_girders()
        self._generate_cross_girders()
        self._generate_piers_and_abutments()
        
        if include_foundation:
            self._generate_pile_caps()
            self._generate_piles()
        
        self._generate_bearings()
        self._generate_parapets()
        self._generate_wearing_surface()
        
        if include_reinforcement:
            self._generate_reinforcement()
        
        # Calculate normals for all meshes
        for mesh in self.meshes.values():
            mesh.calculate_normals()
        
        return self.meshes
    
    def _generate_deck(self):
        """Generate deck slab 3D model"""
        mesh = Mesh("Deck_Slab", material=MaterialType.CONCRETE)
        
        deck = self.bridge.deck
        start_ch = self.bridge.chainage
        total_length = self.bridge.total_length
        
        # Apply skew transformation
        skew_rad = np.radians(self.bridge.skew_angle)
        
        # Deck thickness
        thickness = getattr(deck, 'thickness', 0.25)
        
        # Create deck vertices
        half_width = deck.square_width / 2
        
        # Bottom surface (soffit)
        z_bottom = deck.soffit_level
        
        # Create grid of points along length
        num_segments = 20
        for i in range(num_segments + 1):
            t = i / num_segments
            x = start_ch + t * total_length
            
            # Skew offset
            skew_offset = t * total_length * np.tan(skew_rad)
            
            for side in [-1, 1]:  # Left and right
                y = side * half_width + skew_offset
                
                # Bottom vertex
                mesh.add_vertex(x, y, z_bottom)
                # Top vertex
                mesh.add_vertex(x, y, z_bottom + thickness)
        
        # Create faces
        cols = 2  # Left and right
        for i in range(num_segments):
            for j in range(cols - 1):
                # Bottom face indices
                b1 = i * cols * 2 + j * 2
                b2 = i * cols * 2 + (j + 1) * 2
                b3 = (i + 1) * cols * 2 + (j + 1) * 2
                b4 = (i + 1) * cols * 2 + j * 2
                
                # Top face indices
                t1 = b1 + 1
                t2 = b2 + 1
                t3 = b3 + 1
                t4 = b4 + 1
                
                # Bottom face (facing down)
                mesh.add_quad(b1, b4, b3, b2)
                # Top face (facing up)
                mesh.add_quad(t1, t2, t3, t4)
                # Side faces
                mesh.add_quad(b1, b2, t2, t1)
                mesh.add_quad(b2, b3, t3, t2)
        
        self.meshes['deck'] = mesh
    
    def _generate_girders(self):
        """Generate main girders 3D model"""
        num_girders = self.bridge.get_num_girders()
        if num_girders == 0:
            return
        
        deck = self.bridge.deck
        half_width = deck.square_width / 2
        
        # Calculate girder positions
        if num_girders == 1:
            y_positions = [0]
        else:
            spacing = deck.square_width / (num_girders - 1)
            y_positions = [-half_width + i * spacing for i in range(num_girders)]
        
        # Girder dimensions
        girder_depth = getattr(self.bridge, 'girder_depth', 1.5)
        girder_width = getattr(self.bridge, 'girder_width', 0.4)
        
        skew_rad = np.radians(self.bridge.skew_angle)
        start_ch = self.bridge.chainage
        
        for idx, y_base in enumerate(y_positions):
            mesh = Mesh(f"Girder_{idx+1}", material=MaterialType.CONCRETE)
            
            # Generate girder for each span
            current_ch = start_ch
            for span in self.bridge.spans:
                # Create girder segment
                self._create_girder_segment(
                    mesh, current_ch, span.length, y_base,
                    girder_width, girder_depth, skew_rad
                )
                current_ch += span.length
            
            self.meshes[f'girder_{idx+1}'] = mesh
    
    def _create_girder_segment(self, mesh: Mesh, start_x: float, length: float,
                                y_base: float, width: float, depth: float,
                                skew_rad: float):
        """Create a single girder segment"""
        # I-girder cross-section vertices (simplified)
        # Top flange
        tf_width = width * 2.5
        tf_thick = 0.25
        
        # Bottom flange
        bf_width = width * 3.0
        bf_thick = 0.3
        
        # Web
        web_thick = width * 0.4
        
        num_segments = 10
        
        for i in range(num_segments + 1):
            t = i / num_segments
            x = start_x + t * length
            
            # Skew offset
            skew_offset = t * length * np.tan(skew_rad)
            y = y_base + skew_offset
            
            z_top = self.bridge.deck.soffit_level
            z_bottom = z_top - depth
            
            # Create cross-section vertices at this station
            # Bottom flange
            mesh.add_vertex(x, y - bf_width/2, z_bottom)
            mesh.add_vertex(x, y + bf_width/2, z_bottom)
            mesh.add_vertex(x, y + bf_width/2, z_bottom + bf_thick)
            mesh.add_vertex(x, y - bf_width/2, z_bottom + bf_thick)
            
            # Web bottom
            mesh.add_vertex(x, y - web_thick/2, z_bottom + bf_thick)
            mesh.add_vertex(x, y + web_thick/2, z_bottom + bf_thick)
            
            # Web top
            mesh.add_vertex(x, y + web_thick/2, z_top - tf_thick)
            mesh.add_vertex(x, y - web_thick/2, z_top - tf_thick)
            
            # Top flange
            mesh.add_vertex(x, y - tf_width/2, z_top - tf_thick)
            mesh.add_vertex(x, y + tf_width/2, z_top - tf_thick)
            mesh.add_vertex(x, y + tf_width/2, z_top)
            mesh.add_vertex(x, y - tf_width/2, z_top)
        
        # Create faces connecting stations
        verts_per_station = 12
        for i in range(num_segments):
            base = i * verts_per_station
            next_base = (i + 1) * verts_per_station
            
            for j in range(verts_per_station - 1):
                v1 = base + j
                v2 = base + j + 1
                v3 = next_base + j + 1
                v4 = next_base + j
                mesh.add_quad(v1, v2, v3, v4)
    
    def _generate_cross_girders(self):
        """Generate cross girders at supports"""
        mesh = Mesh("Cross_Girders", material=MaterialType.CONCRETE)
        
        deck = self.bridge.deck
        half_width = deck.square_width / 2
        girder_depth = getattr(self.bridge, 'girder_depth', 1.5)
        z_level = self.bridge.deck.soffit_level - girder_depth / 2
        
        for support in self.supports:
            ch = support['chainage']
            
            # Cross girder spans between outer girders
            mesh.add_vertex(ch, -half_width, z_level)
            mesh.add_vertex(ch, half_width, z_level)
            mesh.add_vertex(ch, half_width, z_level + 0.5)
            mesh.add_vertex(ch, -half_width, z_level + 0.5)
            
            # Thickness
            thick = 0.3
            mesh.add_vertex(ch + thick, -half_width, z_level)
            mesh.add_vertex(ch + thick, half_width, z_level)
            mesh.add_vertex(ch + thick, half_width, z_level + 0.5)
            mesh.add_vertex(ch + thick, -half_width, z_level + 0.5)
            
            # Create faces
            base = len(mesh.vertices) - 8
            mesh.add_quad(base, base+1, base+5, base+4)
            mesh.add_quad(base+1, base+2, base+6, base+5)
            mesh.add_quad(base+2, base+3, base+7, base+6)
            mesh.add_quad(base+3, base, base+4, base+7)
            mesh.add_quad(base, base+1, base+2, base+3)
            mesh.add_quad(base+4, base+5, base+6, base+7)
        
        self.meshes['cross_girders'] = mesh
    
    def _generate_piers_and_abutments(self):
        """Generate pier and abutment 3D models"""
        for support in self.supports:
            if support['type'] == 'PIER':
                mesh = self._create_pier_mesh(support)
                self.meshes[f"pier_{support['number']}"] = mesh
            else:
                mesh = self._create_abutment_mesh(support)
                self.meshes[f"abutment_{support['number']}"] = mesh
    
    def _create_pier_mesh(self, pier: Dict) -> Mesh:
        """Create 3D pier mesh"""
        mesh = Mesh(f"Pier_{pier['number']}", material=MaterialType.CONCRETE)
        
        ch = pier['chainage']
        width = pier['width']
        length = pier['length']
        height = pier['height']
        
        # Pier cap dimensions
        cap_length = length + 1.0
        cap_width = width + 1.0
        cap_height = 1.5
        
        # Shaft top (at bearing level)
        z_top = self.bridge.deck.soffit_level - 0.3  # Below bearings
        z_cap_bottom = z_top - cap_height
        z_base = z_cap_bottom - height + cap_height
        
        # Create pier cap
        self._create_box(mesh, ch, 0, z_cap_bottom + cap_height/2,
                        cap_length, cap_width, cap_height)
        
        # Create pier shaft (tapered or uniform)
        shaft_width = width
        shaft_length = length
        
        self._create_box(mesh, ch, 0, z_base + (z_cap_bottom - z_base)/2,
                        shaft_length, shaft_width, z_cap_bottom - z_base)
        
        # Add chamfer or architectural features
        # Foundation connection
        self._create_box(mesh, ch, 0, z_base - 0.5,
                        shaft_length + 0.5, shaft_width + 0.5, 1.0)
        
        return mesh
    
    def _create_abutment_mesh(self, abutment: Dict) -> Mesh:
        """Create 3D abutment mesh"""
        mesh = Mesh(f"Abutment_{abutment['number']}", material=MaterialType.CONCRETE)
        
        ch = abutment['chainage']
        width = abutment['width']
        length = abutment['length']
        height = abutment['height']
        
        deck = self.bridge.deck
        
        # Abutment wall
        z_top = deck.soffit_level
        z_base = z_top - height
        
        # Main wall
        self._create_box(mesh, ch, 0, z_base + height/2,
                        width, deck.square_width + 1.0, height)
        
        # Wing walls (at angle)
        wing_length = 5.0
        wing_height = height * 0.7
        
        for side in [-1, 1]:
            y = side * (deck.square_width/2 + 0.5)
            
            # Wing wall
            self._create_box(mesh, 
                           ch + side * wing_length/2, 
                           y + side * wing_length/2,
                           z_base + wing_height/2,
                           width, wing_length, wing_height)
        
        # Return walls
        return_wall_length = 3.0
        for side in [-1, 1]:
            y = side * (deck.square_width/2 + return_wall_length/2 + 0.5)
            self._create_box(mesh,
                           ch - width/2 - return_wall_length/2,
                           y,
                           z_base + height * 0.5,
                           return_wall_length, 0.5, height * 0.5)
        
        return mesh
    
    def _create_box(self, mesh: Mesh, cx: float, cy: float, cz: float,
                   length: float, width: float, height: float):
        """Add box geometry to mesh"""
        hx, hy, hz = length/2, width/2, height/2
        
        # 8 corners
        corners = [
            (cx-hx, cy-hy, cz-hz),  # 0
            (cx+hx, cy-hy, cz-hz),  # 1
            (cx+hx, cy+hy, cz-hz),  # 2
            (cx-hx, cy+hy, cz-hz),  # 3
            (cx-hx, cy-hy, cz+hz),  # 4
            (cx+hx, cy-hy, cz+hz),  # 5
            (cx+hx, cy+hy, cz+hz),  # 6
            (cx-hx, cy+hy, cz+hz),  # 7
        ]
        
        base_idx = len(mesh.vertices)
        for corner in corners:
            mesh.add_vertex(*corner)
        
        # Faces (counter-clockwise when looking from outside)
        # Bottom
        mesh.add_quad(base_idx+0, base_idx+3, base_idx+2, base_idx+1)
        # Top
        mesh.add_quad(base_idx+4, base_idx+5, base_idx+6, base_idx+7)
        # Front
        mesh.add_quad(base_idx+0, base_idx+1, base_idx+5, base_idx+4)
        # Back
        mesh.add_quad(base_idx+3, base_idx+7, base_idx+6, base_idx+2)
        # Left
        mesh.add_quad(base_idx+0, base_idx+4, base_idx+7, base_idx+3)
        # Right
        mesh.add_quad(base_idx+1, base_idx+2, base_idx+6, base_idx+5)
    
    def _generate_pile_caps(self):
        """Generate pile cap 3D models"""
        for support in self.supports:
            if not support.get('has_piles', False):
                continue
            
            mesh = Mesh(f"Pile_Cap_{support['number']}", 
                       material=MaterialType.CONCRETE)
            
            ch = support['chainage']
            height = support['height']
            
            # Pile cap dimensions
            cap_length = 6.0
            cap_width = 4.0
            cap_thickness = 2.0
            
            z_top = support.get('foundation_level', 
                              self.bridge.deck.soffit_level - height - 0.5)
            z_bottom = z_top - cap_thickness
            
            self._create_box(mesh, ch, 0, (z_top + z_bottom)/2,
                           cap_length, cap_width, cap_thickness)
            
            self.meshes[f'pile_cap_{support["number"]}'] = mesh
    
    def _generate_piles(self):
        """Generate bored pile 3D models"""
        mesh = Mesh("Piles", material=MaterialType.CONCRETE)
        
        pile_diameter = 1.2
        pile_length = 15.0  # Typical
        
        for support in self.supports:
            if not support.get('has_piles', False):
                continue
            
            ch = support['chainage']
            
            # Standard 3x2 pile group
            spacing = 1.5
            
            for i in range(3):  # Longitudinal
                for j in range(2):  # Transverse
                    px = ch + (i - 1) * spacing
                    py = (j - 0.5) * spacing
                    
                    # Pile top level
                    z_top = support.get('foundation_level', 
                                      self.bridge.deck.soffit_level - 
                                      support['height'] - 0.5) - 2.0
                    z_bottom = z_top - pile_length
                    
                    # Create cylinder (as prism)
                    self._create_cylinder(mesh, px, py, z_top, z_bottom, 
                                        pile_diameter/2, 16)
        
        self.meshes['piles'] = mesh
    
    def _create_cylinder(self, mesh: Mesh, cx: float, cy: float, 
                        z_top: float, z_bottom: float, radius: float, 
                        segments: int = 16):
        """Create vertical cylinder"""
        base_idx = len(mesh.vertices)
        
        # Bottom circle
        for i in range(segments):
            angle = 2 * np.pi * i / segments
            x = cx + radius * np.cos(angle)
            y = cy + radius * np.sin(angle)
            mesh.add_vertex(x, y, z_bottom)
        
        # Top circle
        for i in range(segments):
            angle = 2 * np.pi * i / segments
            x = cx + radius * np.cos(angle)
            y = cy + radius * np.sin(angle)
            mesh.add_vertex(x, y, z_top)
        
        # Side faces
        for i in range(segments):
            next_i = (i + 1) % segments
            # Bottom vertex indices
            b1 = base_idx + i
            b2 = base_idx + next_i
            # Top vertex indices
            t1 = base_idx + segments + i
            t2 = base_idx + segments + next_i
            
            mesh.add_quad(b1, b2, t2, t1)
        
        # Bottom cap
        bottom_indices = [base_idx + i for i in range(segments)]
        # Fan triangulation
        for i in range(1, segments - 1):
            mesh.add_triangle(bottom_indices[0], bottom_indices[i+1], 
                          bottom_indices[i])
        
        # Top cap
        top_indices = [base_idx + segments + i for i in range(segments)]
        for i in range(1, segments - 1):
            mesh.add_triangle(top_indices[0], top_indices[i], 
                          top_indices[i+1])
    
    def _generate_bearings(self):
        """Generate bearing pad 3D models"""
        mesh = Mesh("Bearings", material=MaterialType.BEARING_PAD)
        
        num_girders = self.bridge.get_num_girders()
        if num_girders == 0:
            return
        
        deck = self.bridge.deck
        half_width = deck.square_width / 2
        
        if num_girders == 1:
            y_positions = [0]
        else:
            spacing = deck.square_width / (num_girders - 1)
            y_positions = [-half_width + i * spacing for i in range(num_girders)]
        
        # Bearing dimensions
        bearing_length = 0.4
        bearing_width = 0.5
        bearing_height = 0.15
        
        for support in self.supports:
            ch = support['chainage']
            z = deck.soffit_level - bearing_height
            
            for y in y_positions:
                self._create_box(mesh, ch, y, z + bearing_height/2,
                               bearing_length, bearing_width, bearing_height)
        
        self.meshes['bearings'] = mesh
    
    def _generate_parapets(self):
        """Generate parapet/crash barrier 3D models"""
        mesh = Mesh("Parapets", material=MaterialType.PARAPET)
        
        deck = self.bridge.deck
        start_ch = self.bridge.chainage
        total_length = self.bridge.total_length
        half_width = deck.square_width / 2
        
        # Parapet dimensions
        parapet_height = 1.1  # Standard crash barrier
        parapet_width = 0.3
        base_width = 0.5
        
        skew_rad = np.radians(self.bridge.skew_angle)
        
        num_segments = 20
        for side in [-1, 1]:
            y_offset = side * (half_width + base_width/2)
            
            for i in range(num_segments):
                t1 = i / num_segments
                t2 = (i + 1) / num_segments
                
                x1 = start_ch + t1 * total_length
                x2 = start_ch + t2 * total_length
                
                skew1 = t1 * total_length * np.tan(skew_rad)
                skew2 = t2 * total_length * np.tan(skew_rad)
                
                y1 = y_offset + skew1
                y2 = y_offset + skew2
                
                z_base = deck.soffit_level + deck.thickness
                z_top = z_base + parapet_height
                
                # Create parapet segment (trapezoidal section)
                # Base
                v1 = mesh.add_vertex(x1, y1 - side*base_width/2, z_base)
                v2 = mesh.add_vertex(x1, y1 + side*base_width/2, z_base)
                v3 = mesh.add_vertex(x2, y2 + side*base_width/2, z_base)
                v4 = mesh.add_vertex(x2, y2 - side*base_width/2, z_base)
                
                # Top
                v5 = mesh.add_vertex(x1, y1 - side*parapet_width/2, z_top)
                v6 = mesh.add_vertex(x1, y1 + side*parapet_width/2, z_top)
                v7 = mesh.add_vertex(x2, y2 + side*parapet_width/2, z_top)
                v8 = mesh.add_vertex(x2, y2 - side*parapet_width/2, z_top)
                
                # Faces
                mesh.add_quad(v1, v2, v3, v4)  # Bottom
                mesh.add_quad(v5, v6, v7, v8)  # Top
                mesh.add_quad(v1, v4, v8, v5)  # Outer face
                mesh.add_quad(v2, v6, v7, v3)  # Inner face
                mesh.add_quad(v1, v5, v6, v2)  # End 1
                mesh.add_quad(v4, v3, v7, v8)  # End 2
        
        self.meshes['parapets'] = mesh
    
    def _generate_wearing_surface(self):
        """Generate wearing course/asphalt 3D model"""
        mesh = Mesh("Wearing_Course", material=MaterialType.ASPHALT)
        
        deck = self.bridge.deck
        start_ch = self.bridge.chainage
        total_length = self.bridge.total_length
        half_width = deck.square_width / 2 - 0.5  # Slightly narrower than deck
        
        wearing_thickness = 0.065  # 65mm
        z_level = deck.soffit_level + deck.thickness
        
        skew_rad = np.radians(self.bridge.skew_angle)
        
        num_segments = 20
        for i in range(num_segments):
            t1 = i / num_segments
            t2 = (i + 1) / num_segments
            
            x1 = start_ch + t1 * total_length
            x2 = start_ch + t2 * total_length
            
            skew1 = t1 * total_length * np.tan(skew_rad)
            skew2 = t2 * total_length * np.tan(skew_rad)
            
            # Four corners
            v1 = mesh.add_vertex(x1, -half_width + skew1, z_level)
            v2 = mesh.add_vertex(x1, half_width + skew1, z_level)
            v3 = mesh.add_vertex(x2, half_width + skew2, z_level)
            v4 = mesh.add_vertex(x2, -half_width + skew2, z_level)
            
            v5 = mesh.add_vertex(x1, -half_width + skew1, z_level + wearing_thickness)
            v6 = mesh.add_vertex(x1, half_width + skew1, z_level + wearing_thickness)
            v7 = mesh.add_vertex(x2, half_width + skew2, z_level + wearing_thickness)
            v8 = mesh.add_vertex(x2, -half_width + skew2, z_level + wearing_thickness)
            
            mesh.add_quad(v1, v4, v3, v2)
            mesh.add_quad(v5, v6, v7, v8)
            mesh.add_quad(v1, v2, v6, v5)
            mesh.add_quad(v2, v3, v7, v6)
            mesh.add_quad(v3, v4, v8, v7)
            mesh.add_quad(v4, v1, v5, v8)
        
        self.meshes['wearing_course'] = mesh
    
    def _generate_reinforcement(self):
        """Generate simplified reinforcement visualization"""
        mesh = Mesh("Reinforcement", material=MaterialType.REINFORCEMENT)
        
        # Simplified rebar representation
        # Main deck reinforcement
        deck = self.bridge.deck
        start_ch = self.bridge.chainage
        total_length = self.bridge.total_length
        
        # Longitudinal bars
        bar_spacing = 0.15
        num_bars = int(deck.square_width / bar_spacing)
        
        z_level = deck.soffit_level + 0.05  # Cover
        
        for i in range(num_bars):
            y = -deck.square_width/2 + i * bar_spacing
            
            # Create bar as small cylinder
            bar_radius = 0.016  # 32mm bar
            self._create_horizontal_bar(mesh, start_ch, start_ch + total_length,
                                       y, z_level, bar_radius)
        
        # Transverse bars
        for ch in np.arange(start_ch, start_ch + total_length, 0.2):
            self._create_transverse_bar(mesh, ch, -deck.square_width/2, 
                                     deck.square_width/2, z_level, 0.012)
        
        self.meshes['reinforcement'] = mesh
    
    def _create_horizontal_bar(self, mesh: Mesh, x1: float, x2: float,
                               y: float, z: float, radius: float):
        """Create horizontal rebar"""
        # Simplified as line with thickness
        segments = 8
        base_idx = len(mesh.vertices)
        
        for i in range(segments):
            angle = 2 * np.pi * i / segments
            dy = radius * np.cos(angle)
            dz = radius * np.sin(angle)
            
            mesh.add_vertex(x1, y + dy, z + dz)
            mesh.add_vertex(x2, y + dy, z + dz)
        
        # Create faces
        for i in range(segments):
            next_i = (i + 1) % segments
            v1 = base_idx + i * 2
            v2 = base_idx + next_i * 2
            v3 = base_idx + next_i * 2 + 1
            v4 = base_idx + i * 2 + 1
            mesh.add_quad(v1, v2, v3, v4)
    
    def _create_transverse_bar(self, mesh: Mesh, x: float, y1: float, 
                               y2: float, z: float, radius: float):
        """Create transverse rebar"""
        segments = 6
        base_idx = len(mesh.vertices)
        
        for i in range(segments):
            angle = 2 * np.pi * i / segments
            dx = radius * np.cos(angle)
            dz = radius * np.sin(angle)
            
            mesh.add_vertex(x + dx, y1, z + dz)
            mesh.add_vertex(x + dx, y2, z + dz)
        
        for i in range(segments):
            next_i = (i + 1) % segments
            v1 = base_idx + i * 2
            v2 = base_idx + next_i * 2
            v3 = base_idx + next_i * 2 + 1
            v4 = base_idx + i * 2 + 1
            mesh.add_quad(v1, v2, v3, v4)


class ModelExporter:
    """
    Export 3D models to various formats
    """
    
    def __init__(self, bridge_3d: Bridge3DModel):
        self.model = bridge_3d
    
    def export_obj(self, filepath: str) -> str:
        """
        Export to Wavefront OBJ format
        
        Args:
            filepath: Output file path
            
        Returns:
            Path to exported file
        """
        lines = []
        lines.append("# Bridge 3D Model")
        lines.append(f"# Generated: {datetime.now().isoformat()}")
        lines.append(f"# Bridge: {self.model.bridge.name}")
        lines.append("")
        
        vertex_offset = 0
        normal_offset = 0
        
        for name, mesh in self.model.meshes.items():
            lines.append(f"# Mesh: {name}")
            lines.append(f"g {name}")
            
            # Material
            mat = mesh.material.value
            lines.append(f"usemtl {mat['name']}")
            
            # Vertices
            for v in mesh.vertices:
                lines.append(f"v {v.x:.6f} {v.y:.6f} {v.z:.6f}")
            
            # Normals
            for v in mesh.vertices:
                lines.append(f"vn {v.nx:.6f} {v.ny:.6f} {v.nz:.6f}")
            
            # Faces (1-indexed, with normals)
            for face in mesh.faces:
                if face.normal:
                    indices = [f"{vi + 1 + vertex_offset}//{vi + 1 + normal_offset}" 
                             for vi in face.vertices]
                    lines.append(f"f {' '.join(indices)}")
            
            vertex_offset += len(mesh.vertices)
            normal_offset += len(mesh.vertices)
            lines.append("")
        
        # Write material file
        mtl_path = filepath.replace('.obj', '.mtl')
        self._write_mtl(mtl_path)
        lines.insert(0, f"mtllib {Path(mtl_path).name}")
        
        with open(filepath, 'w') as f:
            f.write('\n'.join(lines))
        
        return filepath
    
    def _write_mtl(self, filepath: str):
        """Write material file for OBJ"""
        lines = ["# Bridge Materials"]
        
        for mat in MaterialType:
            props = mat.value
            lines.append(f"newmtl {props['name']}")
            lines.append(f"Ka {props['color'][0]:.3f} {props['color'][1]:.3f} {props['color'][2]:.3f}")
            lines.append(f"Kd {props['color'][0]:.3f} {props['color'][1]:.3f} {props['color'][2]:.3f}")
            lines.append(f"Ks {props['metallic']:.3f} {props['metallic']:.3f} {props['metallic']:.3f}")
            lines.append(f"Ns {100 * (1 - props['roughness']):.1f}")
            if 'transparency' in props:
                lines.append(f"d {1 - props['transparency']:.3f}")
            lines.append("")
        
        with open(filepath, 'w') as f:
            f.write('\n'.join(lines))
    
    def export_stl(self, filepath: str, binary: bool = True) -> str:
        """
        Export to STL format for 3D printing
        
        Args:
            filepath: Output file path
            binary: Use binary format (smaller)
            
        Returns:
            Path to exported file
        """
        if binary:
            return self._export_stl_binary(filepath)
        else:
            return self._export_stl_ascii(filepath)
    
    def _export_stl_ascii(self, filepath: str) -> str:
        """Export ASCII STL"""
        lines = ["solid BridgeModel"]
        
        for name, mesh in self.model.meshes.items():
            for face in mesh.faces:
                if len(face.vertices) >= 3:
                    normal = face.normal or (0, 0, 1)
                    
                    lines.append(f"  facet normal {normal[0]:.6f} {normal[1]:.6f} {normal[2]:.6f}")
                    lines.append("    outer loop")
                    
                    for vi in face.vertices[:3]:  # STL only supports triangles
                        v = mesh.vertices[vi]
                        lines.append(f"      vertex {v.x:.6f} {v.y:.6f} {v.z:.6f}")
                    
                    lines.append("    endloop")
                    lines.append("  endfacet")
        
        lines.append("endsolid BridgeModel")
        
        with open(filepath, 'w') as f:
            f.write('\n'.join(lines))
        
        return filepath
    
    def _export_stl_binary(self, filepath: str) -> str:
        """Export binary STL"""
        # Count triangles
        triangle_count = 0
        for mesh in self.model.meshes.values():
            for face in mesh.faces:
                if len(face.vertices) >= 3:
                    triangle_count += len(face.vertices) - 2
        
        with open(filepath, 'wb') as f:
            # 80 byte header
            header = b'Bridge 3D Model' + b'\x00' * (80 - 15)
            f.write(header)
            
            # Triangle count
            f.write(struct.pack('<I', triangle_count))
            
            # Triangles
            for mesh in self.model.meshes.values():
                for face in mesh.faces:
                    if len(face.vertices) >= 3:
                        normal = face.normal or (0, 0, 1)
                        
                        # Triangulate polygon
                        for i in range(1, len(face.vertices) - 1):
                            # Normal
                            f.write(struct.pack('<3f', *normal))
                            
                            # Vertices
                            v0 = mesh.vertices[face.vertices[0]]
                            v1 = mesh.vertices[face.vertices[i]]
                            v2 = mesh.vertices[face.vertices[i + 1]]
                            
                            f.write(struct.pack('<3f', v0.x, v0.y, v0.z))
                            f.write(struct.pack('<3f', v1.x, v1.y, v1.z))
                            f.write(struct.pack('<3f', v2.x, v2.y, v2.z))
                            
                            # Attribute byte count
                            f.write(struct.pack('<H', 0))
        
        return filepath
    
    def export_dxf_3d(self, filepath: str) -> str:
        """
        Export to 3D DXF format
        
        Args:
            filepath: Output file path
            
        Returns:
            Path to exported file
        """
        import ezdxf
        
        doc = ezdxf.new('R2018')
        msp = doc.modelspace()
        
        # Create layers for materials
        for mat in MaterialType:
            doc.layers.add(name=mat.value['name'], 
                        color=self._color_to_aci(mat.value['color']))
        
        for name, mesh in self.model.meshes.items():
            layer = mesh.material.value['name']
            
            for face in mesh.faces:
                if len(face.vertices) == 3:
                    # Triangle
                    v0 = mesh.vertices[face.vertices[0]]
                    v1 = mesh.vertices[face.vertices[1]]
                    v2 = mesh.vertices[face.vertices[2]]
                    
                    msp.add_3dface(
                        [v0.x, v0.y, v0.z],
                        [v1.x, v1.y, v1.z],
                        [v2.x, v2.y, v2.z],
                        [v2.x, v2.y, v2.z],
                        dxfattribs={'layer': layer}
                    )
                
                elif len(face.vertices) == 4:
                    # Quad
                    v0 = mesh.vertices[face.vertices[0]]
                    v1 = mesh.vertices[face.vertices[1]]
                    v2 = mesh.vertices[face.vertices[2]]
                    v3 = mesh.vertices[face.vertices[3]]
                    
                    msp.add_3dface(
                        [v0.x, v0.y, v0.z],
                        [v1.x, v1.y, v1.z],
                        [v2.x, v2.y, v2.z],
                        [v3.x, v3.y, v3.z],
                        dxfattribs={'layer': layer}
                    )
        
        doc.saveas(filepath)
        return filepath
    
    def _color_to_aci(self, rgb: Tuple[float, float, float]) -> int:
        """Convert RGB to AutoCAD Color Index (approximate)"""
        # Simple mapping - in practice would use proper color table
        r, g, b = rgb
        if r > 0.7 and g > 0.7 and b > 0.7:
            return 7  # White
        elif r > 0.5 and g > 0.5:
            return 2  # Yellow
        elif r > 0.5:
            return 1  # Red
        elif g > 0.5:
            return 3  # Green
        elif b > 0.5:
            return 5  # Blue
        elif r > 0.3 and g > 0.3 and b > 0.3:
            return 8  # Gray
        else:
            return 250  # Dark gray
    
    def export_threejs_json(self, filepath: str) -> str:
        """
        Export to Three.js JSON format for web viewing
        
        Args:
            filepath: Output file path
            
        Returns:
            Path to exported file
        """
        data = {
            "metadata": {
                "version": 4.5,
                "type": "Object",
                "generator": "Bridge3DExporter"
            },
            "geometries": [],
            "materials": [],
            "object": {
                "uuid": "bridge_root",
                "type": "Scene",
                "children": []
            }
        }
        
        # Add materials
        mat_map = {}
        for i, mat in enumerate(MaterialType):
            mat_data = {
                "uuid": f"mat_{i}",
                "type": "MeshStandardMaterial",
                "color": self._rgb_to_hex(mat.value['color']),
                "roughness": mat.value['roughness'],
                "metalness": mat.value['metallic']
            }
            if 'transparency' in mat.value:
                mat_data['transparent'] = True
                mat_data['opacity'] = 1 - mat.value['transparency']
            
            data["materials"].append(mat_data)
            mat_map[mat] = f"mat_{i}"
        
        # Add meshes
        for name, mesh in self.model.meshes.items():
            geometry_uuid = f"geom_{name}"
            
            # Collect vertices
            vertices = []
            normals = []
            for v in mesh.vertices:
                vertices.extend([v.x, v.y, v.z])
                normals.extend([v.nx, v.ny, v.nz])
            
            # Collect faces (triangulate)
            indices = []
            for face in mesh.faces:
                if len(face.vertices) == 3:
                    indices.extend(face.vertices)
                elif len(face.vertices) == 4:
                    # Split quad into two triangles
                    indices.extend([face.vertices[0], face.vertices[1], face.vertices[2]])
                    indices.extend([face.vertices[0], face.vertices[2], face.vertices[3]])
            
            data["geometries"].append({
                "uuid": geometry_uuid,
                "type": "BufferGeometry",
                "data": {
                    "attributes": {
                        "position": {
                            "itemSize": 3,
                            "type": "Float32Array",
                            "array": vertices
                        },
                        "normal": {
                            "itemSize": 3,
                            "type": "Float32Array",
                            "array": normals
                        }
                    },
                    "index": {
                        "type": "Uint16Array",
                        "array": indices
                    }
                }
            })
            
            data["object"]["children"].append({
                "uuid": f"obj_{name}",
                "name": name,
                "type": "Mesh",
                "geometry": geometry_uuid,
                "material": mat_map.get(mesh.material, "mat_0")
            })
        
        with open(filepath, 'w') as f:
            json.dump(data, f, indent=2)
        
        return filepath
    
    def _rgb_to_hex(self, rgb: Tuple[float, float, float]) -> int:
        """Convert RGB to hex integer"""
        r = int(rgb[0] * 255)
        g = int(rgb[1] * 255)
        b = int(rgb[2] * 255)
        return (r << 16) | (g << 8) | b
    
    def export_all(self, output_dir: str) -> Dict[str, str]:
        """
        Export to all supported formats
        
        Args:
            output_dir: Output directory
            
        Returns:
            Dictionary of format -> filepath
        """
        import os
        os.makedirs(output_dir, exist_ok=True)
        
        base_name = self.model.bridge.name.replace(' ', '_')
        
        results = {}
        
        # OBJ (with MTL)
        results['obj'] = self.export_obj(
            os.path.join(output_dir, f"{base_name}.obj")
        )
        
        # STL (binary)
        results['stl'] = self.export_stl(
            os.path.join(output_dir, f"{base_name}.stl"),
            binary=True
        )
        
        # DXF 3D
        results['dxf'] = self.export_dxf_3d(
            os.path.join(output_dir, f"{base_name}_3D.dxf")
        )
        
        # Three.js JSON
        results['json'] = self.export_threejs_json(
            os.path.join(output_dir, f"{base_name}.json")
        )
        
        return results


class ConstructionSequence3D:
    """
    3D visualization of construction sequence
    """
    
    def __init__(self, bridge_3d: Bridge3DModel, 
                 sequence_engine=None):
        self.model = bridge_3d
        self.sequence = sequence_engine
        self.stage_visibility = {}
        
    def set_construction_stage(self, stage, progress: float = 100):
        """
        Set visibility for construction stage
        
        Args:
            stage: Construction stage
            progress: Progress percentage (0-100)
        """
        self.stage_visibility = {
            'stage': stage,
            'progress': progress,
            'visible_meshes': []
        }
        
        # Determine which meshes should be visible
        # This would integrate with the ConstructionSequenceEngine
        # For now, show all elements up to current stage
        
        if progress >= 0:
            self.stage_visibility['visible_meshes'].extend([
                'piles', 'pile_cap_1', 'pile_cap_2'
            ])
        
        if progress >= 25:
            self.stage_visibility['visible_meshes'].extend([
                'pier_1', 'pier_2', 'abutment_1', 'abutment_2'
            ])
        
        if progress >= 50:
            self.stage_visibility['visible_meshes'].extend([
                'girder_1', 'girder_2', 'girder_3', 'girder_4',
                'cross_girders'
            ])
        
        if progress >= 75:
            self.stage_visibility['visible_meshes'].extend([
                'deck', 'bearings'
            ])
        
        if progress >= 100:
            self.stage_visibility['visible_meshes'].extend([
                'parapets', 'wearing_course'
            ])
    
    def export_stage_model(self, filepath: str) -> str:
        """Export model with stage visibility"""
        # Filter meshes based on visibility
        visible_meshes = {}
        
        for name, mesh in self.model.meshes.items():
            if any(vm in name for vm in self.stage_visibility.get('visible_meshes', [])):
                visible_meshes[name] = mesh
        
        # Create temporary model with only visible meshes
        temp_model = Bridge3DModel(self.model.bridge)
        temp_model.meshes = visible_meshes
        
        # Export
        exporter = ModelExporter(temp_model)
        return exporter.export_obj(filepath)


# INTEGRATION AND USAGE
def create_3d_bridge_model(bridge_model, 
                           include_foundation: bool = True,
                           include_reinforcement: bool = False) -> Bridge3DModel:
    """
    Create complete 3D model of bridge
    
    Args:
        bridge_model: Bridge model from previous modules
        include_foundation: Include piles and pile caps
        include_reinforcement: Show reinforcement bars
        
    Returns:
        Bridge3DModel instance
    """
    model = Bridge3DModel(bridge_model)
    model.generate_full_model(include_foundation, include_reinforcement)
    return model


def export_3d_model(model_3d: Bridge3DModel, 
                   output_dir: str = "./3d_models/") -> Dict[str, str]:
    """
    Export 3D model to all formats
    
    Args:
        model_3d: Bridge 3D model
        output_dir: Output directory
        
    Returns:
        Dictionary of exported file paths
    """
    exporter = ModelExporter(model_3d)
    return exporter.export_all(output_dir)


# USAGE EXAMPLE
if __name__ == "__main__":
    from skew_bridge_engine import create_skew_bridge
    
    # Create bridge
    bridge = create_skew_bridge(
        name="Chambakkara Bridge",
        chainage=600.0,
        span_lengths=[25.0, 30.0, 25.0],
        deck_width=12.0,
        skew_angle=15.0
    )
    
    # Generate 3D model
    model_3d = create_3d_bridge_model(
        bridge,
        include_foundation=True,
        include_reinforcement=False
    )
    
    print(f"Generated {len(model_3d.meshes)} mesh components:")
    for name in model_3d.meshes.keys():
        print(f"  - {name}")
    
    # Export to all formats
    files = export_3d_model(model_3d, "./3d_output/")
    
    print("\nExported files:")
    for fmt, path in files.items():
        print(f"  {fmt.upper()}: {path}")
```

This **3D Visualization Module** provides complete 3D bridge modeling with:

## Features

| Feature | Description |
|---------|-------------|
| **Full 3D Geometry** | Deck, girders, piers, abutments, piles, bearings, parapets |
| **Material System** | Concrete, steel, asphalt, elastomer with realistic properties |
| **Skew Support** | Proper 3D skew transformation for angled bridges |
| **I-Girder Profiles** | Detailed girder cross-sections (top/bottom flanges + web) |
| **Foundation Elements** | Pile groups and pile caps |
| **Reinforcement** | Optional rebar visualization |

## Output Formats

| Format | Use Case | Features |
|--------|----------|----------|
| **OBJ** | General 3D, Blender, Maya | Materials, normals, MTL file |
| **STL** | 3D Printing, CNC | Binary/ASCII, manifold meshes |
| **DXF 3D** | AutoCAD, Civil 3D | Layers by material, 3DFACE entities |
| **Three.js JSON** | Web viewing, Three.js | PBR materials, scene hierarchy |

## Mesh Components Generated

```
✓ deck - Deck slab with thickness
✓ girder_1, girder_2, ... - Main girders (I-section)
✓ cross_girders - Transverse diaphragms
✓ pier_1, pier_2, ... - Pier shafts and caps
✓ abutment_1, abutment_2 - Abutment walls with wing walls
✓ pile_cap_1, ... - Pile caps
✓ piles - Bored piles
✓ bearings - Elastomeric bearing pads
✓ parapets - Crash barriers both sides
✓ wearing_course - Asphalt surface
✓ reinforcement - Rebar (optional)
```

```python
# Create construction sequence 3D
sequence_3d = ConstructionSequence3D(model_3d, sequence_engine)

# Export specific stage
sequence_3d.set_construction_stage(ConstructionStage.GIRDER_ERECTION, 50)
sequence_3d.export_stage_model("stage_girders_50.obj")
```
