```python
"""
DRAWING REGISTER MODULE
Automatic drawing list, revision control, and document management
"""

import json
import hashlib
from dataclasses import dataclass, field, asdict
from typing import List, Dict, Optional, Set, Tuple
from datetime import datetime, date
from enum import Enum, auto
from pathlib import Path
import re
import csv
import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET


class DrawingStatus(Enum):
    """Drawing lifecycle statuses"""
    PRELIMINARY = "PRELIMINARY"
    FOR_REVIEW = "FOR_REVIEW"
    FOR_APPROVAL = "FOR_APPROVAL"
    APPROVED = "APPROVED"
    CONSTRUCTION = "CONSTRUCTION"
    AS_BUILT = "AS_BUILT"
    SUPERSEDED = "SUPERSEDED"
    VOID = "VOID"


class DrawingType(Enum):
    """Standard drawing types with auto-numbering codes"""
    GENERAL_ARRANGEMENT = ("GA", "General Arrangement")
    PLAN = ("P", "Plan")
    LONGITUDINAL_SECTION = ("LS", "Longitudinal Section")
    CROSS_SECTION = ("CS", "Cross Section")
    FOUNDATION = ("FD", "Foundation Detail")
    PIER = ("PI", "Pier Detail")
    ABUTMENT = ("AB", "Abutment Detail")
    GIRDER = ("GD", "Girder Detail")
    DECK = ("DK", "Deck Detail")
    REINFORCEMENT = ("R", "Reinforcement Detail")
    CONSTRUCTION = ("CT", "Construction Detail")
    TEMPORARY_WORKS = ("TW", "Temporary Works")
    UTILITY = ("U", "Utility Detail")
    LANDSCAPE = ("L", "Landscaping")
    ENVIRONMENTAL = ("E", "Environmental")
    SURVEY = ("S", "Survey")
    GEOTECHNICAL = ("GT", "Geotechnical")
    DRAINAGE = ("D", "Drainage")
    SERVICES = ("SV", "Services")
    SIGNAGE = ("SG", "Signage")
    MARKING = ("M", "Road Marking")
    BILL_OF_QUANTITIES = ("BOQ", "Bill of Quantities")
    SPECIFICATION = ("SP", "Specification")
    REPORT = ("RPT", "Report")
    
    def __init__(self, code: str, description: str):
        self.code = code
        self.description = description


class RevisionStatus(Enum):
    """Status of a specific revision"""
    DRAFT = "Draft"
    ISSUED_FOR_REVIEW = "Issued for Review"
    ISSUED_FOR_APPROVAL = "Issued for Approval"
    APPROVED_WITH_COMMENTS = "Approved with Comments"
    APPROVED = "Approved"
    REJECTED = "Rejected"
    HOLD = "On Hold"


@dataclass
class DrawingRevision:
    """Single revision of a drawing"""
    revision_code: str  # A, B, C, 0, 1, 2, etc.
    date_issued: date
    description: str
    status: RevisionStatus
    issued_by: str
    reviewed_by: Optional[str] = None
    approved_by: Optional[str] = None
    review_date: Optional[date] = None
    approval_date: Optional[date] = None
    comments: str = ""
    file_path: Optional[str] = None
    file_hash: Optional[str] = None  # For integrity checking
    
    def __post_init__(self):
        if isinstance(self.date_issued, str):
            self.date_issued = date.fromisoformat(self.date_issued)
        if isinstance(self.review_date, str) and self.review_date:
            self.review_date = date.fromisoformat(self.review_date)
        if isinstance(self.approval_date, str) and self.approval_date:
            self.approval_date = date.fromisoformat(self.approval_date)
    
    def calculate_hash(self, file_path: str) -> str:
        """Calculate file hash for revision control"""
        if Path(file_path).exists():
            with open(file_path, 'rb') as f:
                self.file_hash = hashlib.sha256(f.read()).hexdigest()[:16]
            return self.file_hash
        return None


@dataclass
class Drawing:
    """Complete drawing record"""
    # Identification
    drawing_number: str
    project_code: str
    drawing_type: DrawingType
    sequential_number: int
    
    # Content
    title: str
    description: str
    discipline: str  # CIVIL, STRUCTURAL, ARCHITECTURAL, etc.
    scale: str
    sheet_size: str  # A0, A1, A2, A3, A4
    
    # Status
    current_status: DrawingStatus
    current_revision: Optional[str] = None
    
    # Metadata
    created_date: date = field(default_factory=date.today)
    created_by: str = ""
    
    # Revisions
    revisions: List[DrawingRevision] = field(default_factory=list)
    
    # Relationships
    related_drawings: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)  # Drawing numbers
    supersedes: Optional[str] = None  # Drawing this replaces
    superseded_by: Optional[str] = None  # Drawing that replaces this
    
    # Location
    file_path: Optional[str] = None
    storage_location: Optional[str] = None
    
    # Tags
    tags: Set[str] = field(default_factory=set)
    
    def __post_init__(self):
        if isinstance(self.created_date, str):
            self.created_date = date.fromisoformat(self.created_date)
        if isinstance(self.drawing_type, str):
            self.drawing_type = DrawingType[self.drawing_type]
        if isinstance(self.current_status, str):
            self.current_status = DrawingStatus[self.current_status]
    
    @property
    def full_number(self) -> str:
        """Generate full drawing number"""
        return f"{self.project_code}-{self.drawing_type.code}-{self.sequential_number:04d}"
    
    @property
    def latest_revision(self) -> Optional[DrawingRevision]:
        """Get latest revision"""
        if self.revisions:
            return self.revisions[-1]
        return None
    
    def add_revision(self, description: str, issued_by: str,
                    revision_code: Optional[str] = None) -> DrawingRevision:
        """
        Add new revision to drawing
        
        Args:
            description: Revision description
            issued_by: Person issuing revision
            revision_code: Specific code (auto-generated if None)
            
        Returns:
            New DrawingRevision
        """
        # Auto-generate revision code
        if revision_code is None:
            if not self.revisions:
                # First revision
                revision_code = "A" if self.current_status == DrawingStatus.PRELIMINARY else "0"
            else:
                last_rev = self.revisions[-1].revision_code
                if last_rev.isalpha():
                    # Alphabetic progression: A -> B -> C
                    revision_code = chr(ord(last_rev) + 1)
                else:
                    # Numeric progression: 0 -> 1 -> 2
                    revision_code = str(int(last_rev) + 1)
        
        revision = DrawingRevision(
            revision_code=revision_code,
            date_issued=date.today(),
            description=description,
            status=RevisionStatus.DRAFT,
            issued_by=issued_by
        )
        
        self.revisions.append(revision)
        self.current_revision = revision_code
        self.current_status = DrawingStatus.FOR_REVIEW
        
        return revision
    
    def approve_revision(self, approved_by: str, comments: str = "",
                         with_comments: bool = False):
        """Approve current revision"""
        if self.latest_revision:
            rev = self.latest_revision
            rev.approved_by = approved_by
            rev.approval_date = date.today()
            rev.comments = comments
            
            if with_comments:
                rev.status = RevisionStatus.APPROVED_WITH_COMMENTS
            else:
                rev.status = RevisionStatus.APPROVED
            
            self.current_status = DrawingStatus.APPROVED
    
    def reject_revision(self, reason: str):
        """Reject current revision"""
        if self.latest_revision:
            self.latest_revision.status = RevisionStatus.REJECTED
            self.latest_revision.comments = reason
    
    def to_dict(self) -> Dict:
        """Convert to dictionary"""
        return {
            'drawing_number': self.drawing_number,
            'project_code': self.project_code,
            'drawing_type': self.drawing_type.name,
            'sequential_number': self.sequential_number,
            'title': self.title,
            'description': self.description,
            'discipline': self.discipline,
            'scale': self.scale,
            'sheet_size': self.sheet_size,
            'current_status': self.current_status.value,
            'current_revision': self.current_revision,
            'created_date': self.created_date.isoformat(),
            'created_by': self.created_by,
            'revisions': [
                {
                    'revision_code': r.revision_code,
                    'date_issued': r.date_issued.isoformat(),
                    'description': r.description,
                    'status': r.status.value,
                    'issued_by': r.issued_by,
                    'reviewed_by': r.reviewed_by,
                    'approved_by': r.approved_by,
                    'review_date': r.review_date.isoformat() if r.review_date else None,
                    'approval_date': r.approval_date.isoformat() if r.approval_date else None,
                    'comments': r.comments
                }
                for r in self.revisions
            ],
            'related_drawings': self.related_drawings,
            'supersedes': self.supersedes,
            'superseded_by': self.superseded_by,
            'file_path': self.file_path,
            'storage_location': self.storage_location,
            'tags': list(self.tags)
        }


class DrawingNumberingSystem:
    """
    Automatic drawing numbering system
    """
    
    def __init__(self, project_code: str):
        self.project_code = project_code
        self.counters: Dict[DrawingType, int] = {dt: 0 for dt in DrawingType}
        self.used_numbers: Set[str] = set()
    
    def generate_number(self, drawing_type: DrawingType) -> Tuple[str, int]:
        """
        Generate next available drawing number
        
        Args:
            drawing_type: Type of drawing
            
        Returns:
            (full_number, sequential_number)
        """
        self.counters[drawing_type] += 1
        seq_num = self.counters[drawing_type]
        
        full_number = f"{self.project_code}-{drawing_type.code}-{seq_num:04d}"
        
        # Ensure uniqueness
        while full_number in self.used_numbers:
            seq_num += 1
            full_number = f"{self.project_code}-{drawing_type.code}-{seq_num:04d}"
        
        self.used_numbers.add(full_number)
        return full_number, seq_num
    
    def reserve_number(self, drawing_type: DrawingType, 
                       sequential_number: int) -> Optional[str]:
        """
        Reserve specific drawing number
        
        Returns:
            Full number if available, None if taken
        """
        full_number = f"{self.project_code}-{drawing_type.code}-{sequential_number:04d}"
        
        if full_number in self.used_numbers:
            return None
        
        self.used_numbers.add(full_number)
        self.counters[drawing_type] = max(
            self.counters[drawing_type],
            sequential_number
        )
        return full_number


class DrawingRegister:
    """
    Master drawing register with revision control
    """
    
    def __init__(self, project_name: str, project_code: str):
        self.project_name = project_name
        self.project_code = project_code
        self.numbering_system = DrawingNumberingSystem(project_code)
        
        self.drawings: Dict[str, Drawing] = {}  # By drawing number
        self.title_block_template: Optional[Dict] = None
        
        # Statistics
        self.stats = {
            'total_drawings': 0,
            'by_status': {},
            'by_type': {},
            'by_discipline': {}
        }
        
        # Transmittals
        self.transmittals: List[Dict] = []
        
        # Audit trail
        self.audit_log: List[Dict] = []
    
    def create_drawing(self, title: str, drawing_type: DrawingType,
                      description: str = "", discipline: str = "CIVIL",
                      scale: str = "1:100", sheet_size: str = "A1",
                      created_by: str = "") -> Drawing:
        """
        Create new drawing with auto-generated number
        
        Args:
            title: Drawing title
            drawing_type: Type of drawing
            description: Detailed description
            discipline: Engineering discipline
            scale: Drawing scale
            sheet_size: Sheet size (A0-A4)
            created_by: Creator name
            
        Returns:
            New Drawing instance
        """
        full_number, seq_num = self.numbering_system.generate_number(drawing_type)
        
        drawing = Drawing(
            drawing_number=full_number,
            project_code=self.project_code,
            drawing_type=drawing_type,
            sequential_number=seq_num,
            title=title,
            description=description,
            discipline=discipline,
            scale=scale,
            sheet_size=sheet_size,
            current_status=DrawingStatus.PRELIMINARY,
            created_by=created_by
        )
        
        self.drawings[full_number] = drawing
        self._update_stats()
        self._log_action("CREATE", full_number, f"Created drawing: {title}")
        
        return drawing
    
    def get_drawing(self, drawing_number: str) -> Optional[Drawing]:
        """Get drawing by number"""
        return self.drawings.get(drawing_number)
    
    def search_drawings(self, query: str = None, 
                       drawing_type: DrawingType = None,
                       status: DrawingStatus = None,
                       discipline: str = None,
                       tags: List[str] = None) -> List[Drawing]:
        """
        Search drawings by criteria
        
        Returns:
            List of matching drawings
        """
        results = []
        
        for drawing in self.drawings.values():
            match = True
            
            if query:
                query_lower = query.lower()
                if (query_lower not in drawing.title.lower() and
                    query_lower not in drawing.description.lower() and
                    query_lower not in drawing.drawing_number.lower()):
                    match = False
            
            if drawing_type and drawing.drawing_type != drawing_type:
                match = False
            
            if status and drawing.current_status != status:
                match = False
            
            if discipline and drawing.discipline != discipline:
                match = False
            
            if tags and not all(tag in drawing.tags for tag in tags):
                match = False
            
            if match:
                results.append(drawing)
        
        return results
    
    def revise_drawing(self, drawing_number: str, description: str,
                      issued_by: str, revision_code: Optional[str] = None) -> Optional[DrawingRevision]:
        """
        Create new revision of existing drawing
        
        Args:
            drawing_number: Drawing number
            description: Revision description
            issued_by: Person creating revision
            revision_code: Optional specific revision code
            
        Returns:
            New revision or None if drawing not found
        """
        drawing = self.drawings.get(drawing_number)
        if not drawing:
            return None
        
        revision = drawing.add_revision(description, issued_by, revision_code)
        self._update_stats()
        self._log_action("REVISE", drawing_number, 
                        f"Revision {revision.revision_code}: {description}")
        
        return revision
    
    def approve_drawing(self, drawing_number: str, approved_by: str,
                       comments: str = "", with_comments: bool = False) -> bool:
        """Approve current revision"""
        drawing = self.drawings.get(drawing_number)
        if not drawing:
            return False
        
        drawing.approve_revision(approved_by, comments, with_comments)
        self._update_stats()
        self._log_action("APPROVE", drawing_number, 
                       f"Approved by {approved_by}")
        
        return True
    
    def supersede_drawing(self, old_number: str, new_number: str):
        """Mark drawing as superseded by another"""
        old_drawing = self.drawings.get(old_number)
        new_drawing = self.drawings.get(new_number)
        
        if old_drawing and new_drawing:
            old_drawing.current_status = DrawingStatus.SUPERSEDED
            old_drawing.superseded_by = new_number
            new_drawing.supersedes = old_number
            self._log_action("SUPERSEDE", old_number, f"Superseded by {new_number}")
    
    def create_transmittal(self, drawing_numbers: List[str],
                          recipient: str, purpose: str,
                          sent_by: str) -> Dict:
        """
        Create drawing transmittal
        
        Args:
            drawing_numbers: List of drawings to transmit
            recipient: Recipient organization/person
            purpose: Purpose of transmittal
            sent_by: Sender name
            
        Returns:
            Transmittal record
        """
        transmittal = {
            'id': f"T-{len(self.transmittals) + 1:04d}",
            'date': date.today().isoformat(),
            'recipient': recipient,
            'purpose': purpose,
            'sent_by': sent_by,
            'drawings': []
        }
        
        for num in drawing_numbers:
            drawing = self.drawings.get(num)
            if drawing:
                transmittal['drawings'].append({
                    'number': num,
                    'title': drawing.title,
                    'revision': drawing.current_revision,
                    'status': drawing.current_status.value
                })
        
        self.transmittals.append(transmittal)
        self._log_action("TRANSMIT", ", ".join(drawing_numbers),
                        f"Transmittal {transmittal['id']} to {recipient}")
        
        return transmittal
    
    def generate_drawing_list(self, format: str = "csv",
                             output_path: Optional[str] = None,
                             filter_status: Optional[DrawingStatus] = None) -> str:
        """
        Generate drawing list/register
        
        Args:
            format: Output format (csv, excel, pdf, json, xml)
            output_path: Output file path
            filter_status: Filter by status
            
        Returns:
            Path to generated file
        """
        drawings = list(self.drawings.values())
        if filter_status:
            drawings = [d for d in drawings if d.current_status == filter_status]
        
        # Sort by drawing number
        drawings.sort(key=lambda d: d.drawing_number)
        
        if format.lower() == "csv":
            return self._export_csv(drawings, output_path)
        elif format.lower() == "json":
            return self._export_json(drawings, output_path)
        elif format.lower() == "xml":
            return self._export_xml(drawings, output_path)
        elif format.lower() == "excel":
            return self._export_excel(drawings, output_path)
        elif format.lower() == "html":
            return self._export_html(drawings, output_path)
        else:
            raise ValueError(f"Unsupported format: {format}")
    
    def _export_csv(self, drawings: List[Drawing], output_path: Optional[str]) -> str:
        """Export to CSV"""
        if not output_path:
            output_path = f"{self.project_code}_Drawing_Register.csv"
        
        with open(output_path, 'w', newline='') as f:
            writer = csv.writer(f)
            
            # Header
            writer.writerow([
                'Drawing Number', 'Type', 'Title', 'Description',
                'Discipline', 'Scale', 'Sheet Size', 'Status',
                'Current Revision', 'Date Created', 'Created By',
                'Latest Revision Date', 'Latest Revision Description'
            ])
            
            # Data
            for d in drawings:
                latest = d.latest_revision
                writer.writerow([
                    d.drawing_number,
                    d.drawing_type.value[1],
                    d.title,
                    d.description,
                    d.discipline,
                    d.scale,
                    d.sheet_size,
                    d.current_status.value,
                    d.current_revision or '-',
                    d.created_date.isoformat(),
                    d.created_by,
                    latest.date_issued.isoformat() if latest else '',
                    latest.description if latest else ''
                ])
        
        return output_path
    
    def _export_json(self, drawings: List[Drawing], output_path: Optional[str]) -> str:
        """Export to JSON"""
        if not output_path:
            output_path = f"{self.project_code}_Drawing_Register.json"
        
        data = {
            'project_name': self.project_name,
            'project_code': self.project_code,
            'export_date': date.today().isoformat(),
            'total_drawings': len(drawings),
            'drawings': [d.to_dict() for d in drawings]
        }
        
        with open(output_path, 'w') as f:
            json.dump(data, f, indent=2)
        
        return output_path
    
    def _export_xml(self, drawings: List[Drawing], output_path: Optional[str]) -> str:
        """Export to XML"""
        if not output_path:
            output_path = f"{self.project_code}_Drawing_Register.xml"
        
        root = ET.Element('DrawingRegister')
        root.set('project', self.project_name)
        root.set('date', date.today().isoformat())
        
        for d in drawings:
            d_elem = ET.SubElement(root, 'Drawing')
            d_elem.set('number', d.drawing_number)
            
            ET.SubElement(d_elem, 'Title').text = d.title
            ET.SubElement(d_elem, 'Type').text = d.drawing_type.name
            ET.SubElement(d_elem, 'Status').text = d.current_status.value
            ET.SubElement(d_elem, 'Revision').text = d.current_revision or '-'
            ET.SubElement(d_elem, 'Scale').text = d.scale
            ET.SubElement(d_elem, 'Discipline').text = d.discipline
            
            revs = ET.SubElement(d_elem, 'Revisions')
            for r in d.revisions:
                r_elem = ET.SubElement(revs, 'Revision')
                r_elem.set('code', r.revision_code)
                ET.SubElement(r_elem, 'Date').text = r.date_issued.isoformat()
                ET.SubElement(r_elem, 'Description').text = r.description
        
        tree = ET.ElementTree(root)
        tree.write(output_path, encoding='utf-8', xml_declaration=True)
        
        return output_path
    
    def _export_excel(self, drawings: List[Drawing], output_path: Optional[str]) -> str:
        """Export to Excel (requires openpyxl)"""
        try:
            from openpyxl import Workbook
            from openpyxl.styles import Font, PatternFill, Alignment, Border, Side
        except ImportError:
            # Fall back to CSV
            return self._export_csv(drawings, output_path.replace('.xlsx', '.csv') if output_path else None)
        
        if not output_path:
            output_path = f"{self.project_code}_Drawing_Register.xlsx"
        
        wb = Workbook()
        ws = wb.active
        ws.title = "Drawing Register"
        
        # Styles
        header_font = Font(bold=True, color="FFFFFF")
        header_fill = PatternFill(start_color="366092", end_color="366092", fill_type="solid")
        thin_border = Border(
            left=Side(style='thin'),
            right=Side(style='thin'),
            top=Side(style='thin'),
            bottom=Side(style='thin')
        )
        
        # Headers
        headers = [
            'Drawing Number', 'Type', 'Title', 'Description',
            'Discipline', 'Scale', 'Sheet', 'Status',
            'Revision', 'Created', 'Revisions'
        ]
        
        for col, header in enumerate(headers, 1):
            cell = ws.cell(row=1, column=col, value=header)
            cell.font = header_font
            cell.fill = header_fill
            cell.alignment = Alignment(horizontal='center', vertical='center')
            cell.border = thin_border
        
        # Data
        status_colors = {
            DrawingStatus.APPROVED: "C6EFCE",
            DrawingStatus.FOR_REVIEW: "FFEB9C",
            DrawingStatus.CONSTRUCTION: "B8CCE4",
            DrawingStatus.SUPERSEDED: "D9D9D9",
            DrawingStatus.VOID: "FF6B6B"
        }
        
        for row, d in enumerate(drawings, 2):
            data = [
                d.drawing_number,
                d.drawing_type.value[1],
                d.title,
                d.description,
                d.discipline,
                d.scale,
                d.sheet_size,
                d.current_status.value,
                d.current_revision or '-',
                d.created_date.isoformat(),
                len(d.revisions)
            ]
            
            for col, value in enumerate(data, 1):
                cell = ws.cell(row=row, column=col, value=value)
                cell.border = thin_border
                
                # Color by status
                if col == 8:  # Status column
                    color = status_colors.get(d.current_status, "FFFFFF")
                    cell.fill = PatternFill(start_color=color, end_color=color, fill_type="solid")
        
        # Adjust column widths
        ws.column_dimensions['A'].width = 20
        ws.column_dimensions['B'].width = 15
        ws.column_dimensions['C'].width = 40
        ws.column_dimensions['D'].width = 50
        
        wb.save(output_path)
        return output_path
    
    def _export_html(self, drawings: List[Drawing], output_path: Optional[str]) -> str:
        """Export to HTML report"""
        if not output_path:
            output_path = f"{self.project_code}_Drawing_Register.html"
        
        html = f"""<!DOCTYPE html>
<html>
<head>
    <title>{self.project_name} - Drawing Register</title>
    <style>
        body {{ font-family: Arial, sans-serif; margin: 20px; }}
        h1 {{ color: #366092; }}
        table {{ border-collapse: collapse; width: 100%; margin-top: 20px; }}
        th {{ background-color: #366092; color: white; padding: 10px; text-align: left; }}
        td {{ border: 1px solid #ddd; padding: 8px; }}
        tr:nth-child(even) {{ background-color: #f2f2f2; }}
        .status-approved {{ background-color: #c6efce; }}
        .status-review {{ background-color: #ffeb9c; }}
        .status-superseded {{ background-color: #d9d9d9; text-decoration: line-through; }}
        .revision-cell {{ text-align: center; font-weight: bold; }}
    </style>
</head>
<body>
    <h1>{self.project_name}</h1>
    <h2>Drawing Register</h2>
    <p>Project Code: {self.project_code}</p>
    <p>Generated: {date.today().isoformat()}</p>
    <p>Total Drawings: {len(drawings)}</p>
    
    <table>
        <tr>
            <th>Drawing No.</th>
            <th>Type</th>
            <th>Title</th>
            <th>Status</th>
            <th>Rev</th>
            <th>Scale</th>
            <th>Created</th>
        </tr>
"""
        
        for d in drawings:
            status_class = ""
            if d.current_status == DrawingStatus.APPROVED:
                status_class = "status-approved"
            elif d.current_status == DrawingStatus.FOR_REVIEW:
                status_class = "status-review"
            elif d.current_status == DrawingStatus.SUPERSEDED:
                status_class = "status-superseded"
            
            html += f"""
        <tr class="{status_class}">
            <td>{d.drawing_number}</td>
            <td>{d.drawing_type.value[1]}</td>
            <td>{d.title}</td>
            <td>{d.current_status.value}</td>
            <td class="revision-cell">{d.current_revision or '-'}</td>
            <td>{d.scale}</td>
            <td>{d.created_date.isoformat()}</td>
        </tr>"""
        
        html += """
    </table>
</body>
</html>"""
        
        with open(output_path, 'w') as f:
            f.write(html)
        
        return output_path
    
    def generate_revision_history(self, drawing_number: str,
                                   output_path: Optional[str] = None) -> str:
        """
        Generate revision history report for specific drawing
        
        Returns:
            Path to generated report
        """
        drawing = self.drawings.get(drawing_number)
        if not drawing:
            raise ValueError(f"Drawing not found: {drawing_number}")
        
        if not output_path:
            output_path = f"{drawing_number}_Revision_History.txt"
        
        lines = [
            "=" * 70,
            f"REVISION HISTORY",
            f"Drawing: {drawing_number}",
            f"Title: {drawing.title}",
            f"Project: {self.project_name}",
            "=" * 70,
            ""
        ]
        
        for rev in drawing.revisions:
            lines.extend([
                f"Revision {rev.revision_code}",
                f"  Date Issued: {rev.date_issued}",
                f"  Description: {rev.description}",
                f"  Status: {rev.status.value}",
                f"  Issued By: {rev.issued_by}",
                f"  Reviewed By: {rev.reviewed_by or '-'}",
                f"  Approved By: {rev.approved_by or '-'}",
                f"  Comments: {rev.comments or '-'}",
                "-" * 50,
                ""
            ])
        
        with open(output_path, 'w') as f:
            f.write('\n'.join(lines))
        
        return output_path
    
    def _update_stats(self):
        """Update statistics"""
        self.stats['total_drawings'] = len(self.drawings)
        self.stats['by_status'] = {}
        self.stats['by_type'] = {}
        self.stats['by_discipline'] = {}
        
        for d in self.drawings.values():
            # By status
            status = d.current_status.value
            self.stats['by_status'][status] = self.stats['by_status'].get(status, 0) + 1
            
            # By type
            dtype = d.drawing_type.value[1]
            self.stats['by_type'][dtype] = self.stats['by_type'].get(dtype, 0) + 1
            
            # By discipline
            disc = d.discipline
            self.stats['by_discipline'][disc] = self.stats['by_discipline'].get(disc, 0) + 1
    
    def _log_action(self, action: str, drawing: str, details: str):
        """Add to audit log"""
        self.audit_log.append({
            'timestamp': datetime.now().isoformat(),
            'action': action,
            'drawing': drawing,
            'details': details
        })
    
    def save_register(self, filepath: str):
        """Save complete register to file"""
        data = {
            'project_name': self.project_name,
            'project_code': self.project_code,
            'counters': {k.name: v for k, v in self.numbering_system.counters.items()},
            'drawings': {k: v.to_dict() for k, v in self.drawings.items()},
            'transmittals': self.transmittals,
            'audit_log': self.audit_log,
            'saved_date': datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
        
        with open(filepath, 'w') as f:
            json.dump(data, f, indent=2)
    
    @classmethod
    def load_register(cls, filepath: str) -> 'DrawingRegister':
        """Load register from file"""
        with open(filepath, 'r') as f:
            data = json.load(f)
        
        register = cls(data['project_name'], data['project_code'])
        
        # Restore counters
        for type_name, count in data.get('counters', {}).items():
            try:
                dt = DrawingType[type_name]
                register.numbering_system.counters[dt] = count
            except KeyError:
                pass
        
        # Restore drawings
        for num, d_data in data.get('drawings', {}).items():
            drawing = Drawing(**d_data)
            register.drawings[num] = drawing
        
        # Restore transmittals and log
        register.transmittals = data.get('transmittals', [])
        register.audit_log = data.get('audit_log', [])
        
        register._update_stats()
        return register


class TitleBlockGenerator:
    """
    Generate title blocks for drawings
    """
    
    def __init__(self, register: DrawingRegister):
        self.register = register
    
    def generate_title_block_data(self, drawing_number: str) -> Dict:
        """
        Generate data for drawing title block
        
        Returns:
            Dictionary with all title block fields
        """
        drawing = self.register.get_drawing(drawing_number)
        if not drawing:
            return {}
        
        return {
            'project_name': self.register.project_name,
            'project_code': self.register.project_code,
            'drawing_number': drawing.drawing_number,
            'drawing_title': drawing.title,
            'description': drawing.description,
            'discipline': drawing.discipline,
            'scale': drawing.scale,
            'sheet_size': drawing.sheet_size,
            'revision': drawing.current_revision or '-',
            'status': drawing.current_status.value,
            'date': date.today().isoformat(),
            'drawn_by': drawing.created_by,
            'checked_by': drawing.latest_revision.reviewed_by if drawing.latest_revision else '',
            'approved_by': drawing.latest_revision.approved_by if drawing.latest_revision else '',
            'related_drawings': ', '.join(drawing.related_drawings)
        }
    
    def generate_dxf_title_block(self, drawing_number: str, 
                                  output_path: str) -> str:
        """
        Generate title block as DXF for insertion into drawings
        
        Returns:
            Path to DXF file
        """
        import ezdxf
        from ezdxf.enums import TextEntityAlignment
        
        data = self.generate_title_block_data(drawing_number)
        if not data:
            return None
        
        doc = ezdxf.new('R2018')
        msp = doc.modelspace()
        
        # Title block dimensions (A1 size reference)
        # Coordinates in mm
        x_start = 0
        y_start = 0
        width = 841  # A1 width
        height = 594  # A1 height
        
        # Draw border
        msp.add_lwpolyline([
            (x_start, y_start),
            (x_start + width, y_start),
            (x_start + width, y_start + height),
            (x_start, y_start + height),
            (x_start, y_start)
        ], close=True)
        
        # Title block area (bottom right)
        tb_height = 60
        tb_width = 250
        tb_x = x_start + width - tb_width
        tb_y = y_start
        
        # Title block border
        msp.add_lwpolyline([
            (tb_x, tb_y),
            (tb_x + tb_width, tb_y),
            (tb_x + tb_width, tb_y + tb_height),
            (tb_x, tb_y + tb_height),
            (tb_x, tb_y)
        ], close=True)
        
        # Add text fields
        text_height = 3.5
        
        # Project info
        msp.add_text(f"PROJECT: {data['project_name']}", 
                    height=text_height).set_placement(
            (tb_x + 5, tb_y + 50))
        
        msp.add_text(f"CODE: {data['project_code']}", 
                    height=text_height).set_placement(
            (tb_x + 5, tb_y + 45))
        
        # Drawing info
        msp.add_text(f"DRAWING: {data['drawing_number']}", 
                    height=text_height).set_placement(
            (tb_x + 5, tb_y + 35))
        
        msp.add_text(f"TITLE: {data['drawing_title']}", 
                    height=text_height).set_placement(
            (tb_x + 5, tb_y + 30))
        
        msp.add_text(f"SCALE: {data['scale']}", 
                    height=text_height).set_placement(
            (tb_x + 5, tb_y + 25))
        
        # Revision box
        msp.add_lwpolyline([
            (tb_x + tb_width - 40, tb_y),
            (tb_x + tb_width - 40, tb_y + 25),
            (tb_x + tb_width, tb_y + 25),
            (tb_x + tb_width, tb_y)
        ])
        
        msp.add_text("REV", height=text_height).set_placement(
            (tb_x + tb_width - 35, tb_y + 18))
        msp.add_text(data['revision'], height=5).set_placement(
            (tb_x + tb_width - 20, tb_y + 10), 
            align=TextEntityAlignment.MIDDLE_CENTER)
        
        # Approval block
        approval_y = tb_y + 5
        msp.add_text(f"Drawn: {data['drawn_by'][:15]}", 
                    height=2.5).set_placement((tb_x + 5, approval_y))
        msp.add_text(f"Checked: {data['checked_by'][:15]}", 
                    height=2.5).set_placement((tb_x + 85, approval_y))
        msp.add_text(f"Approved: {data['approved_by'][:15]}", 
                    height=2.5).set_placement((tb_x + 165, approval_y))
        
        doc.saveas(output_path)
        return output_path


# USAGE EXAMPLE AND INTEGRATION
def create_bridge_drawing_register(project_name: str = "Chambakkara Bridge",
                                    project_code: str = "CHB-2024") -> DrawingRegister:
    """
    Create complete drawing register for bridge project
    
    Returns:
        DrawingRegister instance
    """
    register = DrawingRegister(project_name, project_code)
    
    # Create drawings for all bridge components
    
    # General Arrangement
    ga = register.create_drawing(
        title="GENERAL ARRANGEMENT",
        drawing_type=DrawingType.GENERAL_ARRANGEMENT,
        description="Overall bridge layout showing spans, piers, and abutments",
        discipline="CIVIL",
        scale="1:500",
        sheet_size="A0",
        created_by="AutoCAD Generator"
    )
    ga.tags = {"key", "overview", "layout"}
    
    # Plan Views
    plan = register.create_drawing(
        title="PLAN AT DECK LEVEL",
        drawing_type=DrawingType.PLAN,
        description="Plan view showing deck layout, carriageway, and footpaths",
        discipline="CIVIL",
        scale="1:100",
        sheet_size="A1"
    )
    ga.related_drawings.append(plan.drawing_number)
    plan.related_drawings.append(ga.drawing_number)
    
    # Longitudinal Section
    ls = register.create_drawing(
        title="LONGITUDINAL SECTION",
        drawing_type=DrawingType.LONGITUDINAL_SECTION,
        description="Section through bridge centerline showing profile and levels",
        discipline="CIVIL",
        scale="1:100",
        sheet_size="A1"
    )
    
    # Cross Sections
    for i in range(1, 5):  # 4 cross sections
        cs = register.create_drawing(
            title=f"CROSS SECTION AT PIER P-{i}",
            drawing_type=DrawingType.CROSS_SECTION,
            description=f"Cross section showing deck, girders, and pier at P-{i}",
            discipline="STRUCTURAL",
            scale="1:50",
            sheet_size="A1"
        )
        ls.related_drawings.append(cs.drawing_number)
    
    # Foundation Details
    for i in range(1, 5):
        fd = register.create_drawing(
            title=f"FOUNDATION DETAIL - PIER P-{i}",
            drawing_type=DrawingType.FOUNDATION,
            description=f"Pile layout and pile cap details for Pier P-{i}",
            discipline="STRUCTURAL",
            scale="1:50",
            sheet_size="A1"
        )
    
    # Pier Details
    for i in range(1, 3):
        pd = register.create_drawing(
            title=f"PIER P-{i} DETAILS",
            drawing_type=DrawingType.PIER,
            description=f"Reinforcement and concrete details for Pier P-{i}",
            discipline="STRUCTURAL",
            scale="1:50",
            sheet_size="A1"
        )
    
    # Abutment Details
    for side in ["LEFT", "RIGHT"]:
        ab = register.create_drawing(
            title=f"ABUTMENT - {side} DETAILS",
            drawing_type=DrawingType.ABUTMENT,
            description=f"{side} abutment wall and foundation details",
            discipline="STRUCTURAL",
            scale="1:50",
            sheet_size="A1"
        )
    
    # Girder Details
    for i in range(1, 5):
        gd = register.create_drawing(
            title=f"GIRDER G-{i} DETAILS",
            drawing_type=DrawingType.GIRDER,
            description=f"Prestressed concrete girder G-{i} details",
            discipline="STRUCTURAL",
            scale="1:25",
            sheet_size="A0"
        )
    
    # Deck Slab
    dk = register.create_drawing(
        title="DECK SLAB REINFORCEMENT",
        drawing_type=DrawingType.DECK,
        description="Deck slab reinforcement layout and details",
        discipline="STRUCTURAL",
        scale="1:50",
        sheet_size="A1"
    )
    
    # Construction Sequence
    ct = register.create_drawing(
        title="CONSTRUCTION SEQUENCE",
        drawing_type=DrawingType.CONSTRUCTION,
        description="Stage-by-stage construction methodology",
        discipline="CIVIL",
        scale="1:200",
        sheet_size="A0"
    )
    
    # Drainage
    dr = register.create_drawing(
        title="DRAINAGE DETAILS",
        drawing_type=DrawingType.DRAINAGE,
        description="Deck drainage and downpipe details",
        discipline="CIVIL",
        scale="1:50",
        sheet_size="A1"
    )
    
    # Parapet
    pr = register.create_drawing(
        title="PARAPET DETAILS",
        drawing_type=DrawingType.STRUCTURAL,
        description="Crash barrier and parapet details",
        discipline="CIVIL",
        scale="1:10",
        sheet_size="A1"
    )
    
    # Road Marking
    mk = register.create_drawing(
        title="ROAD MARKING LAYOUT",
        drawing_type=DrawingType.MARKING,
        description="Lane markings, pedestrian crossings, and signage",
        discipline="TRAFFIC",
        scale="1:100",
        sheet_size="A1"
    )
    
    # Bill of Quantities
    boq = register.create_drawing(
        title="BILL OF QUANTITIES",
        drawing_type=DrawingType.BILL_OF_QUANTITIES,
        description="Detailed quantities for all bridge components",
        discipline="QUANTITY",
        scale="N/A",
        sheet_size="A4"
    )
    
    # Specifications
    sp = register.create_drawing(
        title="TECHNICAL SPECIFICATIONS",
        drawing_type=DrawingType.SPECIFICATION,
        description="Material and construction specifications",
        discipline="CIVIL",
        scale="N/A",
        sheet_size="A4"
    )
    
    print(f"Created drawing register with {len(register.drawings)} drawings")
    return register


def demonstrate_revision_workflow(register: DrawingRegister):
    """Demonstrate revision control workflow"""
    
    # Get GA drawing
    ga = register.search_drawings(drawing_type=DrawingType.GENERAL_ARRANGEMENT)[0]
    
    print(f"\nRevision Workflow for {ga.drawing_number}:")
    print(f"Initial Status: {ga.current_status.value}")
    
    # Issue first revision
    rev1 = register.revise_drawing(
        ga.drawing_number,
        "Initial issue for review",
        "Design Engineer"
    )
    print(f"Revision {rev1.revision_code}: {rev1.description}")
    print(f"Status: {rev1.status.value}")
    
    # Approve revision
    register.approve_drawing(
        ga.drawing_number,
        "Project Manager",
        "Approved with minor comments",
        with_comments=True
    )
    print(f"Approved by {ga.latest_revision.approved_by}")
    print(f"Current Status: {ga.current_status.value}")
    
    # Issue revision B (revised)
    rev2 = register.revise_drawing(
        ga.drawing_number,
        "Revised: Updated pier locations per site survey",
        "Design Engineer"
    )
    print(f"\nRevision {rev2.revision_code}: {rev2.description}")
    
    # Final approval
    register.approve_drawing(
        ga.drawing_number,
        "Project Manager",
        "Approved for construction"
    )
    print(f"Final Status: {ga.current_status.value}")
    
    # Generate revision history
    history_path = register.generate_revision_history(ga.drawing_number)
    print(f"\nRevision history saved to: {history_path}")


def generate_all_outputs(register: DrawingRegister, output_dir: str = "./drawing_register/"):
    """Generate all register outputs"""
    import os
    os.makedirs(output_dir, exist_ok=True)
    
    # Drawing list in multiple formats
    print("\nGenerating drawing lists...")
    
    csv_path = register.generate_drawing_list("csv", 
                os.path.join(output_dir, "drawing_register.csv"))
    print(f"  CSV: {csv_path}")
    
    excel_path = register.generate_drawing_list("excel",
                os.path.join(output_dir, "drawing_register.xlsx"))
    print(f"  Excel: {excel_path}")
    
    html_path = register.generate_drawing_list("html",
                os.path.join(output_dir, "drawing_register.html"))
    print(f"  HTML: {html_path}")
    
    json_path = register.generate_drawing_list("json",
                os.path.join(output_dir, "drawing_register.json"))
    print(f"  JSON: {json_path}")
    
    # Create transmittal
    all_numbers = list(register.drawings.keys())[:5]  # First 5 drawings
    transmittal = register.create_transmittal(
        all_numbers,
        recipient="Client Representative",
        purpose="Issue for Review",
        sent_by="Project Manager"
    )
    print(f"\nTransmittal {transmittal['id']} created")
    
    # Generate title block
    title_gen = TitleBlockGenerator(register)
    tb_path = title_gen.generate_dxf_title_block(
        all_numbers[0],
        os.path.join(output_dir, "title_block.dxf")
    )
    print(f"Title block: {tb_path}")
    
    # Save register
    register.save_register(os.path.join(output_dir, "register_data.json"))
    print(f"Register data saved")
    
    # Print statistics
    print(f"\nRegister Statistics:")
    for key, value in register.stats.items():
        if isinstance(value, dict):
            print(f"  {key}:")
            for k, v in value.items():
                print(f"    {k}: {v}")
        else:
            print(f"  {key}: {value}")


# MAIN EXECUTION
if __name__ == "__main__":
    # Create register
    register = create_bridge_drawing_register()
    
    # Demonstrate revision workflow
    demonstrate_revision_workflow(register)
    
    # Generate all outputs
    generate_all_outputs(register)
    
    print("\nDrawing Register complete!")
```

This **Drawing Register Module** provides comprehensive document control with:

## Core Features

| Feature | Description |
|---------|-------------|
| **Auto Numbering** | Project-Code-Sequential format (e.g., `CHB-2024-GA-0001`) |
| **22 Drawing Types** | GA, Plans, Sections, Details, BOQ, Specs, etc. |
| **Revision Control** | Alphabetic (A→B→C) or numeric (0→1→2) progression |
| **Status Tracking** | 8 lifecycle states from Preliminary to As-Built |
| **Approval Workflow** | Draft → Review → Approval → Construction |
| **Cross References** | Link related drawings |
| **Supersession** | Track drawing replacements |
| **Audit Trail** | Complete action logging |

## Output Formats

| Format | Use Case |
|--------|----------|
| **Excel** | Sortable, filterable register with color-coded status |
| **CSV** | Import to other systems |
| **HTML** | Web-viewable register with styling |
| **JSON/XML** | Data exchange, BIM integration |
| **Text** | Revision history reports |

## Drawing Types & Codes

| Code | Type | Description |
|------|------|-------------|
| GA | General Arrangement | Overall layout |
| P | Plan | Plan views |
| LS | Longitudinal Section | Profile sections |
| CS | Cross Section | Transverse sections |
| FD | Foundation Detail | Piles, pile caps |
| PI | Pier Detail | Pier construction |
| AB | Abutment Detail | Abutment walls |
| GD | Girder Detail | Girder fabrication |
| DK | Deck Detail | Deck reinforcement |
| R | Reinforcement | Rebar schedules |
| CT | Construction | Methodology |
| TW | Temporary Works | Formwork, scaffolding |
| BOQ | Bill of Quantities | Quantities |
| SP | Specification | Technical specs |

## Revision Workflow

```
PRELIMINARY
    ↓
Revision A (Draft) → Issued for Review
    ↓
[Comments] → Revise → Revision B
    ↓
Approved → CONSTRUCTION
    ↓
As-Built → AS_BUILT
    ↓
Superseded → SUPERSEDED
```

## Integration Example

```python
# Create register
register = DrawingRegister("Chambakkara Bridge", "CHB-2024")

# Auto-create drawing with numbering
drawing = register.create_drawing(
    title="PIER P-1 DETAILS",
    drawing_type=DrawingType.PIER,
    scale="1:50"
)
# → Creates: CHB-2024-PI-0001

# Add revision
register.revise_drawing(
    "CHB-2024-PI-0001",
    "Initial issue",
    "Engineer"
)

# Approve
register.approve_drawing(
    "CHB-2024-PI-0001",
    "Manager",
    "Approved for construction"
)

# Generate register
register.generate_drawing_list("excel", "register.xlsx")
```
