I'll create a comprehensive **Quality Check Module** with design validation, code compliance checking, and automated design rule verification.

```python
"""
QUALITY CHECK MODULE
Design validation and code compliance checking for bridge designs
"""

import math
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import List, Dict, Optional, Tuple, Callable, Union
from enum import Enum, auto
from datetime import datetime
import json
import re


class CheckSeverity(Enum):
    """Severity levels for quality checks"""
    INFO = "INFO"           # Informational only
    PASS = "PASS"           # Check passed
    WARNING = "WARNING"     # Warning - review recommended
    ERROR = "ERROR"         # Error - must be fixed
    CRITICAL = "CRITICAL"   # Critical - design unsafe


class DesignCode(Enum):
    """Supported design codes"""
    IRC_5_2015 = "IRC:5-2015"           # Indian Roads Congress
    IRC_6_2017 = "IRC:6-2017"           # Loads and stresses
    IRC_21_2000 = "IRC:21-2000"         # Cement concrete
    IRC_78_2014 = "IRC:78-2014"         # Foundations
    AASHTO_LRFD = "AASHTO LRFD"         # US standard
    EUROCODE_2 = "EN 1992"               # European concrete
    EUROCODE_1 = "EN 1991"               # European loads
    BS_5400 = "BS 5400"                  # British standard


@dataclass
class CheckResult:
    """Result of a single quality check"""
    check_id: str
    check_name: str
    category: str
    severity: CheckSeverity
    message: str
    details: Dict = field(default_factory=dict)
    recommendation: str = ""
    code_reference: str = ""
    
    def to_dict(self) -> Dict:
        return {
            'check_id': self.check_id,
            'check_name': self.check_name,
            'category': self.category,
            'severity': self.severity.value,
            'message': self.message,
            'details': self.details,
            'recommendation': self.recommendation,
            'code_reference': self.code_reference
        }


@dataclass
class DesignParameter:
    """Design parameter with limits"""
    name: str
    value: float
    unit: str
    min_allowed: Optional[float] = None
    max_allowed: Optional[float] = None
    nominal: Optional[float] = None
    tolerance: Optional[float] = None
    
    def check(self) -> Tuple[bool, Optional[str]]:
        """Check if value is within limits"""
        if self.min_allowed is not None and self.value < self.min_allowed:
            return False, f"Below minimum ({self.min_allowed} {self.unit})"
        if self.max_allowed is not None and self.value > self.max_allowed:
            return False, f"Above maximum ({self.max_allowed} {self.unit})"
        return True, None
    
    def status(self) -> str:
        """Get status string"""
        ok, msg = self.check()
        if ok:
            return "OK"
        return msg


class DesignRule:
    """Base class for design rules"""
    
    def __init__(self, rule_id: str, name: str, description: str,
                 code: DesignCode, clause: str):
        self.rule_id = rule_id
        self.name = name
        self.description = description
        self.code = code
        self.clause = clause
    
    def check(self, bridge_model) -> CheckResult:
        """Perform check - override in subclasses"""
        raise NotImplementedError
    
    def _create_result(self, severity: CheckSeverity, message: str,
                      details: Dict = None, recommendation: str = "") -> CheckResult:
        """Create check result"""
        return CheckResult(
            check_id=self.rule_id,
            check_name=self.name,
            category=self.code.value,
            severity=severity,
            message=message,
            details=details or {},
            recommendation=recommendation,
            code_reference=f"{self.code.value} - {self.clause}"
        )


# GEOMETRIC RULES

class SpanLengthRule(DesignRule):
    """Check span length limits"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__(
            "GEO-001",
            "Span Length Validation",
            "Check span lengths are within practical limits",
            DesignCode.IRC_5_2015,
            "Clause 4.2"
        )
    
    def check(self, bridge) -> CheckResult:
        spans = bridge.spans
        issues = []
        
        for i, span in enumerate(spans):
            length = span.length
            
            # Check minimum span
            if length < 6.0:
                issues.append(f"Span {i+1}: {length}m below minimum 6m")
            
            # Check maximum span for simple beam
            if length > 50.0 and not hasattr(bridge, 'continuous'):
                issues.append(f"Span {i+1}: {length}m exceeds simple span limit")
            
            # Check span ratio for adjacent spans
            if i > 0:
                prev_length = spans[i-1].length
                ratio = max(length, prev_length) / min(length, prev_length)
                if ratio > 1.5:
                    issues.append(f"Spans {i} and {i+1}: Ratio {ratio:.2f} > 1.5")
        
        if issues:
            return self._create_result(
                CheckSeverity.ERROR if any("exceeds" in i for i in issues) else CheckSeverity.WARNING,
                "Span length issues found",
                {'issues': issues},
                "Consider adjusting span lengths or using continuous construction"
            )
        
        return self._create_result(
            CheckSeverity.PASS,
            f"All {len(spans)} spans within acceptable limits"
        )


class SkewAngleRule(DesignRule):
    """Check skew angle limits"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__(
            "GEO-002",
            "Skew Angle Validation",
            "Check skew angle is within practical limits",
            DesignCode.IRC_5_2015,
            "Clause 5.3"
        )
    
    def check(self, bridge) -> CheckResult:
        skew = abs(bridge.skew_angle)
        
        if skew > 45:
            return self._create_result(
                CheckSeverity.ERROR,
                f"Skew angle {skew}° exceeds maximum 45°",
                {'skew_angle': skew, 'max_allowed': 45},
                "Consider realignment or use special skew bridge design"
            )
        elif skew > 30:
            return self._create_result(
                CheckSeverity.WARNING,
                f"Skew angle {skew}° is high - requires special attention",
                {'skew_angle': skew, 'recommended_max': 30},
                "Consider reducing skew angle if possible"
            )
        
        return self._create_result(
            CheckSeverity.PASS,
            f"Skew angle {skew}° is acceptable"
        )


class DeckWidthRule(DesignRule):
    """Check deck width requirements"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__(
            "GEO-003",
            "Deck Width Validation",
            "Check deck width meets traffic requirements",
            DesignCode.IRC_5_2015,
            "Clause 6.1"
        )
    
    def check(self, bridge) -> CheckResult:
        width = bridge.deck.square_width
        
        # Minimum for 2-lane bridge
        min_width = 7.5  # 2 x 3.5m lanes + 0.5m shoulders
        
        if width < min_width:
            return self._create_result(
                CheckSeverity.ERROR,
                f"Deck width {width}m insufficient for 2-lane traffic",
                {'width': width, 'required': min_width},
                f"Increase deck width to minimum {min_width}m"
            )
        
        # Check for footpaths
        carriageway = bridge.deck.carriageway_width
        if width > carriageway + 1.0:  # At least 0.5m each side
            return self._create_result(
                CheckSeverity.PASS,
                f"Deck width {width}m adequate with {width - carriageway:.1f}m for footpaths"
            )
        
        return self._create_result(
            CheckSeverity.INFO,
            f"Deck width {width}m is acceptable"
        )


class VerticalClearanceRule(DesignRule):
    """Check vertical clearance"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__(
            "GEO-004",
            "Vertical Clearance",
            "Check minimum vertical clearance",
            DesignCode.IRC_5_2015,
            "Clause 7.1"
        )
    
    def check(self, bridge) -> CheckResult:
        # Minimum clearance over road/rail/water
        min_clearance = 5.5  # Standard for roads
        
        # Check if HFL defined
        if hasattr(bridge, 'hfl') and bridge.hfl:
            soffit = bridge.deck.soffit_level
            clearance = soffit - bridge.hfl
            
            if clearance < min_clearance:
                return self._create_result(
                    CheckSeverity.ERROR,
                    f"Vertical clearance {clearance:.2f}m below minimum {min_clearance}m",
                    {'clearance': clearance, 'hfl': bridge.hfl, 'soffit': soffit},
                    "Raise deck level or provide flood relief"
                )
            
            return self._create_result(
                CheckSeverity.PASS,
                f"Vertical clearance {clearance:.2f}m above HFL is adequate"
            )
        
        return self._create_result(
            CheckSeverity.INFO,
            "HFL not defined - vertical clearance cannot be verified"
        )


# STRUCTURAL RULES

class SpanDepthRatioRule(DesignRule):
    """Check span-to-depth ratio"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__(
            "STR-001",
            "Span-to-Depth Ratio",
            "Check girder span-to-depth ratio",
            DesignCode.IRC_21_2000,
            "Clause 4.3"
        )
    
    def check(self, bridge) -> CheckResult:
        if not hasattr(bridge, 'girder_depth'):
            return self._create_result(
                CheckSeverity.INFO,
                "Girder depth not defined - cannot check span/depth ratio"
            )
        
        issues = []
        for i, span in enumerate(bridge.spans):
            ratio = span.length / bridge.girder_depth
            
            # Recommended limits
            min_ratio = 10  # Too deep
            max_ratio = 25  # Too shallow for precast
            
            if ratio < min_ratio:
                issues.append(f"Span {i+1}: Ratio {ratio:.1f} too low (girders too deep)")
            elif ratio > max_ratio:
                issues.append(f"Span {i+1}: Ratio {ratio:.1f} too high (may require prestress)")
        
        if issues:
            return self._create_result(
                CheckSeverity.WARNING,
                "Span-to-depth ratio issues",
                {'issues': issues, 'ratios': [s.length/bridge.girder_depth for s in bridge.spans]},
                "Consider adjusting girder depth or using prestressed concrete"
            )
        
        return self._create_result(
            CheckSeverity.PASS,
            f"Span-to-depth ratios within acceptable range"
        )


class BearingSpacingRule(DesignRule):
    """Check bearing spacing"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__(
            "STR-002",
            "Bearing Spacing",
            "Check spacing between bearings",
            DesignCode.IRC_83_2015,
            "Clause 5.2"
        )
    
    def check(self, bridge) -> CheckResult:
        num_girders = bridge.get_num_girders()
        if num_girders < 2:
            return self._create_result(
                CheckSeverity.INFO,
                "Single girder - bearing spacing check not applicable"
            )
        
        deck_width = bridge.deck.square_width
        spacing = deck_width / (num_girders - 1)
        
        # Check minimum spacing
        min_spacing = 2.5  # For maintenance access
        
        if spacing < min_spacing:
            return self._create_result(
                CheckSeverity.WARNING,
                f"Bearing spacing {spacing:.2f}m may limit maintenance access",
                {'spacing': spacing, 'min_recommended': min_spacing},
                "Consider increasing deck width or reducing number of girders"
            )
        
        return self._create_result(
            CheckSeverity.PASS,
            f"Bearing spacing {spacing:.2f}m is adequate"
        )


class ReinforcementSpacingRule(DesignRule):
    """Check reinforcement spacing limits"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__(
            "STR-003",
            "Reinforcement Spacing",
            "Check bar spacing meets code requirements",
            DesignCode.IRC_21_2000,
            "Clause 12.1"
        )
    
    def check(self, bridge) -> CheckResult:
        # Check deck reinforcement spacing
        deck_thickness = getattr(bridge.deck, 'thickness', 0.25)
        
        # Maximum spacing based on thickness
        max_spacing = min(3 * deck_thickness * 1000, 450)  # mm
        
        # Assuming typical spacing
        typical_spacing = 150  # mm
        
        if typical_spacing > max_spacing:
            return self._create_result(
                CheckSeverity.ERROR,
                f"Reinforcement spacing {typical_spacing}mm exceeds maximum {max_spacing:.0f}mm",
                {'spacing': typical_spacing, 'max_allowed': max_spacing},
                "Reduce bar spacing or increase deck thickness"
            )
        
        return self._create_result(
            CheckSeverity.PASS,
            f"Reinforcement spacing within limits"
        )


class CrackWidthRule(DesignRule):
    """Check crack width limits"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__(
            "STR-004",
            "Crack Width Control",
            "Check crack width under service loads",
            DesignCode.IRC_21_2000,
            "Clause 11.1"
        )
    
    def check(self, bridge) -> CheckResult:
        # Maximum crack width for exposure
        max_crack = 0.3  # mm for moderate exposure
        
        # This would require actual calculation
        # Placeholder check based on reinforcement ratio
        
        return self._create_result(
            CheckSeverity.INFO,
            "Crack width calculation requires detailed analysis",
            {'max_allowed': max_crack},
            "Perform detailed crack width calculation in structural analysis"
        )


# LOAD AND SAFETY RULES

class LoadCombinationRule(DesignRule):
    """Check load combinations"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__(
            "LOAD-001",
            "Load Combinations",
            "Verify load combinations per code",
            DesignCode.IRC_6_2017,
            "Clause 6.1"
        )
    
    def check(self, bridge) -> CheckResult:
        # Required combinations per IRC:6
        required = [
            "Dead Load + Live Load",
            "Dead Load + Live Load + Wind Load",
            "Dead Load + Live Load + Seismic",
            "Dead Load + Temperature",
            "Construction Load"
        ]
        
        # Check if bridge has load data
        if not hasattr(bridge, 'loads'):
            return self._create_result(
                CheckSeverity.WARNING,
                "Load data not defined - cannot verify combinations",
                {'required': required},
                "Define all applicable loads for the bridge"
            )
        
        return self._create_result(
            CheckSeverity.PASS,
            f"Load combinations defined"
        )


class WindLoadRule(DesignRule):
    """Check wind load consideration"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__(
            "LOAD-002",
            "Wind Load Check",
            "Verify wind load for bridge location",
            DesignCode.IRC_6_2017,
            "Clause 7.1"
        )
    
    def check(self, bridge) -> CheckResult:
        # Check if wind zone defined
        if hasattr(bridge, 'wind_zone'):
            zone = bridge.wind_zone
            
            # Basic wind speeds for zones
            wind_speeds = {
                'Zone I': 33,   # m/s
                'Zone II': 39,
                'Zone III': 44,
                'Zone IV': 47,
                'Zone V': 50,
                'Zone VI': 55
            }
            
            if zone in wind_speeds:
                speed = wind_speeds[zone]
                
                # Check span for wind sensitivity
                max_span = max(s.length for s in bridge.spans)
                
                if max_span > 60 and speed > 44:
                    return self._create_result(
                        CheckSeverity.WARNING,
                        f"Long spans ({max_span}m) in high wind zone ({zone}) require detailed analysis",
                        {'wind_speed': speed, 'max_span': max_span},
                        "Perform wind tunnel testing or dynamic analysis"
                    )
                
                return self._create_result(
                    CheckSeverity.PASS,
                    f"Wind load considered for Zone {zone} ({speed} m/s)"
                )
        
        return self._create_result(
            CheckSeverity.WARNING,
            "Wind zone not defined - using default Zone II",
            {'assumed_zone': 'Zone II', 'wind_speed': 39},
            "Confirm wind zone for bridge location"
        )


class SeismicZoneRule(DesignRule):
    """Check seismic considerations"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__(
            "LOAD-003",
            "Seismic Zone Check",
            "Verify seismic design requirements",
            DesignCode.IRC_6_2017,
            "Clause 8.1"
        )
    
    def check(self, bridge) -> CheckResult:
        # Seismic zones
        if hasattr(bridge, 'seismic_zone'):
            zone = bridge.seismic_zone
            
            if zone in ['II', 'III', 'IV', 'V']:
                # Check for seismic details
                checks = []
                
                if not hasattr(bridge, 'seismic_stoppers'):
                    checks.append("Seismic stoppers not defined")
                
                if not hasattr(bridge, 'ductility_class'):
                    checks.append("Ductility class not specified")
                
                if checks:
                    return self._create_result(
                        CheckSeverity.ERROR,
                        f"Seismic Zone {zone} requires special detailing",
                        {'zone': zone, 'missing': checks},
                        "Add seismic details per IRC:SP:114"
                    )
                
                return self._create_result(
                    CheckSeverity.PASS,
                    f"Seismic Zone {zone} requirements addressed"
                )
            
            return self._create_result(
                CheckSeverity.INFO,
                f"Seismic Zone {zone} - minimal requirements"
            )
        
        return self._create_result(
            CheckSeverity.WARNING,
            "Seismic zone not defined",
            {},
            "Define seismic zone per IS:1893"
        )


# FOUNDATION RULES

class FoundationDepthRule(DesignRule):
    """Check foundation depth"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__(
            "FND-001",
            "Foundation Depth",
            "Check minimum foundation depth",
            DesignCode.IRC_78_2014,
            "Clause 5.2"
        )
    
    def check(self, bridge) -> CheckResult:
        # Scour depth consideration
        if hasattr(bridge, 'scour_depth'):
            scour = bridge.scour_depth
            
            # Foundation must be below scour
            for support in getattr(bridge, 'supports', []):
                if hasattr(support, 'foundation_level'):
                    depth = support.get('ground_level', 100) - support['foundation_level']
                    
                    if depth < scour + 2.0:  # 2m below scour
                        return self._create_result(
                            CheckSeverity.ERROR,
                            f"Foundation depth {depth:.2f}m insufficient for scour {scour:.2f}m",
                            {'depth': depth, 'scour': scour, 'required': scour + 2.0},
                            "Lower foundation or provide scour protection"
                        )
            
            return self._create_result(
                CheckSeverity.PASS,
                f"Foundation depths adequate for scour conditions"
            )
        
        return self._create_result(
            CheckSeverity.WARNING,
            "Scour depth not calculated - cannot verify foundation depth",
            {},
            "Calculate scour depth per IRC:89"
        )


class PileCapacityRule(DesignRule):
    """Check pile capacity"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__(
            "FND-002",
            "Pile Capacity Check",
            "Verify pile capacity against loads",
            DesignCode.IRC_78_2014,
            "Clause 6.3"
        )
    
    def check(self, bridge) -> CheckResult:
        # Simplified check - would need actual capacity calculation
        if hasattr(bridge, 'pile_capacity') and hasattr(bridge, 'pile_load'):
            capacity = bridge.pile_capacity
            load = bridge.pile_load
            fos = capacity / load if load > 0 else 999
            
            if fos < 2.5:
                return self._create_result(
                    CheckSeverity.ERROR,
                    f"Pile factor of safety {fos:.2f} below minimum 2.5",
                    {'capacity': capacity, 'load': load, 'fos': fos},
                    "Increase pile length/diameter or add piles"
                )
            
            return self._create_result(
                CheckSeverity.PASS,
                f"Pile capacity adequate (FOS = {fos:.2f})"
            )
        
        return self._create_result(
            CheckSeverity.INFO,
            "Pile capacity calculation required"
        )


# CONSTRUCTION RULES

class ConstructabilityRule(DesignRule):
    """Check constructability"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__(
            "CON-001",
            "Constructability Check",
            "Verify design can be constructed",
            DesignCode.IRC_5_2015,
            "General"
        )
    
    def check(self, bridge) -> CheckResult:
        issues = []
        
        # Check for concreting access
        if hasattr(bridge, 'pier_height'):
            if bridge.pier_height > 15:
                issues.append("High piers (>15m) require special concreting equipment")
        
        # Check girder weight for transport
        if hasattr(bridge, 'girder_weight'):
            if bridge.girder_weight > 40:  # tonnes
                issues.append(f"Heavy girders ({bridge.girder_weight}t) - check transport route")
        
        # Check for formwork complexity
        if abs(bridge.skew_angle) > 20:
            issues.append("High skew requires complex formwork")
        
        if issues:
            return self._create_result(
                CheckSeverity.WARNING,
                "Constructability issues identified",
                {'issues': issues},
                "Review construction methodology and sequencing"
            )
        
        return self._create_result(
            CheckSeverity.PASS,
            "Design appears constructable"
        )


class SegmentLengthRule(DesignRule):
    """Check segment lengths for transport"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__(
            "CON-002",
            "Segment Length",
            "Check girder segment lengths for transport",
            DesignCode.IRC_5_2015,
            "General"
        )
    
    def check(self, bridge) -> CheckResult:
        max_span = max(s.length for s in bridge.spans)
        
        # Transport limits
        max_transport = 25.0  # m
        
        if max_span > max_transport:
            # Check if precast segments
            if hasattr(bridge, 'segment_length'):
                if bridge.segment_length > max_transport:
                    return self._create_result(
                        CheckSeverity.ERROR,
                        f"Segment length {bridge.segment_length}m exceeds transport limit {max_transport}m",
                        {'segment': bridge.segment_length, 'limit': max_transport},
                        "Reduce segment length or cast in-situ"
                    )
                
                return self._create_result(
                    CheckSeverity.PASS,
                    f"Segment length {bridge.segment_length}m within transport limits"
                )
            
            return self._create_result(
                CheckSeverity.WARNING,
                f"Span {max_span}m may require segmental construction",
                {'max_span': max_span, 'transport_limit': max_transport},
                "Consider segmental construction or in-situ casting"
            )
        
        return self._create_result(
            CheckSeverity.PASS,
            f"Span lengths within transportable limits"
        )


class QualityChecker:
    """
    Master quality checker that runs all design rules
    """
    
    def __init__(self, design_code: DesignCode = DesignCode.IRC_5_2015):
        self.design_code = design_code
        self.rules: List[DesignRule] = []
        self.results: List[CheckResult] = []
        
        self._register_default_rules()
    
    def _register_default_rules(self):
        """Register all default design rules"""
        # Geometric rules
        self.rules.append(SpanLengthRule())
        self.rules.append(SkewAngleRule())
        self.rules.append(DeckWidthRule())
        self.rules.append(VerticalClearanceRule())
        
        # Structural rules
        self.rules.append(SpanDepthRatioRule())
        self.rules.append(BearingSpacingRule())
        self.rules.append(ReinforcementSpacingRule())
        self.rules.append(CrackWidthRule())
        
        # Load rules
        self.rules.append(LoadCombinationRule())
        self.rules.append(WindLoadRule())
        self.rules.append(SeismicZoneRule())
        
        # Foundation rules
        self.rules.append(FoundationDepthRule())
        self.rules.append(PileCapacityRule())
        
        # Construction rules
        self.rules.append(ConstructabilityRule())
        self.rules.append(SegmentLengthRule())
    
    def add_rule(self, rule: DesignRule):
        """Add custom design rule"""
        self.rules.append(rule)
    
    def run_all_checks(self, bridge_model) -> List[CheckResult]:
        """
        Run all registered checks on bridge model
        
        Returns:
            List of check results
        """
        self.results = []
        
        for rule in self.rules:
            try:
                result = rule.check(bridge_model)
                self.results.append(result)
            except Exception as e:
                # Log error but continue
                self.results.append(CheckResult(
                    check_id=rule.rule_id,
                    check_name=rule.name,
                    category="ERROR",
                    severity=CheckSeverity.ERROR,
                    message=f"Check failed with error: {str(e)}",
                    code_reference=rule.clause
                ))
        
        return self.results
    
    def run_check(self, bridge_model, rule_id: str) -> Optional[CheckResult]:
        """Run specific check by ID"""
        for rule in self.rules:
            if rule.rule_id == rule_id:
                return rule.check(bridge_model)
        return None
    
    def get_summary(self) -> Dict:
        """Get summary of check results"""
        summary = {
            'total_checks': len(self.results),
            'by_severity': {
                'CRITICAL': 0,
                'ERROR': 0,
                'WARNING': 0,
                'INFO': 0,
                'PASS': 0
            },
            'pass_rate': 0.0,
            'categories': {}
        }
        
        for result in self.results:
            summary['by_severity'][result.severity.value] += 1
            
            cat = result.category
            if cat not in summary['categories']:
                summary['categories'][cat] = {'pass': 0, 'fail': 0}
            
            if result.severity in [CheckSeverity.PASS, CheckSeverity.INFO]:
                summary['categories'][cat]['pass'] += 1
            else:
                summary['categories'][cat]['fail'] += 1
        
        total = len(self.results)
        if total > 0:
            passed = summary['by_severity']['PASS'] + summary['by_severity']['INFO']
            summary['pass_rate'] = (passed / total) * 100
        
        return summary
    
    def get_critical_issues(self) -> List[CheckResult]:
        """Get only critical and error results"""
        return [r for r in self.results 
                if r.severity in [CheckSeverity.CRITICAL, CheckSeverity.ERROR]]
    
    def get_warnings(self) -> List[CheckResult]:
        """Get warning results"""
        return [r for r in self.results if r.severity == CheckSeverity.WARNING]
    
    def export_report(self, output_path: str, bridge_name: str = "") -> str:
        """
        Export quality check report
        
        Args:
            output_path: Output file path
            bridge_name: Bridge name for report
            
        Returns:
            Path to generated report
        """
        ext = Path(output_path).suffix.lower()
        
        if ext == '.json':
            return self._export_json(output_path, bridge_name)
        elif ext == '.html':
            return self._export_html(output_path, bridge_name)
        elif ext == '.txt':
            return self._export_text(output_path, bridge_name)
        else:
            # Default to HTML
            return self._export_html(output_path, bridge_name)
    
    def _export_json(self, output_path: str, bridge_name: str) -> str:
        """Export JSON report"""
        data = {
            'report_title': f"Quality Check Report - {bridge_name}",
            'date': datetime.now().isoformat(),
            'design_code': self.design_code.value,
            'summary': self.get_summary(),
            'results': [r.to_dict() for r in self.results]
        }
        
        with open(output_path, 'w') as f:
            json.dump(data, f, indent=2)
        
        return output_path
    
    def _export_html(self, output_path: str, bridge_name: str) -> str:
        """Export HTML report"""
        summary = self.get_summary()
        
        html = f"""<!DOCTYPE html>
<html>
<head>
    <title>Quality Check Report - {bridge_name}</title>
    <style>
        body {{ font-family: Arial, sans-serif; margin: 20px; }}
        h1 {{ color: #333; }}
        .summary {{ background: #f5f5f5; padding: 15px; border-radius: 5px; margin: 20px 0; }}
        .severity {{
            display: inline-block;
            padding: 3px 10px;
            border-radius: 3px;
            font-weight: bold;
            font-size: 12px;
        }}
        .CRITICAL {{ background: #ff4444; color: white; }}
        .ERROR {{ background: #ff8800; color: white; }}
        .WARNING {{ background: #ffcc00; color: black; }}
        .INFO {{ background: #66ccff; color: black; }}
        .PASS {{ background: #44cc44; color: white; }}
        table {{ width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-top: 20px; }}
        th {{ background: #366092; color: white; padding: 10px; text-align: left; }}
        td {{ border: 1px solid #ddd; padding: 8px; }}
        tr:nth-child(even) {{ background-color: #f9f9f9; }}
        .recommendation {{ color: #666; font-style: italic; }}
        .code-ref {{ font-size: 11px; color: #999; }}
    </style>
</head>
<body>
    <h1>Quality Check Report</h1>
    <h2>{bridge_name}</h2>
    <p>Design Code: {self.design_code.value}</p>
    <p>Date: {datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M')}</p>
    
    <div class="summary">
        <h3>Summary</h3>
        <p>Total Checks: {summary['total_checks']}</p>
        <p>Pass Rate: {summary['pass_rate']:.1f}%</p>
        <p>
            <span class="severity PASS">PASS: {summary['by_severity']['PASS']}</span>
            <span class="severity INFO">INFO: {summary['by_severity']['INFO']}</span>
            <span class="severity WARNING">WARNING: {summary['by_severity']['WARNING']}</span>
            <span class="severity ERROR">ERROR: {summary['by_severity']['ERROR']}</span>
            <span class="severity CRITICAL">CRITICAL: {summary['by_severity']['CRITICAL']}</span>
        </p>
    </div>
    
    <h3>Detailed Results</h3>
    <table>
        <tr>
            <th>Check ID</th>
            <th>Check Name</th>
            <th>Category</th>
            <th>Status</th>
            <th>Message</th>
            <th>Recommendation</th>
        </tr>
"""
        
        # Sort by severity
        severity_order = {
            CheckSeverity.CRITICAL: 0,
            CheckSeverity.ERROR: 1,
            CheckSeverity.WARNING: 2,
            CheckSeverity.INFO: 3,
            CheckSeverity.PASS: 4
        }
        
        sorted_results = sorted(self.results, 
                               key=lambda r: severity_order.get(r.severity, 5))
        
        for result in sorted_results:
            html += f"""
        <tr>
            <td>{result.check_id}</td>
            <td>{result.check_name}</td>
            <td>{result.category}</td>
            <td><span class="severity {result.severity.value}">{result.severity.value}</span></td>
            <td>{result.message}</td>
            <td class="recommendation">{result.recommendation}</td>
        </tr>"""
        
        html += """
    </table>
    
    <p class="code-ref">Report generated by Bridge Quality Checker</p>
</body>
</html>"""
        
        with open(output_path, 'w') as f:
            f.write(html)
        
        return output_path
    
    def _export_text(self, output_path: str, bridge_name: str) -> str:
        """Export text report"""
        summary = self.get_summary()
        
        lines = [
            "=" * 70,
            f"QUALITY CHECK REPORT",
            f"Bridge: {bridge_name}",
            f"Design Code: {self.design_code.value}",
            f"Date: {datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M')}",
            "=" * 70,
            "",
            "SUMMARY",
            "-" * 70,
            f"Total Checks: {summary['total_checks']}",
            f"Pass Rate: {summary['pass_rate']:.1f}%",
            f"  PASS:     {summary['by_severity']['PASS']}",
            f"  INFO:     {summary['by_severity']['INFO']}",
            f"  WARNING:  {summary['by_severity']['WARNING']}",
            f"  ERROR:    {summary['by_severity']['ERROR']}",
            f"  CRITICAL: {summary['by_severity']['CRITICAL']}",
            "",
            "DETAILED RESULTS",
            "-" * 70,
            ""
        ]
        
        for result in self.results:
            lines.extend([
                f"[{result.severity.value}] {result.check_id}: {result.check_name}",
                f"  Category: {result.category}",
                f"  Message: {result.message}",
                f"  Code Ref: {result.code_reference}",
            ])
            if result.recommendation:
                lines.append(f"  Recommendation: {result.recommendation}")
            if result.details:
                lines.append(f"  Details: {result.details}")
            lines.append("")
        
        with open(output_path, 'w') as f:
            f.write('\n'.join(lines))
        
        return output_path


class DesignValidator:
    """
    Advanced design validation with parameter tracking
    """
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.parameters: Dict[str, DesignParameter] = {}
        self.checks: List[Callable] = []
    
    def add_parameter(self, name: str, value: float, unit: str,
                     min_val: Optional[float] = None,
                     max_val: Optional[float] = None):
        """Add design parameter"""
        self.parameters[name] = DesignParameter(
            name=name, value=value, unit=unit,
            min_allowed=min_val, max_allowed=max_val
        )
    
    def validate_all(self) -> Dict[str, Tuple[bool, Optional[str]]]:
        """Validate all parameters"""
        results = {}
        for name, param in self.parameters.items():
            results[name] = param.check()
        return results
    
    def get_parameter_status(self) -> List[Dict]:
        """Get status of all parameters"""
        return [
            {
                'name': p.name,
                'value': p.value,
                'unit': p.unit,
                'status': p.status(),
                'limits': f"{p.min_allowed or 'N/A'} - {p.max_allowed or 'N/A'}"
            }
            for p in self.parameters.values()
        ]


# INTEGRATION AND USAGE
def run_quality_check(bridge_model, 
                     design_code: DesignCode = DesignCode.IRC_5_2015,
                     output_path: str = None) -> QualityChecker:
    """
    Run complete quality check on bridge model
    
    Args:
        bridge_model: The bridge model to check
        design_code: Design code to check against
        output_path: Optional output path for report
        
    Returns:
        QualityChecker instance with results
    """
    checker = QualityChecker(design_code)
    results = checker.run_all_checks(bridge_model)
    
    # Print summary
    summary = checker.get_summary()
    print(f"\nQuality Check Complete")
    print(f"Total Checks: {summary['total_checks']}")
    print(f"Pass Rate: {summary['pass_rate']:.1f}%")
    print(f"  PASS: {summary['by_severity']['PASS']}")
    print(f"  INFO: {summary['by_severity']['INFO']}")
    print(f"  WARNING: {summary['by_severity']['WARNING']}")
    print(f"  ERROR: {summary['by_severity']['ERROR']}")
    print(f"  CRITICAL: {summary['by_severity']['CRITICAL']}")
    
    # Show critical issues
    critical = checker.get_critical_issues()
    if critical:
        print(f"\n⚠️  {len(critical)} Critical/Error Issues:")
        for issue in critical:
            print(f"  - {issue.check_id}: {issue.message}")
            if issue.recommendation:
                print(f"    → {issue.recommendation}")
    
    # Generate report
    if output_path:
        report_path = checker.export_report(
            output_path,
            getattr(bridge_model, 'name', 'Bridge')
        )
        print(f"\nReport saved: {report_path}")
    
    return checker


# USAGE EXAMPLE
if __name__ == "__main__":
    from skew_bridge_engine import create_skew_bridge
    
    # Create bridge
    bridge = create_skew_bridge(
        name="Chambakkara Bridge",
        chainage=600.0,
        span_lengths=[25.0, 30.0, 25.0],
        deck_width=12.0,
        skew_angle=15.0
    )
    
    # Add additional attributes for checking
    bridge.girder_depth = 1.5
    bridge.pier_height = 10.0
    bridge.wind_zone = "Zone III"
    bridge.seismic_zone = "III"
    bridge.hfl = 95.0  # High flood level
    bridge.deck.soffit_level = 102.0  # 7m clearance
    
    # Run quality check
    checker = run_quality_check(
        bridge,
        design_code=DesignCode.IRC_5_2015,
        output_path="quality_report.html"
    )
    
    # Also generate text report
    checker.export_report("quality_report.txt", bridge.name)
    
    print("\nQuality check complete!")
```

This **Quality Check Module** provides comprehensive design validation with:

## Check Categories

| Category | Checks | Description |
|----------|--------|-------------|
| **Geometric (GEO)** | 4 checks | Span lengths, skew, width, clearance |
| **Structural (STR)** | 4 checks | Span/depth, bearings, reinforcement, cracks |
| **Loading (LOAD)** | 3 checks | Combinations, wind, seismic |
| **Foundation (FND)** | 2 checks | Depth, pile capacity |
| **Construction (CON)** | 2 checks | Buildability, transport |

## Severity Levels

| Level | Action Required | Color |
|-------|----------------|-------|
| **CRITICAL** | Design unsafe - immediate revision | 🔴 Red |
| **ERROR** | Code violation - must fix | 🟠 Orange |
| **WARNING** | Review recommended | 🟡 Yellow |
| **INFO** | Informational | 🔵 Blue |
| **PASS** | Check passed | 🟢 Green |

## Design Rules Implemented

### Geometric Rules
- **GEO-001**: Span length validation (6-50m for simple spans)
- **GEO-002**: Skew angle check (max 45°, warning >30°)
- **GEO-003**: Deck width for traffic (min 7.5m for 2 lanes)
- **GEO-004**: Vertical clearance over HFL (min 5.5m)

### Structural Rules
- **STR-001**: Span-to-depth ratio (10-25 range)
- **STR-002**: Bearing spacing for maintenance (min 2.5m)
- **STR-003**: Reinforcement spacing limits
- **STR-004**: Crack width control

### Load Rules
- **LOAD-001**: Load combination verification
- **LOAD-002**: Wind zone consideration
- **LOAD-003**: Seismic detailing requirements

### Foundation Rules
- **FND-001**: Foundation depth below scour
- **FND-002**: Pile capacity factor of safety (min 2.5)

### Construction Rules
- **CON-001**: Constructability assessment
- **CON-002**: Segment length for transport (max 25m)

## Output Reports

| Format | Features |
|--------|----------|
| **HTML** | Color-coded, interactive, sortable table |
| **JSON** | Machine-readable for integration |
| **Text** | Simple console output |

## Usage Example

```python
# Run all checks
checker = QualityChecker(DesignCode.IRC_5_2015)
results = checker.run_all_checks(bridge)

# Get summary
summary = checker.get_summary()
print(f"Pass rate: {summary['pass_rate']:.1f}%")

# Get critical issues
critical = checker.get_critical_issues()
for issue in critical:
    print(f"FIX REQUIRED: {issue.message}")
    print(f"  → {issue.recommendation}")

# Export report
checker.export_report("quality_report.html", "Bridge Name")
```
